aracaṉukkup periya māḷikaikaḷ, pala maṉaiviyar, vilai uyarnta uṭaikaḷ, maṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ, poṉ, veḷḷi, muttu, irattiṉam, cempu, irumpu, tantam, cantaṉamaram, māṉtōl mutaliya pala celvaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṉ vēṭṭai nāykaḷōṭu vēṭṭaiyāṭac ceṉṟāṉ; matu arunti ṉāṉ; peṇkaḷum (perumpālum naṭaṉamātar) matuvaruntiṉar. aracaṉatu pūṅkāvaṉam patiṉeṭṭu muḻam uyarnta matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭatu. ataṉ orupakkattē periyakatavukaḷ iruntaṉa. aṅku aracaṉum tēviyarum nīrāṭik kaḷikkum kēṇiyiruntatu. aṅku avaṉukkeṉa amaikkappaṭṭa irukkaiyil aracaṉ iruntāṉ appoḻutu avaṉetirē naṭaṉamātar nāṭṭiyam āṭiṉar. cilar iṉiya vāttiyaṅkaḷai olittaṉar. vāttiyaṅ kaḷai iyakkip pāṭutal ciṟanta poḻutupōkkākak karutappaṭṭatu.
nālvakaip paṭaikaḷ
aracaṉiṭattil tēr yāṉai kutirai kālāḷ eṉṉum nālvakaip paṭaikaḷ iruntaṉa. tērkaḷiṟ perumpālum iraṇṭu kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avai mītu veḷḷaik kuṭaikaḷum palaniṟak koṭikaḷum nāṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kaitērnta pākaṉ tērai yōṭṭiṉāṉ. avaṉ kavaca maṇintu kaiyil villaip piṭittiruntāṉ.
cila nēraṅkaḷil aracaṉē paṭaiyai naṭattic ceṉṟāṉ. ak kālaṅ kaḷil avaṉ yāṉai mītiruntu pakaivarait tākkiṉāṉ. yāṉaikaḷ perumpālum matiṟ katavukaḷait tiṟappataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. aṟupatu vayatu vantatum uṟutiyāṉa kompuṭaiyatumākiya yāṉai pōrukkuc ciṟantateṉak karutappaṭṭatu. pākar kuttuk kōlum tōṭṭi yum vaittiruntārkaḷ. viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷaṇintu vāḷaik kaiyiṟpiṭitta aracar yāṉai mītiruntārkaḷ. valiya maṟavar koṭikaḷai vīcik koṇṭu pakkattē iruntaṉar. yāṉaikaḷaip paḻakkuvataṟkeṉat taṉi āṭkaḷ aracaṉiṭamiruntārkaḷ.
kutiraikaḷukku irumpuk kavacaṅkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. kutirai vīrar villum vāḷum vaittiruntārkaḷ.1 kālāṭkaḷ vil, vāḷ, vēl, kēṭakam mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar; avarkaḷ kavacam aṇintiruntaṉar.
paṭaiyiṉ piṉṉē taccar āyutaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟārkaḷ.2 caṅku mēḷam mutaliyavaikaḷai olikkum vāttiyakkārarum ceṉṟaṉar.
paṭai pala pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ellāp paṭaikkum talaivaṉāka oru cēṉāpatiyiruntāṉ. avaṉ perumpālum araca kuṭumpattiliruntu terinteṭukkappaṭṭāṉ. amaitiyāṉa kālaṅkaḷil avaṉ nītipatiyākak kaṭamai pārttāṉ.
perumpālum, pōr, talainakaraic cuṟṟi naṭantatu. paṭaikaḷ kūṭāraṅkaḷiṟṟaṅkiṉa. avai nirai niraiyākavum oḻuṅkākavum ceṉṟaṉa. pōr toṭaṅkuvataṉ muṉ aracaṉ allatu aracaṉōṭu ceṉṟa pulavaṉ paṭaikku ūkkam eḻakkūṭiya coṟpoḻivu ceytāṉ. ceṉṟa paṭai, nakarai muṟṟukaiyiṭṭu māṟṟaracaṉaip paṇiyumpaṭi allatu pōr toṭukkumpaṭi tūṇṭum. paṇitaṟku oruppaṭāta māṟṟaracaṉ pōr toṭuppāṉ. ik kāraṇam paṟṟik kōṭṭai mikavum valuppaṭuttappaṭṭiruntatu. kōṭṭai yiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē kōpuraṅkaḷum katavukaḷum iruntaṉa. matilaic cuṟṟi akaḻ iruntatu. atu pakaivarai aṇukātapaṭi taṭuttatu. matilmītu pakaivar kāṇātu niṉṟu pārkkakkūṭiya kōpuraṅkaḷiruntaṉa. muṟṟukaiyiṭuvōr akaḻiyait tūrttu allatu ataṉai nīntik kaṭakka muyaṉṟaṉar. akaḻi āḻamuṭaiyatāyum pāmpu mutalaikaḷ uṟaivatāyum iruntatu. ataṉaik kaṭappatu eḷitaṉṟu. kōṭṭai matil mītu niṟpavarkaḷ urukkiya ulōkaṅ kaḷaiyum, kotikkum eṇṇeyaiyum, cūṭāṉa kal cēṟu eṉpavai kaḷaiyum pakaivar mītu vīci eṟintārkaḷ. akaḻai nīntik kaṭakka muyaṉṟa pōtu matiliṉ mītuḷḷōr ampu, īṭṭi, vēl mutaliyavaikaḷaip pakai varmītu corintaṉar. kōṭṭaiyuḷ iruppōr, tam muyaṟci payaṉaḷiyā viṭil paṇintārkaḷ. muṟṟukaik kālaṅkaḷil uṇavukkuṟaivu nērātapaṭi vēṇṭiya uṇavuvakaikaḷ uḷḷē cēmittu vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. irukaṭci1 oṟṟarkaḷum etiriyiṉ maṟaivukaḷai aṟiya muyaṉṟārkaḷ. ceytikaḷ ōlai naṟukkukaḷil eḻuti ampiṟ kaṭṭi eytu aṟivikkappaṭṭaṉa. ceyti koṇṭu varupavaṉai allatu tūtaṉaik kollutal kūṭāteṉpatu nīṇṭa nāḷ vaḻakku. pōriṟ kāyappaṭṭavarkaḷ palakaikaḷ mītu vaittu eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭuc cikiccai ceyyappaṭṭaṉar. veṟṟipeṟṟa aracaṉ taṉatu nakarattukku āṭamparattōṭu ceṉṟāṉ. vīrar viruntu koṇṭāṭi ṉar. irāk kālaṅkaḷil nakark katavukaḷ aṭaikkappaṭṭaṉa. nakar kāvalar nakaraip paṟaikoṭṭik kāval ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ civanta pūmālaikaḷai aṇintiruntaṉar.
palavakait toḻil purivōr
makkaḷ perumpālum vēṭṭaiyāṭiṉārkaḷ. ūṉ peritum virumpappaṭṭatu. cuvaiyuḷḷa iṟaicciyaip peṟumporuṭṭum aracaṉ vēṭṭaiyāṭac ceṉṟāṉ. vēṭṭaiyāṭuvataiyē vāḻkkaiyākak koṇṭa kūṭṭattiṉarum palar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ vil, ampu, valai kaḷuṭaṉ vilaṅkukaḷait tēṭi malaikaḷil alaintārkaḷ; tōlāṟ piṉṉiya valaikaḷai ēṟṟi māṉkaḷaip piṭittārkaḷ; tōlukkākap puliyum, tantattukkāka yāṉaiyum vēṭṭaiyāṭappaṭṭaṉa.
vaḷarppataṟkākavum, iṟaiccikkākavum kuruvikaḷ piṭittu viṟkap paṭṭaṉa. cila kirāmaṅkaḷil ellōrum kuruvikaḷ piṭippōrākavēyirun tārkaḷ. avai valaiyēṟṟiyum poṟivaittum piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. valai kutiraivālmayirāṟ piṉṉit taṭiyiṟ kaṭṭi nilattil vaikkappaṭṭatu. cilar paḻakkappaṭṭa paṭcikaḷiṉ utaviyāl avaikaḷaip piṭittārkaḷ. paḻak kappaṭṭa kuruvikaḷiṉ kuralaik kēṭṭu avviṉappaṭcikaḷ vantu kūṭiṉa. appoḻutu avaikaḷ valaiyil akappaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
āṟṟōraṅkaḷilum kaṭalōraṅkaḷilum vāḻntōr mīṉkaḷai mikutiyum uṇṭārkaḷ. mīṉkaḷaip piṭippataṟku valai, tūṇṭil, kūṭu eṉpaṉa payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
aḻakiya paṭṭāṭaikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ carikait talaip pākai tarittāṉ. paṭṭattu yāṉai carikait tuṇikaḷāl alaṅkarikkap paṭṭatu. makkaḷ perumpālum pañcu āṭai uṭuttārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ pañcu āṭṭi nūl nūṟṟaṉar. cīlai neyvōrum cīlaikaḷukkuc cāyantōyp pōrum iruntaṉar.
poṉ veḷḷi irattiṉakkal muttu mutaliyavaikaḷāl aḻakiya nakaikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ nakaikaḷukku irattiṉakkal patikkappaṭṭatu. vaḷaiyal, mōtiram, mālai, kuṇṭalam, mēkalai, taṇṭai, koṇṭai yūci mutaliya palavakai aṇikaḷ vaḻakkiliruntaṉa. aracaṉ araṇmaṉai yil veḷḷi taṅkak kalaṅkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ. nāṟkālikaḷ, paṭukkaikaḷ, ciṅkācaṉaṅkaḷ poṉṉaḻutti naṉku aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aḻuttañ ceyyappaṭṭa ulōkat takaṭukaḷ mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭi kaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. uṇavaruntum kalaṅkaḷ veḷḷiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pāṉai, taṭṭu, caṭṭi mutaliyaṉa cempu pittaḷai veṇkalam eṉpavaikaḷālāṉavai. irumpu urukku ākkappaṭṭu, ataṉāl arivāḷ, uḷi, nāvitaṉ katti, cuttiyal, pōrkkaruvikaḷ, kavacam, ūci, icaikkaruvikaḷiṉ narampukaḷ eṉpaṉa ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. tantattilum ariya vēlaikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
kuyavar nakarukku veḷiyē vāḻntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ aḻakiya caṭṭi pāṉai cāṭi mutaliyaṉa ceytaṉar. kottar ceṅkalliṉāl aḻakiya vīṭu kaḷaik kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. vīṭukaḷukkuk kaimaraṅkaḷ tūṇkaḷ katavukaḷ cāḷa raṅkaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷai marattiṉāṟ ceytutavum taccarkaḷiruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷāl aḻakiya nāṟkāli, mukkāli, kaṭṭil, ēṇi, pēḻai mutaliya vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. kayiṟu, ceruppu, pai, kēṭakam mutaliyavaṟṟait tōliṉāṟ ceyyum toḻilāḷar palar iruntaṉar.
mūlikaikaḷaip paiyiliṭṭukkoṇṭu tiriyum maruttuvar, mūk killātōrkkup poymūkku vaippōr, maṉaikku iṭaṅkuṟippōr, uṟuppukaḷai nōkki makkaḷiṉ naṉmai tīmaikaḷaik kūṟuvōr, vāyppuk kūṟuvōr, kōḷnilaiyaṟintu payaṉkūṟuvōr, pēyōṭṭuvōr, kāṇātu ōṭiyavarkaḷiṉ aṭiyait toṭarntu avarkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭippōr, pāṭṭukkaḷ pāṭi mikunta poruḷīṭṭum pulavar, kuraṅkaip paḻakki ata ṉutaviyāl pāmpaip piṭippōr, kīriyaip paḻakkuvōr, vāttiyakārar, muracaṟaivōr, kūttar, naṭikar, māyavittai kāṭṭuvōr, kampaṅkūttar, pāvaiyāṭṭuvōr mutaliya pala toḻilāḷarum vāḻntaṉar.
takappaṉ ceyta toḻilaiyē makaṉum ceytāṉ. itaṉāl ov voru kūṭṭattiṉarum avaravar ceyyum toḻilāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. vēṭar, mīṉ piṭippōr, taccar, kuyavar, nāvitar, terukkūṭṭuvōr kīḻ makkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kūli vēlai ceyvōrum aṭimaikaḷum irunta ṉar. vaṟiyavarkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷ nakarppuṟattē iruntaṉa. aṭimaikaḷ vīṭṭi lēyē iruntu vīṭṭuvēlaikaḷaic ceytaṉar. uṇavu camaittal, taṇṇī reṭuttal, māvaraittal, vayalaik kāvalkāttal, vayalukku uṇavu eṭuttuppōtal, piccai koṭuttal, vīṭṭut talaivaṉukkup puttikūṟutal, uṇṇum taṭṭukaḷaik kaḻuvutal, vīṭṭiluḷḷōr uṇavaruntumpōtu viciṟiyāl viciṟutal pōṉṟaṉa avarkaḷ puriyum vēlaikaḷākum.
cēmippu
mācā eṉṉum poṉ nāṇayaṅkaḷum cempu nāṇayaṅkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa. celvar tamatu paṇattaip poṉṉāka māṟṟi uṟutiyāṉa peṭṭi kaḷilum pātukāppāṉa aṟaikaḷilum pūṭṭivaittaṉar. aṇikaḷum paṇa mum perumpālum talaiyaṇaikaḷukkuḷ vaittuk kākkappaṭṭaṉa.
uṇavu
potumakkaḷ uyarnta uṇavaik koḷḷavillai. kañci, paṇiyāram, kīraivakai, kāykaṟikaḷ, pāl mutaliyaṉa potumakkaḷiṉ uṇavukaḷ. celvar vellam, tēṉ, ney, pāl mutaliyaṉa peytu camaitta kañciyai uṇṭaṉar. kālaiyil oṉṟum mālaiyil oṉṟumāka iru uṇavukaḷ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
uṭai
pañcu āṭaikaḷ perumpālum uṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. āṭavar arai yil oṉṟum tōḷil oṉṟum talaiyiloṉṟumāka mūṉṟu tuṇikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avarkaḷ araiyil paikaḷaikkaṭṭi avaikaḷil vilaiyuyarnta poruḷkaḷai vaittiruntaṉar.
vīṭukaḷ
potumakkaḷ, vīṭukaḷaic ceṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum katavukaḷ iruntaṉa. cāḷaraṅkaḷ vītiyai nōkki yiruntaṉa. katavukaḷ tāḻiṭappaṭṭaṉa. periya vīṭukaḷ cāntupūci veḷḷai yaṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.
ullāca vāḻkkai
makkaḷ ullāca vāḻkkaiyai virumpiṉar. āṭavar mayiraiyum tāṭiyaiyum kattarittu maṭṭañ ceytārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ aḻakiya nakai kaḷai aṇintaṉar; maṇapporuḷkaḷaiyum pūkkaḷaiyum peritum payaṉ paṭuttiṉar.
pirāmaṇar
oḻukkamuṭaiya pirāmaṇar peritum matippup peṟṟaṉar. uṇavu aruntumpaṭi avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷukku aḻaikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. nīrāṭiya piṉ avarkaḷ vīṭṭukku vantu vīṭṭut talaivaṉ cempiṟkoṭukkum nīrai vāṅki mukattaik kaḻuviya piṉ iruntu uṇṭārkaḷ. uṇṭu muṭintatum vīṭṭut talaivaṉ koṭukkum takkaṇaiyaip peṟṟu maṅkalaṅ kūṟic ceṉṟaṉar.
ulakappirāmaṇar eṉa iṉṉoru vakaiyiṉar iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ tamakku vitikkappaṭṭa oḻukkaṅkaḷil nillātavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷuṭ cilar, maruttuvarākavum, tūtu pōvōrākavum, vēḷāṇmai ceyvōrākavum, āṭu vaḷarppōrākavum, vāyppuk kūṟuvōrākavum, vāḷ, kōṭari, kēṭakam, kaṇṭakōṭarikaḷait tāṅki viyāpārak kūṭṭaṅkaḷukku muṉ celvōrākavum iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ tam makaḷiraip poṉṉukku viṟpar; tām virumpiya ettoḻilaiyum purivar.
caṇṭāḷar mutaliyōr
kūttāṭikaḷum vāttiyakkārarum kirāmaṅkaḷtōṟum ceṉṟu kampaṅkūttu māyavittai purintum, pāvai yāṭṭiyum poruḷ peṟṟaṉar. pāmpāṭṭikaḷum ivvāṟē ūr ūrākat tirintaṉar. caṇṭāḷar nakarp puṟattē vāḻntaṉar. ivarkaḷ tīṇṭappaṭātavarāvar. ivarkaḷ tamakkuḷ oruvakaik kuḻūukkuṟiyaip pēciṉar. piṇañ cuṭutal vītikaḷaip perukkutal mutaliya vēlaikaḷai ivarkaḷ purintaṉar.
makaḷir
icaiyum naṭaṉamum makaḷirkkuc ciṟanta kalvikaḷākak karutap paṭṭaṉa. cuyamvaram, kāntaruvam, peṟṟōrāl oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭuvatu eṉa maṇam mūvakaippaṭṭatu. nalla mukūrtta vēḷaiyil maṇamakaṉ peṇ vīṭṭukku vantu peṇṇai maṇantāṉ. araciyar aracakumāriyar pirapukkaḷiṉ makaḷir eṉpōr maṭṭum mūṭappaṭṭa vaṇṭikaḷiṟ ceṉṟārkaḷ. makaḷir paṭṭu, pañcu, caṇal āṭaikaḷai uṭuttārkaḷ. mālai, kuṇṭalam, kātōlai, mēkalai, kaivaḷai, kālvaḷai, kaṭayam pōṉṟa nakaikaḷ aṇintu koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. kūntal palavāṟu vakirntu piṉṉi muṭikkappaṭṭatu. nakaṅkaḷ civappūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. uyar kuṭumpa makaḷir yāṉait tantak kaippiṭiyuṭaiya mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭikaḷaiyum, ceruppaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. mēlvakuppup peṇkaḷait tavira maṟṟavarkaḷ tamatu vāḻkkaiyiṉ poruṭṭup palavakai vēlaikaḷaic ceytaṉar. kirāmap peṇ vayalaik kāval kāttal, necavu toṭarpāṉa vēlaikaḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷaip purintāḷ. cilar pū viṟṟaṉar. vaṟiya peṇkaḷ vīṭukaḷil vēlaikkāriyarākavum tātiyarākavum amarntaṉar. naṭaṉa mātar parattaiyarkaḷāka vāḻntaṉar. piccai eṭuttu uṇṇum peṇ tuṟavikaḷum iruntārkaḷ.
vāṉa ārāycci
vāṉanūl cōtiṭam eṉpaṉa vaḷarcciyaṭaintiruntaṉa. kōḷkaḷiṉ celavukaḷ muṉṉamē aṟintu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. tiṅkaḷil muyal iruppatu tiṅkaḷai irāku viḻuṅkuvatu mutaliya nampikkai iruntatu.1
icai
icaikkaruvikaḷuḷ vīṇai mikavum mukkiyamuṭaiyatu. āṭavarum makaḷirum virumpi atiṟ payiṉṟaṉar. atu taṉiyē allatu pāṭṭu kaḷuṭaṉ vācikkappaṭṭatu. itu kaiviraliṉ nuṉikaḷāl narampukaḷait taṭaviyum teṟittum mīṭṭappaṭum. mayiṟpeṭaikaḷum cēvalkaḷum kaikoṭṭum tāḷattukku āṭumpaṭi payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.
ōviyamum, ciṟpamum
cuvarkaḷilum palakaikaḷ mītum ōviyaṅkaḷ varaiyappaṭṭaṉa. acantāk kukai ōviyaṅkaḷē ak kālac cittiraṅkaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ciṟuvar viḷaiyāṭum aḻakiya pāvaikaḷum, maṟṟum piṟa viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cila araṇmaṉaikaḷ oṟṟait tūṇuṭaiyaṉa vākavum, cila eṭṭuc caturamuṭaiya āyirantūṇuṭaiyaṉavākavum kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa. periya nilavaṟaikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. iṟantavarkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷaip putaittu avai mītu kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ eḻuppappaṭṭaṉa. aṅkup pūmālaikaḷ tūkki naṟumpukai kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
cila nampikkaikaḷum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum
matiyam, amāvācaik kālaṅkaḷil makkaḷ viratamiruntārkaḷ. oruvaṉ tummiṉāl ‘cāvillai’ allatu ‘nīṭu vāḻka’ eṉṟu collap paṭṭatu. oruvarai niṟaivāka oru karumattaic ceytāl avaṉ mutukiṟ ṟaṭṭi ataṉ makiḻcci aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. peṇṇiṉ valakkaṇ tuṭippatu tīmai nērvataṟku aṟikuṟi eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. peṇkaḷ tamakkut tuṉpam iḻaittavarkaḷait tiṭṭiṉārkaḷ. kuruviṉ peyaraic colvatu pāvam eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. kuruvai mūṉṟu muṟai valam vantu viḻuntu kumpiṭuvatu potuvaḻakku. maṉitaṉ nūṟu allatu nūṟṟirupatu āṇṭu vāḻvāṉ eṉa nampappaṭṭatu. vāyiliṟ kataviṭappaṭṭa cuṭalaikaḷ iruntaṉa. pantu viḷaiyāṭṭuc ciṟuvarāl mikavum virumpappaṭṭatu.
ūñcalil āṭuvatai aracarum virumpiṉārkaḷ. aracarum peru makkaḷum āṟukaḷilum kuḷaṅkaḷilum nīntik kaḷittaṉar. viḻākkaḷ paṟai yaṟaintu makkaḷukku aṟivikkappaṭṭaṉa. viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil vaṟiyavar kaḷum putu āṭai uṭuttup pūmālaiyaṇintu naṟumaṇam pūci maṉaiviya ruṭaṉ matuvaruntik kaḷittaṉar. kārttikait tiṅkaḷiṉ mattiyil varum viḻā mikac ciṟappuṭaiyatu. aṉṟu aracaṉ nakaraic cuṟṟi ulāvaruvāṉ. vaḻakkamāṉa matukkaṭaikaḷait tavira viḻākkālaṅkaḷil putiya matuk kaṭaikaḷ tiṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. makaḷir maintar ciṟuvar eṉpōr tiraṇṭu niṉṟu kūttu, vāttiyam, pāṭṭu, yāṉaippōr, āṭṭuppōr, maṟpōr mutaliya vaikaḷaip pārttu āravārañ ceytaṉar.
maravaṇakkam
makkaḷ maraṅkaḷait teyvamāka vaṇaṅkavillai; āṉāl maraṅkaḷiluṟaiyum teyvaṅkaḷai vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ. it teyvaṅkaḷukkuc cilavēḷai narapalikaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. marattil mālaiyum kompu kaḷil viḷakkukaḷum tūkkappaṭṭaṉa. marattiṉ aṭiyil āṭu kōḻi paṉṟi mutaliyaṉa paliyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. uyirp palikaḷ niṟuttappaṭṭapiṉ irattat tiṟkup patil marattil kuṅkumam pūcappaṭukiṉṟatāyiruttal kūṭum.
taṇṭaṉai
ak kālat taṇṭaṉai, taṇṭam, ciṟai, uṟuppukaḷaik kaḷaital, cirattaik kōṭariyāṟ paṭṭaṭaiyil vaittut taṟittal pōṉṟaṉa.

iyal - 4
puttarukkup piṟpaṭṭa intiyā
mekastiṉ eṉṉum kirēkkar cantirakupta mayūraṉiṉ araṇmaṉai yil ki. mu. 302 mutal cilakālam taṅkiyiruntār. ivar intiyāvaippaṟṟiya oru nūl eḻutiyiruntār. atiṟ kāṇappaṭum cila pakutikaḷ akkāla intiya makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷai naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa. avar kūṟuvaṉa:
intiya makkaḷiṉ ēḻu pirivukaḷ
intiyar ēḻu vakaiyākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉar. tattuva cāttirat tiṟṟērnta oru kūṭṭattiṉar mutaṟ kulattiṉarākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. maṟṟaik kūṭṭattiṉaraiviṭa ivarkaḷiṉ tokai kuṟaivāyiruntatu. ivarkaḷ ecamāṉarākavō vēlaikkārarākavō irukkavillai. maṟu ulakattiṟkuriya poruḷkaḷai ellām ivarkaḷ naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉa makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. ākavē, avarkaḷ ivarkaḷaip piṇaccaṭaṅ kukaḷ purivataṟkum uriya kālaṅkaḷiṟ kaṭavuṭpaliyaic celuttuvataṟkum aḻaittārkaḷ. iv vakaik kiriyaikaḷ purivataṟkuk kaimmāṟāka ivarkaḷ mikunta poruḷaip peṟṟaṉar. potumakkaḷ ivarkaḷāl periya naṉmai kaḷai aṭaintaṉar. āṇṭiṉ ārampakālattil makkaḷ kūṭṭaṅkūṭi iruppārkaḷ. appoḻutu ivarkaḷ av vāṇṭil nikaḻavirukkum piṇi, pañcam, kāṟṟu, maḻai ākiyavaṟṟai aṟivippārkaḷ. ivaṟṟai aṟintukoṇṭa makkaḷum, aracaṉum nikaḻa virukkum tuṉpaṅkaḷukku āyattamākaviruntaṉar. tām kūṟiya varuṅkālap palaṉkaḷiṟ poytta tattuva cāttirikaḷ tamatu āyuḷiṉ mītikkālattai nikaḻkāla varuṅkāla palaṉkaḷaik kūṟātu mauṉamākak kaḻittaṉar.
ivarkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷōr uḻutūṇ vāḻkkaiyar. ivarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷilum kūṭiya tokaiyiṉar. ivarkaḷ pōr mutaliya potuk karumaṅkaḷukkuriya kālattait tavira mīti nēram muḻuvataiyum nilattaip paṇpaṭuttip payiriṭuvatiṟ celaviṭukiṉṟaṉar. nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭai eṭuttu varuvōr ivarkaḷait tuṉpuṟuttuvatillai. potu naṉmaik kuriyōr eṉṉum eṇṇattiṉāl ivarkaḷ ellā āpattukkaḷi ṉiṉṟum kākkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. pakaivarāl aḻikkappaṭātaṉavum, mikunta viḷaivaik koṭuppaṉavumākiya nilaṅkaḷiṉ payaṉ nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ uṇavukkup pōtumāṉatāyiruntatu. ivarkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷōṭu nāṭṭai uṟaiviṭamākak koṇṭaṉar. nakara vāḻkkaiyai ivarkaḷ virumpiṉārka ḷallar. intiya nāṭu muḻumaiyilumuḷḷa nāṭu aracaṉukkuc contamāṉatu. evarēṉum contamāṉa nilam vaittirukka aṉumatikkappaṭavillai. nila variyait tavira viḷaivil nālil oṉṟum aracaṉukku uriyatāyiruntatu.
mūṉṟāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr iṭaiyar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum āṭu māṭukaḷai mēyppar. ivarkaḷ nāṭṭilāvatu nakaraṅkaḷilāvatu nilaiyāka vāḻātu kūṭāraṅkaḷiṉ kīḻt taṅkiṉar; vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum poṟi vaittum tuṭṭa vilaṅkukaḷaip piṭittaṉar.
nāṉkāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr ciṟpikaḷ. ivarkaḷiṟ cilar āyutaṅ kaḷaic ceytaṉar. cilar payiriṭuvōrukkum piṟarukkum vēṇṭiya karuvi kaḷaic ceytukoṭuttu avarkaḷukkut tuṇai purintaṉar.
aintāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr paṭaiyāccikaḷ. ivarkaḷ pōr ceyyum muṟaiyiṟ payiṉṟiruntaṉar. tokaiyaḷavil ivarkaḷ iraṇṭāva tāka viruppar. pōr illāta kālaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ poḻutu pōkku kaḷilum cōmputtaṉattilum kālattaip pōkkiṉar.
āṟāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr kaṇkāṇimār. nāṭṭil naṭakkum ellāvaṟṟaiyum aṟintu colvatu ivarkaḷukku vēlaiyāyiruntatu; aracaṉ illāta viṭaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ tām aṟintavaṟṟai niyāyam vaḻaṅkum atikārikaḷiṭam kūṟiṉar.
ēḻāvatu paṭiyiluḷḷōr potuk karumaṅkaḷil ālōcaṉai kūṟum kūṭṭattiṉar. maṟṟavarkaḷ ellōraiyumviṭa ivarkaḷ ciṟu kūṭṭat tiṉar. uyarnta oḻukkattukkum aṟivukkumāka ivarkaḷ matikkappaṭuki ṟārkaḷ. aracaṉatu karuvūlattukkum, aracaṉukku ālōcaṉai kūṟutaṟkum ik kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟutāṉ cilar terinteṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. paṭaittalai vaṉum talaimai nītipatiyum iv vakuppaic cērntavarkaḷē.
intiya aracāṅkam ivvāṟu pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. tattam kūṭṭattaik kaṭantu maṇam muṭikka evarum aṉumatikkappaṭuvatillai. tamatu vakuppukkuriyatallāta toḻil purivataṟkum oruvarum aṉumatikkap paṭavillai. utāraṇamākap pōrvīraṉ orupōtum payiriṭuvōṉāka varamuṭiyātu; ciṟpi aṟivaṉāy (tattuva cāttiri) varamuṭiyātu.
makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkam
intiya makkaḷ ellōrum ceṭṭāṉa vāḻkkaiyuṭaiyavar. ciṟappāka, kūṭāraṅkaḷil taṅkumpōtu ivarkaḷ ivvāṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. oḻuṅkaṟṟa kūṭṭattiṉarai avarkaḷ veṟuttaṉar. ākavē avarkaḷ oḻuṅkāka naṭantukoṇṭaṉar. kaḷavu ciṟitāka iruntatu. cantirakuptaṉiṉ kūṭārattil 400,000 vīrar taṅkiṉārkaḷ. eppoḻutāvatu 200 tiṟāmac1 poṉṉukku atikamākak kaḷavu naṭantatākak kūṟappaṭavillai. avar kaḷukku eḻuttu mūlam piramāṇaṅkaḷ kiṭaiyā.
avarkaḷ eḻuttaippaṟṟi aṟiyār. koṭukkal vāṅkalkaḷ ñāpa kattaip poṟuttiruntaṉa. āṭamparamallāta vāḻkkai naṭattiyapōtum avarkaḷ makiḻcciyuṭaṉ iruntārkaḷ. vēḷvikaḷilaṉṟi avarkaḷ matu aruntavillai. matu nelliliruntu vaṭikkappaṭṭatu. ariciyāṟ camaitta kañci avarkaḷiṉ piratāṉa uṇavu. avarkaḷiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷaik kavaṉikkum pōtu avarkaḷ, arumaiyāka vaḻakkukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ. koṭukkal vāṅ kalkaḷ oruvar maṟṟavariṭattiṟ koṇṭa nampikkaiyaik koṇṭu naṭai peṟṟamaiyāl pattiraṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭavillai. avarkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟapōtu vīṭṭaiyum vīṭṭiluḷḷa poruḷkaḷaiyum kāppataṟku evaraiyum niṟuttavillai. avarkaḷ eppoḻutum taṉimaiyākavē uṇa varuntiṉārkaḷ. iṉṉa nērattil uṇavarunta vēṇṭum eṉṉum niyati irukkavillai; paci koṇṭa nēraṅkaḷil ovvoruvarum uṇṭaṉar.
uṭampai uraiñcuvataiyē avarkaḷ ciṟanta uṭaṟ payiṟciyākak karutiṉar. ataṟkākap palamuṟaikaḷ kaiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. aḻuttamāṉa karuṅ kāli uruḷaikaḷai uṭampil aḻutti uruṭṭuvatē avaṟṟuṭ ciṟantatu. avarkaḷiṉ camātikaḷ āṭamparamiṉṟi eḷiyavakaiyil iruntaṉa. camātikkumēl maṇkoṭṭi mēṭu ceyyappaṭṭatu. avarkaḷ aḻakaiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷaiyum virumpiṉar. uṭaikaḷ poṉṉiṉāl alaṅkarikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. āparaṇaṅkaḷil vayiraṅkaḷum vilaiyuyarnta kaṟkaḷum aḻuttappaṭṭiruntaṉa. pūvēlai ceyta maciliṉ (callā) tuṇikaḷai avarkaḷ uṭuttaṉar. parivāraṅkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ piṉṉāṟ kuṭai piṭittuc ceṉṟaṉa. uṇmaiyum pukaḻum pōṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. aṟivut tiṟamillāta mutiyavarkaḷukku matippuk koṭukkappaṭavillai.
avarkaḷ pala peṇkaḷai maṇantaṉar. avarkaḷ oru cōṭi erutaip peṇkaḷukku vilaiyākak koṭuttu avarkaḷaip peṟṟōr kaḷiṭamiruntu vāṅkiṉar. cilar tamakku vāḻkkait tuṇai vēṇṭumeṉṟu maṇañ ceykiṉṟaṉar. palar iṉpattiṟkāvum vīṭukaḷaip pala kuḻantai kaḷāl nirappuvataṟkum maṇañ ceykiṉṟaṉar. vēḷvic cālaikaḷilum kaṭavuṭ pūcaikaḷilum oruvarum muṭi aṇiyavillai. paliyiṭum vilaṅ kukaḷ veṭṭik kollappaṭavillai; mūccaip piṭittut tirukikkol lappaṭṭaṉa.
poyccāṭci colvōṉukkuk kaṭuntaṇṭam vitikkappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉatu uṟuppukkup paṅkam viḷaittavaṉatu atē uṟuppu veṭṭap paṭṭatu; kaiyum veṭṭappaṭṭatu. ciṟpikaḷiṉ kaṇṇukku allatu kaikku ūṟu viḷaittavaṉ kolai ceyyappaṭṭāṉ.
aracaṉuṭaiya uyir peṇkaḷiṉ kaiyil iruntatu. ap peṇkaḷ tāy tantaiyariṭamiruntu vilaikku vāṅkappaṭṭavarkaḷ. vāyilukku veḷiyē kāvalāḷarum pōr vīrarum kāval purintaṉar. aracaṉ matu vuṇṭa mayakkattilirukkumpōtu avaṉaik koṉṟavaḷ iṉivarum aracaṉukku maṉaiviyākiṉṟāḷ. aracaṉ pakalil tuyil koḷḷāṉ. taṉakku māṟāka naṭakkakkūṭiya catikaḷukkup payantu avaṉ aṭikkaṭi taṉatu paṭukkaiyai māṟṟuvatuṇṭu. pōrkkālaṅkaḷil maṭṭumallāmal vaḻakkut tīrppaḷittaṟkum avaṉ veḷiyē celkiṉṟāṉ. nīti celuttum maṇṭa pattil avaṉ muḻu nērattaiyum celaviṭukiṟāṉ. an nērattil nāṉku vēlaiyāṭkaḷ avaṉatu uṭampai mara uruḷaikaḷāl uraiñcukiṉṟaṉar. uruḷaikaḷāl uraiñcumpōtē avaṉ vaḻakkukaḷai vicāraṇai cey kiṉṟāṉ. vēṭṭaiyāṭutaṟkum, vēḷvic cālaikaḷiṟ pali celuttu taṟkum avaṉ veḷiyē celkiṉṟāṉ. vēṭṭaikkuc cellumpōtu peṇkaḷ palar avaṉaic cūḻntu niṟkiṉṟaṉar.1 ivarkaḷukkup puṟattē īṭṭi taritta vīrar cūḻntu niṟkiṉṟaṉar. vītikaḷiṟ kayiṟukaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉa. kayiṟṟaik kaṭantu cellum āṇ allatu peṇṇukku maraṇa taṇṭaṇai vitikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēḷam, callari mutaliya vāttiyaṅka ḷōṭu parivāraṅkaḷ celkiṉṟaṉa. aracaṉ vaḷaittaṭaikkappaṭṭa iṭattē uyaramāṉa iṭattil niṉṟu ampukaḷai eykiṟāṉ. avaṉukkup pakkattil iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu āyutantaritta peṇkaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉar. veḷi yiṭaṅkaḷil vēṭṭaiyāṭumpōtu aracaṉ yāṉaiyiṉ ampārimītiruntu ampukaḷai eykiṟāṉ. peṇkaḷ cilar tērmītum cilar yāṉai mītum celkiṉṟaṉar. pōrukkuc celvatai oppa avarkaḷ pala āyutaṅkaḷait tāṅkic celkiṉṟaṉar.
aracāṅka amaippu
aracāṅka uttiyōkattariṟ cilar cantaikaḷaiyum, cilar irāṇu vattaiyum, cilar nakarattaiyum mēṟpārkkiṉṟaṉar. cilar āṟuvaḻiyāka nilaṅkaḷukku nīrpāyccuvataiyum, cilar nila aḷavaikaḷaiyum, cilar āṟṟiṉ ciṟiya kālvāykaḷāl nīrcellum maṭaikaḷaiyum kavaṉikkiṉ ṟaṉar. cilar viṟaku veṭṭuvōr, taccuvēlai, kol vēlai, curaṅkavēlai mutaliyaṉa purivōrai mēṟpārttu vari aṟa viṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ vīti kaḷaiyum amaikkiṉṟaṉar. tūrattaiyum kiḷaippātaikaḷaiyum aṟivik kum kaṟkaḷ ‘mailukku’ oṉṟu naṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nakara āṭci āṟu capaiyiṉarāl naṭattappaṭukiṉṟaṉatu. mutaṟpiri viṉar kaittoḻil toṭarpāṉa ellāvaṟṟaiyum kavaṉippar. iraṇṭā vatu piriviṉar piṟa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum varuvōraik kavaṉittu avarkaḷ taṅkuvataṟkēṟṟa vacatikaḷaip purivar. ap putiyavarkaḷukkut tuṇaiyāka viruppōr avarkaḷiṉ pōkkaik kavaṉittu atikārikaḷukku aṟivik kiṉṟaṉar. piṟa nāṭṭavarkaḷ iṟakka nērntāl avarkaḷiṉ uṭaikaḷ uṟavi ṉariṭam cērppikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mūṉṟāvatu piriviṉar nāṭṭil ēṟpaṭum iṟappup piṟappukaḷaik kavaṉikkiṉṟaṉar. uyarntōr tāḻntōr eṉṟa vēṟupāṭiṉṟi ellōrukkiṭaiyilum nērum iṟappup piṟappukkaḷ tavaṟāmal pativu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāṉkāvatu piriviṉar cantaikaḷaik kavaṉikkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷē niṟaikaḷukkum aḷavukaḷukkum poṟuppā yuḷḷōr. makkaḷukku viḷamparañ ceytapiṉ kālattiṟ kiṭaikkum viḷaiporuḷkaḷ pakiraṅkattil viṟkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oṉṟukku atikamāṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷil vāṇikam puriya evarum aṉumatikkappaṭṭilar. aintāvatu piriviṉar kaittoḻiṟ poruḷkaḷaik kavaṉikkiṉṟaṉar. putiya poruḷ kaḷum paḻaiya poruḷkaḷum vevvēṟāka vaittu viṟkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. putiyavaṟṟaiyum, paḻaiyavaṟṟaiyum kalantu viṟpōrukkut taṇṭam vitik kappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṟāvatu piriviṉar vāṇipap poruḷkaḷiṉ vilaiyiṟ pattiloṉṟai aṟaviṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu āṟu piriviṉarkaḷum kaṭamai yāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ oṉṟucērntu potukkaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉ puḻakkam, avaṟṟiṉ paḻutupārppu, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ vilai, cantai, tuṟai mukam, kōyilkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkup poṟuppāyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
nakariṉ niyāya atikārikaḷ irāṇuvattai naṭattuvikkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ aintu uṟuppiṉar aṭaṅkiya āṟu capaikaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. oṉṟu, kaṭaṟpaṭai atikāriyōṭu ottuḻaikkiṉṟatu. iraṇ ṭāvatu pōrukkuriya iyantiraṅkaḷaiyum, pōrvīrariṉ uṇavaiyum piṟa poruḷkaḷaiyum koṇṭu cellum erutukaḷaiyum vaṇṭikaḷaiyum kavaṉikkiṉṟatu. itu, vēlaiyāṭkaḷaiyum, paṟai koṭṭuvōraiyum, callari pōṭuvōraiyum, kutiraip pākaraiyum, iyantiram paḻutu pārp pōraiyum avarkaḷiṉ utaviyāḷaraiyum aḷikkiṉṟatu. callarikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭum ōcaiyai aṟintu vēlaiyāṭkaḷ kutiraikaḷukkup pul koṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. mūṉṟāvatu pirivu kālāṭpaṭaikkup poṟuppā yirukkiṉṟatu. nālāvatu kutiraippaṭaikkum, aintāvatu tērkaḷukkum, āṟāvatu yāṉaip paṭaikkum poṟuppāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. aracaṉiṉ āyutaccālai uṇṭu. ovvoru pōr vīraṉum 1āyutaṅkaḷai āyutac cālaiyilum, kutiraiyai allatu yāṉaiyaip pantiyilum viṭṭuc cel kiṉṟāṉ. avarkaḷ yāṉaikkuk kaṭivāḷamiṭuvatillai. tērkaḷai erutukaḷ iḻuttuc celkiṉṟaṉa. tērai iḻuttuk kaḷaippaṭaiyātapaṭi kutiraikaḷ pakkattē naṭattic cellappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tērppākaṉiṉ iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷi lum ovvoru vīrar irukkiṉṟaṉar. pōr yāṉai nāṉku vīraraik koṇṭu celkiṉṟatu. oruvaṉ pākaṉum mūvar pōr ceyvōrumāvar.
tattuva cāttirikaḷ
tattuva cāttirikaḷiṉ oru piriviṉar pirācmēṉ (pirāmaṇar) eṉṟum, maṟṟap piriviṉar cārmēṉ1 eṉavum aḻaikkappaṭuvar. pirāc mēṉiṉ koḷkaikaḷ uṇmaiyākavirukkiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, avarkaḷ mikavum matikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. karuppattil iruppatu mutal avarkaḷ aṟi vāḷikaḷiṉ pātukāppil irukkiṉṟaṉar. tāyiṉatum kuḻantaiyiṉatum nalattukkākac cila kiriyaikaḷaic ceyvatākap pācāṅku ceytu aṟivāḷikaḷ avaḷukkup pala puttimatikaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ puttimatikaḷai āvalōṭu kēṭkiṉṟavaḷ mikavum puṇṇiyacāli eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉ ṟāḷ. piṟanta piṉ kuḻantai, oruvariṉpiṉ oruvarāka aṟivil uyarnta palariṉ pātukāppil iruntu varukiṟatu.
tattuva cāttirikaḷ, vaḷaittaṭaitta cōlaikaḷiṉ mattiyil vāḻ kiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai eḷitāṉatu; pul, māṉtōl mutaliyavaṟ ṟiṉmītu avarkaḷ paṭukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟaicciyaip pucippatillai; peṇpōkattai avarkaḷ veṟuppar. avarkaḷ pēcu vataik kēṭpōr mauṉamākavum amaitiyākavum iruttal vēṇṭum. ip pirāmaṇattait tāṇṭum evarum irakkamiṉṟi ak kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum vilakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu vāḻum ovvoruvarum muppattēḻu āṇṭukaḷiṉpiṉ tattam illaṅkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. appoḻutu avarkaḷ aḻakiya maciliṉ (callā) āṭaikaḷai uṭukkiṉṟaṉar; viral kaḷil āḻikaḷum, kātiṟ kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum aṇikiṉṟaṉar; toḻil cey kiṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷallāta piṟavaṟṟiṉ ūṉaip pucikkiṉṟaṉar. kāramuḷḷatum tāḷitañ ceytatumākiya uṇavai avarkaḷ virumpuvatillai. ettaṉai maṉaiviyaraiyum avarkaḷ maṇantukoḷḷalām. aṭimaikaḷiṉmaiyiṉ kūṭiya tokai maṉaiviyarkaḷaiyum, piḷḷaikaḷaiyum uṭaiyarāyirut tal tamatu vāḻkkaikku vacatiyākumeṉa avarkaḷ eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.
pirācmēṉiyar cāttira uṇmaikaḷait tam maṉaiviyarukkuc collikkoṭuppatillai. piṟarukku maṟaikkappaṭṭa avvuṇmaikaḷai veḷippaṭutti avaṟṟiṉ tūymaiyaik keṭuttuviṭuvārkaḷ, allatu uṇmai aṟivu mikuntu tammai viṭṭup pirintuviṭuvar eṉa avarkaḷ añcukiṉṟaṉar. nalla maṉaiviyarum, kaṇavaṉum iṉpatuṉpaṅkaḷaic camamākap pāvittu vāḻkkai naṭattukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi pēcum poruḷkaḷuḷ maraṇam oṉṟākum. piṟappaippōla iṟappum varum eṉṟu avarkaḷ cāttiraṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. maṉitaṉukku nērum iṉpatuṉpaṅka ḷellām kaṉavu pōṉṟa māyai eṉṟum, illāviṭiṉ oru poruḷ cilarukkut tuṉpattaiyum vēṟu cilarukku iṉpattaiyum koṭukkamuṭiyā teṉṟum, orē poruḷ vevvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil nērmāṟāṉa nukarvu kaḷaik koṭukkak kāraṇameṉṉaveṉṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
iyaṟkai nikaḻcciyaik kuṟitta aḷavil avarkaḷ kuṟaivāṉa viḷakkamuṭaiyavarkaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kaṭṭukkataikaḷai nampukiṉṟaṉar; ūkai ārāyvilum pārkkac cātaṉaiyil tiṟamaiyuṭaiya varkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ koḷkaikaḷ palavakaiyil kirēkkar koḷkaikaḷōṭu oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉavāyk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kirēkkaraippōlavē avarkaḷum ulakam oru kālattil uṟpattiyāṉa teṉṟum, atu uruṇṭai vaṭiviṉateṉṟum, ataṉai uṇṭākkiya kaṭavuḷ ataṉ ellāp pākaṅkaḷilum ceṟintirukkiṟāreṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iv vulakiṉ uṟpattikku mutaṟ kāraṇamuṇṭeṉṟum, atu nīriṉiṉṟu uṇṭā yiṟṟeṉṟum, nilam, tī, nīr, kāṟṟu eṉṉum nālu pūtaṅkaḷaiviṭa aintāvatu pūtam oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṟum, atiṉiṉṟum vāṉamum naṭcattiraṅkaḷum uṇṭāyiṉaveṉṟum avarkaḷ nampukiṉṟaṉar. pūmi ulakiṉ mattiyil iruppatākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ciruṭṭi uyiriṉ ilakkaṇam ivai pōṉṟa pala poruḷkaḷ kirēkkar koḷkaikaḷōṭu ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. cārmēṉkaḷiṉ hailēpiyōy eṉṉum piriviṉar mikavum mariyātai kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kāṭṭil vacittu aṅkuk kiṭaikkum ilai kaḷaiyum, paḻaṅkaḷaiyum pucikkiṉṟaṉar: marappaṭṭaiyāl taitta uṭaiyai uṭukkiṉṟaṉar. peṇ pōkamum, matuvum, ūṉum avarkaḷukku vilakku. aracaṉ tūtuvar mūlam ivarkaḷiṭam taṉakku vēṇṭiya ālōcaṉai kaḷaik kēṭkiṟāṉ; ivarkaḷ mūlam kaṭavuḷukku vaḻipāṭu ceyvikkiṟāṉ.
ivarkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷōr maruttuvar. ivarkaḷ maṉitaṉiṉ iyaṟkaiyaip paṟṟiya ārāycci ceykiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ eḷiyavai. avarkaḷ vayalkaḷil vacippatillai. avarkaḷiṉ uṇavu ariciyum piṟavum. ivaṟṟai avarkaḷ evariṭamēṉum kēṭṭup peṟṟukkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar; allatu viruntāḷikaḷākat taṅku miṭaṅkaḷiṟ peṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kalaikaḷiṉ ciṟappiṉāl makiḻccikkuriya maṇaṅkaḷai oḻuṅkupaṭuttuvar; piṟakkum kuḻantaikaḷ āṇ allatu peṇ eṉṟu muṉ aṟintu kūṟuvar. avarkaḷ uṇavu muṟai kaḷāl nōyai māṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. maruntai arumaiyākap payaṉpaṭuttu kiṉṟaṉar; maṟṟaiya muṟaikaḷ iyaṟkaikku māṟupaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉ ṟaṉar. ivarkaḷum vēṟu cila vakuppiṉarum nāḷmuṟṟum acaiyātu ōriṭattil niṟṟalpōṉṟa paḻakkaṅkaḷaic ceykiṉṟaṉar.
ivarkaḷaiyaṉṟi mantira vittaikkārarum vēṭam pūṇṭōru mirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ tāmum uyarnta tiruttamuṭaiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṟu ulakattaippaṟṟiya niṉaivu aṉpaiyum tūymaiyaiyumuṇṭākkumeṉṟu avarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ cilariṭamiruntu peṇkaḷ camaya cāttiraṅ kaṟkiṉṟaṉar.
pāṭaliputrā
kaṅkaiyāṟṟiṉ akalam 100 srēṭiyā.1 ataṉ kuṟainta āḻam 120 aṭi. kaṅkānatiyum iṉṉoru āṟum kūṭum iṭattil ‘palipōttirā’ eṉṉum oru nakar irukkiṟatu. itaṉ nīḷam eṇpatu srēṭiyā; akalam patiṉaintu srēṭiyā. atu nīṇṭa catura vaṭivamāṉatu. ataic cuṟṟi maratti ṉāl iṭappaṭṭa araṇ uṇṭu. ampukaḷ eyyakkūṭiyatākac cuvariṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē cantukaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. cuvariṉ muṉṉē akaḻi oṉṟu irukkiṉṟatu. nakariṉ kaḻivu nīr itaṉuḷ viḻukiṉṟatu. iṅku vāḻvōr intiyā muḻumaiyilum vāḻvōriṉum ciṟantōr. aracaṉ taṉatu kuṭumpap peyarōṭu ‘palipōttirā’ allatu ‘cantira kōttirā’ eṉṉum peyaraic cērttukkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.
akaḻikaḷ 600 aṭi akalamum, 30 muḻa āḻamumuṭaiyaṉa. matil 570 kōpuraṅkaḷum aṟupattu nāṉku vāyilkaḷum uṭaiyatāyiruntatu.1 intiya makkaḷ cutantira vāḻkkaiyiṉar. aṭimaikaḷ oruvarum kāṇappaṭa villai.
pāṇṭiya cātiyiṉar peṇkaḷāl āḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ mutal araci kekuliciṉ putalvi eṉappaṭukiṉṟāḷ. in nāṭṭiluḷḷa nicā (Nysa) eṉṉum paṭṭiṉamum mēṟkūṟiyappaṭi peyaruṭaiya malaiyum yūpitar kaṭavuḷiṉ paricutta iṭaṅkaḷ, immalaiyiluḷḷa kukaiyil pakkas kaṭavuḷ vaḷarntār eṉṟu intiyar uṟutiyākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
ekulis, taṉatu putalvikkuk koṭutta nāṭṭai 365 kirāmaṅ kaḷākap pirittu ovvoru kirāmattaiyum muṟaiyē varuṭattiṉ 365 nāḷum tiṟai koṭukkumpaṭi vitittār.2
pakkas (Father Bacchus) kālam mutal alakcāntar kālam varaiyil pāṇṭināṭṭai āṇṭa aracar 154 pēr. ivarkaḷiṉ āṭci 6431 āṇṭu 3 tiṅkaḷ naṭaipeṟṟatu.
tapirapēṉ eṉṉum tīvu intiyāviṉiṉṟum oru ciṟiya āṟṟiṉāṟ pirikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. aṅku intiyāviṟ kiṭaippatilum atika poṉṉum muttum kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. aṅkuḷḷa yāṉaikaḷ intiya yāṉai kaḷilum periyaṉa. tapirapēṉ 7000 srēṭiyā nīḷamum, 5000 srēṭiyā akalamumuḷḷatu. aṅku 700 kirāmaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. kaṭal ōraṅkaḷil āmaikaḷ atikam. avaikaḷiṉ ōṭu 15 muḻa nīḷam. tāmpiraparṇi eṉṉum kaṭaluḷ irukkum it tīvil teṉṉaikaḷ niraiyāka naṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅku yāṉaikaḷum atikam. at tīviṉ makkaḷ avaikaḷaip piṭittuk kaliṅka tēcattukku aṉuppukiṉṟaṉar. karai ōraṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ mīṉ piṭittu vāḻvar. ak kaṭalkaḷil ciṅkattalai vilaṅkut talaikaḷuḷḷa mīṉkaḷ ulāvukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷiṉ talaiyuṭaiya mīṉkaḷum aṅku uṇṭu; avaikaḷukku mayirukkup patil muṭkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.

iyal - 5
tamiḻ vēntarum avar āṭciyum
piṟkālat tamiḻnāṭṭuk kalveṭṭukaḷālum paṭṭayaṅkaḷālum tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇa muṭiyāta pala ariya ceytikaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. araciyal toṭarpāṉa taṉittamiḻc coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ avvāṭci muṟai mikap paḻaimai toṭṭē tamiḻnāṭṭil naṭaipeṟṟu varukiṉṟa teṉat tuṇiyalām.
tamiḻnāṭṭilē mūvēntaratu āṭci muṟaiyum orē vakaiyāka naṭai peṟṟatu. ellā atikāramum aracaṉukku iruntatu. avaṉukku uṟutikūṟum amaiccar nakarap perumakkaḷ mutaliya cuṟṟattiṉar iruntaṉar. araṇ maṉaiyil ēval purivōr pala kūṭṭattiṉarākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉar. camaiyal aṟai kuḷikkum aṟaikaḷiṟ perumpālum āṭavarē vēlai purintaṉar. vēlaiyāṭkaḷ vēḷam eṉṉum kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum terin teṭukkappaṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ, pōriṟ ciṟai piṭikkappaṭṭavarkaḷāvar.
cōyukuvā eṉṉum cīṉaṉ, cōḻar āṭciyaip paṟṟi eḻutu miṭattuk kuṟippiṭṭiruppatu varumāṟu: araṇmaṉai viruntuk kālaṅ kaḷil aracaṉum nāṉku amaiccarkaḷum ciṅkācaṉattiṉ aṭiyai vaṇaṅku kiṟārkaḷ; uṭaṉē aṅkuk kuḻumiyiruppōr vāttiyaṅkaḷai olittu āṭal pāṭalkaḷait toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. aracaṉ matu aruntamāṭṭāṉ; ūṉ aruntuvāṉ. nāṭṭu vaḻakkappaṭi avaṉ pañcu āṭaiyai aṇintu koṇṭu tāṉiya māviṉāṟ ceyta paṇiyāraṅkaḷai uṇkiṟāṉ. uṇa varuntum aṟaiyiṟ cēvippataṟkum taṉṉaic cūḻntu tirivataṟkum avaṉ tiraḷāṉa naṭaṉa mātarai vēlaikku amarttiyirukkiṟāṉ. nāḷ oṉṟukku muṟai muṟaiyāka avaṉaic cēvikkum pēr mūvāyiram mātar.1 aracaṉukkum tēvikkum taṉic cēvakam puriyum paṇiyāḷarumiruntaṉar.
paṭaiyil yāṉai kutirai vīrar, kālāḷ, tērvīrar eṉpōr irunta ṉar. yāṉaivīrar yāṉaiyāṭkaḷ kuñcaramaḷḷar eṉavum, kutirai vīrar kutiraic cēvakar eṉavum, kālāṭpaṭaiyiṉar kaikōḷap perum paṭai yiṉar eṉavum, villāḷar villikaḷ eṉavum, vāṭ paṭaiyiṉar vāṭpeṟṟa kaikkōḷar eṉavum paṭṭaṉar. yutta kālattil vēlaikku amarttik koḷḷappaṭum vīrar aracaṉiṉ valaṅkai eṉappaṭṭaṉar. nakaraṅkaḷil paṭai taṅkumiṭaṅkaḷ kaṭakaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. paṭaikaḷiṉ talaivaṉ paṭaittalaivaṉ cēṉāpati allatu paṭaimutali eṉappaṭṭāṉ. vari taṇṭum karumi (mēl uttiyōkattaṉ) nāṭuvākai eṉappaṭṭāṉ. vari taṇṭum vēlaiyil pōr vīrarkaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉar. kālvaḻiyāka varum paṭai mūlappaṭai eṉappaṭṭatu.
mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi cīṉa āciriyaṉ 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: iv virācciyattil (cōḻa nāṭṭil) patiṉāyiram yāṉaikaḷ niṟkiṉṟaṉa. ovvoṉṟum ēḻu allatu eṭṭu muḻam uyara muṭaiyatu. pōr puriyumpōtu avai mutukiṉmītu vīṭukaḷaikkoṇṭu tirikiṉṟaṉa. vīṭukaḷ nirampa vīrarkaḷ iruntu ampukaḷai eykiṉṟaṉar. veṟṟiyaṭaintapiṉ yāṉaikaḷukkuk kauravap paṭṭaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. nāḷum avai aracaṉukkumuṉ koṇṭuvarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pōril koḷḷaiyaṭikkappaṭum poruḷ aracaṉukkuc conta māṉatu.
aracaṉatu vāy uttaravu ‘tiruvāyk kēḷvi’ eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉaik kēṭṭu eḻutum karumikaḷ palar avaṉuṭaṉ iruntaṉar. eḻutuvōṉ ‘tīṭṭukkāraṉ’ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. aracaṉ vaḻakkukaḷai nēriṟ kēṭṭu nāṭṭu vaḻakkappaṭi tīrppaḷittāṉ. periya karumikaḷukku ēṉāti, mārāyaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. mārāyaṉ eṉpataṉ peṇ pāl mārāci. uyarnta karumikaḷ atikārikaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. mēl kīḻ atikārikaḷaik kuṟikkap ‘peruntaram’ ‘ciṟutaram’ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅ kiṉa. uttiyōkattar ‘karumikaḷ’ eṉavum, vēlaiyāṭkaḷ ‘paṇi makkaḷ’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. niyāyātipatikaḷ ‘niyāyattār’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. oru kirāmattiṉ allatu ataṉ pakutiyiṉ kaṭamaikaḷaip pārkkum karumikaḷ ‘uṭaiyār’ allatu ‘kiḻār’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ovvoru kirāmamum peru makkaḷ kūṭṭaṅkaḷāl āḷappaṭṭatu. kirāmaṅkaḷ pala cērntatu kūṟṟam, nāṭu allatu kōṭṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. pala kūṟṟaṅkaḷ cērntatu oru vaḷa nāṭu. vaḷa nāṭṭilum periyatu maṇṭalam.
1nāṭṭu āṭci
mācaṉaṅkaḷum caṉaṅkaḷum kūṭṭaṅkaḷum araciyaṟ karumaṅkaḷai naṭattiṉa. ovvoru kūṭṭamum taṉittaṉi ovvor karumattaik kavaṉit tatu. kirāmaṅkaḷ, cērikaḷ, terukkaḷ eṉṉum vakaiyākap pirivukaḷ cey yappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru teruviluḷḷōrum taṉittaṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷākac cērntu ovvoru karumattaik kavaṉittārkaḷ. aṟac cottukaḷai mēṟpārvai ceytōr ‘tāṉattār’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. tirukkōyilkaḷuk kuḷḷa nilaṅkaḷukku varikaḷ nīkkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kirāmam allatu paṭṭiṉam, ūr eṉappaṭṭatu. ūr, āḷaṉkaṇam eṉṉum kaṇattāl āḷappaṭṭatu. ūr āḷvār eṉpatum ak kūṭṭattiṉ peyar. ik kūṭṭattiṉ kīḻ ūrvāriyam eṉṉum kūṭṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. ic col vāri (iṉam) eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. taṉippaṭṭa kaṭamaikaḷaip pārkka ik kūṭṭattārāl amaikkappaṭṭōr vāriyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ kāriyakkūṭṭa uṟuppiṉar ‘vāriyap perumakkaḷ’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ūriṉ ovvoru pirivum ‘kuṭumpu’ (Ward) eṉappaṭṭatu. ovvoru kuṭumpilum uḷḷa uṟuppiṉar ‘kuṭaōlai’ vāyilākat terinteṭukkappaṭṭaṉar.2 periya kuṟṟam ‘mēṟpaṭu kuṟṟam’ eṉappaṭṭatu. viṉaikkēṭu (keṭṭa naṭattai) uḷḷavarkaḷum kaikkūli vāṅkuvōrum taṇṭikkappaṭṭaṉar. ūravar kaḷiṭam uḷvari (maṟaivāka vāṅkum vari) vāṅkutal ūrkkukkaravu (turōkam) ceytalākak karutappaṭṭatu.3 capai mācapai eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē kuṟi, peruṅkuṟi eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉa. uṟuppiṉar perumakkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. capaikaḷ perumpālum kōyil maṇṭapaṅkaḷiṟ kūṭiṉa. capai paṟai cāṟṟik kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. capai kūṭṭuvatu (cēmak) kalam allatu iraṭṭaik kalam taṭṭi aṟivikkappaṭṭatu.
aracaṉatu vāykkaṭṭaḷai (tirumukam) tirumantira ōlai eṉṉum karumiyāl eḻutappaṭṭup pottakattiṟ pukuttumuṉ ‘tirumantira ōlai nāyakam’ eṉpavaṉāl oppu nōkkappaṭṭatu. piṉpu ōlai nāyakam variyiḻuttuk koḷka eṉak karumi oruvaṉukkuk kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭa, atu pottakattiṟ pukuttappaṭṭut tīṭṭu (Document) āṉatu. ēṟpāṭukaḷ (āvaṇaṅkaḷ) toṭarpākap puravu vari, varip pottakam, mukaveṭṭi, varip pottakak kaṇakku, variyiḻuttu, kaṇkāṇi, paṭṭōlai, naṭuvirukkai mutaliya karumikaḷ iruntaṉar.
nāṭṭiluḷḷa capaikaḷukku aṉuppappaṭum ceyti ōlaikaḷ ‘tirumukam’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ‘naṭuvirukkai’ eṉpavaṉ maṉuc ceypavaṉukkum karumikkum iṭaiyil niṉṟu ceytikaḷai aṟivippavaṉ. āvaṇatti eṉpavaṉ kāriya (Executive) uttiyōkattaṉ. varip pottakam nāṭṭu vari toṭarpāṉa kaṇakkukaḷ tīṭṭappaṭum pottakam. ataṟkup poṟuppā yuḷḷa karumiyum ap peyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭāṉ. puravuvari eṉpatu nilavari. puravuvarit tiṇaikkaḷam eṉpatu nilavarip pakuti (Land Tax Department). vaḻakkāḷiyiṉ vākku mūlam muṟaippāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. nilam toṭarpāṉa vaḻakkukaḷ āṭci āvaṇam mutaliyavaikaḷai nōkki muṭivu ceyyap paṭṭaṉa.
makkaṭ pirivu
makkaḷ toḻiṟ pirivukaḷ paṟṟik kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamākap pirintu vāḻntapōtum potuk karumaṅkaḷil ottuḻaittārkaḷ. 1īḻavar ak kālattil paṉai teṉṉaikaḷil ēṟi matu iṟakkavillai. varuṇam eṉpatu teṉṉintiya makkaḷāṟ kaikkoḷḷappaṭavillai. cila piriviṉar ciṟcilavaṟṟaic ceytal kūṭāteṉṉum vaḻakku iruntatu. itu kammā ḷarukkuc cōḻa aracaṉ eḻutiyaḷitta paṭṭayamoṉṟāl viḷaṅkukiṟatu. vīṭṭil naṭakkum naṉmai tīmaikaḷil iraṇṭu caṅku olittal, mēḷamaṭit tal, vīṭṭai viṭṭu veḷiyē cellumpōtu mitiyaṭitarittal, vīṭṭukku veḷḷaiyaṭittal iraṇṭu māṭi vīṭu kaṭṭutal vīṭṭiṉ muṉpuṟattait tāmarai āmpal mutaliya pūmālaikaḷāl alaṅkarittal pōṉṟa cakā yaṅkaḷ appaṭṭayattil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. toḻilāḷarkaḷiṭaiyē ‘valaṅkai’ ‘iṭaṅkai’ eṉṉum kaṭcikaḷ iruntaṉa. iṭaṅkaiyiṉar pirāma ṇarkaḷiṉ mitiyaṭikaḷaiyum kuṭaikaḷaiyum tūkkic ceṉṟaṉar.
aracarum perumakkaḷum pala maṉaiviyaruṭaiyarāyiruntaṉar. maṉaiviyar 1uṭaṉkaṭṭai ēṟum vaḻakkam iruntatu. varalāṟṟuk kālat tukku muṉtoṭṭu āṭal pāṭal mātar mikavum kavarccikkuriyavarkaḷā yiruntārkaḷ. viḻāk kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ varavu mikavum varavēṟpuk kuriyatāyiruntatu. avarkaḷ āṭavarkaḷuṭaṉ tārāḷamākap paḻakiṉārkaḷ. ampalaṅkaḷil (Rest Houses) taṇṇīr vaikkappaṭṭiruntatu.
variyum varuvāyum
aracaṉatu niti nilaiyam talam eṉappaṭṭatu. nitiyiṟ perumpālaṉa, nakaikaḷākavum vilaiyuyarnta maṇikaḷākavum iruntaṉa. apucā ceṭ (Apuza Zed) eṉpavaṉ pattām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: taṅkattil irattiṉakkaṟkaḷ patitta kuḻaikaḷai intiya aracar aṇi kiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaḻuttil irattiṉam vaiṭūriyam mutaliyavaikaḷāṟ ceyta mālaikaḷ ilaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, muttukkaḷ mikavum vilai yēṟappeṟṟavai. aracariṉ nitic cēmippup perumpālum muttukkaḷā yirukkiṉṟatu. atikārikaḷum uyarnta karumikaḷum aracaṉ aṇivatu pōṉṟa vilaiyuyarnta muttu mālaikaḷai aṇikiṟārkaḷ.
vari perumpālum iṟai eṉappaṭṭatu. paṇam iṟukkumpaṭi vitik kappaṭum taṇṭaṉai taṇṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. āyam eṉṉum collum iṟai eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. aracaṉatu vāyil ‘tirukkoṟṟa vāyil’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
mattiya aracaic cēravēṇṭiya nilavarikkuk kirāma capaikaḷ poṟuppāyiruntaṉa. varikaḷ nila viḷaivukaḷiliruntum piṟa vakaikaḷi liruntum vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. 2veṭṭi eṉpatu kūli illāta kaṭṭāya vēlai. nakaravari (ūriṭuvari), ērivari, vētaṉaivari (aṇaikaṭṭum kaṭṭāya vēlai), cilavari, makaṉmaivari, pērvari (Poll Tax), kārttikai yarici, kārttikaip paccai (kācu āyam), kaṭai iṟai, paṭikāval, nakarkāval eṉap pala varikaḷ iruntaṉa.
cila nēraṅkaḷil vari koṭātārait taṇṇīriliṭṭum veyilil niṟuttiyum vētaṉai koṭuttum vari vāṅkappaṭṭatu.
vāṇikam
viyāpārattiṉ poruṭṭu makkaḷ iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum ceṉṟārkaḷ. periya terukkaḷ ‘peruvaḻi’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. paṇṭaṅkaḷ kaḻutai, poti māṭu, erumaikaḷil koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa. paṇam kaṭaṉ koṭuttavar, vāṅkiyavariṭattiṟ peṟum cīṭṭu (Pronote) ‘kaiyeḻuttōlai’ eṉap paṭṭatu. tamiḻ viyāpārikaḷ malāyat tīpakaṟpam. intu cīṉam, cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. māvalipuram, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam, cāliyūr, koṟkai mutaliyaṉa kiḻakkilum, kollam mēṟkilum periya viyāpārap patikaḷāyiruntaṉa. ilaṅkai nikkavāram (Nicobars) ilakka tīvu, mālai tīvukaḷ mēṟkilum, kappalkaḷ taṅkutaṟku ēṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa. cīṉa nāvāykaḷ malaiyāḷakkaraiyiluḷḷa kollamvaraiyiṟ ceṉṟaṉa. arāpiyāviliruntu kutirai viyāpārikaḷ kutiraikaḷaikkoṇṭu vantaṉar.
aṭimaikaḷ
ak kālattil paṇattukku viṟṟu vāṅkappaṭum aṭimaikaḷ irun tārkaḷ. uyarkulattavarkaḷum cila camayaṅkaḷil aṭimaikaḷāka vantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ perumpālum tammaik kōyilkaḷukku viṟṟārkaḷ. aṭimai kaḷai viṟṟu vāṅkuvataṟku araciyal āvaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. kōyilkaḷukku viṟkappaṭum peṇkaḷ ‘tēvarāṭṭiyar’ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. vēlaiyāṭkaḷukkuk kūli tāṉiyaṅkaḷākavē koṭukkappaṭṭatu.
kirāmam
kirāmaṅkaḷil makkaḷ eḷiyavāḻkkai naṭattiṉar. paṇṇaikkāraṉ allatu nilam vaittiruppōṉ mēlāṉa nilaimaiyil iruntāṉ. kirāma capaikaḷ perumpālum nilam vaittirukkum perumakkaḷiṉ kūṭṭamā yiruntatu. kirāmaṅkaḷaic cuṟṟic cila pakutikaḷ ūrp potuvākaviruntaṉa. vari yiṟukkum makkaḷ kirāma āṭciyil perum paṅku paṟṟiṉar. ovvoru kirāmattilum kālvaḻiyākavarum vēlaiyāṭkaḷ iruntaṉar. cēkkiḻār, periya purāṇattil nantaṉāraippaṟṟik kūṟum pakuti, akkālak kirāma vāḻkkaikku eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. tāṉiyam cērttu vaikkappaṭum iṭam ‘koṭṭakāram’ eṉappaṭṭatu. nilam ceyvōṉ kāṇiyāḷaṉukkuk koṭukkum pākam mēlvāram eṉappaṭṭatu.
aḷavaikaḷ
nilaṅkaḷ kōlāl aḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. oru kōl 16 cāṇ; 18 kōl koṇṭatu oru kuḻi; 256 kuḻi koṇṭatu oru mā; 20 mā koṇṭatu oru vēli. iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum kuḻi aḷavu māṟupaṭṭatu.
āṇṭu 360 nāṭkaḷākavum, cila kālaṅkaḷil 365 nāṭkaḷākavum kaṇakkuc ceyyappaṭṭatu.
tāṉiya aḷavai
2 ceviṭu orupiṭi; 5 ceviṭu ōr āḻākku; 2 āḻākku 1 uḻakku;
2 uḻakku 1 uri; 2 uri oru nāḻi; 8 nāḻi oru kuṟuṇi; 2 kuṟuṇi 1 patakku;
2 patakku 1 tūṇi allatu kāṭi; 3 tūṇi oru kalam.
poṉ aḷavai
oru mañcāṭi 2 kuṉṟi; 1 kuṉṟi 5 mā; 1 mā 4 kāṇi; (oru mañcāṭi=2 kuṟuṇi=10 mā=40 kāṇi) 20 mañcāṭi oru kaḻañcu (68 mutal 72 tāṉiya eṭai;) mā 1\20-yum kāṇi 1\80-yum kuṟikkum. paṇa eṭai eṉpatu 1\10 kaḻañcu.
nīṭṭalaḷavai
8 tōrai (tāṉiyam) oru viral; 12 viral oru cāṇ; 2 cāṇ
oru muḻam.
paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ
muṟkālam paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷ marattaṭi, 1kōyiṟcālai allatu maṭaṅkaḷ allatu uvāttiyāyar vīṭṭut tiṇṇaiyiṟ kūṭiṉa. pēr periya tiṇṇaiccaṭṭampiyār eṉpatu iṉṟum vaḻakku. āciriyaṉukku māṇavar uṟṟuḻi utaviyum uṟuporuḷ koṭuttum kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. ēṭṭil eḻutappaṭṭa puttakaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ēṭum eḻuttāṇiyum kaṇak kāyarāṟ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟuvar maṇalil eḻutip payiṉṟārkaḷ. āciriyaṉiṉ mutal māṇavaṉ avaṉukku utaviyāḷaṉāyiruntāṉ; avaṉ caṭṭāmpiḷḷai eṉappaṭṭāṉ. māṇavar avaṉukku aṭaṅki naṭan tārkaḷ. kālaiyil māṇavar marattaṭiyilō tiṇṇaiyilō kūṭiṉārkaḷ. āciriyaṉ vantatum avarkaḷ ellōrum eḻuntu iruveṉa iruntu avaṉ kūṟuvataik karuttākak kēṭṭup payiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyar kālattil iv vakaip paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷē iruntaṉa. māṇākkar āṇ ṭil oru muṟaiyō iru muṟaiyō uvāttiyāyarukku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟai (perumpālum tam vayalkaḷilum kollaikaḷilum kiṭaippavaikaḷai) kaiyuṟaiyākak koṭuttaṉar. māṇākkaṉ kalviyai niṟuttuṅkāl periya kūṭṭaṅkūṭi avaṉ payiṉṟa nūlaic capaiyōrukkup piracaṅkippittu avaṉ tiṟamaiyaik kāṇpippatum marapāyiruntatu.
ilaṅkai aracar varalāṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭuvaṉa cila
ilaṅkai aracar perumpālum tamiḻarkaḷ allatu tamiḻ irattak kalappuṭaiyavarkaḷāka iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ araṇmaṉaip paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ tamiḻ aracaruṭaiyaṉapōlavē iruntaṉa. aracaṉukkup pakkattil ‘valaṅkai’ ‘iṭaṅkai’ eṉṉum perumakkaḷ iruntaṉar. cēṉāpati aracaṉukku aṭuttapaṭiyil atikāra muṭaiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. araṇmaṉaic camaiyalaṟaiyil āṭavarē vēlai ceytaṉar. camaiyalaṟai yil puḻaṅkum taṭṭukkaḷum pāttiraṅkaḷum veḷḷi taṅkam eṉpavai kaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavai. nāṭōṟum kālaiyil maruttuvaṉ aracaṉatu uṭal nilaiyaic cōtittu ataṟkēṟṟa uṇavukaḷaic camaikkumpaṭi paṭṭōlai eḻutic camaiyalaṟait talaivaṉiṭam koṭuttāṉ. uṇavu camaittatum aracaṉ uṇpataṟku oru maṇi nērattukku muṉ camaiya laṟait talaivaṉukkuc, camaitta ellāvakai uṇavilum ciṟitu ciṟitu eṭuttuk koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṉ uṇṭu oru maṇi nērattukkuppiṉ aracaṉ uṇavu aruntiṉāṉ. akkāla aracar uṇavil nañcu iṭappaṭu vataik kuṟittup peritum añciṉar.1 aracaṉukkuc cōṟum, nūṟu (pala) vakaiyāṉa kaṟikaḷum camaikkappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ avaikaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟai maṭṭum koṇṭu varumpaṭi ceytu uṇṭāṉ. avaṉukkuc camayalaṟaiyil cēvippōrellōrum vāyait tuṇiyāṟ kaṭṭiyiruntaṉar. periyōrkku muṉ kaikaṭṭi vāy putaittu niṟṟal eṉpaṉa paḻaiya marapukaḷ. iṉṟum katirkāmat tirukōyiliṟ pūcai ceyyum ciṅkaḷa kurumār pūcai nērattil tamatu vāyait tuṇiyāṟ kaṭṭikkoḷvar. purōkitaṉ, aracaṉ veḷiyē celvataṟkum piṟa karumaṅkaḷaic ceyvataṟkum nalla nēram aṟintu kūṟiṉāṉ. aracaṉaik kāṇac celvōr avaṉaip pārttapiṉ ciṟitu tūram piṉ nōkki naṭantu ceṉṟu appāl tirumpic celvar. araca ṉukkup piṉkāṭṭic celvatu mariyātaikkuṟaivu eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu. makkaḷ aracaṉuṭaṉ pēcumpōtu tammai aṭimaikaḷ (aṭiyēṉ) nāykaḷ (nāyēṉ) eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭup pēcuvārkaḷ.2

iyal - 6
kiḻakkē tamiḻariṉ kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷ
kiṟittava āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉar tamiḻmakkaḷ cumattirā, yāvā, pāli mutaliya tīvukaḷilum, parmā, cīyam, intuc cīṉam, malāy, kuṭānāṭu mutaliya nāṭukaḷilum kuṭiyēṟi irācciyaṅkaḷaik kōli āṭcipurintaṉar. ak kālattil ak kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷil caivamatam paraviyiruntatu. an nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟa paḻaiya aḻipāṭukaḷum kalveṭṭukaḷum paḻaṅkataikaḷum naṉku ārāyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷai ātāramākak koṇṭu annāṭṭu varalāṟukaḷ eḻutappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. av varalāṟu kaḷāl ak kālattuk kaṭal kaṭantu ceṉṟa tamiḻaraippaṟṟi yām naṉku aṟital kūṭum. maṇimēkalai eṉṉum tamiḻnūl (ki.pi. 200) cāvakat tīvilē nākapurattiliruntu aracāṇṭa pūmicantiraṉ, puṇṇiyarācaṉ eṉṉum aracar iruvaraik kuṟippiṭuvatōṭu tamiḻvaṇikar marakkalaṅkaḷil carakkukaḷai ēṟṟit tolaiviṭaṅkaḷiṟ koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṟṟup perum poruḷīṭṭiṉārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu.
tiritirayatā eṉṉum pirāmaṇaṉ kampōtiyāviṉiṉṟum eṇṇūṟu kiḷiṅkuk (Kling) kuṭumpaṅkaḷōṭu ceṉṟu piramāri kāḷi eṉṉum yāvaka mātai maṇantu an nāṭṭukku aracaṉāṉāṉ eṉa an nāṭṭup paḻaṅ kataikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. yāvakattiṉ mutal aracaṉukku srīmāṟaṉ eṉṟu peyar. ip peyar cāvaka nāṭṭiṉ araca paramparai tamiḻ uṟpattiyuṭaiya teṉpatai naṉku viḷakkum. yāvāviluḷḷa perok eṉṉum makkaḷiṭaiyē iṉṟum tirāviṭak kuṭumpap peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.1 yāvāvilē mikap periya cila ālayaṅkaḷiṉ aḻipāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻaiya purāṇaṅkaḷil yāvakat tīvu, cuvarṇa pūmi eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. yāvakat tīvilum ataṉai aṭuttuḷḷa tīvukaḷilum kāṇappaṭum aḻipāṭukaḷālum, kiṭaittuḷḷa paḻamporuḷkaḷālum piṟavaṟṟālum aṟiyakkiṭappaṉa piṉvarumāṟu: 1yāvāt tīviṟ kāṇappaṭum tirukkōyilkaḷuṭ pala civaṉukku uriyaṉa. yāvaka makkaḷiṉ amaramālai eṉṉum nūl paḻamai yuṭaiyatu. aḵtu am makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa teyvaṅkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉ ṟatu. an nūliṉāl an nāṭṭiṉar civaṉaiyē talaimaiyākakkoṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷeṉat terikiṉṟatu. civaṉ, kuru, īsvaraṉ eṉṉum peyar kaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. cumattiyāvilē lārā yoṅṟāṅ (Lara Yongrang) eṉṉumiṭattil ālayak kūṭṭaṅkaḷiṉ periya aḻipāṭu uḷḷatu. itaṉ naṭuvil periya civaṉ kōyilum, irumaruṅkilum piramaṉ, tirumāl ālayaṅkaḷum etirē nantiyiṉ ālayamum uḷḷaṉa. valappāti civaṉum iṭappāti umaiyumākavuḷḷa arttanārīcuvarat tiruvaṭivamum aṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. vināyaka vikkirakaṅkaḷ mikap pala iṅkuḷḷaṉa. ivai intiya ālayaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷaic cālavum ottaṉa.
pōrk kaṭavuḷākiya kārttikēyak kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷum yāvāviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṉita vaṭivam mayilmītu ēṟiccelvatu pōṉṟa uruvaṅkaḷē perumpālum uḷḷaṉa. āṟu talaiyum paṉṉi raṇṭu kaiyumuḷḷa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ cilavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. civaṉ iliṅka vaṭivilum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār.
caṅku cakkaram tāmarai kēṭakam eṉpavaikaḷaip piṭittirukkum nāṉku kaikaḷuṭaiya tirumāliṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷum kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. pāmpiṉmītu paṭuttut tuyil koḷḷum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ cilavum iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ilakkumiyum cattiyapāmaiyum iru pakkaṅkaḷilum niṟkum tiruvuruvam pālit tīviṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. pūmālai cāmarai tāmarai karakam eṉpavaikaḷaik kaikaḷil vaittirukkum nāṉku mukamuṭaiya piramāviṉ vikkirakam yāvāviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cēram (Seram) eṉṉum pakutiyil amarāya eṉṉumiṭattil mummūrttikaḷiṉ tiruvuruvaṅkaḷōṭu poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa civaṉ tiruvuruvam oṉṟuṅ kiṭaittatu.
celipiciyil (Celebes) civa vaṇakkattukkuriya ciṉṉaṅkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. an nāṭṭu makkaḷiṉ paḻaṅkataikaḷil civaṉ pārata kuru eṉappaṭukiṉṟār. kiṟāpāṭ (Crawfurd) eṉpār niyukiṉiyiṟ kāṇap paṭum camayakkiriyaikaḷ civavaḻipāṭṭiṉ tēyvukaḷ eṉak kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. mēṟkup pōrṉiyōvil nanti, kaṇēcar, iliṅkam mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivaikaḷuṭaṉ oru poṉ iliṅkamum kāṇappaṭṭatu iṅkuk kuṟippiṭattakkatu.
1malāyk kuṭā nāṭṭiluḷḷa tīvukkuc ciṅkapuram eṉṉum peyar an nāṭṭu makkaḷāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭataṉṟu; yāvāt tīviṟ kuṭiyēṟi aṅku niṉṟum veḷippōnta teṉṉintiya makkaḷāl iṭappaṭṭatu. ataṟkut tamacak allatu tamacā2 eṉṉum peyar iruntateṉak koḷḷi ṉum, atuvum intiya uṟpattikku uṭaiyatākum. cumattirā eṉpatum intiya makkaḷāṟ koṭukkappaṭṭa peyarē. cumattirā eṉpataṟku nalla poruḷ eṉṟa karuttu.3 ki.pi. 1601 mutal ciṅkapuram ciṅkappūr eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟatu malāyk kuṭā nāṭṭilē kopurā icuvam (Hopura-Isuom) eṉṉumiṭattil kāṇappaṭṭa kaṇēca vikkirakattil teṉṉintiya eḻuttukkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṅku aḻakiya naṭarācat tiruvuruva moṉṟum kāṇappaṭṭatu. iṉṟu malāyk kuṭā nāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭum makkaḷ tamiḻariṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyavarkaḷāvar eṉac cuvaṟṟiṉkām eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kampōtiya makkaḷiṉ paḻaṅkataikaḷil kampucuvāyam pava eṉpavarukkuc civaṉ aḷitta mērā allatu pērā eṉpavaḷiṭam piṟantu perukiyavarkaḷē an nāṭṭu makkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. kampōtiyā kocciṉ cīṉā teṟkuc cīyam mutaliyaṉa aṭaṅkiya pakutiyaic cīṉar pūṉāṉ (Funan) eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. kavuṇṭiṉiyā eṉṉum pirāmaṇaṉ nākiṉi cōmā eṉṉum aracakulap peṇṇai maṇantu kampōtiyāvukku aracaṉāṉāṉ eṉa an nāṭṭup paḻaṅkataiyuḷḷatu.4 ilaṅkai teṉṉintiya kiḻakkuk karaiyōraṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷē kampōtiyāvil kuṭiyēṟi ṉārkaḷ.5 kampōtiya aracar, varmaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭappeyar puṉaintu muṭicūṭṭappeṟṟaṉar. teṉṉintiya pallava aracarkaḷum varmaṉ eṉṉum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉar. cantiravarmaṉ eṉṉum kampōtiya aracaṉ ki. pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāvukku vantāṉ. aṅkōrvāṭ (Ankor-Vat) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭum kōviṟ kōpuraṅkaḷ teṉṉintiya kōyiṟ kōpuraṅkaḷai ottuḷḷaṉa. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭum naṭarācat tiruvuru vaṅkaḷum piṟacāṉṟukaḷum iṅkuk kuṭiyēṟiṉōr teṉṉintiya makkaḷē eṉpatai viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa.1 kampōtiya aracaruḷ pāvavarmaṉ eṉpōṉ ciṟanta civapattaṉ. ivaṉ ki. pi. 616il kampīravāṇar eṉṉum oru civaliṅkattai nāṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭu vantāṉ. kampōtiyar kaṭavuḷaruḷ civaṉē talaimaipeṟṟiruntār. piracār, ciṉeṅ, kaṟāpi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ civaṉ umaiyōṭu iṭapattil vīṟṟirukkum tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. iṭapattiṉmītu vīṟṟirukkum vaṭivattilum pārkka naṭarāca vaṭivamē makkaḷāl peritum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. aracarum potumakkaḷum iliṅkaṅkaḷai nāṭṭiyataik kuṟippiṭum kalveṭṭukaḷ pala kampōti yāviṟ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
cīyattilē pāṅkok nakarukku arukēyuḷḷa kuḷam oṉṟiṉ pakkattē kālaṉ iṭapam iliṅkam eṉṉum cilaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.2 ivai kaḷiṉ muṉṉē potumakkaḷ cattiyañ ceytārkaḷ. pāccima eṉṉum putta ālayattiṉ veḷiyē civaliṅkamoṉṟu uḷḷatu. peṇkaḷ itaṉaic cuṟṟivantu tutittup piḷḷaivaram vēṇṭukiṉṟārkaḷ. ivvālayam muṉpu civaṉ kōyilāyiruntateṉpatiṟ ciṟitum aiyamillai. malāyar yāvakar makamatiyar mutaliya ellāc camayattiṉarum civaṉukkuc ceyvatu pōlap pūvum paḻamum poṅkalum vaittu iv vālayattil vaḻipaṭukiṉ ṟaṉar. kocciṉ cīṉa aracaṉ oruvaṉ vicaya īsvarar ālayaṅkaṭṭi aṅkuc civaliṅkap piratiṭṭai ceyta varalāṟu kalveṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
kampōtiyāvilē civaṉ kōyil oṉṟiṟ cetukkappaṭṭuḷḷa kalveṭṭiṟ kāṇum viparam piṉvarumāṟu:
yōkavarmaṉ,3 ācciramattukkuk koṭuttuḷḷa muttu, poṉ, veḷḷikkācu, kutirai, erumai, yāṉai, āṭavar, makaḷir, pūmālai mutaliya vaṟṟai aracar evarum kavarntukoḷḷutal ākātu. pirāmaṇar, aracaṉ, aracaṉiṉ piḷḷaikaḷ māttiram āparaṇaṅkaḷaik kaḻaṟṟātu kōviliṉ uḷḷē cellalām. perumakkaḷiṉ parivāraṅkaḷākac cellum potumakka ḷum āṭamparamillāta uṭaiyuṭaṉ uḷḷē cellām; avarkaḷ nanti yāvartta mālaiyaiyaṉṟi vēṟu mālaikaḷai aṇintukoṇṭu cellutal ākātu. ālayattuḷ iruntu evarēṉum veṟṟilai pōṭṭukkoḷḷavō uṇavu aruntavō kūṭātu. potumakkaḷ ālayattiṉ uḷḷē cellutal ākātu; uḷḷē caṇṭai allatu parikācam mutaliyaṉavum ceytal kūṭātu. civaṉaiyum tirumālaiyum vaṇaṅkum pirāmaṇarum nalla paḻakka vaḻakkamuṭaiyavarkaḷum māttiram uḷḷē taṅki niṉṟu metuvākat tutipāṭik kaṭavuḷait tiyāṉikkalām. aracaṉaiyaṉṟi vēṟevarāvatu ālayattukku muṉṉāl cella nērntāl tēriṉiṉṟum kīḻē iṟaṅkik kuṭaipiṭiyātu naṭantu cellutal vēṇṭum. piṟa nāṭṭavarkaḷukku ivviti vilakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pirāmaṇar, aracar, mantirimār, aracaṉiṉ piḷḷaikaḷ, tirumālai vaḻipaṭum kurumār, potumakkaḷuḷ ciṟantōr mutaliyavarkaḷ viruntāka vantāl ālayattiṉ talaimaik kuru ivar kaḷai varavēṟṟuc coṉṉa muṟaippaṭi uṇavu, nīr, veṟṟilai mutaliyaṉa koṭuttum avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiyaṉa purintum upacarittal vēṇṭum.
itil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭaḷaikaḷaik kaṭakkiṉṟavar evarum ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum uḷḷa aḷavum narakattaic cērvārkaḷ; ik kaṭṭaḷai kaḷukkuṭpaṭṭu avaikaḷai valuppaṭuttukiṉṟavarkaḷ it tarumattai uṇṭākkiyavar peṟum palaṉiṟ pātiyaip peṟuvar.
kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum eḻunūṟu tōḻarōṭu ceṉṟa vicayaṉeṉ ṉum irācakumāraṉē ilaṅkaiyiṉ mutal aracaṉ eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. kaliṅkar tamiḻarkaḷē eṉpatu evarumaṟinta uṇmai. muṉ ‘ellam’ eṉa vaḻaṅkiya ilaṅkaittīvu ciṅka vamicattavaṉākiya kaliṅka aracaṉ āṇṭa maiyiṉ ciṅkalam (ciṅkaḷam) eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. iṉṉum ciṅkaḷa makkaḷ tām vicayaṉatum avaṉ 700 tōḻarkaḷatum vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷ eṉa nampi varukiṉṟaṉar.
cumattirā, yāvā, intuccīṉam, malāyā, ilaṅkai mutaliya nāṭukaḷi lellām nākavaḻipāṭu kāṇappaṭṭatu. itu kāraṇamāka an nāṭukaḷiṉ makkaḷ nākar eṉavum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. yāvāt tīvilē aracaṉum avaṉaic cūḻntu tiriyum perumakkaḷum poṉ pāmpu, poṉ yāṉai, poṉ māṉ, poṉ kōḻi, arccuṉaṉiṉ aṉumakkoṭi eṉpavaikaḷaik kaiyil vaittiruntārkaḷ. iṅkuk ki. pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ ṟamiḻiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭa paṭṭaiyamoṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.1 itaṉāl oru kālattil tamiḻ yāvāttīvil araciṉar moḻiyāka iruntateṉa naṉku aṟiyalām. aruccuṉaṉ nākar kulak koṭiyākiya yāvakamātai maṇantu aravāṉaip peṟṟāṉeṉṟu yāvaka makkaḷ nampukiṉṟaṉar.
intiyāvilē pautta camayam talaiyeṭuttapiṉ pali marapiṉar kaṭal vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu pālittīvilum ayalēyuḷḷa tīvukaḷilum kuṭi yēṟiṉar. it tīvukaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷai otta oḻukka muṭaiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉa akaram eṉpavar kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār.1 māpali acuraṉ eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. acurarkaḷ vētakālattukku muṉpum ataṉ muṟpakutiyilum tēvarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉar. piṟkālattil avarkaḷ irākkatar allatu tēvarkaḷiṉ pakaivar eṉappaṭṭaṉar.2 irāmāyaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭum vāliyum civaliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉaṉ. vāliyiṉ marapum paliyiṉ marapum oṉṟōveṉpatu ārāyattakkatu.

iyal-7
tamiḻar camaya varalāṟu
iṉṟu iv vulakil palvēṟu niṟattiṉarum moḻiyiṉarumākiya makkaḷ āṅkāṅku vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. maṉitavaralāṟṟu, moḻi varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷar, ivarkaḷ ellōrum oru maiyattiṉiṉṟum pirintu ceṉṟavarkaḷēyeṉa ārāyntu aṟutiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. itaṉai oppavē makkaḷiṉ camayamum oṉṟāyiruntu vaḷarcciyuṟṟup piṉ palavēṟu kiḷaikaḷākap pirintateṉpatu uyttaṟiyappaṭum.
ammai vaḻipāṭu
makkaḷiṭaiyē ātiyiṟṟōṉṟiyatu tāy vaḻipāṭu. vīram celvam kalvi pōṉṟavaikaḷukkellām talaimai peṟṟirukkum kaṭavuḷar peṇpāliṉarē. ulakil ātiyiṟṟōṉṟiyatu peṇ āṭci. ak kālattil maṇat toṭarpukaḷ iṟukkam aṭaiyavillai. tāy peṟum kuḻantaikaḷ kuṭumpattukkuc contamāṉavai; tantaikku avaikaḷiṭam urimai uṇṭākavillai.1 iṉṟum iv vaḻakkut tiruntāta makkaḷiṭai yum tiruntiya makkaḷ cilar iṭaiyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. malaiyāḷa nāṭṭil marumakkaṭṭāyam eṉṉum vaḻakkuṇṭu. itaṉpaṭi tantaiyiṉ cottup piḷḷaikaḷaic cērātu avaṉ cakōtariyiṉ piḷḷaikaḷai aṭaikiṉṟatu.2 tiruvitāṅkūr aracurimaiyum peṇvaḻiyaiyē cērkiṉ ṟatu. aracaṉiṉ marumakaṉum marumakaḷumē iḷaiya irācar, iḷaiya irāṇi eṉṉum paṭṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉar. aracaṉiṉ piḷḷaikaḷukku irācciyattil oruvakai urimaiyum illai.1 iv vaḻakkukaḷellām peṇ āṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattiṉ etirolikaḷē. cakōtariyiṉ makaṉ makaḷai maṇam muṭittaliṉālēyē peṇṇiṉ tantaiyiṉ cottu marumakaṉaic cītaṉamāka aṭaikiṟateṉat tēyilar eṉṉum maṉitaviyalār kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. ‘tāyam’ eṉpatu peṇ āṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattut tōṉṟiya col. itu, cottut tāyiṭamiruntu piḷḷaikaḷai aṭaintatu eṉpatai naṉku viḷakkuvatu. tāyāṭci uṇṭāyirunta kālattil piḷḷaikaḷ tāyaic cūḻntiruntārkaḷ. tāyiṭattil ellā atikāramum iruntaṉa. ellā atikāramuṭaiya tāy teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāḷ. ‘tāyiṟ ciṟan toru kōyilillai’ allavā? iv vaḻipāṭu makkaḷ vāḻkkaip paruvattiṉ mika muṟkālattilēyē tōṉṟiyiruntatu. iṉṟu tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ cilaikaḷ, vaḷarnta kaṭaivāyp paṟkaḷuṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teṉṉintiya makkaḷiṉ nākarikam paṭaippukkālam mutal paṭippaṭiyē vaḷarntuvantuḷḷateṉpatu muṉṉamē kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
it tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ vaṭivamum ātikālattil makkaḷ koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭa vaṭivaip piṉpaṟṟiyē veṭṭappaṭṭum eḻutappaṭṭum varu kiṉṟateṉakkoḷḷutal tavaṟākātu. tārviṉ (Darwin) eṉpār ellā rālum uccimēl vaittup pukaḻappaṭum uyirnūṟ pulavar. ivar ātikāla makkaḷukku vaḷarnta kaṭaivāyppaṟkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟum, avai paccai iṟaicciyaik kiḻippataṟkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum, avaikaḷiṉ payaṉ kuṟaintakālattil avai vaḷarccik kuṉṟi vēṭṭaip pal (Canine teeth) aḷavil irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.2 ākavē, tāykkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu makkaḷukkuk kaṭaivāyp paṟkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭaintirunta oru kālattil ārampittateṉak koḷḷalām.
tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ vākaṉam ciṅkam. avaḷ kaiyilirukkum āyu taṅkaḷ taṇṭum kēṭakamum. kaṭavuḷarukku nāṉku kaikaḷum pala kaikaḷum koṭukkum vaḻakkam kiṟittava āṇṭukkup piṉṉaiyatu eṉa 1mākṭaṉal eṉṉum āciriyar ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. avar kūṟuvatu:
“intuk kaṭavuḷariṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷai amaikkum muṟaiyil kiṟittava āṇṭiṉ ārampattil cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. ataṟku muṉ ik kaṭavuṭcilaikaḷ maṉitaṉaippōla iraṇṭu kaikaḷuṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. ivvakai vaṭivaṅkaḷē pautta ciṟpaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. iraṇṭām kaṟpīs (Kadphis II; 50. A.D.) eṉṉum kirēkka intiya aracaṉiṉ nāṇayattil civaṉ iraṇṭu kaikaḷuṭaiyava rākavē kāṇappaṭukiṟār.” mokañ cotarōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa civaṉvaṭivukku iraṇṭu kaikaḷ iruppatum mēṟpaṭi kūṟṟai valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu. ciṉṉa āciyāvilē kitaiti eṉṉum nāṭu oṉṟu uḷḷatu. im makkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu nākarikam mutaliyaṉa tamiḻ makkaḷuṭaiyaṉa pōṉṟaṉa. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭa paḻaiya nāṇayamoṉṟil oru kaiyiṟ ṟaṇṭum maṟṟak kaiyiṟ kēṭakamum piṭittuc ciṅkattiṉ mītu vīṟṟirukkum tāyk kaṭavuḷiṉ uruvam poṟikkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu. itaṉāl paṇṭaiya tāykkaṭavuḷiṉ vaṭivam iraṇṭu kaika ḷuṭaiyatākavē iruntateṉat tuṇiyappaṭum. peṇ ciṅkattiṉ mītirukkum kuṭaicu eṉṉum tāykkaṭavuḷē ekiptiyaratu pōrt teyvamākum.
tāykkaṭavuḷ mēṟku āciya makkaḷāl asreṟet herā, ṟiyā mutaliya pala peyarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. makkaḷ mikat tāḻnilai aṭaintirunta kālattē tāyk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ārampittateṉpatu ataṉ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟaikaḷāl naṉku aṟitum. tamiḻ makkaḷ, kāṭukiḻāḷ, koṟṟavai, vintai, kāḷi mutaliya peyarāl tāykkaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭ ṭārkaḷ. tāyk kaṭavuḷukku narapaliyum, erumai āṭu mutaliya piṟa palikaḷum iṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷāl aṟikiṉṟōm. kāḷi pālai nilatteyvam. pālainila makkaḷ, vēṭar, iṭaiyar, uḻavar eṉṉum makka ḷiṉ vāḻkkaip paṭikaḷil ārampap paṭiyiluḷḷōr. ākavē, tāyk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu, makkaḷ payiriṭātum, mirukaṅ kaḷaip piṭittup paḻakki vaḷarkka aṟiyā tum irunta oru kālattil ārampitta tākum. tāyk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭiṉpōtu pāmpu vaḻipāṭu ārampikka villai pōlum.
nāṇayattil iṭapattiṉ mītu irukkum apparum, arukil ciṅka vāka ṉattiṉ mītirukkum ammaiyum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. ciriyā, pāpilōṉ, ekiptiya nāṭukaḷilum ciṅka vākaṉamuṭaiya tāyk kaṭavuḷē mēlāṉa teyvamāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu1.
appar vaḻipāṭu
tāykkaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ivvāṟu tōṉṟi naṭaipeṟṟu varumpōtu tiru maṇak kaṭṭuppāṭu iṟukkamaṭaintatu; tantai āṭci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. tantaiyē ellā atikāramum uṭaiyavaṉāṉāṉ. ākavē, tantai vaḻipāṭu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. oḷavaippirāṭṭiyar. “aṉṉaiyum pitāvum muṉṉaṟi teyvam” eṉa aruḷicceytirukkiṉṟamaiyum ammai appar vaḻipāṭṭai ōraḷavu viḷakkuvatākum. tantai vaḻipāṭu, kālattil vēntaṉ vaḻipāṭāka māṟiṟṟu. tāykkaṭavuḷai oppap paḻaiya makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa tantai vaṭivam iṉṟaiya civaṉ vaṭivamē eṉat terikiṉṟatu. kittaiti nāṭṭilē kāṇappaṭṭa paḻaiya nāṇayattil tāyk kaṭavuḷukku valappakkattē, muttalaivēlum kaṇṭakōṭariyum kaiyiṟ ṟāṅkiya appar vaṭivam oṉṟu iṭapattiṉmītu niṟkiṉṟatu.1 itē vaṭiviṉa tākak kallāṟ cetukkappaṭṭa cilaikaḷaiyum annāṭṭu makkaḷ vaḻi paṭṭārkaḷ. civaṉukkuriyaṉavākac collappaṭum elumpu, ūmattai mālai, caṅka veṇkuḻai, pulittōluṭai mutaliyavaikaḷāl makkaḷ, tōlai uṭaiyākavum, elumpu ippi mutaliya vaikaḷai aṇikaḷākavum koṇṭa oru kālat til iv vaḻipāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉṟu naṉku cintit taṟiyalākum. muṟ kālattil intiya makkaḷukku vākaṉamākap erutukaḷē payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟum ālayaṅkaḷil erutukaḷiṉmītu vāttiyakkārar ēṟiyiruntu pērikai koṭṭiccelvatai nām pārkkalām. ākavē, ak kālat tantaikkaṭa vuḷukku iṭapam ciṟanta ūrti ākkappaṭṭatu. kitaiti, cīriyā, kirēttā, cumēriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉ2 nāṇayaṅkaḷil imil uḷḷa intiya iṭapam poṟikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ākavē iṭapattai ūrtiyākavuṭaiya tantai vaḻipāṭu intiya nāṭṭilēyē ārampittiruttal vēṇṭum.
kitaiti nāṭṭu aracaṉukkum iraṇṭām iramce eṉṉum ekiptiya aracaṉukkum iṭaiyil eḻutappaṭṭa uṭaṉpaṭikkai oṉṟu cūḷaiyiṭṭa kaḷimaṇ takaṭukaḷuṭ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. av vuṭaṉ paṭikkai ki. mu. 1290il eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atil iṭiyēṟṟaik kaiyiṟ piṭittirukkum tantaik kaṭavuḷē ellāk kaṭavuḷarilum mēlāṉavar eṉṟu kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. Golden Bough-IV, P. 137.
“His hands are raised, in his right he holds a single headed axe or hammer, in the left a trident of wavy lines, which is thought to stand for forked lightning or a bundle of thunderbolts...... The combination of the bull with the thunderbolt as emblems of deity suggests that the animal may have been chosen to represent the sky God not merely of its virility but of its voice; for in the peal of thunder premitive man may well have heard the bellowing of a cellestial bull.-Ibid.
It is from the Indian mythology that we get our nearest insight into the character of these deities and the meanings of those sculptures. For the bull represents Siva whose emblem was a trident and also an exe-The Hittite empire-John Garstang.
kittaiti nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭṭa vēṟu cila ciṟpaṅkaḷil ak kaṭavuḷ mukkōṇa vaṭivāṉa villum cūlamum piṭittu iṭapattiṉmītu niṟkiṉṟār. iṭapattiṉ kompukaḷ pūmālaikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
“In his right hand which is drawn pack there is a triangular bow. The horns of the bull are wreathed with garlands-Ibid P. 202.
ciriyā makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa aṭāṭ eṉṉum civaṉ kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaṭivam oṉṟu av viṭattiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa veṇkalat taṭṭu oṉṟiṉ mītu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ak kaṭavuḷ oru kaiyil iraṇṭu alakuḷḷa kōṭariyaiyum (maḻu) maṟṟak kaiyil kīḻum mēlum muttalaikaḷuṭaiya vēlai yum piṭittirukkiṉṟār. mēṟku āciya makkaḷ mut talai vēl allatu cūlattai iṭiyēṟu eṉa vaḻaṅ kiṉārkaḷ. “At his(The Jupiter of Syria) side hangs his sword in its scabbard. His right hand brandishes a double axe; his left grasps a thunderbolt consisting of six spirally-twisted lines, each of which is tipped with arrow head. The bull that supports the God has a rosette on the forehead between eyes-Zeus P. 619.
iṭapattiṉ neṟṟiyil pūmālai kaṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. “....for in the Roman art he reappears under the title of Jupiter Dolichenus, wearing a phrygian cap, standing on a bull, and weilding a double axe in one hand and a thunderbolt on the other. In this form his worship was transported from the native home by soldiers and slaves till it had spread over a large part of the Roman empire, specially on the frontiers where it flourished in the camps of the legions”-The golden bough-a study of art and religion-part IV, P. 134-J.G. Fracer.
ammai appar vaḻipāṭu
ivvāṟu ammai appar vaḻipāṭukaḷ ātiyiṟ ṟaṉittaṉiyē tōṉṟiṉa. piṉpu makkaḷ ivarkaḷai maṉaivi kaṇavaṉ eṉṉum muṟaiyiṉa rākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭalāyiṉar. kitaiti nāṭṭil nāṇayattiṟ kāṇap paṭṭa vaṭivaṅkaḷum itaṉaiyē kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēṟku āciya nāṭu kaḷil ammai appar vaḻipāṭu iṉṉoru vakaiyilum ārampittatu. ñāyiṟṟut, tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭukaḷ avarkaḷiṭaiyē tōṉṟiya kālattil avarkaḷ tiṅkaḷait tāykkaṭavuḷākavum ñāyiṟṟait tantaik kaṭavuḷāka vum koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. ākavē, paḻaiya ammai appar vaḻipāṭukaḷ mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil ñāyiṟṟut, tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭukaḷuḷ nuḻain tuḷḷaṉa.
ñāyiṟṟu vaḻipāṭu
itaṉmēl ivvulakil iruḷaippōkki oḷiyaik koṭukkum ñāyiṟē muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷ eṉṟu makkaḷ uṇara ārampittaṉar. avarkaḷ civantavaṉ allatu civanta oḷiyuṭaiyavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷil ñāyiṟṟukkuc civaṉ eṉṉum peyar koṭuttu vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. ip peyarē ātiyil ñāyiṟṟukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉpataic civaṉ eṉṉum talaippiṉkīḻ viḷakkutum. civaṉ eṉpataṟku aṭuttapaṭiyil el eṉṉum peyar katiravaṉiṉ peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. mēṟku āciyāvil elliṉ tiripākiya peyarkaḷē ñāyiṟṟaik kuṟikkap peritum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ulaka makkaḷ ellōrum kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarāka āṇṭa peyarkaḷai ārāyu miṭattu avai oḷi allatu oḷiyuṭaiyavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷaiyē koṭukkiṉṟaṉa.1 el eṉpatu ñāyiṟṟaiyum oḷiyaiyum kuṟikkat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiya cālap paḻaiya peyar. “ellē ilakkam” eṉpatu tolkāp piyam. ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷai makkaḷ malaimukaṭukaḷiṟ kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. ākavē ulakam muḻumaiyilum ik kaṭavuḷiṉ iruppiṭam malaimukaṭu eṉṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. iṉṟum civaṉ kailāya malaiyil irukkiṟār eṉap potumakkaḷum piṟarum nampi varukiṉṟaṉar. malaikaḷ illāta nāṭukaḷil ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ ceykuṉṟukaḷ mītu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. iv vakaic ceykuṉṟukaḷi loṉṟē pēpel eṉṉum kōpuram2 ivvakaic ceykuṉṟukaḷ mēṟku āciyā, āpirikkā, amerikkā eṉṉum nāṭu kaḷiṟ pala uḷḷaṉa. ulaka viyappukkaḷ eṉappaṭum ekiptiya kūrnutik kōpuraṅkaḷum iv vakaiyiṉavē. ivai ñāyiṟu naṭu uccikku varum nāḷil atu eḻum ticaiyaip pārkkumpaṭi kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1 teṉṉin tiya ālayaṅkaḷum kiṟittavarkaḷiṉ ālayaṅkaḷum ivvāṟē kaṭṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. calamaṉiṉ ālayamum kiḻakkut tikkai nōkkiyē niṉṟatu.
iliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉ toṭakkam
aṭuttapaṭiyil makkaḷ ceykuṉṟukaḷukkup patilaṭi nimirntu talaikuvinta kaṟkaḷaic cōlaikaḷilum veḷikaḷilum naṭṭu vaḻipaṭuvā rāyiṉar. tiruvāṉaikkā, tillaiyampalam, tiruvālaṅkāṭu mutaliya teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷāl itaṉ uṇmai naṉku teḷitum. muṉ iliṅkaṅkaḷ niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷilēyē piṉpu ālayaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ulakam muḻumaiyilum ivvakai vaḻipāṭu oru kālattiṟ kāṇappaṭṭatu.
ālamar kaṭavuḷ
ovvoru kirāmaṅkaḷilum maṉṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. maṉṟameṉpatu ūrukku naṭuvē makkaḷ kūṭum marattaṭi. marattaic cuṟṟit tiṇṇaiyiruntatu. ñāyat tīrppu, paḷḷikkūṭam, āṭal pāṭal mutaliya poḻutu pōkkukaḷ iṉṉum kirāma makkaḷiṉ ellāk kiḷarcci, nikaḻcci eṉpavaikaḷukku iṭam ituvē. maṉṟattē perumpālum ālamaram niṉṟatu. niḻal maraṅkaḷil āl talaimaiyuṭaiyatu. maṉṟattu maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ iliṅkam niṟuttap paṭṭiruntatu. itaṉaic cuṟṟit tiṉamum alakum meḻukkumiṭappaṭṭu viḷakku ēṟṟappaṭṭatu.1 āliṉ kīḻ iruntamaiyiṉ kaṭavuḷukku ‘ālamar kaṭavuḷ’ ‘ālamar celvaṉ’ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil ip peyarkaḷē civaṉaik kuṟikkap peritum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āl aiyaṉ iṭam ‘ālayam’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum aruṭkuṟikaḷ karuvāli maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa;2 kirēttāvil yūkāvipras maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ kaṟṟūṇkaḷ niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiyarum kirēkkarum āli ṉattaic cērnta atti marattaip paricuttamuṭaiyatākak koṇṭu, ataṉāl iliṅkaṅkaḷ ceytaṉar.
tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭu
ñāyiṟṟu vaḻipāṭṭukkup piṉ tiṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭu tōṉṟiṟṟu. intiya nāṭṭil cantiraṉ āṇ teyvamākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. mēṟku āciya nāṭṭavar itaṉaip peṇ teyvamākak koṇṭaṉar. aṉṉōr marattiṉkīḻ niṟuttappaṭṭa kaṟṟūṇai ñāyiṟākavum marattait tiṅka ḷākavum koḷvārāyiṉar. piṉpu tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷiṉ maṉaiviyākkappaṭṭatu. maraṅkaḷum cōlaikaḷum tiṅkaṭkaṭavuḷāka vaḻi paṭappaṭṭaṉa. marattukkup patil marattūṇkaḷum niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil āṇ kaṭavuḷ peṇ kaṭavuḷaraik kuṟikka iliṅkaṅkaḷ niṟuttappaṭalāyiṉa. tamiḻ nāṭṭil ivvāṟu nikaḻa villai.
iliṅka vaṇakkam ulakam muḻumaiyum kāṇappaṭṭatu
ec. ci. vels eṉṉum āciriyar iliṅka vaḻipāṭu iṅkilāntu mutal kiḻakkē yappāṉ varaiyum kāṇappaṭṭateṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. amerikkāviṉ cila pākaṅkaḷil iṉṟum iv vaṇakkam kāṇappaṭuvatai ārāycciyāḷar ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. mukkērci eṉpavar intiya nākarikamum ataṉ paḻamaiyum eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa vaṟṟiṉ curukkattai īṇṭut tarukiṉṟōm.
1“intiya makkaḷ civaṉiṉ aruṭkuṟiyākiya civaliṅkattait toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. iv vaṇakkam intiyāvuḷ māttiram niṟkavillai. iḵtu ulakiṉ ellā nāṭukaḷilum orukāṟ paraviyiruntateṉpatai viḷakkuvataṟku vēṇṭiya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa. cīṉā, yappāṉ, intuk kaṭal, pacipikkaṭaṟ ṟīvukaḷ mutaliya iṭaṅ kaḷil iv vaḻipāṭu muṟṟākat tūrntu viṭavillai. āpirikka amerikka2 makkaḷiṭaiyum iḵtu orukāṟ paraviyiruntatu. acīriya, yūtēya, cīriya, ciṉṉa āciya, papilōṉiya makkaḷiṭaiyum iv vaṇakkam kāṇappaṭṭa teṉpataik kiṟittava maṟai vāyilāka aṟikiṉṟōm. ciṉṉāṭkaḷiṉmuṉ papilōṉ nāṭṭil civaliṅkaṅkaḷ pala akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiṉ cila pakutikaḷil hem, hōṟas, isṟas, carāpis mutaliya peyarkaḷāl ik kaṭavuḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu. civaliṅkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ pulikaḷum pāmpukaḷum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiya camātic cuvarkaḷil civaliṅ kaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. paḻaiya airōppāvil iv vaṇakkam eṅkum paraviyiruntatu. itaṉai ōṭṭuvataṟkuk kiṟittava kurumār mika ūkkam eṭuttārkaḷ. kirīcilē vicā (Viza) eṉṉumiṭattil civaliṅkat toṭarpāṉa kiriyaikaḷ iṉṟum naṭaipeṟukiṉṟaṉa. ayarlāntil civaliṅkaṅkaḷ, pala kiṟittava ālayaṅkaḷuṭ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai ‘cīlanākik’ (Sheila-na-gig) eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iḵtu orupōtu civa liṅkam eṉpataṉ tiripu ākalām. paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷar iṅki lāntilum iskottilāntilum civaliṅkaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaṇṭu piṭittaṉar. ivai kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ urōmar kōṭṭaikaḷai yum māḷikaikaḷaiyum kaṭṭi vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷākum. urōmar iv vaṇakkattai iṅkilāntilē parappiyiruttalkūṭum. cermaṉiyilum pirāṉciyilum iv vaṇakkam mikku vaḻaṅkiyiruntataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. lituvēṉiya makkaḷ (Lithuanians) 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil iv vaṇakkattaiyē kaikkoṇṭiruntu piṉpu kiṟittava matattait taḻuviṉārkaḷ. tipettu, puttāṉ (Bhutan) eṉṉu miṭaṅkaḷil civaliṅka vaṇakkam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cintōyicim (Shin-toism) eṉṉum yappāṉiya matattil civaliṅkam mutaṉmaiyāṉatu. amerikkāviṉ pala pakutikaḷil ciṟappāka meccikkō, pēru, kaiti tīvu (Hyti)kaḷil civaliṅkam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. spāṉiyar mutal mutal amerikkāvukkuc ceṉṟapōtu ālayaṅkaḷil civaliṅkaṅkaḷ vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭataik kaṇṭārkaḷ. āppirikkāvil takōmi (Dahomi) eṉṉum iṭattil iliṅkam leṅpā (Lengba) eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.
“iliṅka vaṇakkam acīriyāviṟṟōṉṟi ayal nāṭukaḷiṟ paraviṟṟu eṉṟu cilar niṉaikkiṟārkaḷ. kirēkka nāṭṭil pakkas (Bacchus) tayōṉicas (Dionysus) vaḻipāṭukaḷ toṭarpākac civaliṅkam vaḻipaṭap paṭṭatu. kirīcil pakkas viḻāvil iliṅkam vītikaḷ vaḻiyē ulāk koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. ekiptiyariṉ ōsṟis eṉṉum teyvam pakkas eṉpataṉ iṉṉoru vēṟupāṭē. pakkas vaḻipāṭu ittāliyil mikavum parantiruntatu. pakkas kepaṉ (Hebin) allatu kampāṉiyā (Campania) eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. kepaṉ kepi (Hebe - kauri) eṉṉum tēviyuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. pakkacukku iṭapamum puliyum mikap piriyamāṉavai. avar 1pulittōlai yuṭuttuk kaiyil tiricūlattaiyum vaittiruntār. ātalāl kepaṉ civaṉ eṉṟum, kepi kauri eṉṟum kūṟalām. pakkas kaṭavuḷaip piṉtoṭarntu iṭapamum puliyum ceṉṟaṉa. avar kaiyil aruntum maṇṭaiyum iruntatu. ciṉṉa āciyāvil iliṅkakkaṭavuḷ kemōs (Chemos) molōc (Moloch) meratok (Merodock) ātōṉiyis (Adonais) capāciyas (Sabazius) pakkas allatu pākēōs (Bagaios) eṉṉum peyarkaḷuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. ves top (Westropp) eṉpavar atōṉicas eṉpatu arttanārīcuvarar eṉṟu ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ciṉṉāṭkaḷiṉmuṉ ekiptilē ‘turkkammā’ eṉa aṭiyil eḻutiya oru turkkai uruvam oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.”
ṟoṭēciyāvil (āppirikkā) kāṇappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷilum ciriyāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷilum pūmālaikaḷ veṭṭappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. poṉīciya makkaḷ tayar nakarilē pakalavaṉ ālayattil marakatat tiṉālum poṉṉiṉālum ceyta iliṅkaṅkaḷai vaittu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. ivvakai iliṅkaṅkaḷai avarkaḷ cipiṟālṭaril nāṭṭiyiruntamaiyiṉ avviṭam iṉṟum “kekkuviciṉ tūṇkaḷ” eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷiṟ1 pēḻaikaḷ (peṭṭi) vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟu ilaṅkaiyiluḷḷa katirkāmam eṉṉum ālayattil vaḻipaṭappaṭu vatum pēḻaiyē. mēṟku āciya nāṭṭup pēḻaikaḷuḷ ciṟiya iliṅkam vaikkappaṭṭiruntatu. nilaipōla iraṇṭu kaṟkaḷai niṟutti mēl oru pāvu kallai vaittu mūṭiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉkīḻ (Dolmen) vaittu iliṅkaṅkaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. itiliruntē pēḻaiyil iliṅkattai vaittu vaḻipaṭum muṟai ārampittatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. viviliya maṟaiyil pēḻaiyil vaikkappaṭṭa cāṭcip pattiram eṉpatu nammavariṉ “iyantirat takaṭu” eṉpatai ottatē.
iliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉ vaḷarcci
kaṭavuḷ oruvarē uḷar eṉa uṇarnta makkaḷ ammai apparkaḷiṉ aruṭkuṟikaḷē iṟaivaṉiṉ āṇ taṉmai peṇ taṉmai eṉpavai kaḷai uṇarttum eṉak koṇṭaṉar. āṇ taṉmai peṇ taṉmaikaḷai viḷakkum aṟikuṟiyāka avarkaḷ āṇkuṟi peṇkuṟi vaṭivāṉa aṭai yāḷaṅkaḷaiyum vaittu vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. piṉpu iv viraṇṭum oṉṟu cērkkappaṭṭuk kaṭavuḷ āṇ peṇ iyalpiṉar eṉṉum ilappiṉum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. iv vaṭivaṅkaḷ elōkim eṉappaṭṭaṉa. mokeñ cotarōvilum āṇ kaṭavuḷaik kuṟikka āṇkuṟiyum, peṇ kaṭavuḷaik kuṟikkap peṇ kuṟiyum vaḻaṅkiṉa.2 elōkim vaḻipāṭē intiyāvil āvuṭaiyār vaḻipāṭākum. paṇṭaiya iliṅkaṅkaḷ tūṇ vaṭiviṉa. āṇ peṇkuṟi vaṭivukaḷ airōppāvilē kiṟittava ālayaṅ kaḷil iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.3 erucalēmilē oru kālattil ov voru vītiyilum iliṅkaṅkaḷ vaittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.1 ñāyiṟṟaiyum tiṅkaḷaiyum oppat tīyum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iliṅkam tīyiṉ vaṭivākavum koṇṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.
tī vaḻipāṭu
ñāyiṟṟaiyum tiṅkaḷaiyum oppa oḷiyuṭaip poruḷākiya tīyum makkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. viviliya maṟaiyiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭil nim ṟoṭ eṉṉum aracaṉ tīvaḻipāṭṭaip palastīṉ nāṭṭil ārampittāṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaḻipāṭu iṉṉum pāracīkariṭaiyē corāsriya matak koḷkaiyāka nilavukiṉṟatu. tīyum iliṅka vaṭivil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. pīṭattōṭu kūṭiya iliṅkattukkut tīkkaṭai kōlai oppiṭṭut, tīk kaṭaikōlaiyē iliṅka vaṭivamāka amaittu makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ eṉac cila ārāycciyāḷar karutiṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷiṭaiyē muccuṭar vaḻi pāṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. tīyai enta nēramum uṇṭākki vaḻipaṭutal kūṭum. ñāyiṟṟaiyum tiṅkaḷaiyum enta nērattilum kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭutal aritu. ākavē avarkaḷ ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṉum muccuṭarkaḷiṉ vaṭivaik kuṟikkum vaṭṭam, vil allatu piṟai, mukkōṇam vaṭivāṉa kuṇṭaṅ kaḷai amaittu avaikaḷil tī vaḷarttuk kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. ituvē muttī vēṭṭal eṉṉum vaḻakku. āriya makkaḷiṉ tī vaḻipāṭu iv vuṇmaikaḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu eḻuntataṉṟu. avarkaḷ kālaiyil eḻuntu tōyntu, tīmūṭṭi erittuk kuḷir kāyntu ataṉ pakkattē niṉṟu kaṭavuḷait tutittaṉar. iv vaḻakkiliruntē avarkaḷatu akkiṉikōttiram eṉappaṭum tī vaḻipāṭu eḻuntatu. caṉakaṉ yākñaval kiyaiyum avaruṭaṉ niṉṟa pirāmaṇaraiyum nōkki nīvir akkiṉikōt tiram eppaṭic ceykiṟīrkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭapōtu avarkaḷ cariyāṉa viṭai aḷikkavillai. itaṟkuk kāraṇam āriyaratu tī vaḻipāṭum tamiḻaratu tī vaḻipāṭum vevvēṟu vakaiyiṉavātalē. ākamaṅkaḷum vētaṅkaḷum nērmāṟāṉavai eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar muṭivu. tī vaḻipāṭu turāṉiya makkaḷukkuriyateṉṟē varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.2
“koṭinilai kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa
vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalaṉa mūṉṟum
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē” (tol. puṟa. 33)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭum vaḻipāṭukaḷ muṟaiyē ñāyiṟu tī, tiṅkaḷkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭukaḷē. paḻaṅkālap palipīṭaṅkaḷum piṟkālam iliṅkamāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa.1 tiruvaṇṇāmalaiyil iṟaivaṉ cōti vaṭivāy niṉṟāṉeṉpatu iliṅkavaḻipāṭu tī vaḻipāṭṭiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi yatu eṉṉum aitīkattaik kuṟippatu. muṟkālattu oṭuṅkiya koṭumuṭi kaḷuṭaṉ nimirntu vaḷarnta malaikaḷ iliṅkaṅkaḷāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa.2
vēntaṉ
makkaḷ ñāyiṟṟait taṉipperuṅ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭa kālattil aracaṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉ 3putalvaṉeṉṟum iṟantapiṉ avaṉ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷāka māṟukiṟāṉ eṉṟum makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.4 ākavē aracaṉiṉ cāmātiyiṉmītu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷiṉ aruṭkuṟiyāka iliṅkam naṭap paṭṭatu. av viṭaṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya ālayaṅkaḷākavum iruntaṉa. iv vakai ālayaṅkaḷ pala ekiptil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavu ḷāka māṟiya aracarkaḷ ekiptil iṟā (iravi) eṉṉum peyar peṟṟaṉar. ṟā eṉpatiliruntu irācaṉ eṉṉum col piṟantateṉac cilar kūṟuvar.
nantivaḻipāṭu
appaṉ eṉṉum tantaik kaṭavuḷiṉ ūrtiyākiya nanti mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriyatāyiruntatu. palastīṉ nāṭṭil āṉkaṉṟu allatu nanti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭamai kiṟittava maṟaiyiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāvītiṉ (David) maṉaivikkē āṉkaṉṟu eṉṟu peyar.1 kitaiti nāṭṭilē eyuk (Eyuk) eṉṉumiṭattilē nantic cilai karuṅkaṟ pīṭattil vaittu makkaḷ ellōrālum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iv viṭapak kaṭavuḷaic cēvikkat taṉikkuru iruntār. ekiptiya makkaḷ ōciṟis kaṭavuḷiṉ vākaṉamākaya apis eṉṉum iṭapattai vaḻipaṭ ṭārkaḷ. ellā mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷālum iṭapam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iṭapat tiṉmītu cattiyañ ceyvatai makkaḷ peritum añciṉārkaḷ.2
mukkaṭ ceylavar
makkaḷ, ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ tī eṉpavaikaḷukku mēlāṉa taṉip perumporuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉṟu koṇṭa kālattil avarkaḷ paḻaiya appaṉ vaṭivattukku muccuṭarkaḷaik kaṇkaḷ eṉak koṇṭaṉar. āṅkāṅku makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa pēykaḷ avariṉ parivāraṅkaḷāyiṉa. ulaka makkaḷellārālum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa pāmpu avariṉ āparaṇaṅkaḷāyiṉa; payaṅkaramāṉa tāyk kaṭavuḷ ippoḻutu cānta vaṭiviṉaḷāy avariṉ pāriyāyiṉaḷ. umai, eññāṉṟum kaṉṉi eṉappaṭṭār.3 mokañcota rōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa civaṉ tiruvuruvattukku mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.4
mātorupāti iṟaivaṉ
ammai appark kaṭavuḷar, pāti āṇ, pāti peṇ vaṭivilum vaittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉar. iv vaṭivu “artta nārīcurar” vaṭivam eṉap paṭum. arttanārīcura vaṭivamē ātōṉis eṉa mēṟkutticai nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉa muṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. iv vaṭivam paḻaiya cumēriya makka ḷālum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu.5 mokañcotarō makkaḷ apparai āṇ eṉṟum ammaiyai ammai eṉṟumē vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. cumēriyā nāṭṭi lum itē peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ammai appariṉ putalvar cumēriyarāl eṉlil eṉappaṭṭār. mokañcotarōvil ammai mīṉkaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭār. ippeyarē mīṉāṭci eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
pāmpu vaṇakkam
iv vulakiṉ ellāp pākaṅkaḷilum pāmpu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu. mocē veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyta pāmpai isravēlar vaṇaṅki vanta varalāṟu, kiṟittuva maṟaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kiṟittuva maṟaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum 
paṭaippu varalāṟu mutaliyaṉa cālaṭiya makkaḷiṭamiruntu kiṭaittaṉa eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cālaṭiya makkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷē eṉpatu varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷatu muṭivu. pāmpu vaṇakkam illāta nāṭu iv vulakil illai. itaṉai ārāyntu viḷakkum taṉi nūlkaḷ pala āṅkila moḻiyil eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟum cila nāṭukaḷil uyirp pāmpukaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa; cila nāṭukaḷil pāmpiṉ cilaikaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
“āppirikka makkaḷiṉ mukkiya teyvam pāmpu. maḻaiyiṉmai, pañcam, piṇi mutaliya kālaṅkaḷil makkaḷ pāmpukaḷaic ciṟappāka vaḻipaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. iv vakaiyāka urōmarum orukālattiṟ ceytaṉar. oru nikirō vaṉāvatu vēṇṭumeṉṟu pāmpukku oruvakaiyāṉa tīmaiyuñ ceyyamāṭ ṭāṉ. taṟceyalāyp pāmpu oṉṟaik kāyappaṭuttukiṟavaṉ niccayamākak kollappaṭuvāṉ. orumuṟai āṅkilak kappaṟkārar cilar tāṅkaḷ taṅkiya viṭṭiṉuḷ vanta pāmpu oṉṟai aṭittukkoṉṟārkaḷ. ataṟkāka ivarkaḷ aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷāl koṭumaiyākat tākkik kollappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. pāmpukaḷ uṟaivataṟkeṉak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kuṭicaikaḷ nāṭu eṅkum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷukku mutiya peṇkaḷ tiṉamum uṇavu koṭukkiṟārkaḷ. pāmpukaḷukku aḻakiya periya kōyilkaḷum irukkiṉ ṟaṉa. ovvoru vakaip pāmpaik kavaṉippataṟkum ovvoru vēlaik kāraṉ irukkiṟāṉ.”
“niyukiṉiyil cōlaikaḷil pāmpukaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ iruk kiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷukkup paruva kālaṅkaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu paṉṟi, āṭu, kōḻi mutaliyaṉa uṇavāka aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa”1 itu pāmpu vaṇakkattiṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
ivvaṇakkam evvāṟu uṇṭāṉateṉṟu varalāṟṟu āciriyar kaḷāl naṉku aṟintu kūṟa muṭiyavillai. itaṉ toṭakkam maṟaivāka irukkiṉṟatu. ulakil kāṇappaṭum makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉar tattam kulakkuṟi kaḷāka (Totem) mayil, āṭu, muyal, āmai pōṉṟa yātēṉum oṉṟaik koṇṭiruntaṉar; iṉṟum koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ik kulakkuṟikaḷ piṟpāṭu vaḻipaṭavumpaṭṭaṉa. iv vakaik kāraṇattaip pāmpu vaṇakkat tiṟkum koḷḷalāmō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu. intiya nāṭṭil pāmpu vaḻipāṭu naṭaipeṟṟu varuvataik kūṟavēṇṭiyatillai. pāmpuc cilaikaḷ illāta ālayaṅkaḷē illai eṉak kūṟalām.1
muruka vaḻipāṭu
muruka vaḻipāṭu ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkattiṉ iṉṉoru vakai. kaṭalmīteḻum utayakāla ñāyiṟu,2 mayilmīteḻuntaruḷum murukaṉāka uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭaṭuḷḷatu. murukaṉ eṉpataṟku iḷaiyavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ. yūtamakkaḷum pakaṟ kaṭavuḷai mutiyapakal, iḷampakal (Ba-al) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. mutiya pakal tāṭiyuṭaiya mutiya vaṭivuṭaṉum, iḷampakal kuḻantai vaṭivuṭaṉum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. yūta makkaḷukkuc camaya aṟivu koḷuttiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ marapaic cērntōr eṉappaṭukiṉṟamaiyiṉ avarkaḷiṭaiyē tamiḻarkaḷuṭ kāṇappaṭum pala koḷkaikaḷ nilaviṉa. iḷam kaṭavuḷ yūtarāl pōrk kaṭavuḷākavum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. iḷampakalē piṉ urōmariṉ mārs eṉṉum pōrk kaṭavuḷākavum māṟiṟṟu. murukakkaṭavuḷukkum acurar eṉappaṭṭa makka ḷukkum iṭaiyil nikaḻnta pōrkaḷ eṉpaṉa muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṉarukkum civavaḻipāṭṭiṉarukkum nikaḻnta pōrkaḷākum. acurar civavaḻipāṭṭiṉar eṉpatu muṉṉamē kūṟappaṭṭatu. yekōvā eṉpatu el kaṭavuḷaiyē kuṟikkum peyareṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. yekōvā vaḻipāṭ ṭiṉar elliṉ vēṟuvakai vaḻipāṭukaḷākiya pakal (Ba-al) moloc mutaliya vaḻipāṭukaḷai aḻikka muṉaintu niṉṟa varalāṟṟuṭaṉ itaṉai oppu nōkkuka. makkaḷiṭaiyē nikaḻnta pōrkaḷ makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa tey vaṅkaḷmītu ēṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. vēta pāṭalkaḷ, intiraṉ tācukkaḷiṉ celvaṅkaḷaik koḷḷaikoṇṭu avarkaḷai aḻittāṉeṉṟu kūṟu kiṉṟaṉa. āriya makkaḷ tām peṟṟa veṟṟikaḷait tām vaḻipaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ mītu ēṟṟik kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. ivvāṟu kūṟutal akkāla marapupōlum.
māl vaṇakkam
māl (mā+1el) vaṇakkamum ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkattiṉ iṉṉoru vakai. tirumāliṉ kaiyil irukkum cakkaram ñāyiṟṟaiyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu. “ariyallāṟ ṟēviyillai aiyaṉaiyāṟaṉārkkē”. perumāḷ (peru māṉiṉ peṇpāḵ) eṉa varuvaṉa ārāyattakkaṉa. orupōtu kariya tāyk kaṭavuḷ māel eṉa vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatō eṉpatu cintikkattakkatu. pārata kālattukkup piṉ tirumāl vaḻipāṭu kaṇṇaṉ vaḻipāṭāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. tirumāl mūvulakai aḷantāreṉa varum purāṇakkatai ñāyiṟu kālai, naṇpakal, mālai eṉṉum mūṉṟu kālaṅkaḷil cellum celaviṉai uṇarttumeṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
civaṉ
