toṉmaitoṭṭu intiyāviṉiṉṟum miḷaku veḷi nāṭukaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. ki. pi. 47 varaiyil iv vāṇikam mummaramāka naṭai peṟṟatu. ak kālattuc ceṅkaṭal vaḻiyāka intiyāvukkum alakcāntiri yāvukku miṭaiyil marakkalap pōkkuvarattu iruntatu. intiya arāpiya viyāpārikaḷ paruvakkāṟṟuk kālaṅkaḷai naṉku aṟintu kappaṟpayaṇañ ceytaṉar. ceṅkaṭal mukattuvārattiluḷḷa ōcilis (Ocelis) eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattiliruntu pāyeṭutta kappalkaḷ tuṭuppukaḷāl ōṭṭappaṭṭu muciṟip paṭṭiṉattai nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷil aṭaintaṉa. ivvāṟu alakcān tiriyā vaḻiyāka intiyāvukkum urōmaikkumiṭaiyil ki. pi. 215 varai yum iv vāṇikam mummuramāka naṭaipeṟṟatu. kiḻakkiliruntu urō mukkuc ceṉṟa poruḷkaḷuḷ mukkāṟpakuti miḷaku. ak kāla vāṇipat tiṟkut tēvaippaṭṭa miḷaku muḻumaiyum malaiyāḷak karaiyiliruntu ceṉṟatu. mikap paḻaṅkālantoṭṭu miḷaku airōppiya nāṭukaḷil iṟaic ciyaip paḻutupaṭāmaṟ pātukākkavum, uṇavukaḷukkuc cērkkavum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. kirēkkar maruntu muṟaiyil itaṉai mikutiyākap payaṉ paṭuttiṉar. kippōkkiṟēṟcu (Hippocrates) eṉpavar itaṉai intiya maruntu eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ākastas kālattil miḷaku urōmil mika matippuḷḷa carakkākak karutappaṭṭatu. ak kālattu ōr irāttal miḷakiṉ vilai ēḻu rūpāy; intiyāvil ataṉ vilai itaṉ pāti aḷavu tāṉum illai. paruvakālattai aṟintu kappalkaḷ mikutiyāka intiyā vukku ōṭattoṭaṅkiya kālattil miḷakiṉ vilai kuṟaintuviṭṭatu. atu māvāka iṭittup poṭṭaṇaṅkaḷāka viṟkappaṭṭatu. alāṟik eṉṉum kotiyaṉ urōmai muṟṟukai iṭṭapōtu, muṟṟukaiyai viṭuvataṟku, avaṉ ēṉaiya poruḷkaḷōṭu 3,000 irāttal miḷakaiyum kēṭṭāṉ. urōmiliruntu intiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ mikakkuṟaivu. ākavē, urōmiliruntu ērāḷamāṉa celvam intiyāvai aṭaintatu. urōmiliruntu ērāḷamāṉa taṅka, veḷḷi nāṇayaṅkaḷ intiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. intiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa pallāyirakkaṇakkāṉa urōmaṉ nāṇayaṅkaḷuṭ perumpālaṉa malaiyāḷakkaraiyilum ataṉai yaṭutta kōyamuttūrk kōṭṭam, maturai eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. in nāṇayaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa tipēriyas kuḷōṭiyas kēyas (Tiberius, Gaius and Claudius) kālattaṉa.
“yavaṉartanta viṉaimāṇ naṉkalam
poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyoṭu peyarum”
eṉa akanāṉūṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
muciṟi malaiyāḷakkaraiyilē pēr āṟṟu mukattuvārattiluḷḷatu. tamiḻ nūlkaḷ itaṉaip periya tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉamākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. poṉīciyārum arāpiyarum it tuṟaimukattil viyāpāram naṭattiṉar. urōmiliruntu pāvai, aṉṉa viḷakkukaḷ, nakaippēḻaikaḷ, maṇa muḷḷa matu eṉpaṉa iṟakkumatiyāyiṉa. aracarum celvarum im matu viṉai uṇṭaṉar. peturuṅkēriyariṉ aṭṭavaṇaiyiṉpaṭi muciṟiyil ākastasiṉ kōyil oṉṟiruntatu. tamiḻ aracariṉ paṇṭacālaikaḷai yavaṉa vīrar kāval purintaṉar. muciṟiyil yūtar arāpiyar pāracīkar kuṭiyēṟi yiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ taṉittaṉi vītikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. tōmas ñāṉi yār (Apostle Thomas) muciṟikku ayaliluḷḷa mallaṉkārāvil ki. pi. 50il vantu iṟaṅkiṉāreṉac cīriya kiṟittavarkaḷ nampi varukiṉṟaṉar.
ki.pi. 215kkuppiṉ iv vāṇikam viḻuntuviṭṭatu; pāracīkak kuṭāk kaṭal vaḻiyākavum tarai mārkkamākavum ōraḷavu naṭaipeṟṟatu. cīṉar tam marakkalaṅkaḷil cīṉāvukku miḷaku koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar.
iṭaikkālattil kōyilkaḷukku miḷaku kāṇikkaiyākak koṭukkappaṭṭatu. ak kālattil ōr irāttal miḷakiṉ vilai nālu civiṉ. atu taccu vēlai ceypavaṉ oruvaṉiṉ nālu nāṭkūli. iv vāṇikam veṉis, ceṉōvā (Genoa) viyāpārikaḷiṉ kaiyil irun tatu. avarkaḷ itaṉai arāpiyariṭamiruntu vāṅkiṉar. iv viyāpārikaḷ paṇañ campātippatai aṟinta mēṟku airōppiya makkaḷ kiḷarcci koṇ ṭaṉar. ataṉāl avarkaḷ intiyāvukkuc cellum kiṭṭiya pātaiyai aṟiya muyaṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl kolampas amerikkāvaik kaṇṭupiṭit tāṉ. vāskōṭakāmā eṉṉum pōrttukkēyar kaḷḷikkōṭṭaiyai 1498ām āṇṭu aṭaintār. 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil ṭaccukkārar airōppāvil miḷaku viyāpārattaik kaippaṟṟiṉar. ak kālattil ōr irāttal miḷakiṉ vilai mūṉṟu ‘civiṉ’; muṉ ‘āṟuciviṉ’ varaiyi liruntatu. itaṉāl ilaṇṭaṉ viyāpārikaḷ kiḷarcci koṇṭārkaḷ. cilar elicapet irāṇiyiṭam uttaravupeṟṟuk kiḻakku nāṭukaḷuṭaṉ viyā pārañ ceyyap puṟappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ kiḻakkē aracukaḷaik kōliṉar.

iyal - 9
moḻi
avarkaḷ tam eṇṇaṅkaḷaip piṟarkkuṇartta viḻaikiṉṟaṉar. appoḻutu moḻi uṇṭākiṉṟatu. ātiyil makkaḷ ūmarum ceviṭa rum pēcuvatupōlac cāṭaikaḷālum caikaikaḷālum mukakkuṟikaḷālum pēciṉar. itu naṭippu muṟaiyil uḷḷa pēccu. im muṟaiyiliruntē nāṭakat tamiḻ vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. makkaḷaic cūḻntu palavakai iyaṟkaip poruḷkaḷum kuruvikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷum iruntaṉa. ivai ovvoṉṟum ovvōr oliyai eḻuppiṉa. itu ātimakkaḷiṉ kavaṉattukku vantatu. avarkaḷ ivvolikaḷai uṟṟukkēṭṭu av volik kuṟikaḷai avvap poruḷkaḷaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṉar. ‘kā kā’ eṉak kattum paṟavaiyaik kākam eṉṟum, ‘kū kū’ eṉak kūvum kuruviyaik kuyileṉ ṟum, ‘mā’ eṉṟu aḻum vilaṅkai māṭu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkutal itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu. ippoḻutu naṭippōṭu olikkuṟikaḷum kalantu moḻi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ataṉ piṉpu avarkaḷ tammaic cūḻntirunta ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum, kuṇaṅkaḷaiyum, ceyalkaḷaiyum kuṟikka ovvōr olikkuṟikaḷai amaittu olimuṟaiyāṉa moḻiyai ākkikkoṇṭa ṉar. iṉṟum pēccāḷar mēṭaikaḷil niṉṟu pēcum pōtum pēccukkēṟṟa pala avinayaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivai paḻaiya pēccu vaḻakkiṉ tēyvukaḷē. āti makkaḷ coṟkaḷai ākkuvataṟkuk kaikkoṇṭa 1kāraṇaṅkaḷ (moḻip poruṭkāraṇam) perumpālum iṉṟu nammāl aṟintu kūṟamuṭiyāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa.
coṟkaḷ talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyāka nīṇṭa kālam toṭarntu varukiṉṟaṉa. kālantōṟum putitāka aṟiyappaṭum poruḷkaḷaiyum ceyalḷaiyum kuṟikkap putiya coṟkaḷ tōṉṟum. ivvāṟu oruvakaiyil moḻi vaḷarntukoṇṭu cellumpōtu paḻaiya coṟkaḷ cila vaḻak koḻiyum; avaṟṟiṉ iṭattaip putiya coṟkaḷ cila ēṟkum. itaṉāl makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum moḻiyil kālantōṟum vēṟupāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭum. muṉṉōr evvāṟu coṟkaḷai uccarittārkaḷō avvāṟu avaikaḷai uccarikka aṟitalum, avaikaḷai avarkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya marapil payaṉpaṭuttutalumē tiruntiya moḻippayiṟciyākum. paratanāṭṭiyam pōṉṟu naṭippu māttirattiluḷḷa kūttait tamiḻar nāṭakattamiḻ eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. makkaḷiṉ aka uṇarccikaḷukkēṟpa eḻum iyaṟkai oli kaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi, makiḻcci, vīram, cōkam pōṉṟa uṇarccikaḷai eḻuppun toṉiyiṟ pāṭappaṭum pāṭalkaḷ icaittamiḻ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivai pōlallāta pāṭalkaḷum, ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum iyaṟṟamiḻ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
ātikālat tamiḻukkum piṟkālat tamiḻukkum ciṟitu vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. “moḻikaḷ taṟkālam tāṅki niṟkum vikuti, vēṟṟumai yurupu mutaliya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ yāvum, nāḷaṭaivil vantu pukuntaṉa. itu moḻiyārāycci yāḷar kaṇṭu aṟutiyiṭṭa muṭivu. taṉiyē niṉṟu poruḷ payavāk kuṟiyīṭu kaḷākiya iv vurupukaḷ yāvum taṉiyē niṉṟu poruḷ tantu utaviya moḻikaḷiṉ citaivil vanta miccameṉavum avarkaḷ tuṇintuḷḷārkaḷ. oru kālattil tiṇai pāl iṭam cuṭṭā viṉaimuṟṟu āṭci naṭaipeṟ ṟirukkalām eṉṟum tuṇiyalām. malaiyāḷattil tiṇaipāl iṭam cuṭṭātu kālam oṉṟaṉaiyē cuṭṭum viṉai muṟṟu āṭci kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu.”
moḻi, eḻuttunilai ilakkiyanilai ilakkaṇanilai eṉṉum nilaikaḷai muṟaiyē eytukiṟatu. ulakiyal moḻiyiṉ teḷivē centamiḻ.
pēccumoḻi, eḻuttunilai aṭaiyumpōtu oru cīrtiruttamum, eḻuttunilai kaṭantu ilakkiyanilai peṟum pōtu oru tiruttamum, ilakkiya nilaiyil niṉṟu ilakkaṇanilai aṭaiyumiṭattu iṉṉoru tiruttamum aṭaikiṉṟatu.
1koṭuntamiḻeṉpatu makkaḷiṭaiyē eḷiya vaḻakkiluḷḷa tamiḻ. koṭumai - vaḷaivu. “iyaṟkaik kalaiyiṉ tiṭṭattaiyum moḻivaralāṟ ṟuṇmaiyaiyum kaikkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉvaḻi nōkkiṉ tiṇai pāl iṭam vikutik kuṟiyīṭaṉaittum viṉaimuṟṟuc coṟkaḷukkup piṟkālattut tāṉ vantaṭaintaṉaveṉpatu pōtarum. paṉṉeṟippaṭṭu varampu uṇarttaṟkiṉṟip pēccunilaiyil māttiramirunta moḻiyai ilakkaṇa varampu koṭuttuc centamiḻākkiṉa kālattukkuc ciṟitu muṉṉar ivai vaḻakkiṟ pukuntirukkalām.
“ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟappeṟṟa kālattil an nūlkaḷ vititta varampukku uṭpaṭātu niṉṟa iyaṟkai moḻi oṉṟu iruntirukka vēṇṭu meṉap pōtiya niyāyaṅkaḷāl ūkikkalām. avviyaṟkai moḻiyil tiṇai pāl iṭañcuṭṭā viṉaimuṟṟu vaḻakkup pirayōkam naṭaipeṟ ṟirukka vēṇṭumeṉavun tuṇiyalām.
“moḻiyuṟpattik kālattil mutaṟṟōṉṟiyaṉavākiya poruṭkuṟip pum, iyakkappaṇpukkuṟippum, nilaippaṇpuk kuṟippum paṟṟiya peyarc col viṉaiccol uriccol eṉṉum mūṉṟaiyum nīkkiya piṟacoṟka ḷellām iṭaiccol eṉṉum oru peyaruḷ aṭakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.”1
arappā mokañcotarōp putaiporuḷ ārāycciyiṟ kiṭaitta paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟai heras pātiriyār vācittuḷḷār. ip paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷai olimuṟaiyil vācippataṟkuk karuviyāyiruntavai paḻaiya cumēriya eḻuttukkaḷēyākum. moḻikaḷiṉ iyaṟkai eṉṉum nūlil māksmūlar eṉṉum paṇṭitar cīrtiruntiya makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷ māṟṟameytum iyalpaiyē iḻantuviṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉap pukaṉṟuḷḷār. itaṟkut tamiḻmoḻi ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu.2
intiya nāṭṭuḷ āriyar, pāracīkar airōppiya kirēkkar, skītiyar, kaṉs (Huns), apkāṉiyar, tāttāriyar, maṅkōliyar, arāpiyar mutaliya pala iṉamakkaḷ pukuntu intiyamakkaḷuṭaṉ kalantuḷḷārkaḷ. ataṉāl putiya makkaṭ kūṭṭaṅkaḷum putiya moḻikaḷum eḻuntaṉa. 3iṟaisli eṉṉum āciriyar intiya makkaḷ eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
mēṟku intiyāvil vāḻvōr cittiya tirāviṭar. ip pirivil marāṭṭiyap pirāmaṇar kuṉpiyar (Kunbis) kuṟukkar mutaliyōraṭaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷ cittiyar tirāviṭar kalappiṟṟōṉṟiṉōr. pañcāp, cintu, kūrccaram, irācaputtāṉa, mattiya intiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil skitiyar orupōtu kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. āriyat tirāviṭar aikkiya mākāṇattiluḷḷa akrā, ayōtti, irācaputtāṉā, pikār mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. maṅkōliya tirāviṭar vaṅkāḷattiṉ teṉpākattilum, oricāvi lum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. nēpāḷam, acām, parmā, imayamalaip pakkaṅkaḷi luḷḷa tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷ maṅkōliya makkaḷiṉ kaṇkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa. parmiyar, maṅkōliyaruṭaṉ kalanta tamiḻar. turukkiya irāṉiya tamiḻar paluccistāṉattilum mēṟku ellaip puṟaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. arāpiya allatu apkāṉiya kalappuṭaiya tamiḻar tōṟṟattil skitiyar allatu turukkiyaraip pōṉṟōr. ivarkaḷ pala iṉa makkaḷiṉ kalappi ṉāl tōṉṟiyavarkaḷ ākalām. tirāviṭar ilaṅkai mutal kaṅkaiveḷi varaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
eḻuttu
makkaḷ pēcciṉāl tam eṇṇaṅkaḷai aṇmaiyiluḷḷavar kaḷukku viḷakkiṉārkaḷ. cēymaiyiluḷḷavarkaḷukkut tam eṇṇaṅ kaḷai viḷakkuvataṟku atu payaṉ aḷikkavillai. ākavē cēymaik kaṇ uḷḷavarkaḷukkut tam karuttukkaḷai uṇarttum mukattāṉ eḻut tukkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ātiyil makkaḷ taṅkaruttukkaḷaic cittiraṅkaḷ vāyi lāka viḷakkiṉar. oruvaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉai vāḷiṉāṟ koṉṟāṉ eṉṉum karuttai veḷiyiṭa vēṇṭumāyiṉ, kīḻē oruvaṉ utiram nāṟpuṟamum paravumpaṭi paṭuttukkiṭappatu pōlavum, maṟṟoruvaṉ kaiyil vāḷai vaittukkoṇṭu ōṅki veṭṭuvatupōlavum eḻutikkāṭṭuvatu vaḻakkam. iv vakaic cittiraṅkaḷum, uruvak kāṭcikaḷum maṉitaruṭaiya puṟat tuṟuppaip paṟṟiya ceyalkaḷaik kāṭṭuvataṟkāka māttiram payaṉpaṭṭu vantaṉavēyaṉṟi, maṉattiṉ nikaḻccikaḷākiya kōpam, poṟāmai, tukkam, makiḻvu, cerukku, vīram, payam mutaliya tōṟṟaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭavillai. ākavē makkaḷ iv vakai maṉattōṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum mēṟpaṭi uruvaṅkaḷ mūlamākat terivikkaveṇṇip pāṭupaṭṭu vantamai yāl, avarkaḷ, cittiravēlaiyilum kal, maram mutaliyavaṟṟāl uruvaṅ kaḷaic cetukkuvataip paṟṟiya vēlaikaḷilum tiṟamaiyaṭaintaṉar. eḻut tukkaḷ uṇṭāyirāta kāraṇattiṉālēyē ātiyil cittira vēlaikaḷum uruvam cetukkum vēlaikaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭaintiruntaṉaveṉṟu kūṟalām.
cittiraṅkaḷ perumpālum kaṭpulaṉākum poruḷkaḷai yaṉṟik kaṭpulaṉākāta maṉavuṇarccikaḷaiyum piṟa paṇpukaḷaiyum viḷakkuva taṟkup payaṉpaṭāmaliruntaṉa. ākavē maṉa uṇarccikaḷaiyum paṇpu kaḷaiyum uṇarttum cila kuṟiyīṭukaḷ cittiraṅkaḷuṭaṉ kalantu eḻutappaṭ ṭaṉa. itu aṟivaik kuṟikkak kaṇṇaiyum, kālattaik kuṟikka vaṭṭattai yum, naṉṟiyiṉmaikku viriyaṉ pāmpaiyum, viṭāmuyaṟcikkut tēṉīyai yum, veṟṟikkuk karuṭaṉaiyum eḻutik kāṭṭutal pōṉṟatu. pēru (teṉ amerikkā) tēcamakkaḷ eḻuttukkup patil kayiṟukaḷil palavāṟu muṭiccu kaḷiṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar.1
ivvāṟu eḻutuvatālum makkaḷ uccarippu muṟaiyāṉa moḻiyil tam eṇṇaṅkaḷaip piṟarkku uṇartta māṭṭātavarkaḷ āyiṉar. ākavē, avarkaḷ cila cittiraṅkaḷai eḻuti ac cittiraṅkaḷ kuṟikkum poruḷ kaḷukkuriya peyarkaḷiṉ mutal acaikaḷaic cērttu, vācikkak karutiya coṟkaḷiṉ uccarippu varum muṟaiyil eḻuta ārampittārkaḷ. itu kattarikkāyaiyum ataṉ pakkattē oru kōlaiyum eḻutik kattarikkōl eṉa vācittal pōṉṟatu. im muṟaiyil eḻutuvatum mikak kaṭiṉam aḷittamaiyiṉ ovvoru colliṉ uccarippukkum ovvoru kuṟiyīṭu amaikkappaṭṭatu. in nilaimaiyiluḷḷaṉavē cīṉa eḻuttuk kaḷum, mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷumākum. piṉpu moḻiyil ettaṉai olikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu uccarittu aṟintu ovvoru oliyaiyum kuṟikka ovvōr kuṟiyīṭu amaikkappaṭṭatu. ak kuṟiyīṭukaḷākiya eḻut tukkaḷaic cērttuc collai uccarikkum muṟai kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ekiptiya moḻiyil cittira eḻuttukkaḷ aṭainta palavakai māṟṟaṅkaḷ aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cittira eḻuttukkaḷ maṇ takaṭukaḷil caturamāṉa nāṇaṟ kuccikaḷāl eḻutappaṭṭapōtu kīṟukaḷāka amaintaṉa. avvakai eḻuttukkaḷ āppeḻuttukkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ cittira eḻuttil ārampittup paṭippaṭiyāṉa pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ aṭaintuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻar payaṉpaṭuttiya av vakai eḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟēṉum namakkuk kiṭaittila; cīṉa eḻuttukkaḷ pōṉṟa arappā mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷē kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. cittira eḻut tukkaḷ tamiḻil vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpataṟkuriya pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ yāpparuṅkala virutti mēṟkōḷ, tivākaram mutaliya nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
“kāṇap paṭṭa vuruva mellām
māṇak kāṭṭum vakaimai nāṭi
vaḻuvi lōviyaṉ kaiviṉai pōla
eḻutap paṭuva turuveḻut tākum.” (yāpparuṅkalavirutti. mēṟkōḷ)
“uruvē yuṇarvē yoliyē taṉmai
eṉavī reḻuttu mīriru pakutiya.” (ilakkaṇak kottu. mēṟkōḷ)
mokañcotarō eḻuttiṉiṉṟum pirāmi eḻuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu. itu acōkaṉ kālattum ataṟku muṉpum vaṭapirāmi, teṉpirāmi eṉa iru vakaiyāka naṭaipeṟṟatu. pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ ki. mu. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉpu uḷḷateṉpataṟku ātāramuṇṭu. iv vakai eḻuttup poṉiciyā vilē mōpnakariṉ (Moab) aracaṉatu (ki.mu. 850) paṭṭaiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ik kāraṇattiṉāl poṉīciya viyāpārikaḷiṭamiruntu intiya makkaḷ eḻutteḻutum muṟaiyai aṟintārkaḷ eṉṟu varalāṟṟu nūlār ellōrun tuṇiyalāyiṉar.1 āṉāl, mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷ ārāyappaṭṭapiṉ im muṭivu nērmāṟāka māṟiyuḷḷatu.2 pirāmi eḻuttuk kaḷiṉiṉṟu poṉīciya eḻuttukkaḷum, poṉīciya eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟu kirēkka, urōmai eḻuttukkaḷum, ivaikaḷiṉiṉṟum ēṉaiya airōppiya eḻuttukkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟum tamiḻ vaṭṭeḻut tukkaḷum, vaṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiliruntu ik kālat tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷum vaḷarcciyaṭaintaṉa. vaṭṭeḻuttu aṇmaikkālam varaiyil malai yāḷattilē kōleḻuttu eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
mattiyataraimakkaḷ iṉattiṉar ellōrukkum moḻiyum eḻuttum oṉṟē
ulakil makkaṭkulam eppaṭi oṉṟāyiruntatō appaṭiyē moḻi yum oṉṟāyiruntateṉpatu moḻinūlār naṉku ārāyntu kāṭṭiya uṇmai. ulakil mikap paḻaiya makkaḷ mattiyatarai mutal kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷvaraiyil vāḻntōrāvar.3 in nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiya mutiyamoḻikaḷ ōr aṭimarattiṉiṉṟum kavarviṭṭa komparkaḷ pōṉṟaṉa eṉṟu ārāycci vīrar nilaināṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. itaṉāl, tamiḻ, ātimoḻikku mika aṇmaiyil uḷḷatu eṉat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. cintunatip paḷḷattākkiṟ kiṭaitta muttiraikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷōṭu ulakiṉ pala pākaṅ kaḷil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷaiyum oppunōkkiya keras pātiriyār kūṟi yiruppatu varumāṟu:
“mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷaip pōṉṟaṉa ilipiya vaṉān tarattai aṭutta celimā eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉaip pērāciriyar ilāṅṭaṉ eṉpavarum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
“mokañcotarō eḻuttukkuṟikaḷ pōṉṟa (spāṉiya eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷaiyum avaipōṉṟa uccarippu olikaḷaiyum aipīriya moḻiyiṟ kāṇalām. (im moḻi pāsku eṉavum vaḻaṅkum.)
“etirūskiya (paḻaiya ittāliya) moḻi eḻuttukkaḷ ilipiya moḻi iṉattaic cārntaṉa. im moḻiyil mokañcotarō eḻuttuk kuṟiyīṭukaḷaiyum uccarippu olikaḷaiyum kāṇpatu kaṭiṉamaṉṟu.
“pintiya ilipiya moḻi eḻuttukkaḷum numitiya pēperiya (Numidic and Berberic) eḻuttukkaḷum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa iyalpiṉavē.
“miṉōva (Crete) eḻuttukkaḷ mikap paḻaiyaṉa. ivai mokañ cotarō eḻuttukkaḷōṭu kiṭṭiya oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa. caipiras eḻuttu miṉōva eḻuttiṉ oru piriviṉatē.
“cila moḻikaḷ eḻuttukkaḷ illāmal iruntaṉa. am moḻi kaḷukkuriyōr tirāviṭa makkaḷōṭu ūṭāṭiya piṉ mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷaik kaiyāṇṭaṉar. piṉpu avarkaḷ av veḻuttukkaḷait tāmē vaḷarcci ceytaṉar.”
“cumēriya moḻikkum mokañcotarō moḻikkum neruṅkiya uṟavu uṇṭu. ik koḷkaiyaip pērāciriyar ilāṅṭaṉ tam virivurai kaḷil naṉku valiyuṟuttiyuḷḷār.
“āppeḻuttukkaḷ cumēriya eḻuttukkaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟkālaṅ kaḷil vaḷarcciyaṭaintaṉa.”
“paḻaiya ellam moḻi eḻuttukkaḷ iṉṉum vācikkappaṭāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa.”
“paḻaiya cīṉa eḻuttukkum mokañcotarō eḻuttukkum neruṅkiya toṭarpuṇṭu. itu ivvaḷavu kālamum oruvar karuttilum paṭavillai. mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷum paḻaiya cīṉa eḻuttuk kaḷum oru tāy vayiṟṟiṟ piṟanta iraṇṭu piḷḷaikaḷ eṉṟu kūṟalām. kōyaṅ (Hoang ki. mu. 2500) kūṟṟāl itu valiyuṟukiṉṟatu.
“cāpiya allatu teṉ arāpiya eḻuttukkaḷ, iṉṉum mokañ cotarō eḻuttukkuṟikaḷ cilavaṟṟaik kāppāṟṟi vaittirukkiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkut tīvukaḷil eḻuttukkaḷ perumpālum mokañcotarō eḻut tukkaḷaippōlavēyirukkiṉṟaṉa: cila oṉṟākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.”1
pērāciriyar ci.ār. kaṉṟar kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
“tuḷaiyiṭṭa paḻaiya intiya nāṇayaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum aṭai yāḷaṅkaḷum mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟiyaṉavē. mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu pirāmi eḻuttu māttiramaṉṟu; teṉ arēpiya, miṉōva, eḻuttukkaḷum piṟantaṉa. mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷ teṟku āciyā vaḻiyāka vantu poṉīciya kirēkka urōmai eḻuttukkaḷaip piṟappittirukkiṉṟaṉa.”2
“cumēriyam ellam mutaliya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷōṭu oli muṟaiyil oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa. ākavē ṭākṭar cartēci eṉpavar kirēttā, iliciya, cumēriya, ellam moḻikaḷ orē toṭarpuṭaiyaṉa veṉṟum, aiciyaṉ tīvukaḷ ciṉṉa āciyā, mecapetēmiyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷ orē nākarika iṇaippuṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉaveṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.” (“tirāviṭa intiyā” pakkam 41-42)
eppaṭi mattiyatarai makkaḷum tirāviṭa makkaḷum orē iṉattavar kaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟārkaḷō, appaṭiyē avarkaḷ moḻikaḷum orē iṉattaic cārntaṉa eṉa ik kāla moḻi nūlār ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. moḻi nūlār kūṟiya muṭivukaḷai ellām oruṅku vaittu oppunōkkiya velcu eṉṉum āciriyar mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷil ātiyil vaḻaṅkiya āriyamallātatum kamattiyam allātatumākiya moḻi, pāskku (Basque) eṉappaṭum eṉṟum, atu tirāviṭa moḻikku neruṅkiya uṟavuḷḷateṉ ṟum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ataṉ curukkam varumāṟu:
“pāskkumoḻi, kākkēciya malaippakkaṅkaḷiṟ pēcappaṭum moḻikku iṉamuṭaiyatu; orukālattu atu mikavum paravalai aṭain tiruntatu; muntiya kamattiya moḻik kūṭṭaṅkaḷākap parantu mattiyatarai makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭṭatu; teṟku airōppāvilum mēṟku āciyāvilum vaḻaṅkiyatu; intiyāviluḷḷa tirāviṭa moḻikku neruṅkiya toṭar puṭaiyatu.
mēṟkut, teṟku airōppāvil pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ moḻikkūṭṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruntaṉa. āriyam varutalum, avai maṟain taṉa. paḻaiya moḻiyaic cārntavai kirēttā (Crete) ilitiya moḻikaḷ, ivai 1pāskku moḻiyaic cērntavai ākalām.
maṅkōliyam, pāskku, kākkēciyam mutaliya moḻikaḷai iṇaikkum moḻi cumēriyamāka virukkalām. itu meyyākaviruntāl, pāskku, kākkēciyam, tirāviṭam, maṅkōliyam mutaliya moḻikaḷ iṉṉum mikap paḻaiya moḻikaḷākum. āriyam cemittiyam kamittiyam mutaliya moḻikaḷait toṭukkum moḻi oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭāma lirukkalām. kuṭṭiyīṉum vilaṅkukaḷ eppaṭip palli muṉṉavaiyiliruntu (ancestors) vaḷarcciyaṭaintu pallikku iṉamāyirukkiṉṟaṉavō atu pōṉṟa iṉamē am moḻikaḷukkum im moḻikkum irukkalām.”2
mokañcotarō eḻuttukkaḷ
iv veḻuttukkaḷaip paṟṟik keras pātiriyār veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa cila uṇmaikaḷai īṇṭut tarutum. “iv veḻuttukkaḷ ōviya eḻut tukkaḷ toṭarpāṉavai. ivai kiḻakkut tīvukaḷ mutal ispeyiṉ tēcam varaiyil vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivai ōviya eḻuttukkaḷukkum olimuṟaiyil eḻutappaṭum eḻuttukkaḷukkum iṭaippaṭṭaṉa; intiya nāṭṭukku ayalil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷukkum cīṉa cumēriya ekiptiya eḻuttukkaḷukkum iṉamuṭaiyaṉa. maṉitaṉ, mīṉ, paṟavai, maram, nāṟkāli, mēl, kīḻ eṉpa vaṟṟai aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷāṟ kuṟippiṭṭuk kāṭṭuvatu eḷitu.
mokañcotarōvil vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷil pala tirāviṭa moḻi kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē tirāviṭa moḻikaḷukkut tāy mokañ cotarō moḻi eṉak kūṟalām. tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum mokañ cotarō moḻiccoṟkaḷil pala tamiḻiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē tamiḻ maṟṟaiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum mūppu uṭaiyatu eṉṟu karutappaṭu kiṉṟatu. maṟṟaiya tirāviṭa moḻikaḷil cila coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tuṉpattil āḻntiruttal eṉṉum poruḷ tarum pakil (Pagil) eṉṉum col kaṉṉaṭattil uḷḷatu. mutalilum kaṭaiyilum eṉpataik kuṟikkum kaṭitala, koṭitala eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tuḷu moḻiyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻaiya tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ pirākūy moḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭalām. kaṇ kal eṉṉum coṟkaḷ kkaṇ (Khan) kkal (Khal) eṉa am moḻiyil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
“yāṉ mokañcotarōp paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭavaṟṟai olimuṟaiyil eḻuti vaittiruntu avaikaḷai yāḻppāṇam cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācarukku vācittuk kāṭṭiṉēṉ. avar avaikaḷiṉ iṭaiyē irupattaintu ceyyuṭkaḷiṉ pakutikaḷ iruppataik kaṇṭu piṭittār. ap pāṭṭukkaḷ palavakaip pā iṉaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ aintu, kuṟaḷ veṇpākkaḷ. paṭippavarkaḷ atil oṉṟaik kēṭka virumpalām. ākavē avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai īṇṭut tarukiṉṟēṉ.
naṇṟuṟuṟu tūkkuaṭu kārmukilūr vēliorūr
eṭueṭṭu ṟuuyarel irāriri pērkaṭavuḷ
ōr ūr eṉṉum āṇṭai aṭaikiṉṟa cattamiṭum uyarac cellum ñāyiṟṟiṉ iraṇṭu vaḻikaḷaiyuṭaiya paccaik (payir?) kaṭavuḷ malaimukilkaḷum iṭiyumuṭaiya nāṭṭukku veḷiyē uḷḷatu.1
iṉṉoru paṭṭaiyattiṟ kāṇpatu,
“kallar mīṉavar kāṉ kaṭaer valviṭaṭu
kalkuṟava val kuraṅkar nāl” eṉpatu.
kalanta (uṟavukoṇṭa) nāṭukaḷiṉ makkaḷiṭaiyē nālu valiya kuraṅkar tōṉṟum (ammakkaḷiṉ) kōṭṭai valiya kāluḷḷa mīṉavarāṟ piṭikkappaṭṭatu eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
tal tā viṭṭil eṉpataṟku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vīṭu eṉṟu poruḷ. tal viḷaṅkutalaik kuṟikkum. mukil eṉpatu maḻaiyillāta mukilaiyum, karumukil eṉpatu maḻai mukilaiyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
kalpēṭ eṉṉum āciriyar kōriyā moḻi ilakkaṇam tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇattai ottiruppatai ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2
iṭappeyarum makkaṭpeyarum
muṟkālattē ūrkaḷ perumpālum kōyilaic cuṟṟi eḻuntaṉa. avvāṟu eḻunta iṭaṅkaḷ kōyiliṉ peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.1 mēṟku āciyāvilē acur eṉṉum kaṭavuḷiṉ kōyil eḻunta iṭam aciriyā eṉavum, pēpel eṉṉum kōyil tōṉṟiya iṭam pāpilōṉ eṉavum, niṉēv eṉṉum āṟṟut teyvam kōyil koṇṭa iṭam niṉōvā eṉavum peyar peṟṟaṉa. ciṟiya ūrkaḷ pala cērntatu oru nāṭākum. ciṟiya ūrkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ ciṟappāka nāṭṭiṉ peyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. oru nāṭṭu makkaḷ iṉṉoru nāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi ṉāl an nāṭu kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷiṉ peyarai ēṟṟalum iyalpu. itu pirit taṉil aṅkiḷar kuṭiyēṟiṉamaiyiṉ atu aṅkiḷlāntu (iṅkilāntu) eṉappaṭṭatu pōṉṟa vaḻakku. ātiyil āṇṭa aracaṉiṉ peyarkaḷaik koṇṭu iṭaṅkaḷukkup peyar uṇṭāvatumuṇṭu.2
makkaṭ peyariliruntu moḻippeyar
makkaḷ eppeyarāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟārkaḷō ap peyariliruntē moḻippeyar tōṉṟutal iyalpu. cērmaṉi, cērmaṉiyar, cērmaṉiyam; kirīs, kirēkkar, kirēkkam; malaiyāḷam, malaiyāḷar, malaiyāḷam; teluṅkāṉam, teluṅkar, teluṅku pōṉṟaṉa itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
tamiḻccol tōṟṟamum iv vakaiyiṉatē
tamiḻ ātiyil moḻikku uṇṭāṉa peyareṉṟum, ap peyari ṉiṉṟum makkaḷukkum nāṭṭukkum peyaruṇṭāyiṟṟeṉṟum, aṇmaik kālattu āciriyarkaḷ piṟaḻa uṇarntamaiyāl tamiḻiṉ tōṟṟattaip paṟṟip palavāṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. makkaṭ peyariliruntē moḻip peyar uṇṭātal viti eṉpatai viḷakki āciriyar civarācapiḷḷai avarkaḷ āṅkilattil eḻutiya kaṭṭuraiyiṉ pakuti varumāṟu:
“ataṉaip (moḻiyaip) pēcum makkaḷiliruntu moḻi peyar peṟṟateṉak koḷvatē takuti. moḻippeyariliruntu makkaḷiṉpeyar tōṉṟiṟṟeṉpatu ēṟpuṭaittaṉṟu. caṅkakālap pulavarkaḷiṉ collāṭci yiliruntu tamiḻ eṉpatu moḻip peyarilum pārkkac cātippeyaraḷavil mikap paḻamaiyuṭaiyateṉat terikiṉṟatu. itu ulakiṉ maṟṟap pākaṅkaḷi luḷḷa makkaḷ varalāṟṟōṭum ottirukkiṉṟatu. ākavē itukāṟum tamiḻc col tōṟṟattaippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa aṭiyōṭu māṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻiṉ tōṟṟattaik kūṟavanta ellōrum atu cātiyaiyaṉṟi moḻi yaiyē kuṟittateṉak koṇṭaṉar. uṇmai itaṟku etirmaṟai. ic colliṉ tōṟṟam kulattoṭarpākavaṉṟi moḻittoṭarpāka iruttal muṭiyātu. anēka cātikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷaippōlak kulattukkuriya ip peyarum viḷakkappaṭuttakkūṭāmal irukkiṉṟatu. ic colliṉ tōṟṟat tiṟkuriya kālam ellā varalāṟṟuk kālattaiyum tuḷaittuc ceṉṟu, nammai iv vulakam muḻumaiyilum vāḻum makkaṭkulaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉ piṟappaiyum, avai paravuvataiyuṅ kaṇṭa ōr ulakattukkuk koṇṭu celkiṉṟatu. atu ellā varalāṟṟaiyuṅ kaṭantu ceṉṟu maṉitappaḻamai yiṉ āḻattiluḷḷa varalāṟṟukku appāṟpaṭṭa kālam oṉṟaik kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. ak kālattaippaṟṟi makkaṭkula ārāycci, kaṉavu kāṇ kiṉṟatēyaṉṟi ataippaṟṟiya muṭivu oṉṟum kūṟavillai. av vakai yāṉa tiraiyiṉ piṉ ic collum irukkiṉṟatu. im moḻi ulakiluḷḷa ellā moḻikaḷilum paḻamaiyuṭaiyatu.”1
tamiḻ
tamiḻ eṉṉum col moḻikkup peyar maṭṭumallāmal iṉimai nīrmai eṉṉum poruḷkaḷaiyum uṇarttum.2 ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ‘tēṉuṟai tamiḻ’ (kallāṭam), “tamiḻ taḻīiya cāyal” (cintāmaṇi) eṉat tamiḻ iṉimai eṉṉum poruḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ic col iṉimaiyai uṇart taṟkuriya kāraṇam, ataṉ mūlam mutaliyaṉa aṟiya muṭiyātaṉa. civaṉ, ñāyiṟu, murukaṉ mutaliya kaṭavuḷarkaḷōṭum tamiḻar matattōṭum tamiḻ toṭarpuṭaiyateṉpatu tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkum paḻaiya aitīkam. iv vaitīkampaṟṟiyē, “teṉ moḻiyai ulakamelāntoḻutēttum kuṭa muṉikku valiyuṟuttār kol lēṟṟup pākar.” “centamiḻp paramāciri yaṉākiya murukak kaṭavuḷ akattiyarukkut tamiḻ aṟivuṟuttiṉār” eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa vākkiyaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
“cantaṉamum puḻukun taṇpaṉi nīruṭaṉē
kontalar caṇpakamuṅ koṇṭu vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ
vantiṭum valviṉainōy māṟṟuva tuṉpatamē
centami ḻākaraṉē civaciva cūriyaṉē.” (paḻaiya pāṭal)
“acalē curarpat tiraṉē kuṇatik
karuṇō tayamut tamiḻōṉē.” (tiruppukaḻ)
“cuḷḷeṉ ṟeṟikkuñ cuṭarōṉpāl tōṉṟiyuyir
uḷḷam tulakki yulāvaliṉ-teḷḷupukaḻp
paṇṭaip periyārtām paintamiḻai oṇṭamiḻ
oṇṭami ḻeṉmaṉār pōntu”1
ic ceyyuṭkaḷ ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum tamiḻ piṟantateṉṉum vaḻak kuṇmaiyai viḷakkuvaṉa. civaṉ, murukaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ ātiyil ñāyiṟṟaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṉa. ākavē tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar ñāyiṟṟaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya yātō paḻaiya col oṉṟiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉa ūkittal piḻaiyākātu. itu orupōtu el eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantirukkalāmeṉpataip paṇṭait tamiḻar eṉṉum ematu nūliṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷēm. teluṅku moḻiyai ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ aruḷic ceytār eṉṉum aitīkam āntirarkaḷiṭaiyē uṇṭu.
“tamiḻp” peyarkkāraṇam
tamiḻ eṉṉum coṟpiṟappaippaṟṟi yām ematu karuttai veḷi yiṭukiṉṟēm. itu muṭivāṉataṉṟu; ēṉaiyōr kūṟiyavaikaḷōṭu op piṭṭu ārāyattakkatu. iv vulaka mattiyil periya pūkaṇṭam (nāvalantīvu) oṉṟu iruntateṉṟum ataṉ mattiyil mēruvum ataṉaic cūḻntu iḷāvirutamum uḷḷateṉṟum, ilaṅkait tīvu mērumalaiyiṉ oru koṭumuṭi eṉṟum purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. iḷāvirutam eṉpatil virutam nāṭṭaik kuṟikkum. iḷā eṉpatu el eṉpataṉ tiripu. iḷāvirutattiṉ pakutiyākiya ilaṅkai īḻameṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. īḻam eṉpataṉ aṭi el eṉṟē tamiḻ oppiyal akarātiyil nallūr cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācar avarkaḷ kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ellam eṉpatu ilaṅkaiyiṉ paḻaiya peyar. ilaṅkai eṉṉum colliṉ aṭiyum el. ilaṅkai makkaḷiṉ paḻaiya moḻi elu eṉa vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. itaṉālum ilaṅkaiyiṉ paḻaiya peyar el allatu ellam eṉa naṉku tuṇitum. ilaṅkaiyai āṇṭa mutal aracaṉ ciṅkamarapiṉaṉ. ākavē ellam ciṅka ellam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. makkaḷ ciṅka ellar eṉṟu aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. ellam īḻamāṉa pōtu ciṅkaellam ciṅkaḷamāyiṟṟu. ḻakaram ḷakaramātalaṉṟi ḷakaram ḻakaramāta lillai eṉac cilar kūṟuvar. cōḷar eṉṟirukka vēṇṭiyatu cōḻar eṉa vaḻaṅkuvatai nōkkuka. ḷakaram ḻakaramāka uccarikkappaṭum coṟkaḷ pala moḻiyārāycciyāl aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
mekastiṉas kālattil intiyāvaiyum ilaṅkaiyaiyum oru ciṟiya āṟupōṉṟa kaṭal pirittatu. ākavē orukālattil teṉṉintiyā vum ilaṅkaiyum oṉṟupaṭṭu iruntaṉa eṉakkoḷḷutal piḻaiyākātu. oru kālattil tāmiravarṇi āṟu ilaṅkaivaraiyiṟ ceṉṟateṉak karutap paṭukiṉṟatu. tāpirapēṉ eṉṉum ilaṅkaiyiṉ peyar tāmiravarṇi eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉa varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ cilar eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ. ivai pōṉṟa cāṉṟukaḷāl intiyā ilaṅkai eṉṉum iru nāṭukaḷum oṉṟu cērntiruntaṉaveṉṟu tuṇitum. teṉ intiyāvum ilaṅkaiyum cērnta nāvalantīviṉ pakuti; muṉpu ellam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkiyiruttal kūṭum. cumēriya makkaḷ teṉṉintiyā allatu ilaṅkaiyiliruntu ceṉṟavarkaḷ eṉpatu tuṇivu.1
tamiḻnāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟa cumēriyar tamatu nāṭṭiluḷḷa iṭam oṉṟukkum malai oṉṟukkum ellam eṉṉum peyar koṭuttuḷḷārkaḷ. oru nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu iṉṉoru nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟum makkaḷ tam nāṭṭu iṭappeyarkaḷ cilavaṟṟait tām kuṭiyēṟiya nāṭṭiṉ iṭaṅ kaḷukkum iṭṭu vaḻaṅkutal iyalpu. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷ vēṇṭā. itaṉālum kiḻakkil ōr ellam iruntateṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum. muṟkālattu iṭappeyarkaḷ perumpālum vaḻipaṭu kaṭavuḷar toṭarpāka uṇṭāyiṉa eṉpatu muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. mēṟku āciyāvil el toṭarpuṭaiya nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa peyarkaḷaik kāṇalām. cumēriyāviṟ pōlavē kiḻakkilum ellam māttiramaṉṟu, ellam malaiyum iruntatu. ataṟkuc civaṉoḷimalai eṉpatum paḻaiya peyar. ippeyar puttar kālattukku muṟpaṭṭatu. maṉuviṉ pēḻai taṅkiyatākap purāṇaṅ kūṟum vaṭamalai ituvē yātal kūṭum.
cumēriyar vaḻipaṭṭa ñāyiṟṟukkaṭavuḷ tāmus2 allatu tāmci eṉappaṭṭār. ip peyaruṭaṉ ik kaṭavuḷ poṉīciyāvilum maṟṟum nāṭu kaḷilum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. tamiḻ eṉṉum col iru coṟkaḷiṉ marūu eṉpatu naṉku aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟatu. ataṉ iṟuti, el eṉpataṉ tiripākiya il. illē piṉ iḻ āyiṟṟu.
tamiḻ, ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉṉum vaḻakkuṇmaiyum, ellam eṉpatu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ nāṭṭaik kuṟittalum, mākaṟal kārttikēya mutaliyār tamiḻ ñāyiṟṟaik kuṟikkum oru colli ṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉak kūṟutalum mika ottuḷḷaṉa. uṇmai ivaikaḷukku aṇmaiyil kiṭakkiṉṟatu. kārttikēya mutaliyār, tamiḻ, tāmam eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉkiṟār. itilum periya uṇmaiyirukkiṉṟatu. tāmam eṉṉum col malaiyaiyum ñāyiṟṟaiyum iṉṉum pala poruḷ kaḷaiyum kuṟikkum. oru collukkup pala poruḷkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉ atu mikap paḻaiyacol eṉak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. vevvēṟu aṭiyākap piṟantu vevvēṟu poruḷkaḷai uṇarttiya coṟkaḷ pala, kālattil tirintu maruvi uccarippil oru collāka varutaluṇṭu. iv vuṇmai moḻiyārāycciyāl naṉku aṟiyappaṭum. tāmam eṉṉum col mikap paḻaiyatāyiruttalālum, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟa cumēriyar ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷait tāmus eṉa vaḻaṅkiyamaiyālum ilaṅkaiyiluḷḷa ñāyiṟṟu malai ‘tāmam’ eṉavum, am malaiyaiyuṭaiya nāṭu tāmam ellam eṉavum vaḻaṅkiyirukkalāmō eṉpaṉa cintikkattakkaṉa. avvā ṟāyiṉ, tāmam tām eṉa maruvit tā mellamāki, ellam īḻamāṉapōtu tāmīḻamāyp piṉ tamiḻam, tamiḻāyiṟṟeṉak koḷḷalām. civaṉ eṉpatu ñāyiṟṟiṉ paḻaiya peyareṉpatu muṉ viḷakkappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟu koḷḷiṉ, tamiḻ, ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum piṟantatu; atu civaṉ murukaṉ mutaliya kaṭavuḷarkaḷōṭum caivacittāntattōṭum toṭarpuṭaiyatu eṉṟu varum paḻaiya vaḻakkuṇmaikaḷukku oruvāṟu viḷakkam uṇṭā kiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu kūṟutal tamiḻai ilaṅkaiyōṭu toṭarpupaṭuttutaṟ kuriya cūḻcci eṉac cilar karutalām. oru kālattil tamiḻariṉ iruppiṭam 1teṟkilēyē iruntateṉṟum, ilaṅkaiyum teṉṉintiyāvum ak kālattu vevvēṟāka irukkavillaiyeṉṟum iṉṉum orumuṟai niṉaivuṟuttukiṉṟēm.
vaṭakkē vaḻaṅkiya moḻiyiṉ varalāṟu
āriyar intiyāvai aṭaivataṉ muṉ intiyā muḻumaiyum tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. āriyarkaḷuṭaiya vēta pāṭalkaḷ pirākiruta moḻiyil ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pirākirutam ilakkaṇa varampillāta moḻi. pirākirutat tiṉ tiruttamē camaṟkirutam eṉap peyarpeṟṟatu. āriya makkaḷ pirākiruta moḻiyaip pēciṉārkaḷ. vēta pāṭalkaḷil pala tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ pirākirutattōṭu tirāviṭac coṟ kaḷaiyum kalantu pēciṉārkaḷ. uṟpattiyil vaṭamoḻiyaic cārntaṉa eṉappaṭum vaṭaintiya moḻikaḷiṉ amaipput tamiḻai ottiruttaliṉ nāḷaṭaivil avvāriya makkaḷ tamiḻaiyē pēcat toṭaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻil āriyac coṟkaḷ peritum kāṇap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟum koḷḷutal amaiyum. ki.mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiya kautamaputtar tam aruḷmoḻikaḷai makata moḻiyil (pāḷiyil) ceytār.1 vaṭamoḻiyilillāta e.o. mutaliya kuṟilkaḷ pāḷiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamaiyāl pāḷi moḻi vaḻaṅkiya nāṭṭil muṉpu tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyateṉpatum pāḷimoḻikkuriyōr tamiḻareṉpatum muṉ ōriṭattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. puttaruṭaiya matakkoḷkaikaḷ cāṅkiya matattait taḻuviyavai.2 cāṅkiyamatam vētamatattukku māṟupaṭṭatu. ki. mu. 5ām, 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil pautta nūlkaḷ pirāmi eḻuttil eḻutap paṭṭaṉa. marappaṭṭaiyai vārntu eṭutta mirutuvāṉa ataṉ uṭpakutiyum ōlaikaḷum eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil poṟikkappaṭṭa pauttap paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷil maci eṉṉum col kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, akkālattu eḻutuvataṟku maciyum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu.
acōkar kālattil pāḷi moḻi vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. pāṇiṉiyiṉ ilakkaṇam pirāmi eḻuttil eḻutappaṭṭatu. acōkariṉ paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷ pirākirutattil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. pauttarum caiṉarum camaṟkirutattaip poruṭpaṭuttavillai. avarkaḷ piṟkālattilēyē camaṟkirutam payiṉ ṟaṉar. appoḻutu pirākirutam pāḷikkum camaṟkirutattukkum iṭaiyi luḷḷa orunilaiyai aṭaintiruntatu. ammoḻi puttariṉ varalāṟṟaik kūṟum lalitavistarā eṉṉum pāṭalil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu katā moḻi eṉavumpaṭṭatu. ki.pi. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭil vaṭakkē uḷḷa maturai yil poṟikkappaṭṭa caiṉa paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷil pirākirutam kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. itaṟkup piṉ camaṟkirutam vaḻakkil iruntatu. camaṟkirutap paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷ ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil ārampikkiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal camaṟkirutam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu. āṉāl, caiṉar itaṉaip payaṉpaṭuttavillai. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil putta nūlkaḷ camaṟkirutattil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ki.pi. 7-ām nūṟṟāṇṭil intiyāvukku vanta tiyāṉciyāṅ eṉṉum cīṉap pirayāṇi putta caṉṉi yācikaḷ cātāraṇa pēccilum camaṟkirutattaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar eṉa eḻuti yuḷḷāṉ. makamatiyar kālam varaiyil camaṟkirutam eḻuttu moḻiyāka viruntatu. pañcāpi, inti, kucarātti, marāṭṭi mutaliyaṉa pirākirutatti ṉiṉṟum piṟantaṉavāyiṉum amaippil tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai ottaṉa.
camaṟkiruta nūlkaḷ tēvanākariyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. inti, marāṭṭip puttakaṅkaḷ iv veḻuttil acciṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. iv veḻuttil ki.pi. 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa paḻaya paṭṭaiyaṅ kaḷum, ki.pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa paḻaiya kaiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āriya varttam allatu intustāṉam eṉappaṭṭa vintattukkum imayattukkum iṭaippaṭṭa nāṭṭil ki.mu. 200 varaiyil āriyam vaḻaṅkiyateṉak kūṟalām. camaṟkiruta nāṭakaṅkaḷil aracarum iruṭikaḷum camakkirutattilum, peṇkaḷum potumakkaḷum pirākirutat tilum pēcuvatāka eḻutappaṭṭiruttal kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, pirākirutamē makkaḷ moḻiyāyiruntateṉat terikiṉṟatu. camaṟkirutam ilakkiya moḻiyāyiruntataṉṟi orupōtum pēcappaṭavillai. vaṭakkē kāṇappaṭum moḻikaḷuḷ mukkiyamuṭaiyaṉa piṉ varuvaṉa: (1) mēṟku inti, (2) kiḻakku inti, (3) irācastāṉi, (4) kucarātti, (5) pañcāpi,
(6) kāsmīri, (7) lākiṉṭā (Lahinda), (8) cinti, (9) marāṭṭi, (10) pīkāri,
(11) oriyā (Orya), (12) vaṅkāḷi, (13) acāmi, (14) intucīṉam.1 (intiyāviṉ tolaivil (Further India) vaḻaṅkuvatu.)
tamiḻc caṅkam
talaiccaṅkam, iṭaiccaṅkam, kaṭaiccaṅkameṉa mūvakaippaṭṭa caṅkam niruviṉār pāṇṭiyarkaḷ. ivaruḷ talaiccaṅkamiruntār akattiyaṉārum, tiripurameritta viricaṭaik kaṭavuḷum, kuṉṟameṟinta kumaravēḷum, murañciyūr muṭināka rāyarum nitiyiṉ kiḻavaṉumeṉa it toṭakkattār aiññūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭa nālāyirattu nūṟṟu nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa ettuṇaiyō paripāṭalum, mutunāraiyum mutukurukum kaḷariyā viraiyumeṉa it toṭakkattaṉa. avar nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟiyāṇṭu caṅka miruntāreṉpa. avarkaḷaic caṅka mirīiyiṉār kāyciṉavaḻuti mutalākak kaṭuṅkōṉīṟāka eṇpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa maturai yeṉpa. avarkku nūl akattiyam.
iṉi iṭaiccaṅka miruntār akattiyaṉārum tolkāppiyaṉā rum, iruntaiyūrk karuṅkōḻiyum, mōciyum, veḷḷūrk kāppiyaṉum, ciṟu pāṇṭaraṅkaṉum, tiraiyaṉ māṟaṉum, tuvaraikkōṉum kīrantaiyu meṉa it toṭakkattār aimpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruḷḷiṭṭa mūvāyirat teḻunūṟṟuvar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa kaliyum, kurukum, veṇṭāḷiyum, viyāḻa mālaiyum, viyāḻamālai yakavalumeṉa it toṭakkattaṉaveṉpa. avarkku nūl akattiyamun tolkāppiyamum, māpurāṇamum, icai nuṇukkamum, pūtapurāṇamumeṉa ivaiyeṉpa. avar mūvāyiratteḻunūṟṟiyāṇṭu caṅkamiruntāreṉpa. avarkaḷaic caṅka mirīiyiṉār veṇṭērc ceḻiyaṉ mutalāka muṭat tirumāṟaṉ īṟāka aimpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār aivar pāṇṭiyareṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntatu 1kapāṭapurat teṉpa. ak kālattuppōlum pāṇṭiya nāṭṭaik kaṭalkoṇṭatu.
“iṉik kaṭaiccaṅkamiruntu tamiḻārāyntār ciṟu mētāviyārum, cēntampūtaṉārum, aṟivuṭai yaraṉārum. peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻārum, iḷan tirumāṟaṉum, maturaiyāciriyar nallantuvaṉārum, marutaṉiḷa nākaṉārum, kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉārumeṉa it toṭakkattār nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar pāṭiṉāreṉpa. avarkaḷāṟ pāṭappaṭṭaṉa, neṭuntokai nāṉūṟum (akanāṉūṟu), kuṟuntokaiyum, naṟṟiṇaiyum, puṟanāṉūṟum, aiṅkuṟunūṟum, patiṟṟuppattum, nūṟṟaimpatu kaliyum, eḻupatu paripāṭalum, kūttum, variyum, ciṟṟicaiyum, pēricaiyumeṉṟu it toṭakkattaṉa. avarkku nūl akattiyamun tolkāppiyamumeṉpa. avar caṅkamiruntu tamiḻ ārāyntatu āyiratteṇṇūṟ ṟaimpatiṟṟiyāṇṭu eṉpa. avar kaḷaic caṅka mirīiyiṉār, kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭup pōntirunta muṭattiru māṟaṉ mutalāka ukkirap peruvaḻuti yīṟāka nāṟpattoṉpatiṉmareṉpa. avaruṭ kaviyaraṅkēṟiṉār mūvar pāṇṭiyareṉpa” (iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyal urai.)
itaṉ curukkam:- talaiccaṅkam kaṭalkoṇṭa teṉ maturaiyiṟ kūṭiṟṟu. ik kaḻakattukku varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭa uṟuppiṉar 549 pēr. ic caṅkam 89 pāṇṭiyariṉ āṭcik kālattil 4,440 āṇṭu naṭaipeṟṟatu. ic caṅkattil nūlkaḷaiyō taṉippāṭalkaḷaiyō pāṭikkoṇṭu vantu araṅkēṟṟiya pulavar 4,449 pēr. iṭaiccaṅkam, teṉ maturaiyiṉ aḻivuk kuppiṉ kapāṭapurattil naṭaipeṟṟatu. ic caṅka uṟuppiṉar 59 pēr. ic caṅkam 59 pāṇṭiyar āṭciyil 3,780 āṇṭu naṭaipeṟṟatu. ic capaiyil pāṭalkaḷai araṅkēṟṟiya pulavar 3,700 pēr. ivarkaḷuḷ aivar pāṇṭiya maṉṉar.
kapāṭapurattaik kaṭal koṇṭapiṉ caṅkam maturaiyiṟ kūṭiṟṟu. ic caṅkam 49 aracar kālattil 1800 āṇṭu naṭaipeṟṟatu. ic caṅkattup, pulamaiyil araṅkēṟiya pulavariṉ eṇ 449. ivaruḷ mūvar pāṇṭiyar. ic caṅka uṟuppiṉar 49 pēr.
tamiḻccaṅkam 197 aracar kālattil 9,990 āṇṭukaḷ naṭaipeṟ ṟirukkiṉṟatu.
caṅka ārāycci
iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviya luraikārar ic caṅka varalāṟṟai ak kāla nūlkaḷil kaṇṭavaṟṟai ātāramākakkoṇṭē eḻutiyuḷḷār eṉa uyttuṇartal vēṇṭum. iv vakai varalāṟukaḷ pala, āṅkāṅku ak kāla nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟku, “cakarar vēḷvik kutirai nāṭit toṭṭa kalakattuṭpaṭṭuk kumariyāṟum paṉaināṭṭōṭu keṭuvataṟku muṉṉum” eṉṟu pērāciriyarum, kaṭalkoṇṭa āṟum malaiyum nāṭum ākiyavaṟṟai aṭiyārkku nallārum, “vaṭivē leṟinta vāṉpakai poṟātu paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmalai yaṭukkattuk kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa” eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum, “malitirai yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal veḷavaliṉ, meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu mēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap, puliyoṭu vilnīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭai, valiyiṉāl vaṇakkiya vāṭāc cīrtteṉṉavaṉ” eṉa nallantuvaṉārum kūṟumāṟṟāṉ naṉku tuṇiyappaṭum. tamiḻ makkaḷ tam varalāṟukaḷai eḻutivaippatil karuttuc celuttiṟṟilar eṉak kūṟuvatu tavaṟu tamiḻar tam paṇṭai varalāṟukaḷaiyum aracarkaḷaiyum paṟṟi eḻuti vaittiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu makastīṉas, pakkas mutal alakcāntariṉ paṭaiyeṭuppu varaiyil 154 pāṇṭiyar āṇṭārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷiṉ āṭcikkālam 6,431 āṇṭu mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷamaiyāl1 naṉku tuṇitalākum. mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum kōyiṟ kurumārē aracarkaḷaiyum avar kālattu nikaḻccikaḷaiyum paṟṟi eḻutivaittārkaḷ. ak kālaṅkaḷiṉ iṉṉa aracaṉiṉ āṭciyiṉ ittaṉaiyāvatu āṇṭu eṉak kālam kuṟik kappaṭ ṭu vantamaiyiṉ aracaraip paṟṟiya varalāṟukaḷ eḻutappaṭāmal iruntaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal amaiyātu. mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil nūlkaḷ kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukaḷil eḻutic cūḷaiyiliṭṭuk kāppāṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē, avai maṇ cel mutaliyavaikaḷāl aḻikkappaṭātu piḻaittaṉa; avai kaḷāl an nāṭukaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷ ōraḷavu aṟiyakkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. ekipti yariṉ varalāṟukaḷ perumpālum camātic cuvarkaḷilum camātikaḷuḷ vaik kappaṭṭa paipiras nūlkaḷilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻar eḻutap payaṉ paṭuttiya ōlai ciṟitu kālattil oṭintum, cel, maṇ mutaliyavaikaḷāl uṇṇappaṭṭum, irāmapāṇattāl tuḷaikkappaṭṭum aḻintupōkat takkaṉa. mēṟku āciyāvil ki. mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉṉarē periya nūl nilaiyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa.1 mokañcotarōt tamiḻar nākarikam mēṟku āciyamakkaḷ nākarikattaiviṭa mēlāṉatu eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ulaka makkaḷukkē nākarikattai nalkiyavarkaḷ eṉṟu collappaṭum makkaḷ tamatu varalāṟṟai eḻuti vaiyātiruntaṉar eṉak kūṟamuṭiyātu. nūlkaḷai eḻutip pātukāttu vaikkum muṟaiyum oru naṭu iṭattiṉiṉṟum parantu ceṉṟa vaḻakkamē yātal vēṇṭum. varalāṟukaḷai eḻutik kāppāṟṟum paḻaiya vaḻakkaip piṉpaṟṟiyē ilaṅkaip pautta kurumārum puttar kālam mutal varalāṟukaḷai eḻutik kāppāṟṟiṉar. avaikaḷai ātāra mākakkoṇṭē tīpavamicam, makāvamicam mutaliya varalāṟukaḷ virittu eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.2
purāṇaṅkaḷil uṇmai aṇuvaḷavum, poy malaiyaḷavum kāṇappaṭuvatai nōkkiya cila ārāycciyāḷar tamiḻppulavarkaḷ kūṟi yuḷḷa varalāṟṟuṇmaikaḷilum aiyam uṟukiṉṟaṉar. purāṇaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kaṟpaṉaik kataikaḷukkup poṟuppup pirāmaṇarēyaṉṟit tamiḻp pulavar kaḷallar eṉpatai ñāpakattil vaittukkoṇṭāl, im mayakkam eḻa vaḻiyillai. tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ uḷḷavaṟṟai uḷḷavāṟē kūṟum iyal piṉareṉpatu caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷāṟ ṟeḷitum. ‘irāmar 11 āyiram āṇṭu aracu purintār, tacarataṉ aṟupatiṉāyiram āṇṭu uyir vāḻntāṉ’ eṉa iyaṟkai kku māṟāṉa etaiyum iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyalurai kūṟāmai karuttiṟ patikkattakkatu. keratōtacu āciriyar ōr aracaṉukkuḷḷa carācari kālam 33 āṇṭukaḷ eṉak kaṇakkuc ceytuḷḷār. mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷum naṭaipeṟṟa kālattil irunta aracariṉ eṇ 197. iv varacar ovvoru varukkum muppatu āṇṭukaḷ īntāl 5970 āṇṭukaḷākum. iv vāṇṭukaḷ cantira aḷaviṉpaṭi kaṇakkucceytu cūriya āṇṭākkiṉ 5470 varaiyilākum. iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyaluraiyil mūṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷum 9990 āṇṭukaḷ naṭaipeṟṟaṉavākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paḻaiya kālak kaṇakku kaḷil ivvāṟu cila piḻaikaḷ nērtal iyalpu. keratōtacu mūṉṟu ekiptiya aracar taṉittaṉi 300 āṇṭukaḷ āṇṭaṉar eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. iṭaiyē cila aracariṉ eṇ viṭappaṭumāyiṉ avarkaḷiṉ āṭcikkālam nīṇṭateṉa k kaṇakkiṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. ilaṅkai varalāṟṟil iv vakaip piḻai nērntamaiyāl cila aracar nīṇṭa kālam āṭcipurintaṉar eṉak karutappaṭṭup piṉpu tiruttappaṭṭatu. kiṟittava maṟaiyiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭu maṟaintupōkap piṉpu atu ki. mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum ki. mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkumiṭaiyil eḻutappaṭṭatu. atil cila aracar 800 allatu 900 āṇṭukaḷ uyirvāḻntavarkaḷākak kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ataṉai nampa muṭiyāviṭiṉum atil kūṟappaṭuvaṉa varalāṟṟu nūlārāl ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷai ellām vaittunōkki iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭa kālakkaṇakkai ārāytal vēṇṭum. kālakkaṇakku mikutiyāyiruppatāl caṅkam naṭaipeṟavillaiyeṉak koḷḷutal ākātu. e ṭṭuttokai pattuppāṭṭu mutaliya nūlkaḷaip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ pala kōṇaṅkaḷil palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil vāḻntōr. ap pāṭalkaḷ, tokukkappaṭuvataṟku ōriṭattil evvāṟu kiṭaittaṉa eṉpatu ārāyappaṭutal vēṇṭum. caṅkattē araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷ aṅku paṭiyeṭukkappaṭṭuk kiṭantamaiyālē avaikaḷ ōriṭattiṟ kiṭaittaṉa veṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅkum. caṅka varalāṟu ki. pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭilum ataṟku muṉṉum paḻaiya varalāṟāka viruntateṉpatu1 “kūṭalilāynta oṇ tīntamiḻiṉ tuṟaivāy nuḻaintaṉaiyō” eṉṉum māṇikkavācakariṉ tiruvākkāṉum “naṉpāṭṭup pulavaṉāy caṅkamēṟi naṟkaṉakak kiḻitarumik karuḷiṉāṉkāṇ” eṉṉum tirunāvukkaracar tēvārattāṉum naṉku viḷaṅkum. caṅkattiṉ iṟutik kālam ki. pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil eṉap perumpāluṅ koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu.1
iṭaiccaṅkam kapāṭapurattiliruntateṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu tāmira paraṇi āṟṟu mukattuvārattiluḷḷateṉa irāmāyaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. kapāṭa purattiṉ aḻivukkup piṉ pāṇṭiyariṉ talainakaram maṇalūriliruntatu. itu tiruccentūrukkup pakkattil iruntateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. māpāratam pāṇṭiyariṉ talainakaram maṇalūriliruntatākavē kūṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉ piṉpē ippoḻutuḷḷa maturai pāṇṭiyariṉ talainakarāyiṟṟu. ippōtaiya maturai muṉ kaṭampavaṉamāyiruntateṉat tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇaṅ kūṟum. “kiṉṉarampayil kaṭampavāṉattiṉ kīḻcārt, teṉṉaṉ cēkaraṉ eṉṉuṅ kulacēkaraṉāka, maṉṉar cēkara ṉaracu ceytiruppatu maṇa vūr” eṉpatu tiruviḷaiyāṭal. mekastiṉacum kauṭaliyarum maturaiyaik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. kaṭaiccaṅka kālattil irunta aracar 49 pēr eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. taṟkāla muṟaiyiṉpaṭi ōr aracaṉukku 20 āṇṭu īyiṉ avarkaḷiṉ motta āṭcik kālam 980 āṇṭukaḷākum. ākavē, kaṭaiccaṅkam ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil toṭaṅkiṟṟu eṉak kūṟutal tava ṟākātu. muṟaikkumuṟai nērnta kaṭalkōṭ kuḻappaṅkaḷāl aracarāl tiraṭṭi vaikkappaṭṭa tamiḻnūlkaḷ aḻiveytiṉaveṉṟu uyttuṇartalkūṭum. talamicōttar eṉpavarāl alakcāntiriyāvil tokuttu vaikkappaṭṭa 700,000 nūlkaḷ aḻintu pōṉamai itaṉōṭu oppunōkkattakkatu. kaṭaic caṅkam kulaintapiṉ ki.pi. 470il vaccirananti eṉpavariṉ talaimaiyil tikamparacaiṉarāl tamiḻccaṅkam toṭaṅki naṭattappaṭṭatu.
akattiyar
akattiyar talaiccaṅkattait talaimai tāṅki naṭattiyavar eṉa iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyalurai kūṟukiṉṟatu. akattiyar eṉṉum viṇmīṉ oṉṟaip paripāṭal kūṟukiṉṟatu. maṇimēkalaiyil (ki. pi. 200) akattiyar amaramuṉivar eṉac cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟār. “kañca vēṭkaiyiṟ kakantaṉ vēṇṭa, amara muṉiva ṉakattiyaṉ ṟaṉātu, karakam kaviḻtta kāvirip pāvai.”
“kaṭavuṭ kaṇikai kātalañ ciṟuvaṉ, antaṇa riruvarum” eṉa varum maṇimēkalai aṭikaḷukku “tilōttamai piḷḷaikaḷākiya vatiṣṭar akattiyar” eṉap poruḷkūṟi “vaciṣṭaṉum akastiyaṉum pirahmā tilōttamai yeṉṉum apsarasaik kaṇṭa kālattu jaṉit taṉar.” (nīla-camayam) eṉṉum mēṟkōḷaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār ṭākṭar cāmināta aiyaravarkaḷ. purāṇaṅkaḷ vatiṣṭar, akattiyar, vicuvā mittirar mutaliyōriṉ tantai mittiravaruṇaṉ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.1 mittiravaruṇaṉ tamiḻ marapiṉarē eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar tuṇipu.
tirumantiram ki. pi. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭu aḷaviṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. ataṉakattē akattiyaraippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
“naṭuvunil lātiv vulakañ carintu
keṭukiṉṟa temperu māṉeṉṉa vīcaṉ
naṭuvuḷa aṅki akattiya nīpōy
muṭukiya vaiyattu muṉṉireṉ ṟāṉē.” (tirumantiram)
puṟapporuḷ veṇpā mālaip pāyirattil,
“maṉṉiya ciṟappiṉ vāṉōr vēṇṭat
teṉmalai yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaṉ.”
eṉa varum aṭikaḷum mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi karuttait taḻuvic ceyyappaṭṭa tākum.
iv varalāṟukaḷāl kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattukku muṉṉar akattiyar varalāṟu kaṟpaṉaik kataikaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ kalanta paḻaṅkatai yāka māṟiyuḷḷateṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
vētapāṭalkaḷ cilavaṟṟaic ceytavarum ulōpā muttiraiyai maṉaiviyākap peṟṟavarumākiya akattiyar oruvar vētakālattil viḷaṅkiṉār. irāmāyaṇattil akattiyar ācciramam kōtāvariyāṟṟukku vaṭakkē uḷḷateṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.2 teṉṉāṭṭil akat tīccuram akattiyāṉ paḷḷi mutaliya ālayaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa.
akattiyar tamiḻukku āti3 ilakkaṇañ ceytāreṉṟum, avariṭat til ataṅkōṭṭāciriyaṉ, tolkāppiyar, paṉampāraṉār, cempūṭcēy, vaiyāpikar, avinayar, kākkai pāṭiṉiyar, turāliṅkar, vāyppiyar, kaḻārampar, naṟṟattar, vāmaṉar eṉṉum paṉṉiruvar kalvi payiṉṟārka ḷeṉṟum varalāṟukaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷukku,
“vīṅkukaṭal uṭutta viyaṉkaṇ ñālattut
tāṅkā nallicait tamiḻkkuviḷak kākeṉa
vāṉōr ēttum vāymoḻip palpukaḻ
āṉāp perumai akattiyaṉ eṉṉum
aruntava muṉivaṉ ākkiya mutaṉūl
poruntak kaṟṟup puraitapa uṇarntōr
nallicai niṟutta tolkāp piyaṉum.”
eṉṉat tolkāppiya uraiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum paṉṉiru paṭalap pāyiram eṉṉum mēṟkōḷaiyum,
“teṉmalai yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaraṉ
taṉpāṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ tāviṉ ṟuṇarnta
tuṉṉaruñ cīrttit tolkāppiyaṉ mutaṟ
paṉṉiru pulavarum pāṅkuṟap pakarnta.”
eṉa varum puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālaip pāyira aṭikaḷaiyumaṉṟi vēṟu mēṟkōḷkaḷ kāṇpataritu.
“tamiḻ nāṭṭil akattiyarkaḷ” eṉṉum ārāycci nūl eḻutiya kē. eṉ. civarācapiḷḷai avarkaḷ, akattiyaraip paṟṟiya kataikaḷ kaṟpaṉai eṉak karutiṉārkaḷ. muṟ kālaṅkaḷil ovvoru kuṭumpattiṉa rum avvakkuṭumpaṅkaḷiṉ āti mutalvarkaḷ peyarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. vicuvāmittirar, vatiṭṭar, akattiyar eṉṉum peyarkaḷum am muṟaiyil vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivvāṟu palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil vāḻnta pala akat tiyarkaḷiṉ kataikaḷ ellām oruṅku tiraṇṭu pala kaṭṭukkataikaḷōṭu piṉṉi iṉṟaiya akattiyar varalāṟāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉpatu iṟṟai ñāṉṟai ārāycciyāḷaratu tuṇipākum. iññāṉṟu akattiyar peya ruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkum vaittiya nūlkaḷ pōṉṟaṉavē muṟkāla akattiyaṅkaḷu māyiruttal kūṭum.
ceṅkōṉ taraiccelavil ēḻteṅkanāṭṭu muttūr akattiyaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻp pulavar oruvariṉ peyar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
cumattirā yāvā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil akattiyar vikkirakaṅkaḷ makka ḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. vāyupurāṇam, akattiyar pōṇiyō, kucattīpam, varākatīpam, malaiyat tīpaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟāreṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. avar cumattirāvilē uḷḷa malāyu eṉṉum malaiyil iruntār eṉṉum varalāṟu aṅku vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ātiyil cantaṉa marattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa akattiyar vikkirakam malāyarāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 7ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa akattiyar uruvam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. akattiyar cilai caṭāmuṭi kavacam uruttirākkam mutaliyavaikaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.1 akattiyar marapiluḷḷōr palar aracarāyiruntār kaḷeṉat terikiṉṟatu.
mattiya yāvāvilē cāṇṭipāṉ (Chandi Bann) eṉṉumiṭattil uḷḷa civaṉ kōyilil aḻakiya akattiya viṣṇu vikkirakaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cittiya (Scythians) makkaḷiṭaiyē akattiricikaḷ eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉar iruntārkaḷ eṉak keratōtacu eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāti pāmpum pāti maṉita vaṭivumuṭaiyatāyuḷḷa, kekkulis eṉṉum tantai akattiricikaḷiṉ āti mutalvar eṉa avar kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. akattiricikaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭṭa pala āṭavar oru peṇṇai maṇakkum vaḻakkampōṉṟatu, malaiyāḷak karaikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.2 malaiyāḷam muṟkālattil nākanāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. cēra eṉpataṟku nākam eṉṟē cāyaṇar poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār.3 oru kālattil malaiyāḷak karaikaḷiṉiṉṟum veḷippōnta akattiyar marapiṉarē akattiricikaḷ eṉac cittiya makkaḷiṭaiyum kāṇappaṭṭārkaḷām. cittiya makkaḷum tamiḻarai oppat turāṉiyamakkaḷāvar eṉa makkaṭkula nūlār kūṟuvar.
tolkāppiyar
tolkāppiyar eṉpatu tolkāppiyam eṉṉum nūlaic ceyta āciriyarukku annūl ceytamai kāraṇamāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa oru kāraṇappeyar. itu naṉṉūl ceytārai naṉṉūlār eṉak kūṟutal pōṉṟatoru vaḻakku. ivaratu iyaṟpeyar yāteṉat teriyavillai. āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ivar iyaṟpeyar tiraṇa tūmākkiṉi eṉa ōriṭattuk kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tolkāppiyariṉ iyaṟpeyar pulattiyaṉ (pulam-aṟivu) eṉṟum, appeyarai vaṭamoḻippaṭuttiyavarkaḷ ataṉaip pul+akam+tīyaṉ eṉap pirittut (tiraṇam+tūmam+akkiṉi) tiraṇa tūmākkiṉi eṉa māṟṟiṉareṉṟum aṟiñar cilar kūṟuvar. itu ik kālam vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷait tamiḻppaṭutti vaḻaṅkuvatupōṉṟatōr muṟkāla vaḻakku. cūriyanārāyaṇa cāttiri eṉpataip paritimāl kalaiñaṉ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu itaṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. vaṭamoḻiyiṉait teyva moḻi eṉa makkaḷ nampat talaippaṭṭa oru kālattil teṉṉāṭṭut tirukkōyiṟ peyarkaḷum makkaṭ peyarkaḷum vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ṟiruppap paṭṭu vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa. tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇattiṟ kāṇappaṭum pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷ peyarkaḷum ivvāṟu māṟṟappaṭṭiruttal nōkkattakkatu. tolkāppiyam ceyyappaṭṭakālattil tiraṇatūmākkiṉi pōṉṟa peyar tōṉṟiyiruttal cālāteṉpatu varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷukku naṉku teḷivākum.
tolkāppiyam eṉṉum nūlai nuṇuki ārāyumiṭattu atu paṇṭait tamiḻ marapukaḷaip pātukāttaṟku eḻunta nūleṉpatu teḷḷi tiṟ pulappaṭum. ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyē tolkāppiyam (tol + kāppu + iyam = paḻamaiyaik kāppatākiya nūl) eṉṉum peyar an nūlukku iṭap paṭṭatu. kāppiyam eṉpatu kāviyam eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉac cilar vaḻakkiṭuvar. tolkāppiyattil kāviyattukkuriya poruḷkaḷ amaintirāmaiyiṉ aṉṉōr kūṟṟup poruḷaṉṟeṉa viṭuka.
tolkāppiyam ki. mu. 350kkup piṟpaṭṭa nūlaṉṟeṉpatu varalāṟ ṟāciriyarkaḷ tuṇipu, tolkāppiyattil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷa ōrai eṉṉum col kirēkkariṉ “hōrā” eṉṉum colliṉ tiripeṉṟum, varāka mikirar (ki. pi. 550) kālattukkup piṉpē intiya makkaḷ hōrā eṉṉuñ collai vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷeṉṟum ākavē, tolkāppiyam ki. pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkup piṟpaṭṭateṉṟum cilar kūṟuvar. tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟap paṭum ilakkaṇa vitikaḷāl atu cilappatikārattukku muṟpaṭṭa nūl eṉa naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. kirēkkar ki. mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkumuṉ vāṉa nūlaippaṟṟi aṟintilar. ataṟkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarē mēṟku āciyamakkaḷum tamiḻarum vāṉanūl, cōtiṭanūl eṉpavaikaḷil tiṟamai peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉar. ākavē, kirēkka makkaḷ vāṉa ārāycci, cōtiṭam eṉpavaikaḷukkuriya coṟkaḷaip piṟa makkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟṟiruppār kaḷēyaṉṟi ak kalaikaḷaip paḻamaiyē aṟintirunta piṟar kirēkkariṭa miruntu peṟṟirukkamāṭṭārkaḷ. ulaka moḻikaḷil tamiḻccol mūlaṅkaḷ ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkuvatai moḻi ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kaṟkaḷai eṇṇik kaṇakkiṭutal eṉṉum poruḷil, ilattīṉ moḻiyil vaḻaṅkum kalkulas (calcules) āṅkila moḻiyil vaḻaṅkum kalkulēṭ (Calculate) mutaliya coṟkaḷ kal eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉiṉṟu piṟan taṉavātalai nōkkuvārkku ōrai eṉṉum collē kirēkka moḻiyil ceṉṟu hōrā eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉak koḷḷutal iyaipuṭaiyateṉat tōṉṟum.
tolkāppiyap pāyirattil aintiram niṟainta tolkāppiyaṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭutaliṉ avar aintira meṉṉum vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇattaik kaṟṟiruntār eṉac cilar karutuvar. aintiram eṉṉum ilakkaṇanūl ip poḻutu kāṇappaṭavillai. pāṇiṉikku muṉ aintirameṉṉum vaṭamoḻi ilakkaṇamoṉṟu iruntatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. pāṇiṉikkuppiṉ aintirameṉṉum ilakkaṇanūl vaḻaṅkavillai. pāyirattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa aintiram vaṭamoḻi nūlāyiṉ tolkāppiyar pāṇiṉi muṉivarukku (ki.mu. 400) muntiyarāvar. oru moḻikku ilakkaṇam ceyyumiṭattu maṟṟoru moḻikkuriya ilakkaṇattaiyum kaṟṟu oppunōkki oṟṟumai vēṟṟumai kāṇutal ciṟanta muṟaiyākum.
iṉṉorucārār, aintirameṉpatu caiṉanūl; ākavē, tolkāp piyar caiṉar eṉak kūṟuvar. cilappatikārattil, 1“kappat tintiraṉ kāṭṭiya nūliṉ, meyppāṭ ṭiyaṟkai viḷaṅkak kāṇā”, “viṇṇavar kōmāṉ viḻunūl” eṉa intiraṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa oru nūl cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu mikap paḻaiya kālattu (kaṟpa kālattil) intiraṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa teṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. intiraṉatu talainakarākiya amarāpati kiruṣṇānati mukattuvārattiluḷḷamaiyum, intiraṉ teṉṉāṭṭilē civaliṅkam vaittup pūcittamaiyumākiya varalāṟukaḷ ārāyappaṭal vēṇṭum.
tolkāppiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭum cila akaccāṉṟukaḷālum,2 ataṉ pāyirattālum tolkāppiyar kālattil tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkellai vēṅkaṭameṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. akanāṉūṟṟil varum māmūlaṉār pāṭalkaḷiṉ,
“paṉipaṭu cōlai, vēṅkaṭat tumpar
moḻipeyar tēyattar eṉpa”
“palpukaḻ niṟainta velpōr nantar
cīrmiku pāṭaliṟ kuḻīik kaṅkai
munnīr karanta nitiyaṅ kollō”
“kaṉaikura licaikkum viraicelaṟ kaṭuṅkaṇ
muraṇmiku vaṭukar muṉṉuṟa mōriyar
teṉṟicai mātira muṉṉiya varaviṟku”
eṉavarum aṭikaḷāl māmūlaṉār kālattukkumuṉ vēṅkaṭattukku vaṭakkē piṟamoḻi vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉṟum māmūlaṉār ki. mu. 300 varaiyil vāḻntavarāvar eṉṟum varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷ ḷārkaḷ. ākavē, ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭukkumuṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭakku ellai vēṅkaṭamāka viruntiruntatu eṉat tuṇital kūṭum. kaliṅka iḷavaracaṉākiya vicayaṉ kālam mutal (ki. mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) tōṉṟi vaḷara ārampitta ciṅkaḷa moḻiyil ilakkiyam mutaṉ mutal ki. pi. 10ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻuntatu. āntira moḻiyilum ilakkiyaṅkaḷ ki. pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal eḻa ārampittaṉa. ākavē iru moḻikaḷiṉ kālamum oṉṟu eṉa (ki. mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) uttēcamākak kūṟalām. ak kālat tilō ataṉ ciṟitu pintiyō tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkellai vēṅkaṭamāyiṟṟu.
“parantupaṭṭa poruṇmaiyavākiya māpurāṇam, pūtapurāṇa meṉpaṉa, cil vāḻnāṭ ciṟṟaṟiviṉ mākkaṭku upakārappaṭāmaiyiṉ tokuttuc ceyyappaṭṭa vaḻakku nūlākiya tolkāppiyam iṭaic caṅkam mutalāka iṉṟukāṟum uḷatāyiṟṟeṉka. kaṭaiccaṅkattāruṭ kaḷaviyaṟ poruḷ kaṇṭa kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉār iṭaiccaṅkattārkkum kaṭaiccaṅkattārkkum nūlāyiṟṟut tolkāppiyam eṉṟārākalāṉum (pērāciriyar urai. mara. 649).
“tolkāp piyampulavar tōṉṟa viritturaittār
palkāya ṉārvakuttup paṉṉiṉār-nalyāppuk
kaṟṟār matikkuṅ kalaikkākkai pāṭiṉiyār
coṟṟārtan nūliṟ ṟokuttu.”
eṉa varuvaṉavaṟṟāl tolkāppiyar mutal piṉvanta āciriyarkaḷ ellām muṉ virintu kiṭanta nūlkaḷaic curukki nūlkaḷ ceytārkaḷ eṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyiram
tolkāppiyattukkup paṉampāraṉār ceyta ciṟappuppāyira moṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ip pāyiram ārāyccikkuriyatu. tolkāp piyattil nūlkaḷukkup pāyiram ceytaṟku vitikāṇappaṭavillai. caṅka nūlkaḷukkum iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyalukkum pāyiram illāmai karut tiṟkoḷḷattakkatu. cilappatikāram maṇimēkalai mutaliya nūlkaḷukkup poruḷaṭakkamē pāyiramāka eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vāṉmīkar irāmāyaṇat til kāṇappaṭum pāyiramum iv vakaiyiṉatē. itaṉāl cilappatikāra kālattum piṟkālattum vaṭamoḻi nūlkaḷiṉ muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟip pāyirañ ceyyappaṭalāyiṟṟu eṉak karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
pāyiram nūlukku iṉṟiyamaiyātateṉa iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyal urai mutal mutal valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu. iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyalurai nakkīraṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu nīṇṭakālam cevi vaḻakkil vantu ki. pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil eḻuti vaikkappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl ki. pi. 7ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil allatu ataṟkuc ciṟitumuṉ nūlukkup pāyiram iṉṟiyamai yātatu eṉṉum koḷkai valuvaṭaintiruttal vēṇṭum.1
tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyiram, maṇimēkalai, cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷukkuḷḷa pāyiram pōlallātu, nūl, nuvalvōṉ, nuvalun tiṟaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟaik kūṟuvaṉavāyumuḷḷatu. ivaikaḷai ellām oruṅkuvaittu ārāyumiṭattut tolkāppiyac ciṟappuppāyiram, ki.pi. 5ām allatu 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil irunta paṉampāraṉār eṉṉum pulavar oruvarāl, ak kālam tolkāppiyattaip paṟṟi vaḻaṅkiya kaṉṉa paramparai varalāṟukaḷ ellāvaṟṟaiyum tiraṭṭic ceyyappaṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. pāyirattil tolkāppiyar akattiyariṉ māṇavareṉak kūṟappaṭāmai nōkkattakkatu.
ilakkaṇak kuṟippukaḷ cila
tolkāppiyar viyaṅkōḷ viṉai, taṉmai muṉṉilai eṉṉum īriṭattum varāteṉak kūṟuvar. “avaṟṟuḷ-muṉṉilai taṉmai yāyī riṭattōṭu, maṉṉātākum viyaṅkōṭ kiḷavi” (tol.col.viṉai.) puṟa nāṉūṟṟiṉ iraṇṭām pāṭṭaic ceyta murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar, “vāṉa varampaṉai nīyō peruma” “naṭukkiṉṟi niliyarō” eṉa muṉṉilai yiṭattu viyaṅkōḷ viṉaiyaip payaṉpaṭutti yirukkiṉṟār. itaṉāl murañciyūr muṭinākarāyar tolkāppiyar kālattukkup piṟpaṭṭavareṉa uyttaṟiyalām. tolkāppiyar kālattil viyaṅkōḷviṉai paṭarkkai iṭattumaṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. piṟkālattil atu muṉṉilaiyiṭat tum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. naṉṉūlār ataṉai mūviṭattum payaṉpaṭuttiṉār. “kayavoṭu ravvoṟ ṟīṟṟa viyaṅkōḷ, iyalu miṭampā leṅku meṉpa” (naṉ-338). ic cūttirattiṉāl naṉṉūlārukku muṉṉum viyaṅkōḷ taṉmaiyiṭattum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉat terikiṉṟatu.
eṭṭut tokai pattuppāṭṭu mutaliya nūlkaḷil uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷ kaḷ vikuti ēṟkavillai. maṇimēkalai cilappatikāram mutaliya nūlkaḷil uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷ kaḷ vikuti ēṟkum vaḻakkuk kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. ‘yāṅkaḷum nīṇeṟippaṭarutum’ (cilap.) eṉavum, “nōṉpikaḷ viḻumaṅ koḷḷavum” (maṇi.) eṉavum varutal kāṇka. piṟkālaṅkaḷil kaḷ vikuti uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇaip peyarkaḷuṭaṉum irutiṇai viṉai muṟṟukkaḷuṭaṉum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tolkāppiyar uyartiṇai eṉṟu koṇṭa taṉmaip peyarkaḷ naṉṉūlār kālam mutal viravutiṇai (uyartiṇaiyum aḵṟiṇaiyum) eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. “taṉmai nāṉkum muṉṉilai yaintum........potuppeyar” (naṉ. 282)
ceyyum eṉṉum muṟṟu nikaḻkālattukku māttiram uriyateṉat tolkāppiyaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. “pallōr paṭarkkai muṉṉilai taṉmai, avvayiṉ mūṉṟum nikaḻum kālattu, ceyyumeṉṉuṅ kiḷaviyoṭu koḷḷā” (tol. col. viṉai.) naṉṉūlāriṉpaṭi atu varuṅkālat tukku muriyatākum. “ceyyu nikaḻ petirvum” (naṉ. 145)
naṉṉūlār nikaḻkāla viṉaimuṟṟu ceykiṟēṉ, ceykiṉṟēṉ, ceyyāniṉṟēṉ eṉa varum eṉak koḷvar. tēvārattukkum nālāyirap pirapantattukkum muṉ mēṟkuṟittavaikaḷil oṉṟēṉum āḷappaṭa villai. vīracōḻiya āciriyar ceyyāniṉṟēṉ eṉpataic ceyyā-niṉ ṟēṉ eṉa iru coṟkaḷākap pirippar. āṉāl, naṉṉūlār irucoṟkaḷai yum oṉṟākakkoṇṭu āniṉṟu nikaḻkālattiṉai uṇarttumeṉṟār.
iv vārāycciyiṉāl tolkāppiyam ellāt tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukkum pārkka muṟpaṭṭateṉpatum, atu cilappatikāra kālattukkup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ maṟpaṭṭateṉpatum naṉku viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
urainaṭai varalāṟu
moḻi, vaḻakku ceyyuḷ, eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. makkaḷ oruvarōṭu oruvar uraiyāṭuṅkāl āḷum naṭai vaḻakkeṉappaṭum. vaḻakkeṉiṉum, vacaṉa naṭai urainaṭai eṉiṉum okkum.
uḷḷatteḻum uṇarccikaḷukkēṟpa makkaḷāṟṟōṟṟuvikkappaṭum ōcaikaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyē pāṭaliṉ toṭakkamākum. av vōcai, aḷantu ceyyappaṭṭamaiyiṉ ceyyuḷ eṉappaṭṭatu. ceyyuḷ eṉpataṟkuc cey yappaṭṭa teṉpatu poruḷ; tūkku eṉpatu ceyyuḷukku maṟṟoru peyar. tūkku eṉpatu aḷavu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. ākavē, tūkku, ceyyuḷ eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷ orē poruḷaṉavātal kāṇka. pā eṉpatum ceyyuḷai uṇarttum. ōcai toṭarpākac ceyyuṭkup ‘pā’ eṉṟu peyar iṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.
“pā eṉpatu cēṭpulattirunta kālattum oruvaṉ eḻuttuñ collum teriyāmaṟ pāṭa mōtuṅkāl, avaṉ collukiṉṟa ceyyuḷai vikaṟpittu iṉṉa ceyyuḷeṉṟu uṇartaṟ kētuvākiya parantupaṭṭuc celvatōrōcai” eṉap pērāciriyar pāveṉpataṟkup poruḷ virit turaittār.
muṟkāla makkaḷ uraiyāṭumpōtum eḻutum pōtum āṇṭa naṭaikaḷ vevvēṟu vakaiyiṉa. pēccunaṭai eppoḻutum vacaṉa naṭai yāka amaivuṟṟatu. eḻutuṅkāl eppoḻutum ceyyuḷ naṭaiyē kai yāḷappaṭṭatu. itaṟkuk kāraṇam pala ākalām. ceyyuḷ naṭaimikac curukkamāṉatu; vacaṉa naṭaiyil atikam kūṟavēṇṭiyavaṟṟaic ceyyuḷ muṟaiyiṟ cila varikaḷiṟ kūṟiviṭalām. ōlaiyil eḻuttāṇiyāl eḻutavēṇṭiyiruntakālattil iccurukku muṟaiyai makkaḷ virumputal iyalpākum. accuccātaṉamillāta muṟkālattil nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭaṟkum, paṭieṭuttaṟkum ceyyuḷ muṟai vāyppaḷittatu.
ik kāraṇaṅkaḷiṉālēyē muṟkāla nūlkaḷ ellām ceyyuḷ vaṭiviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kālattukkukkālam makkaḷ āḷum coṟkaḷ māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru kālattiṟ peru vaḻakkilirunta coṟkaḷ maṟaintupōkap putiya coṟkaḷ vaḻakkil varukiṉṟaṉa. putiya coṟkaḷ vaḻakkil vantakālattil eḻutap paṭum nūlkaḷil paḻaiya coṟkaḷiṉ vaḻakkup perumpālum niṉṟuviṭu kiṉṟatu. piṟkālattavarkaḷukku muṟkālattil eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷaip payiṉṟu poruḷ viḷaṅkuvatu kaṭumaiyākiṉṟatu. ik kāraṇattāl muṟkāla nūlkaḷukku vaḻakku (vacaṉa) muṟaiyil poruḷ eḻutappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu eḻutappaṭum poruṭpakutikaḷ urai eṉa vaḻaṅkum. urai eḻutum im muṟaiyiliruntē piṟkālattil vacaṉa nūlkaḷ eḻutappaṭalāyiṉa.
urainaṭaikku aṭivaraiyaṟai mutaliyaṉa illai. muṟkāla uraiyāciriyarkaḷiṉ urainaṭaikaḷiṉ pala pakutikaḷ ceyyuṭkaḷ pōlavē celkiṉṟaṉa. ak kāla makkaḷiṉ vaḻakku eṉappaṭṭa pēccu naṭai, avvāṟu iruntateṉak kūṟutal amaiyātu. av vuraiyaip pārttu ak kāla urainaṭai ev vakaiyiṉateṉa oruvāṟu cintaṉai ceytu aṟintukoḷḷalām.
tolkāppiyam eṉṉum paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūl ki. mu. 350-varaiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. an nūl urai nūlkaḷaip paṟṟic ciṟitu kūṟukiṉṟatu (cū. 485.) avai pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē urainaṭaiyi ṉiyaṉṟa pakutikaḷ vaittuc ceyyappaṭuvaṉa; cūttirattukkup poruḷ eḻutuvaṉa pōlvaṉa; oḻintupōṉa pāṭalkaḷukkup poruḷ eḻututal pōlvaṉa; cevi vaḻakkilvarum kaṟpaṉaik kataikaḷ pōlvaṉa; poy eṉappaṭātu ulakiyalōṭiyaṉṟu nakai viḷaikkakkūṭiya kataikaḷ pōlvaṉa.
iv vakaiya nūlkaḷ oṉṟēṉum iṉṟu kāṇappaṭavillai. paṟpala kāraṇac ceṟivāl māṇṭa tamiḻ nūlkaḷōṭu avaiyuṅkūṭa maṭintaṉa vākalām.
ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṉṉum aracap pulavar pāṭiya cilappatikārattil uraip pāṭṭukkaḷ cila kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. avai aṭivaraiyaṟaiyiṉṟip pāṭṭunaṭaiyil amaintuḷḷaṉa. avai perumpālum āciriyattai ottuḷḷaṉa. ki.pi. 1ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭaḷavil ceyyappaṭṭa iṟaiyaṉār kaḷaviyal urai ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭaḷavil eḻuti vaikkappaṭṭatu. av vurainaṭaiyiṉ cila pakutikaḷ ceyyuḷnaṭai pōṟṟōṉṟiṉum perumpakuti ciṟuciṟu vacaṉaṅkaḷāka amaintuḷḷatu. peruntēvaṉār pāratattiṉ iṭaiyiṭaiyē, maṇippira vāḷa naṭaiyil eḻutappaṭṭa vacaṉa naṭai itaṉpiṉ eḻuntatu. ivvacaṉa naṭai, pārata veṇpāp pāṭiya peruntēvaṉār (ki. pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) ceytatākātu. ki. pi. 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuppiṉ eḻunta urainaṭaikku eṭuttukkāṭṭu aṭiyārkku nallār, pērāciriyar, iḷampūraṇar, nacci ṉārkkiṉiyar, parimēlaḻakar mutalāṉōr uraikaḷākum. piṟkāla urai naṭaikku eṭuttukkāṭṭu mātavac civañāṉa yōkikaḷiṉ civañāṉapōta māpāṭiyamākum.
tamiḻ uṇarccikku etir uṇarcci
vētakālattil tācukkaḷukkum āriyar kaḷukkum mikunta vayiram uṇṭāyiruntatu. ataṉait toṭarntu tamiḻai āriyattilum tāḻttik kūṟum kaṭciyoṉṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil nīṇṭanāḷ toṭarntu varāniṉṟatu. camaya kuravar kālattum iv vakai uṇarcciyirun tamaiyiṉālēyē “āriyaṉ kaṇṭāy tamiḻaṉ kaṇṭāy” eṉat tēvārattuṭ kūṟappaṭalāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu kūṟap paṭṭatu iru kaṭcikaḷaiyum cantu ceytal poruṭṭēyākum.
tamiḻai iḻivupaṭuttum koṭumai nāḷukkunāḷ vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu. atiṟpaṭṭu mayaṅkiya makkaḷ tamiḻiṉ uyarvaiyum ataṉ iṉṟiyamai yāmaiyaiyum uṇarātu ataṉai ikaḻvārāyiṉar. kalviyiṟ periya kampaṉē ‘tēvapāṭaiyi ṉikkatai ceytavar’ eṉa moḻintu vaṭa moḻikkup paṇivu kāṭṭukiṉṟamai kāṇka. parañcōti muṉivar, civañāṉa muṉivar kumarakuruparar mutalāyiṉōr am makkaḷiṉ pēraṟiyāmaik kiraṅki avarkaḷ maṉaṅkoṇṭu tamiḻiṟ paṟṟaṭaiyumāṟu tamiḻiṉ peru maiyaip pala ariya iṉiya teḷḷiya pāṭalkaḷāl viḷakkip pōntaṉar. pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷai avarkaḷ maṉōṉmaṇīyattiṟ kūṟiya tamiḻt teyva vaṇakkap pāṭalkaḷāl ak kālattut tamiḻukku etirāṉa uṇarcci evvaḷavu muṉaittu niṉṟateṉpatu teḷḷitil viḷaṅkum. iṉṟum av vuṇarcci cāmpar pūtta neruppup pōla, palar uḷḷattē karantu kiṭavāmal illai. tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāra uraiyil (poruḷ. 490) āriya naṉṟu tamiḻ tī teṉavuraitta, kāriyattāṟ kālakkōṭ paṭṭāṉaic-cīriya, antaṇ potiyil akattiyaṉā rāṇaiyāl, centamiḻē tīrkka cuvā kā” eṉak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa utāraṇac ceyyuḷālum iv vakai uṇarcci tamiḻ nāṭṭil nīṇṭakālam uḷḷateṉpatai naṉku aṟika.
tamiḻiṉ perumaiyai viḷakkum cila pāṭalkaḷ
toṇṭar nātaṉait tūtiṭai viṭuttatum mutalai
uṇṭa pālaṉai yaḻaittatum, elumpupeṇ ṇuruvāk
kaṇṭa tum, maṟaik kataviṉait tiṟantatum kaṉṉit
taṇṭa miḻccolō maṟupulac coṟkaḷō cāṟṟīr.
viṭaiyu kaittavaṉ pāṇiṉik kilakkaṇa mēṉāḷ
vaṭamo ḻikkurait tāṅkiyaṉ malaya māmuṉikkut
tiṭamu ṟuttiyam moḻikketi rākkiya teṉcol
maṭama kaṭkaraṅ keṉpatu vaḻutinā ṭaṉṟō.
kaṭukka viṉpeṟu kaṇṭaṉun teṉṟicai nōkki
aṭukka vantuvan tāṭuvā ṉāṭali ṉiḷaippu
viṭukka vārameṉ kāltiru mukattiṭai vīci
maṭukka vuntamiḻ tiruccevi māntavu maṉṟō. - tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟ purāṇam.
vaṭamoḻiyaip1 pāṇiṉikku vakuttaruḷi yataṟkiṇaiyāt
toṭarpuṭaiya teṉmoḻiyai yulakamelān toḻutēttum
kuṭamuṉikku valiyuṟuttār kollēṟṟup pākareṉiṟ
kaṭalvaraippi ṉitaṉperumai yāvarē kaṇittaṟivār.
irumoḻikkuṅ kaṇṇutalār mutaṟkuravar iyalvāyppa
irumoḻiyum vaḻippaṭuttār muṉivēntar icaiparappum
irumoḻiyum āṉṟavarē taḻīiyiṉār eṉṟāliv
virumoḻiyum nikareṉṉum itaṟkaiyam uḷatēyō. - kāñcip purāṇam.
poruppilē piṟantu teṉṉaṉ pukaḻilē kiṭantu caṅkat
tiruppilē yiruntu vaiyai yēṭṭilē tavaḻnta pētai
neruppilē niṉṟu kaṟṟōr niṉaivilē naṭantō rēṉa
maruppilē payiṉṟa pāvai maruṅkilē vaḷaru kiṉṟāḷ. - villipāratam.
maṟaimutaṟ kiḷanta vāyāṉ matimukiḻ muṭitta vēṇi
iṟaivartam peyarai nāṭṭi yilakkaṇañ ceyyap peṟṟē
aṟaikaṭal varaippil pāṭai yaṉaittumveṉ ṟāri yattō(ṭu)
uṟaḻtaru tamiḻttey vattai yuṇṇiṉain tēttal ceyvām - cīkāḷattip purāṇam.

caṅkakālattut tamiḻakam
kiruṣṇā natikkukkīḻ uḷḷa nāṭukaḷ mūṉṟu irācciyaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mūvēntaruṭaiyavum kuṟunilamaṉṉar eḻuvaruṭaiyavum irācciyaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkum. ivarkaḷai aṉṟic ciṟiya celvākkuṭaiya talaivarkaḷ palarum viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. kaṭaṟkaraip pakutikaḷ mūvēntarukkuriyavāyiruntaṉa. naṭuppakutikaḷum malaikaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉar vacam iruntaṉa. kiḻakkuk karaiyil kiruṣṇānatimutal teṟkil irāmanātapurap pakutiyiluḷḷa toṇṭivaraiyil cōḻa irācciyam iruntatu. iv virācciyattiṉ naṭuvē kāñcikku vaṭakkiluḷḷa tirukkōvalūraic cūḻnta malaināṭu malaiyamāṉ eṉṉum ciṟṟaracaṉiṉ kīḻ iruntatu. cōḻa nāṭṭukkut teṟkil pāṇṭiya irācciyam iruntatu. itu kiḻakku kaṭaṟkaraiyiliruntu mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkarai varaiyum akaṉ ṟiruntatu. maturai, tirunelvēli, tiruvitāṅkūr, kōyamuttūriṉ pakuti, kocci eṉpaṉa itil aṭaṅkum. iv virācciyattukkuṭpaṭṭirunta potiya malaiyai āyum tirunelvēlip pakutiyiluḷḷa koṟkait tuṟai mukattaic cūḻnta iṭaṅkaḷai evviyum āṇṭaṉar. paḻaṉimalaiyaiyum ataiccūḻnta nāṭukaḷaiyum pēkaṉ āṇṭāṉ. ivaṉuṭaiya nāṭu kaḷukku vaṭakkē mēṟkut toṭarccimalai ōramākac cēranāṭu iruntatu. itu pālaikkāṭṭu veḷi (Palghat gap) vaḻiyākak kōyamut tūrvaraiyuñ ceṉṟatu. itaṟkuc carinērē (Parallel) teṉ maicūril pala ciṟṟaracarkaḷiṉ nāṭukaḷ iruntaṉa. avarkaḷil arayam nāṭṭai iruṅkōvēḷum, paṟampu malaiyaip pāriyum, takaṭūrai (tarumapuri) atiyamāṉum, kolli malaiyai ōriyum āṇṭaṉar. iruṅkōvēḷiṉ nāṭṭukku vaṭakkē kaṅkar nāṭukaḷum, teṟkil koṅku nāṭum iruntaṉa. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭakkē (mēṟkuk karaiyil) uḷḷa tuḷunāṭu naṉṉaṉ āṇaikkuṭ paṭṭiruntatu; kiḻakkil pulliyiṉ nāṭum, vaṭakkil vaṭukar nāṭum iruntaṉa.
muṇṭar
intiyāvilē cūṭiyanākapuriyil muṇṭar (Mundar) eṉṉum oru makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉar vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ pēcum moḻi tamiḻukku uṟavuṭaiyatu. kulamuṟaiyil avarkaḷ tirāviṭak kūṭṭattaic cērntōr. varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ cilar, avarkaḷē āriyarukkum tamiḻa rukkum muṟpaṭṭa intiya makkaḷ eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. uṇmaiyil avarkaḷ tamiḻk kūṭṭattiṉariṉ oru piriviṉarē yeṉṟum, avarkaḷ nīṇṭa kālam tamiḻpperuṅkūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintu taṉittu vāḻn tamaiyiṉ avarkaḷ moḻi paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ ākiyaṉa māṟupaṭātu vantuḷḷaṉaveṉṟum, avarkaḷiṉ moḻi mikap paḻaiya tamiḻākumeṉ ṟum, iṟṟai ñāṉṟai ārāycci vallunar pukalvar.1
2“muṇṭā moḻi (kol) moṉ kimer (Mon Khmer) eṉa vaḻaṅkum. iḵtu intu cīṉamoḻik kūṭṭattaic cārntatu. itu makātōk kuṉṟukaḷi lum (Mahadao Hills) imayamalaiyilum vāḻum makkaḷāṟ pēcappaṭukiṉṟatu. cūṭiya nākapuriyilēyē itu peritum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. im moḻiyaip pēcuvōriṉ eṇ 300,000. im moḻikkuriya makkaḷ aṅkum iṅkum citaṟik kāṇappaṭutaliṉ avarkaḷ muṟkālattil mikap parantu vāḻntār kaḷeṉa uyttuṇaralām. cimiṭ eṉṉum pātiriyār (Fr. Schimidt) avarkaḷai āstirēliya toṭarpuṭaiya moḻikaḷai vaḻaṅkum makkaḷōṭu iṇaittu, ivarkaḷ āciya makkaḷaiyum āstirēliya makkaḷaiyum iṇaikkum caṅkilipōṉṟavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. uṇmaiyil avarkaḷ avvāṟē uḷar. piṟisluski eṉpār. (J. Przyluski) muṇṭā moḻi toṉmaiyuḷḷateṉṟum, paḻaiya moḻikaḷiṉ aṭippaṭaiyil aḵtu uḷḷateṉṟum, aḵtu iṉṟum malāya āciyāviṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟa teṉṟum kūṟuvar.
muṇṭā moḻikaḷai vaḻaṅkuvōr potuniṟamuṭaiya kolliyar (Kols); ivarkaḷ neḷinta (curuṇṭatallāta) talaimayiruṭaiyar; paḻaiya parmiya acām intu cīṉa āstirēliya makkaḷukkum ilaṅkai vēṭaruk kum iṉamuṭaiyōr. cantāttīviṉ pantiṉ (Bantin) eṉṉum cātiyiṉarum celipicimakkaḷum iv viṉattaic cārntōr eṉa vekspoṉṭ (Wxbonds) eṉpār kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. im makkaḷ iruṇṭa niṟamuṭaiya tirāviṭa veḷḷattiṟ pukuntu maṟaintaṉar. tirāviṭariṉ uṟpattiyai aṟiya muṭiyāmaiyiṉāl, ārāycciyāḷar avarkaḷai āstirēliyar, eturuskāṉiyar (paḻaiya ittāliya makkaḷ) piṉṉiya ukkiriyar (Finno-ugrians)kaḷōṭu iṇaittaṉar. tirāviṭameṉpatu tamiḻ eṉpataṉ uccarippu vēṟupāṭu.”
cūṭiyanākapuriyil muṇṭā moḻiyai vaḻaṅkum ōriyar eṉṉum makkaḷukku kuraṅkukkoṭiyum, atē kulakkuṟiyum (Totem) uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum, avaipaṟṟi ivarkaḷ kuraṅkar allatu kuraṅkukaḷ eṉa irāmā yaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟum ‘muṇṭarum avar nāṭum’1 eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

1. The Indian God Shiva or Shiba father of Sebo appears in the Ural Altaic astronomy of the Akkadians the third star of their seven parent stars. - History and Chronology of theWith making age - P. 85-J.F. Hewitt.
The name Sin was given first to the land of the Sumerians, the Euphratian Delta. This is called Shinar in the Bible, Singara by Greeks, Sindjar by the Arabian Geographers and was that ruled by the three eyed and two horned wild pull God. This was the three eyed Shiva of the Hindus, whose wife was the weaving Uma, the flax (Uma) Goddess, the Goddess mother of the flax weavers of Asia Minor who become the Egyptian Goddess Neith - Ibid - P. 250.

The earliest stone age culture of India is represented by the hand axe technique of Madras, and the old stone age people may have migrated from South India into Central India where in the Narbada Valley, have been found middle pleistocene tools and a fauna gradually extended through the Ganges and Jamuna valleys to North Western India right up to Himalayan Hills-India and the Pacific World P. 279.
1. God Varuna was himself an Asura. Mr. Banerji believes that the Asuras were different from Dasus but identical with Dravidians. The Dasus he points out were black, while Asuras were brown or rather golden. This synthesis he points out in the adoption of the Cult of Siva which was the Asura Cult; as a part of Vedic System of worship Varuna and other Asura gods were incorporated into the Vedic Pantheon. The gods of the Asuras and the gods of the Trtsus become one. A social amalgamation came into existence with fruitful results. The Asuras and the Devas were made sons of the same Father God. Many Vedic Kings and Rishis came to have Asura Blood in them as is indicated by the colour. Sages like Vasishta, Agastya and Visvamitra were given the same Father Mitra-varuna. Asura in Aryan means Supreme God - Pre-musalman India....P.P. 172, 174,

3. uyirppaliyiṭum vaḻakkam piṟkālattil kāy kaṉikaḷaiyum piṟa uṇavukaḷaiyum paṭaikkum vaḻakkamāka māṟiṟṟu. uyirp palikkup patil iṉṟum pūcaṉikkāykaḷai
veṭṭi naṭuvē kuṅkumam pūcappaṭukiṉṟatu. paliyiṭṭa mirukaṅkaḷait tīyil iṭṭup porittu uṇpataṟkup patil, piṟkālattil uṇavu ōmattil iṭappaṭukiṉṟatu. ōmattīyil uṇavai iṭuvataṟkup patilāka mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil tūpam kāṭṭappaṭṭateṉa varalāṟṟu nūlār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
1. caṟli āṟṟaik kaṭakka muṉ uḷḷa camayakkoḷkaikaḷil intiya pūrva makkaḷukku āriyar kaṭamaippaṭavillai eṉpatu uṇmaiyē. caṟli āṟṟaik kaṭantapiṉ eḻutappaṭṭa camkitaikaḷaiyum pirāmaṇaṅkaḷaiyum paṟṟi avvāṟu kūṟutal iyalātu. - ci.ār. haṉrar. -New Review-1936.
caṉakaṉ yākñavalkiyaraiyum avaruṭaṉ niṉṟa pirāmaṇaraiyum nōkki, ‘nīṅkaḷ akkiṉikōttiram eppaṭic ceykiṟīrkaḷ?’ eṉṟu kēṭṭapōtu avarkaḷ takunta patil aḷikkavillai. ākavē tīyil paliyiṭutal (ōmam) mutaliyaṉa tamiḻariṉ kiriyai muṟaiyē yeṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu. iṉṟu ālayaṅkaḷil naṭakkum kiriyaikaḷai otta paḻaiya tamiḻarkaḷiṉ kiriyaimuṟaikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟiyē pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭiruttalum kūṭum. pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ ki. mu. 800kkuc ciṟitu muṉ ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, ākavē mikappaḻaiya vēta pāṭaṅkaḷ ki. mu. 1200kku muṉ tōṉṟiyirukka muṭiyāteṉṟum paṉēji eṉpār kūṟuvar.-varalāṟṟukku muṟpaṭṭa paḻaiya intu intiyā. pakkam. 44.
1. yākaṅkaḷil pāṭalkaḷ pāṭuvatum iv vakaiyiṉatē.
2. Krishna represented in the Maha-Bharata as black and so is Arjuna-so also is Vyasa who has related the story. And so is Drappathi the wife of pandavas. ‘Here is then Draupathi apuroaching.....with eyes like a lotus petal and with a lusterous dark complexion resembling the colour of a black lotus. By her side is Subathirai who is fair as gold. Here is another wife of Arjuna being the daughter of Naga king fair as pure as gold. The Dravidian dialects affected profoundly by sounds the structure, the idiom and the vocobulary of the former in the course of its growth in India on account of the constant influence of the Dravidian tongue. This language lost the subjunctive mood, many infinitive forms and several noun declentions, forgot its richly varied system of real verb tenses and adopted terms of expressions peculiar to the Dravidian idiom...The Dravidian names of things and operations connected with these arts of peace are native and not foreign (i.e. borrowed from Sanskrit) the question has not yet been investigated but on enquiry it will most probably turn out that many Sanskrit words connected with these were borrowed from the Dravidian. -The age of the mantras-P. 1. ak kāla vīrar payaṉpaṭuttiya paṭaikkalaṅkaḷuḷ vaḷaitaṭi oṉṟu. itu āstirēliya pūrvakuṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum pūmaṟāṅ eṉṉum taṭiyē eṉa ārāycciyāl terikiṉṟatu. ataṉai ōr ilakkai nōkki eṟiyiṉ aḵtu ilakkiṟpaṭṭu mīṇṭum tirumpi varum. ṭākṭar oppēṭ eṉpār itaikkuṟittu eḻutiyiruppatu piṉvarumāṟu:
Dr. Oppert says, “this general belief that the bumerang is a weapon peculiar to Australians, but this is by no means the case. It is well known in many parts of India specially in its Southern peninsula. The Tamilian Maravar and Kallar employ it when hunting and throw it after deer. In the Madras Government Museum are shown three boomerangs two ivory ones which came from the armoury of the late Rajah of Tanjore, and a common one which came from Pudukota. The wood of which the bumerang is made is very dark. I possess a fair black wooden and one iron bumerang, which I received from Pudukota. In the arsenal of the Pudukota Rajah is always kept a stock of these sticks. Their name in Tamil is Valai Tadi, ‘bent stick’ as the stick is bent and flat. When thrown a whirling motion is imparted to the weapon which causes it to return to the place it was thrown. The natives are well acquainted with the peculiar fact - Indo - Aryans - Vol. 2-R. Mitra.

2. The practice of (linga worship) was so general as to have spread itself over a large part of the habitable globe, for it flourished for many ages in Egypt, and Syria, Persia, Asia minior, Greece and Italy; it still is in vigour in India and many parts of Africa and was even found in America on its discovery by the Spaniards. “being regarded as the most sacred objects of worship and consecrated by religion, the cultus was associated with very idea and sentiment which was regarded as ennobling to man....In Egypt the phallus is frequently represented by a symbol of generation. The Jews did not escape this worship; we see their women manufacturing Phalli of gold and silver as we find in Ezakijel.” XVI 17 - Westroppe.

1. There appear to have been a general permit for a learned Brahman to speak his mind in any durbar; and these Brahmans often gave out their opinions most fearlessly. This privilege was similarly accorded also to men of learning - Beginnings of South Indian History, P. 139 - S.K. Aiyengar.
2. First thing in the morning, fire must be lit, a lamp carried round to the master, as they usually rose before the sunrise, then some incense burnt to give divine nourishment of perfume, incense being literally, ‘divine food’ like cooking the morning meal. The master’s door was then opened, obeisance was made to him, with protestations of fidelity, he was anointed, and the divine food of incense put before him. The servant then returned with assurances that he was ready to his master. After the breakfast the servant comes forward again, goes through the same obeisance and calls on him to wake up in peace, declaring that his word is law, and that he will destroy his enemies. Then the master is washed
perfumed, and dressed in various cloths, anointed and decorated. The ground is sanded for him to walk on when he comes forth. Several offerings of incense and another washing represent the meals and cleaning during the day and the service is over. - Flinders Petrie.

1. The discovery of Zimbawe of soap stone clyinder of quern shape with rings of rosettes on the top and sides, which rosettes are believed to represent the sun and are common in phallic decorations. This cylinder which is considered as undoubtedly Phoenician is similar to the one found at the temple of Paphos in Cyprus which was once a leading colony. The rosettes are also similar to those on the sacred cone of the great Phoenician temple of the sun in Emesu in Syria and also to the the rosettes on the Phoenician sepulchral setele in the British museum. The ancient Ruins of Rhodesea P. 38 - R.N. Hall and W.G. Neal.
2. The Jupiter of Syria is represented as a bearded God with phrygian cap....at his side hangs his sword in its scabboard. His right hand brandishes a double axe; his left gashes a thunderbolt consisting of six spirally twisted lines, each of which is tipped with arrow head. The bull that supports the God has a rosette on the forehead between the eyes-Zeus-P. 637, Arthur Bernard Cook.

1. We have interesting evidences of the sacred dancing which we know to have found part of the ritual of the Hebrews and Phoenicians. Indeed they still survive not only in Asia-Minor but in Palastine at Adh Dhaheryth (Debir). In 1874 I saw the village elders dancing solemnly before the shrine of their Noby. David it will be remembered danced with almost the fury of a Bacchic right before the ark. The Derwish dances also must not be forgotten and the dancing of the Jerusalam people at the feast of tabernacles mentioned in the Misbnah - Syrian Stone lore P. 100.
2. The religious prostitutes mentioned by Heradotus, who were certainly a prominent feature in Babylonean religion. They were women who took vows of celebacy though usually dwelling together in special convents could neverthless live in this world and were often normally married. If married (and to possess votaress wife was probably regarded as destinction) a concubine was provided to bear children to the husbands, but had no legal wifely rights, which belonged to the votaresses. -The Ancient History of the Near East - R.H. Hall.
The Syrian emperors brought with them to the Capital of the world (Rome) the Phoenician calus of conical stone and the troops of temple women. -The Syrian stone lore - P. 228.
The woman attached to the Hindu temple exactly reproduce the Kadesh or other town sacred to the great mother Goddess. The menhir, or conical stone was the emblem throughout Syria of the Gods presiding over fertility - Ibid P. 47.
All alike had women allotted to their service, but the Hebrew God went beyond the Hindu, for he was cheered with wine and for long periods the male Kadashism thronged in the santuary - Judges IX 13; Dent XIX 26; Jer. XXXV - Short Studies in the Science of comparative Religions.

2. .......Should here be certainly regarded as the couch shell used Minoan cult for summoning divinity. Such trumpet shells are themselves of frequent occurance in Minoan shrines...The special object of cult in this case is the group of Cyprus like trees shown behind the altar, and in front of which are set the sacred horns and altars while a votary brings down the divinity by blowing blasts - The Palace of Minos at Knossos, Vo. IV - P. 244.
The earliest use of the couch shell trumpet was in the Minoan worship in Crete. Thence it spread for and wide until it came to play apart in religious services of Christian, an Jehovah, Brahman, an Buddhist Shinto, and Shamanislic in widely different parts of the world - In Mediterranean, in India, in central Asia in Indo-nesia and Japan in Ocenia and America. In many of these places it was supposed to have the definite ritual object summoning the Deity - Shells as evidence of the Migration of Early Culture P. XXIV - J. Wilfred Jackson.

3. 1. In Phoenician, Greek and later times these couch shell trumpets were essentially used in the Mediterranean. 1. Tyrian purple and purple stuff were essentially articles of luxury varying in price according to times and quality. They were always costly and vied in value even with gold itself. Consequently we find them reserved for the hanging of temples or employed for the robes of priests and kings. 1. Foot found near the French rocks near Mysore, “part of a large chatty with two ornaments coniform in shape, with a small pap in each re-entering angle and a raised garland like ring round each cross”. This is evidently a Swastika, and it bears a strong resemblance to a Trojen Type of Swastika - Stone age in India, P. 43.
2. Baal was represented by an altar, by a bull, by the cross sign of the sun + (sun and its rays) or by linga stone, or a stone pillar - Links with past ages - P. 22 - E.P. Orton.
The sacred symbol of the Hittites also, including the True Cross or the Sun-Cross or the Red-Cross of St. George of Cappadocia and England and St. Andrews Cross are identified with the Sun-Cross of Sumerian, Trojans, ancient Britons as displayed in my comparative plates of these crosses - The Makers of Civilization - P. 16. Waddell.
We do not know where the Swastika first appeared. It may have appeared in India or some near part of Asia, long before the time of Budda and spread from there to all over the world. The symbol is universal. Anthropoligists draw maps showing the distribution and migration of the Swastika. There are the maps of the whole world spotted with Swastika from New Zealand, Australia and Japan, through Asia, Africa and Europe to North and South America. “This odd little Symbol spins gaily round the world” say H.G. Wells in his out line of History - The Swastika - P. 14 Well Hayes.
