1. The Puranas naturally lent themselves to augmentations, and the Puranic Brahmans used their opportunities to the full, partly with further genuine traditions, but mostly with additions of Brahmanical stories and fables and doctrine and ritual matter.
Brahmanic tradition speaks from the Brahmanical stand-point, describes events and expresses feelings as they would appear to Brahmans, illustrates Brahma-nical ideas, maintains and inculcates the degnity., sanctity, suprimacy and even superhuman character of Brahmans, enunciates. Brahmanical doctrines whatever subserved the interest of the Brahmans often enforcing the moral by means of marvellous incidents that not seldom are made up of absurd and utterly impossible details. It often introduces kings, because kings were their patrons, yet even so the Brahman’s dignity is never forgotten.
It is mainly Brahmanical mistakes and absurdities that have discredited Puranas. - Ancient Indian Historical Traditions - P.P. 37 39 - F, E. Pargiter.
1. Brahman tales generally untrustworthy for traditional history. The Ramayana is highly Bramanical and its stories are fanciful and often absurd - Ibid.
2. After the study of the above one-thousand and eight hundred inscriptions which up to now have been deciphered by the present writer, it is easy to realize that the wave of migrations of the Mediterranean race which as supposed to have been from West to East must now be finally settled as having taken place in the opposite direction, i.e., East to West.
The development of Mohenjo-Daro script, the religion of these two countries and that of Egypt, the titles of kings, the number of Zodiacal constellations
among the Proto Indian people and the relative position of these constellations, the changing of the Proto Indian constellation of the harp (Yal) for Turus (the bull) which might have taken place in Sumer, the tradition of ancient people of Mesopotamia recorded by Berosus, the parallel by Bibilical account in Gen. ii, 1.5 all point to the same conclusion, that the migration of the Mediterranean race commenced from India and extended through South Mesopotamia and Northern Africa; spread through Crete, Cyprus, Greece, Italy and Spain and across the Pyrenees reached Central Europe and the British Isles. This route starting from Ceylon to Ireland is marked by uninterrupted chain of Dolmens and other megaliths that seem to be the relics of the enterprising and highly civilized race which is termed the Mediterranean by the anthropologists and which in India has been quite unreasonably despised under the name Dravidian. - Journal of Indian History, Vol. XVI - Part 1- Fr. Heras.

1. kavu, tukim, oṟicā, iñciper, kapiṟoṉ, ipim, pippili, cintu.
2. There are in all Indo-aryan languages, a considerable number of words which cannot apparently be identified in other; Indo-European languages. This is specially the case in modern vernaculars and the old opinion was that such words had, generally speaking been borrowed from the language of the tribes which inhabited India before the Aryan invation. The steady progress of philological studies in later years has enabled us to trace an overincreasing portion of
such words to Sanskrit, and many scholars now hold that there have hardly been any loan at all. It has however been overlooked that it is sufficient to show that a word is found in Sanskrit or in vedic dialects, in order to prove that it belonged to the original language of the Aryan. The foreign element must reach into the oldest times, and it would be necessary to trace dubeous words not only in Sanskrit, but also in other languages of the Indo-European family. That is exactly what philology has, in many Cases failed to do. There are i.g. a number of verbal roots in Sanskrit which do not occur in other Indo-European forms of speech. The same is the case with a considerable portion of vocabulary. There is how-ever every possibility for the supposition that at least a considerable portion of such words and bases has been borrowed from the Dravidian - Linguistic Survey of India - Vol. IV. pp. 278-9 Grierson.

We might see that it would be more correct to describe Indian religion as Dravidian religion stimulated and modified by the ideas of Aryan invaders. For the great deities of Hinduism Siva, Krishna, Rama, Durga and some of its most essential doctrines such a metempsychosis and divine incarnation are either totally unknown to the Veda or obscurely adumbrated in it. The chief characteristics of mature Indian religion are characteristics of an area not of a race, and they are not the characteristics of religion of Persia, Greece or other Aryan lands - Hinduism and Buddhism - P. XV-Sir Charles Elliot.

The language of the Rig Veda is as yet purely Aryan or Indo-European in its form, structure and spirit, but its phonetics is already affected by the Dravidian and it has already begun to borrow words from Dravidian and Kol. Among words of probable Dravida origin in the Rig Veda, the following may be noted-anu - particle; arani - rubbing wood for fire; Kapi - monkey; Karmara - smith; Kala-time, Kunda-Hole; Gana-Band; nana-several; nila-blue; Puspa - flower; Pujana-worship; Phala-fruit; Bija-seed; Mayura-peafowl; ratri-night; rupa-form-Ibid P. 42.

Sayce in his Hibbert lectures (1887 pp. 137-138) observes that in an ancient list of Babylonian clothing sindu is mentioned as a name of muslin, or woven cloth and that it corresponds Sadin of the Old testament and the...of Greeks. The learned Prof. has further stated that this muslin or woven cloth must have been called sindhu by the Accadians (Chaldians) because it was exported from the banks of Indus (sindhu) to Chaldia in those days (e.g. the word calico from Calicut). He has further noted that this intercourse between the two countries must have been by sea, for the word has passed by land i.e., through Persia the initial of the word would have become he in Persian mouths. - Commemorative essays presented to Sir R.G. Bhandarkar, 1917.
1. Dravidian speaking races of India traded with the ancient Chaldians before the Vedic language found its way into India. Lect. P. 137). The gold and spices mentioned in Assyrian inscriptions of the XIV Cen. B.C. were probably exported from India, the only Country so far as we know they were produced. - Life in Ancient India - P.T.S. Aiyengar.

Gold, Silks, Pearls etc. had been passing from India to Assyrian monarchy carried on both by caravans on land and by the coasting trade by sea. 1. The following accounts from Marco Polo of this coast is worth noticing! “There go forth every year more than a hundred corsair vessels and cruise. These pirates take with them their wives and children and stay out the whole summer. Their method is to go in fleets of twenty or thirty of these pirate vessels together, and then they form what they call a sea cordon, that is that they drop off till there is an interval of five to six miles between ship and ship, so that they cover something like a hundred miles of sea and no merchant ship can escape them. For when any one corsair sights a vessel a signal is made by fire or smoke, and then the whole of them make for this and scize merchants and plunder them.....But now the merchants are aware of this and go so well manned and armed, and with such great ships and they do not fear the corsairs. Still mishaps do befall them at times - The Beginnings of South Indian History.
1. The greatest achievement of Dravidians was the art of navigation. 1. The use of the knots in code for the purpose of reckoning and recording numbers seems to have been as universal as the figures of the cat’s cradle in the practices of the premitive peoples. Both may be said to be indigenous to all lands in which the arts of spinning, weaving and dyeing have been cultivated. In two note-worthy cases tradition makes the knotted Kude serve as letters. In China knot records are said to have preceded the knowledge of writing. -The Ancient Quipu. L. Leland Locke.
1. Brahmi has been traced back to the Phoenician type of writing represented in the inscription of Meshu king of moab. (850 B.C). Dr. Scoff however in his remarkable commentary to the Pereplus of the Erthrean sea, claims a Phoenician origin for the Dravidian alphabet (P. 229) - G. Elliot Smith.
2. It is also shown that those scholars were not mistaken who connected Brahmi with the South Semitic and Phoenician scripts. For there is much evidence to show that they were also derived from the script of Harappa and Mohenjo Daro.- G.R. Hunter.
The two Brahmi Scripts one of Northern and the other of Southern India are developments of Mohenjo-Daro script, that of South India is the direct continuation and development of Mohenjo-Daro script by Dravidian people of South India. Several signs of the Mohenjo-Daro script are found in the prehistoric pottery of Tinnevelly District in rock inscriptions of Nilgiris and tombs of Hyderabad States.- Rev. Fr. Heras.
3. It is clearly the long headed Mediterranean who have the strongest claim to a connection by blood with the Dravidians and are most likely to have used a Dravidic speech. May it be that these same Mediterranean-who are traceable across the whole south of the Africian belt-spoke agglutinative languages and that they, perhaps more than any others, were the race of the back of this far flund civilization of the chalcolithic age? - Mohenjo-Daro and Indus Civilization, P. 42.
Sir John Evans in his Presidential Address to the British Association says: “Southern India was probably the cradle of human race”. Investigation to relation to race show it to be possible that Southern India was once the passage ground by which the ancient progenitors of Northern and Mediterranean races proceeded to the parts of the globe which they inhabit. - Cultural Heritage of India, Vol. III, P. 677.

1. Dr. Grierson has recently proved that these languages belong to the same family. The Munda language of the Bengal hills is of a more archaic type than those of Madras and the linguistic evidence leads to the conclusion that both races came from the south and that they must have remained apart for a period sufficiently long to admit of the development of those peculiarities which differenciate the languages so called Kolarian or Munda Group from those of the other Dravidians. - Natives of Northern India. - P. 29-W. Crooke B.A.
The proto-Austroloids are responsible for the introduction of neolithic culture and pottery in India but their linguistic legacy is more enduring and important. They are known as the speakers of Austro-Asiatic languages distributed over the widest area from the Punjab to New Zealand and from Madagascar to Easter Island. The Indian variety of the languages is known as the Munda, which accordingly is to be considered the earliest language spoken in India. A consideration of the Munda linguistic areas in India thrown light on the course of proto-Austroloid migrations. These may have been from East to West or from West to East. Munda survives now on the inner Himalayan ranges between Ladukh Sikkim in the west of the Central Provinces and southwards among the Ganjam and Vizagapatam hills but not beyond the Godawari. Munda shows affinity not merely with the languages of south east Asia and the Pacific but also with the agglutinative Sumerian languages. This is considered that the various branches of the Austro-Asiatic family of languages originated at some common centre in central or south East Asia from which it spread in a more or less southerly direction.
The Munda speaking peoples are called by the General names, Mundas, Kolarians, Kols and number of six million comprising Santals, Bhils, Kurumbas, Mundas, Savaras and other minor tribes like the Kuravas of Sirguja and Mirzapur. - Hindu civilization P. 34 - R.K. Mookerjee.
2. Ancient India and Indian Civilization P. 11, Paul Masson Oursel.

teṉṉintiya kaṭavuḷar kaiyil irukkum vīṇaikaḷ

tamiḻ intiyā
tōṟṟuvāy
teṉṉintiyāviṉ nila amaippu ulakiṟ paḻamaiyuṭaiyatu.1 ataṉ varalāṟum av vakaiyiṉatē. ataṉ ārāycci kallil nār urittal pōṉṟa kaṭumaiyuṭaiyatātaliṉ ataṉai ātimutal oḻuṅkupaṭutti eḻutiṉār yārumillai.2 varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ ituvaraiyil teṉ ṉintiyāviṉ paḻaiya varalāṟṟiṉai eḻuta muyaṉṟirukkiṟārkaḷ eṉṟu māttiram kūṟalām. ulaka makkaḷiṉ paḻaiya varalāṟṟu ārāycciyil teṉṉintiya pūrva varalāṟṟiṉai viḷakkum pakutikaḷ iṭaiyiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vakai ātāraṅkaḷaiyum varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ āṅkāṅkuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷa kuṟippukaḷaiyum tuṇaikkoṇṭu in nūl varaiyappaṭalāyiṟṟu. varalāṟu kaṟpaṉaik kataipōlak kūṟattakkataṉṟāka liṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāta iṭaṅkaḷil ēṟṟa mēṟkōḷkaḷ kāṭṭappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa.
iyal - 1
toṉmaik kālam
tamiḻ intiyāviṉ paḻaiya nila amaippu
nila nūlār paḻaiyakālattu ip pūmiyiṟṟōṉṟiyirunta kaṇṭaṅ kaḷaiyum kaṭalkaḷaiyumpaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟapaṭi, kiḻakkut tīvukaḷ āstirēliyā intiyā āppirikkā mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷ oru perum nilapparappāka viḷaṅkiṉa. ap periya nilapparappiṟku avarkaḷ konvāṉā (Gondwana) eṉap peyar vaḻaṅkiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. mēṟkuttoṭarccimalai konvāṉāk kaṇṭattaik kiḻakku mēṟkākap pirittatu. arāvali malaikaḷukku vaṭakkē kaṭal iruntatu. konvāṉāk kaṇṭattiṉ perumpākam ciṟitu ciṟitākak kaṭaluḷ maṟaintapōtu intiyā āppirikkā kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷ eṉpaṉa kaṭalāṟ pirikkappaṭṭaṉa. irāta kumutmukkēci eṉpavar teṉṉintiyāviṉ paḻaiya nila amaippaic curukkamākavum viḷakkamākavum kūṟiyuḷḷār. atu varumāṟu:
konvāṉā allatu lemūriyā
“nila nūlār konvāṉā eṉap peyariṭṭuḷḷa periya pūkaṇṭattiṉ miccamāka uḷḷatu intiya nāṭu. ap pūkaṇṭam teṉṉāppirikkā mutal āstirēliyā teṉ amerikkā varaiyil parantu kiṭantatu. iḵtu ap pakutikaḷil kallākac camaintu kiṭakkum pirāṇikaḷ tāvaraṅkaḷiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷaik1 koṇṭu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. mēṟkut toṭarccimalai konvāṉāk kaṇṭattaik kiḻakku mēṟkākap pirikkum mukaṭupōlamain tiruntatu. ātaliṉālēyē intiyāviṉ āṟukaḷ arāpikkaṭalai nōkki uṟpattiyāki vaṅkāḷak kuṭākkaṭaluḷ vīḻkiṉṟaṉa. vaṭakkē iretēk2 kaṭal mattiya airōppā ciṉṉa āciyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷai mūṭip parantu kiṭantatu. intiyāvilē arāvali malaikaḷ māttiram kaṭalaip pārttu niṉṟaṉa. nīṇṭa kālattiṉpiṉ malai (imayam) eḻa ārampittatu. iretēk kaṭal mēṟku nōkkip piṉvāṅkiṟṟu3.” viññāṉattiṉ curukkam eṉṉum nūlil konvāṉāk kaṇṭattaippaṟṟi aticayappaṭat takka nikaḻcci oṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu varumāṟu:
“airōppāvil nilakkari uṇṭāyirukkiṉṟa kālattil āstirēliyā teṉṉamerikkā intiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil orē vakait tāvaraṅkaḷ vaḷarntaṉa; ātaliṉāl in nilaṅkaḷ ellām oṉṟucērntu konvāṉāk kaṇṭa māka viḷaṅkiṉa” eṉṟu muṭivu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu.1 pērāciriyar veciṉar2 putiya koḷkai oṉṟai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷār: avar kūṟuvatu, “ik kaṇṭaṅkaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu aṇmaiyil iruntaṉa. aṇṭār ṭikkā, āstirēliyā, teṉṉame rikkā, intiyā mutaliya nāṭu kaḷait teṉṉāppirikkāvaic cuṟṟip porutti vaikkalām. kaṭaliṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa karuṅkaṟ pāṟai veṭittamaiyāl ik kaṇṭaṅkaḷ viṭupaṭṭup paṉikkaṭṭi taṇṇīril mitantu celvatupōla akaṉṟu ceṉṟuḷḷaṉa. itu nila nūlār kūṟum 3aintāvatu ulakattil nikaḻntatu.” eṉpatākum.
tāvara uyir nūlār konvāṉā eṉṉum kaṇṭattai lemūriyā eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar.
paḻaiya pūmi cāttiram
intiya makkaḷiṉ paḻaṅkataikaḷaik kūṟum purāṇaṅkaḷum itikācaṅkaḷum pūmiyiṉ naṭuvē periya pūkaṇṭamoṉṟu iruntataik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mirukēntira ākamattiṟ kūṟappaṭuvatāka mātavac civañāṉa yōkikaḷ civañāṉapōta māpāṭiyattiṟ kāṭṭiyuḷḷa pakutikaḷ cila ap periya pūkaṇṭattaippaṟṟi ōraḷavu viḷakkuvaṉavākum. avai varumāṟu: ēḻu perun tīviṉ naṭuttīvāvatu nūṟāyiram yōcaṉaip parappuṭaittāy vaṭṭamāy nilamakaṭku untit tāṉamāyuḷḷa nāvalan tīvu........in nāvalattīviṉ naṭuvē mēruvarai........mēruvaraiyaic cūḻnta nilamākiya iḷāvirutam.........imayattiṟkum teṉ kaṇṭattiṟkum naṭukkaṇṭamākiya pāratavaruṭattil vāḻvōr..........ivaṟṟuṭ1 pāratavaruṭamum oṉpatu kaṇṭamāy intirattīvum, kacēruttīvum, tāmiraparṇit tīvum, kapatittīvum, nākattīvum, cāntiramattīvum, kāntaruvattīvum, vāruṇattīvum, kumarittīvu meṉap peyar peṟṟup pala malaikaḷum pala natikaḷum palavēṟu vakaippaṭṭa cātikaḷumuṭaittāy vayaṅkum. iv voṉpatuḷḷum kumarikkaṇṭamoṉṟē vētākamavaḻakkum, cātivarampum, kaṅkai mutaliya puṇṇiya tīrttaṅkaḷum, kāci mutaliya puṇṇiya talaṅkaḷumuṭaittāyc ciṟantatu. ēṉai eṭṭuk kaṇṭaṅkaḷum milēccar vāḻiṭaṅkaḷām.
“pūmiyiṉ mēṟparappu ēḻu kaṭalum ēḻu peruntīvum naṭuvē mēruvaraiyu muṭaittāyp pūlōkameṉap peyar peṟum. in nāvalan tīviṉaṭuvē mēruvarai nūṟāyiram yōcaṉai aḷavuṭaittāy vayaṅkum....” itu mirukēntirattiṟ kaṇṭatu.
mēru ulakukku naṭu
purāṇaṅkaḷum itikācaṅkaḷum paḻaiya kaṭal nilaṅkaḷaippaṟṟip piṟaḻak kūṟumāyiṉum mēru ulakukku mattiyil uḷḷatu eṉpatai ellām orutalaiyāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷum. putta caiṉa nūlkaḷ mēruvai mantaram eṉak kūṟum. pākavata purāṇamum kantapurāṇamum oru kālattil kāṟṟuk kaṭavuḷukkum mērumalaikkum nērntapōril kāṟṟuk kaṭavuḷāl muṟittuk kaṭaluḷ vīcappaṭṭa mēruviṉ cikaramoṉṟē ilaṅkai eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. itu veppa nāṭukaḷil aṭikkaṭi nērum kāṟṟukkuḻappam oṉṟaiyum ak kālattil mēruviṉ oru pakuti kaṭaluḷ maṟainta varalāṟ ṟiṉaiyum kuṟikkumeṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē im malai pūmattiya irēkaiyai aṭuttu ulakukku mattiyil iruntateṉat terikiṉṟatu. piṟkālattil im malai imayamalaiyai aṭuttō ataṟku vaṭakkilō iruntateṉak karutappaṭṭatu. ki. mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiya mekas tīṉas eṉṉum kirēkkar mērumalai pāṇṭi nāṭṭil uḷḷateṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
pāskarāccāriyar eḻutiya vāṉanūṟ kuṟippil pūmattiya irēkai paḻaiya ilaṅkaikku ūṭākac ceṉṟateṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. pūmattiya irēkai cumattirāvukku ūṭākac celkiṉṟatu. ilaṅkaip putta nūlkaḷ ippoḻutuḷḷa ilaṅkai, muṉṉaiya ilaṅkaiyil paṉṉiraṇ ṭil oru pakuti eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu ilaṅkaikku 400 kal tūrattiluḷḷa mālaittīvu eṉappaṭum tīvukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ ilaṅkaiyiṉ pakutiyāka iruntaṉa eṉṟu nilanūlār kūṟuvar. at tīvukaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ āstirēliya makkaḷukkum intiyāviṟ kāṇappaṭum cila makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉarukkum iṉamuṭaiyavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.1 ceṅ kōṉṟaraic celavu eṉṉum nūlil kaṭal koṇṭa nāṭṭiṉ cila malai, āṟṟu iṭappeyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṟaiyaṉārakap poruḷurai talaiccaṅkamirunta maturaiyaik kaṭal koṇṭateṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ paḵṟuḷiyāṟu, kumariyāṟu mutaliyavaikaḷaik kaṭal koṇṭateṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. aṭiyārkku nallār kumarimuṉaikkut teṟkē kiṭantu kaṭal koḷḷappaṭṭa nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭukaḷaik kuṟip piṭṭatōṭu an nilapparappu 700 kāvata2 muṭaiyateṉavuṅ kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivaikaḷ ellām oru kālattil intiyak kuṭānāṭṭiṉ amaippu vēṟu vakaiyil iruntateṉpatai naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa.
kaṭal kōḷ
iv vulaka makkaḷ ellōrum oru periya kaṭal kōḷaippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉarum atu tattam nāṭṭil nikaḻntateṉpar. ik katai ciṟiya ciṟiya vēṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ orē vakaiyāka ellōrālum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē ulaka makkaḷ mattiya iṭa moṉṟiliruntu pirintu celvataṉ muṉ ik kaṭalkōḷ nikaḻntateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.3
intiya makkaḷ kaṭalkōḷaip paṟṟik kūṟum varalāṟu catapatap pirāmaṇattil (ki. mu. 1300) mutaṉmutaṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi nūliṟ ṟaṉittu niṟkum kataiyāyiruppatāl itu akkēṭiyariṭamiruntō tamiḻariṭamiruntō āriyarukkuk kiṭaittateṉa varalāṟṟu nūlār kūṟu kiṉṟaṉar. catapatap pirāmaṇattilum purāṇaṅkaḷilum kūṟappaṭum veḷḷap perukkiṉ varalāṟu varumāṟu:
maṉu taṉatu kālai vaḻipāṭṭaic ceytu koṇṭirukkumpōtu oru mīṉ avaratu kaiyil vantatu. am mīṉ maṉuvai nōkki, “eṉṉaik kāppāṟṟu nāṉ uṉṉaik kāppēṉ; oru veḷḷap perukku ellā vuyirkaḷaiyum aḻittuviṭum” eṉṟatu. avar mīṉai oru muṭṭiyuḷ viṭṭār. atu peritāka vaḷarntapōtu avar ataṉai oru kuḷattil viṭṭār. atu iṉṉum peritāṉapōtu avar ataik kaṭaluḷ viṭṭār. piṉpu mīṉ avarai oru tōṇi ceyyumpaṭi vēṇṭiṟṟu. maṉu appaṭiyē ceytār. veḷḷap perukku nērntatu. mīṉ talaiyil oṟṟaik kompuṭaṉ vantatu. maṉu kompil oru kayiṟṟaik kaṭṭiṉār. mīṉ avarai vaṭamalaikkuk koṇṭu pōyviṭṭatu. mīṉ, “yāṉ uṉṉaik kāppāṟṟi viṭṭēṉ, tōṇiyai marattiṟ kaṭṭu,” eṉṟu maṉuviṭaṅ kūṟiṟṟu.
māpāratattil iv varalāṟu vēṟu vakaiyiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. vaiva cutamaṉu vaicālavaṉattil tavañ ceytu koṇṭiruntār. appoḻutu mīṉ āṟṟaṅkaraiyil vantu maṟṟa mīṉkaḷaip pārttut taṉṉaik kākkumpaṭi kēṭṭatu. maṉu am mīṉai eṭuttuc cāṭiyil viṭṭār. piṉ mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. maṉu ēḻu iruṭikaḷuṭaṉum palavakai vitaikaḷuṭaṉum tōṇiyil ēṟiṉār. mīṉ tōṇiyai imayamalaikkuk koṇṭu pōyiṟṟu. at tōṇi oru marattiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. piṉpu am mīṉ iruṭikaḷai nōkki, “yāṉē piramā; yāṉ mīṉ vaṭiveṭuttu uṅkaḷ ellōraiyum kāttēṉ,” eṉak kūṟiṟṟu.
pākavata purāṇattil iv viṭam teṉṉintiyāveṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. atil maṉu allatu cattiyavirataṉ tirāviṭa vēntaṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. aṅku maṉu malaiya malaiyil ūṟṟeṭuttu varukiṉṟa kirutamālaiyil (vaiyaiyil) pali celuttumpōtu mīṉ avaruṭaiya kaiyil varukiṉṟatu.
purāṇaṅkaḷ paḻaiya ñāpakaṅkaḷai ōraḷaviṟ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉa. iv varalāṟṟuḷ cila uṇmaikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu maṟuttaṟkillai. purāṇaṅkaḷ piṟkālaṅkaḷil kūṭṭiyum tiruttiyum kuṟaittum eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1
ulakamakkaḷiṉ ōr uṟpattikkuriya aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ
ki.mu. 4000 varaiyil ulakamakkaḷai ellām oru kūṭṭattil iṇaippataṟkuriya ātāraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkuriya iraṇṭu ātāraṅkaḷ moḻiyum, vaḻipāṭumākum. ki.mu. 4000 varaiyil kiḻakkut tīvukaḷ cīṉā amerikkā cumēriyā papilōṉ kirēttā caipiras mutaliya tēcaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷ orē uṟpattiyaic cērntaṉa eṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. pirittaṉ tēcam mutal yappāṉ, amerikkā varaiyil cūriyattampa vaḻipāṭu (civaliṅkavaḻipāṭu) kāṇap paṭṭatu. iv viṭaṅkaḷilellām pāmpum iṭapamum paricuttamuṭaiyaṉa vākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. iv viraṇṭu periya ētukkaḷāl ulakamakkaḷ oru peruṅ kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintu āṅkāṅku vāḻntārkaḷ eṉpatu teḷḷitiṟ pulaṉākiṉṟatu. itaṟkātāramāka mēlnāṭṭu varalāṟṟāciri yarkaḷ ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷavai cilavaṟṟai īṇṭut tarukiṉṟōm.
“cempuk kālattil kiḻakkē mattiyatarai mutal cīṉā varaiyil orē vakai nākarikam nilaviyateṉa vikrar kiṟittiyaṉ eṉpavar kūṟiyuḷḷār.”1
“nīlāṟu, taikiras, cintu, kaṅkai, intu āciyā, ocēṉiyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ tōṇikaḷ maṟantu pōkappaṭṭa makkaḷiṉ nākarika iṇaippai veḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa.”2 (ivai orēvakai amaippuṭaiyaṉa.)
“amerikkariṉ muṉṉōr āriyamakkaḷukku muntiyavarkaḷāṉa turāṉiya vakuppāriṉ moḻiyai vaḻaṅkiyōrāyiruttal vēṇṭum. at turāṉiyaruḷ mukkiyam vāyntōr tirāviṭar eṉap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷa makkaḷāvar.”
“amerikka cātikaḷiṉ paḻaiyamoḻi oṭṭuccoṟkaḷai uṭaiyatu. coṟkaḷil vēṟupaṭṭiruntapōtum ilakkaṇa amaivil aḵtu intiyamoḻikaḷai ottirukkiṉṟatu. paḻaiya coṟkaḷ maṟaintu pōkap putiya coṟkaḷ pukuntirukkalām. āṉāl moḻi amaippu orē vakaiyāka irukkiṉṟatu. ivviṉattaiyē āppirikka, amerikka,. pacipikkiya, yappāṉiya, kōṟiya, tirāviṭa, tāttāriya, piṉṉiya, turukkiya, pāsk moḻikaḷ cērntaṉa.”
“ārāycciyil amerikka cātikaḷum teṉṉintiyat tamiḻarum cemittiya āriya makkaḷiṉiṉṟum vaḻakkaṅkaḷāl vēṟupaṭṭa oru potukkūṭṭattiliruntu pirintārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu.”3
“pala teyvaṅkaḷuṭaṉ cūriyacantirak kaṭavuḷar tirāviṭa (turūyitiya - Druid)k kaṭavuḷarāvar. papilōṉilum cālaṭiyāvilum cūriyakkaṭavuḷ vēl mārtuk1 eṉappaṭṭār. cantiratteyvam istār.2 av vālayaṅkaḷil naṭaṉamātar iruntārkaḷ. periya kiriyaikaḷ naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. intiya ālayaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum tēvatāci vaḻakku, tirāviṭa vaḻakkaip piṉpaṟṟiyatu. istār ālayattaic cūḻntu cōlaikaḷ iruntaṉa. ak kaṭavuḷiṉ aṭaiyāḷam maramāka iruntatu.”
“pakaṟ (Baal) kaṭavuḷum, palipīṭamum, iṭapamum (cūriyaṉaik kuṟikkum) ciluvai allatu kaṟṟūṇiṉāl kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. cālaṭiyariṉ ki. mu. 6000kkum ki. mu. 5000kkum iṭaippaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ takaṭukaḷil tiruviṭa (Drauvada) tirāpaṭa (Drapada) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ peritum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. cālaṭiyar tamiḻarēyāvar.”3
“tirāviṭa iṭappeyarkaḷ mecapetēmiyā, īrāṉ mutaliya tēcaṅ kaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mittaṉi,4 eṉṉumiṭattil pēcappaṭṭa moḻi ik kālat tirāviṭa moḻiyuṭaṉ mikavum ottuḷḷatu”5
“cōṭs pēṇvūṭ eṉpavar acīriya kampaḷaṅkaḷum intiya kampaḷaṅkaḷum orē vakaiyāyiruttalaik kāṭṭi avarkaḷiṉ cematti yarukkum āriyarukkum muṟpaṭṭa potu uṟpattiyaip paṟṟip piṉ varumāṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār:”
“teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷiṉ amaippum paḻaiya acīriyariṉ kaṭṭiṭa amaippum orēvakaiyākavuḷḷaṉa. acīriyariṉ kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷilum tāmarai moṭṭum malarum pōṉṟa mātirikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. acīriya intiya ciṟpak kalaikaḷukkiṭaiyil atika oṟṟumai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivviru makkaḷum oru potukkūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum pirintavarkaḷātaliṉ ivvoṟṟumai kāṇappaṭalām.”6
“teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷum, cīṉā yappāṉ (Torii) ālayaṅkaḷum orē aṭippaṭaiyiṉ tōṟṟaṅkaḷākum. tiruttamuṟāta piyūciyar (Figians) ekiptiya intiya ālayaṅkaḷiṉ mātiriyaic cariyākap piṉpaṟṟamāṭṭā maiyāl kōpuraṅkaḷiṉṟik kōyilkaḷai amaittirukkiṟārkaḷ.”
“kirēttā (Crete) papilōṉ kiḻakkintiyat tīvukaḷil 7caṅku vātti yaṅkaḷum muttu alaṅkāramum kavaṉikkattakkaṉa. poṉīciyar iv vaḻakkattai maicīṉiyariṭamiruntu peṟṟirukkalām. piṟkālap pirāmaṇa putta vaḻakkaṅkaḷukku aṭippaṭaiyāyirunta tirāviṭa vaḻakkaṅkaḷuḷ itu naṉṟāka vērūṉṟi yiruntatu. taṟkālattil ip paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaic cilar parmā intucīṉā, cīṉā, yappāṉ varaiyum koṇṭuceṉṟaṉar. piṉpu ivai pacipikkiya tīvukaḷukkuk koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭaiciyil ivai amerikkak karaikaḷai aṭaintaṉa.”1
“cintu kiḻakkut tīvukaḷiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟuvataip paṟṟi aiyappāṭu illai. paḻaiya intiya eḻuttu evvāṟu pacipikkut tīvukaḷukkuc ceṉṟatu eṉpatai oruvarum collamuṭiyātu. marakkaṭṭai kaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa kiḻakkuttīvu eḻuttukkaḷiṉ kālam aṟiyappaṭa villai. avarkaḷiṉ eḻuttu muḻuvatum maṟakkappaṭṭatu. itē eḻuttukkaḷ intiya muttiraikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa pōlap paḻaiya cumēriyā, cūcā, taikiracukkut teṟkē uḷḷa karait tīvukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. varalāṟu aṟiyappaṭāta oru makkaḷiṉ nākarikam iṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.”
“cumēriyar, intiya eḻuttukkaḷaic cumēriyāvukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟa intiya viyāpārikaḷiṭamiruntu avaikaḷai aṟintirukkalām. ap paḻaiya eḻuttuk kuṟikaḷiṉ pakutikaḷ kāppāṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivai kaḷuṭ cila varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa cintu eḻuttukkaḷaip pōla eṉakkut tōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa. cintu moḻi pēcappaṭukiṉṟapōtum eḻutappaṭu kiṉṟapōtum ataṉai muḻutum aṟintavarkaḷ cumēriyarkaḷēyāvar.”
“pirāmi eḻuttukkaḷ ellām arappā mokañcatarō eḻuttuk kaḷiliruntu piṟantaṉa. pirāmi eḻuttait teṉ cemattiya poṉīciya eḻuttukkaḷōṭu iṇaitta āciriyarkaḷ ōraḷavil cariyākavē kūṟi yuḷḷārkaḷ. ivaikaḷum arappā mekāñcatarō eḻuttukkaḷiliruntu tōṉṟiṉa eṉpataṟkup pōtiya ātāraṅkaḷ kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa.”2
“cintu eḻuttukkaḷukkum mēṟku āciya, kiṭṭiya kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷiṉ araikuṟaiyāṉa cittira eḻuttukkaḷukkum tōṟṟattil oṟṟumai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. cintu eḻuttukkaḷai ārāyntōr avai cumēriya, ellam, miṉōva, kittaiti eḻuttukkaḷukku oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyaṉa eṉpataik kāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. iv veḻuttukkaḷiṉ aṭippaṭai oṉṟē. ivaikaḷiṉ potu uṟpattikkuriya kālam putiya kaṟkālamākalām. ōr eḻuttaiyē ovvoru vakaiyārum vevvēṟu vakaiyil virutti ceytaṉar.”3
ecci vels (H. G. Wells) eṉṉum āciriyar ulakil iṉṟu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟa makkaḷ ellōrum tōṉṟuvataṟku aṭippaṭaiyiluḷḷōr mattiyataraikkum kiḻakkut tīvukaḷukkum iṭaiyē kiṭanta periya pūkaṇṭattilē tōṉṟip perukiya kapilaniṟa makkaḷeṉṟum, mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷil ātiyil vaḻaṅkiyatu pāskku (Basqu) moḻi eṉṟum, pāsk moḻiyum tirāviṭamum neruṅkiya uṟavuṭaiyaṉaveṉṟum kūṟuvar.
ṭākṭar pāsṭav (Dr. Basedow) āstirēliyā intiyā teṟku āppirikkā eṉṉum nāṭukaḷ taraiyāl iṇaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉak kūṟuvar. avar kūṟuvatu, “uyir nūlār ak kaṇṭattai lemūriyā veṉ ṟum, nila nūlār konvāṉā veṉṟum peyariṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ik kaṇṭa mirunta iṭattilēyē nām āti makkaḷiṉ toṭṭilait tēṭa vēṇṭum,” eṉpatākum.1
iriveṭ (Revert) eṉṉum āciriyar, cumēriya ocēṉiya moḻikaḷ mattiyatarai mutal amerikkā, yappāṉ, tasmēṉiyā varaiyum ceṉṟu in nāṭṭu moḻikaḷai iṇaittaṉa eṉak kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2
ārampakālap pottakam3 eṉṉum nūlil, ekiptiya moḻic coṟ kaḷōṭu oṟṟumaiyuṭaiya pala mayōriya (niyūcīlantu) moḻic coṟkaḷ eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tēyilar (Tailor) eṉṉum pātiriyār mayōriya moḻi tamiḻukku iṉamuṭaiyatātalai naṉku viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
cumēriyarum tamiḻarum
“cēryōṉ mārcalum ṭākṭar hālum cumēriya makkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷait tōṟṟattil ottavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. itu cumēri yarum intiyarum oru toṭarpiluḷḷavarkaḷeṉpatai valiyuṟuttukiṉ ṟatu. irumakkaḷiṉ tōṟṟappolivum, talaiyai alaṅkāram ceyyum muṟaiyum orēvakaiyiṉa. arappāviṟ kiṭaitta kuttuvāḷkaḷ cumēri yariṉ avvakai vāḷai ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. cumēriyar cintu makkaḷiṉ nīraruntum kiṇṇaṅkaḷ orē vakaiyiṉa. cintu makkaḷiṉ uruḷai vaṭi vāṉa veḷḷip pāttiraṅkaḷ cumēriyariṉ kalnārp pāttiraṅkaḷ pōṉ ṟaṉa. irumakkaḷum vācaṉaippoṭi mutaliya mēṉiyalaṅkārap poruḷ kaḷai vaikkap payaṉpaṭuttiya cimiḻkaḷ orēvakaiyiṉa. pūcceṇṭukaḷ pūmālaikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttum vakaiyum, vaḻipāṭṭil mirukaṅkaḷait toṭarpupaṭuttum muṟaiyum iru nāṭukaḷukku mottaṉa. iru nāṭṭavar kaḷum vaṇṭi ceyyum muṟaiyait taṉittaṉi kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷeṉak kūṟa muṭiyātu. iru nāṭukaḷiṉ vīṭṭu amaippukkaḷaiyum, muttirai veṭṭum muṟaiyaiyum, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ cemmaiyaiyum nōkku miṭattu mūvāyiramāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ cintumakkaḷ pāpilōṉiyarai viṭa uyarnilai aṭaintiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu pulappaṭum. itu intiya nākarika kālattiṉ kīḻ ellaiyākum. ataṟku muṟpaṭṭa intiya nākarika kālattiṉ vīṟṟiṉāl papilōṉiya nākarikam tōṟṟiyiruttalkūṭātā? appaṭi yāyiṉ cumēriya makkaḷ cintu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu allatu ataṉ nākarika vāṭai yaippeṟṟa iṉṉoru iṭattiṉiṉṟu ceṉṟavarkaḷākamāṭṭārkaḷā? iṅkuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa iru makkaḷukku miṭaiyiluḷḷa oṟṟumaikaḷ kula oṟṟumaiyaik kāṭṭuvaṉa. cintuvil uḷḷa cilar allatu avarkaḷ eṇṇaṅkaḷiṟ paḻakiya cilar, cilluḷḷa vaṇṭukaḷaiyum kiḷiñcalkaḷai yum maṟṟum cumēriyaratu aḻakiya poruḷkaḷaiyum teṟkup pāpilō ṉukkuk koṇṭupōyiruttal kūṭum.”1
“iṉṟu kāṇappaṭum intiyaṉ oruvaṉuṭaiya mukaveṭṭu āyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ vāḻnta cumēriya makkaḷiṉ vaṭivam pōṉṟatu. ikkāla intiyar kirēkkar ittāliyarkaḷiṭaiyē āriya rallāta makkaḷiṉ cāyal kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. uruvac cilaikaḷiliruntu aṟiyumaḷavil paḻaiya cumēriyar intiyāviluḷḷa tirāviṭa makkaḷai uṭalamaippil ottiruntaṉar. ak kālac cumēriyaṉ tamiḻai allatu atu toṭarpāṉa moḻikaḷai vaḻaṅkum iṉṟaiya oru tirāviṭaṉai mika ottiruntāṉ. cumēriyar kaṭal vaḻiyālum taraivaḻiyālum taikiras yūpirātas paḷḷattākkukaḷai aṭainta intiya makkaḷ eṉpatu irukkak kūṭātataṉṟu. ivarkaḷiṉ nākarikam intiyāvilē cintunatip paḷḷattākku kaḷil vaḷarcciyaṭaintirukkalāmeṉṟu yāṉ eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ.”2
ulaka makkaḷiṉ paḻaiya varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷar ulakiṉ āṅ kāṅku vatiyum makkaḷiṉ uṭaṟkūṟu, moḻi, vaḻipāṭu ākiyaṉa ottirut talai ivvāṟu āṅkāṅkuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivai makkaḷiṉ oru potu uṟpattiyaiyum ciṟappākat tamiḻariṉ paḻamaiyai yum naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa. maṉita iyalār (Anthropologists) ōr ātit tāy tantaiyari ṉiṉṟē ulakamakkaḷ perukiṉārkaḷ eṉa valiyuṟuttuvar.
ulakilē mikap paḻaiya maṉita elumpu yāvātēcattiṟ kiṭaittatu. itaṉ kālam aintulaṭcam āṇṭukaḷ eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē viñ ñāṉikaḷ, pūmiyiṉ mattiyiṟkiṭanta periya kaṇṭattilēyē makkaḷ tōṉṟip perukiṉārkaḷ eṉat tuṇikiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟāyiṉ periya veḷḷapperukku, pūmattiyil viḷaṅkiya kaṇṭattil nikaḻntatākalām eṉak kūṟutal piḻaiyākātu. periya kaṭalkōḷukkup piṉ ciṟiya kaṭal kōḷkaḷ pala nikaḻntaṉavākalām. paḵṟuḷiyāṟu kumarikkōṭu mutaliyavai kaḷaik kaṭalkoṇṭa varalāṟukaḷ ivvakaiyiṉavē. mattiya irēkaiyai aṭutta nāṭukaḷil aṭikkaṭi erimalaik kuḻappaṅkaḷ nērtalum ataṉāl tarai kaṭaluḷ maṟaintupōtalum iyalpu. 1883il 1karakkaṟṟōvāvil nērnta periya erimalaik kuḻappamē itaṟkuc cāṉṟu.
makkaḷiṉ toṭṭil
kīṉ (Keane) eṉṉum āciriyar, maṉita kuṭumpattiṉ toṭṭil malāya āciyā (yāvā maṉitaṉ) vākavirukkalām eṉṟum iṅkiruntu makkaḷ vaṭakku āciyā mēṟku āppirikkā kiḻakku mēṟku ocēṉiya ulakam eṉpavaikaḷukkuc ceṉṟu paraviṉārkaḷeṉṟum ak kālattil tarait toṭarpu iruntateṉṟum piṟkālaṅkaḷil nilaṅkaḷ kaṭaluḷ maṟaintu curuṅki viṭṭaṉaveṉṟum kūṟuvar.
kīt (Keith) eṉṉum āciriyar, pattulaṭcam āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ tōṉṟiya yāvā maṉitaṉiṉiṉṟum vaḷarntu tiruntiyavarkaḷē āsti rēliya makkaḷeṉṟum ak kūṭṭattiṉiṉṟum perukiyavarkaḷē ulaka makkaḷeṉṟum makkaḷiṉ uṟpattit tāṉattaik kuṟitta tamatu ārāycci yiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.2
kirām villiyam eṉpār, intiya nāṭṭilē tolluyirkaḷ vaḷarcciyaṭaintu perukiṉaveṉṟum maṉitaṉum aṅkut tōṉṟit tattaḷittu mēl nōkki vantiruttal kūṭum eṉṟum pukalvar.3
tamiḻ makkaḷ
tamiḻ makkaḷ piṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantu intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiṉār kaḷā, allatu intiyāviṉ pūrvakuṭikaḷā eṉpaṉa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷāl ārāyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āti makkaḷiṉ kiḷaiyiṉarāy ak kālappōkku kaḷilum koḷkaiyilum māṟupaṭātōrāyt toṉṟutoṭṭu 4intiya nāṭṭil vāḻntu koṇṭiruppōrē tamiḻ makkaḷeṉpatu iṟṟai ñāṉṟai varalāṟṟu āciriyar ellōrālum oppukkoḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intiyāviṟ kaṟkālam
ulaka makkaḷ paḻaiya kaṟkālattiliruntu paṭippaṭiyāṉa muṉṉēṟṟam aṭaintirukkiṟārkaḷeṉa makkaḷ nūlār kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. intiya nāṭṭil paḻaiya kaṟkālam mutal ulōka kālam varaiyil makkaḷ kaiyiṉāṟceytu payaṉpaṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷum maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
narumataip paḷḷattākkil aṭi aḻinta vilaṅkiṉ eṉpukaḷōṭu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa paḻaiya kaṟkāla āyutattiṉ kālam 400,000 āṇṭu varaiyil eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.1
ṭākṭar ṭi. terā (Dr. De. Terra) eṉpavar intiya makkaḷiṉ paḻaiya kaṟkālam ki. mu. 30,000-kkum ki. mu. 20,000-kkum iṭaiyil eṉak kaṇakkiṭṭuḷḷār.2
‘vintiya malaiyil paḻaiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ tīṭṭiya cittiraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku vēṭar īṭṭikaḷāl kāṇṭāmirukattait tākkum cittiram oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. av vakaiyāṉa kāṇṭā mirukam ippoḻutu kāṇappaṭavillai. kāymūr (Kaimur) malaiyil māṉai vēṭṭaiyāṭum cittiraṅkaḷum, koslaṅkapāttil oṭṭaic civiṅkikaḷiṉ cittiraṅkaḷum, ciṅkaṉpūril kaṅkāru māṉ kutirai mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ cittiraṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ic cittiraṅkaḷ varaiyap paṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷil putiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. paḻaiya kālattavarkaḷ piṇaṅkaḷaik kāṭṭil eṟintārkaḷ.’
putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ piṇaṅkaḷaip putaittārkaḷ. putiya kaṟkālamakkaḷiṉ elumpukaḷ misrappūril (Mirazapur) miṉukkamāṉa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. kōlāppūr eṉṉum iṭattil 54 putiya kaṟkālac camātikaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷuḷ maṭpāṇ ṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. pallāvarattiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa īmattāḻikaḷaip pōlac ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, nellūr, āṟkāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. palavakaik kaṟcamātikaḷ ceṉṉai, pampāy, maicūr, nicām irācci yaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivaikaḷuḷ irumpu āyutaṅkaḷum kiṭaittaṉa. ak kālattiṟ piṇaṅkaḷaic cuṭutalum uṇṭu. tāḻikaḷil cāmpal kāṇap paṭavillai; uṭalai veṭṭic cila tuṇṭukaḷ tāḻiyil iṭappaṭṭa putiya kaṟkālattil īmattāḻikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. āticcanallūril 114 ‘ēkkar’ nilattil 1,000 tāḻikaḷ kiḷaṟi eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. āticcanallūr tiru nelvēli māvaṭṭattil tāmiraparaṇi āṟṟōrattiluḷḷatu. cila ulōkap poruḷkaḷ illāviṭil avai putiya kaṟkālattaṉavākalām. avaikaḷuḷ irumpāyutaṅkaḷum veṇkala āparaṇaṅkaḷum kāṇap paṭṭaṉa.
pirāmaṇapāttu, cintu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum tāḻip putaiyalkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
takkaṇattilum, teṉṉintiyāvilum paḻaiya kaṟkālap poruḷkaḷ atikam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai pūkōḷak kuṟukku irēkaikku irupat taintu pākaiyiṉ kīḻk kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. akkāla makkaḷ oruvakaip paḷiṅkuk kallāl (Quartzite) āyutañ ceytārkaḷ. ivvakai āyutaṅkaḷ kuṇṭūr, kaṭappā eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷil atikam kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṉum pala iṭaṅkaḷilum paḻaiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ippoḻutu aṭi aḻintupōṉa kāṇṭāmiruka elumpōṭu ‘kuvārṭ caiṭ’ (Quartizite) eṉṉum paḷiṅkuk kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āyuta moṉṟu kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭatu. mircāppūril putiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷ pala kiṭaittaṉa. kaṅkaip paḷḷattākkil kuṉcippūr mākāṇattil mīṉ muḷḷāṟ ceyta tūṇṭiloṉṟu āṟiṭu maṇalmēṭṭil akaḻntu eṭukkappaṭṭatu.
paḻaiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ kaṭappā, ceṉṉaik karaikaḷil māttiram vāḻntārkaḷ. putiya kaṟkālattil avarkaḷ intiyā muḻumaiyilum parantu vāḻa ārampittārkaḷ. avarkaḷuṭaiya āyutaṅkaḷ ciṟiya tīttaṭṭik kaṟkaḷ, avai arai aṅkulam mutal oṉṟu allatu oṉṟarai aṅkula nīḷam varaiyil uḷḷaṉa. amputtalai, villuppiṟai ivai iraṇṭum kalanta vaṭivamuḷḷa kallāyutaṅkaḷ piṭiyil iṟukkip payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. ivai atikamāka vintiyamalai pākel kaṇṭ (Boghel Kand), rēvā (Rewah), mircāppūr malait tāḻvāraṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ vāḻnta otukkiṭaṅkaḷil aṭuppukari, elumpukaḷ, maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uḷi pōṉṟa oruvakai āyutaṅkaḷ cūṭiya nākapuri acām parmā intuc cīṉam malāyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. paḻaiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ teṉṉintiyāviṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āyutaṅkaḷ pāṟaikaḷil tīṭṭik kūrākkap paṭṭaṉa. 10 allatu 14 aṅkulam nīḷamuḷḷa kuṭaiyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ivaikaḷōṭu uyartara maṭpāṇṭa uṭaivukaḷum ceṅkaṟ kaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. intiyāvilē kaṟcamātikaḷil kōppai aṭai yāḷaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
veṇkalak kālam
teṉṉintiyāvil kaṟkālattai aṭuttu veṇkalak kālamum irumpuk kālamum iruntaṉa. takarattaiyum cempaiyuṅ kalantu veṇ kalañ ceyyappaṭṭatu. teṉṉintiya camātikaḷil aḻakiya veṇkalap poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mika mukkiyamāṉa cempup poruḷkaḷ mattiya intiyāvil kaṅkēriyā (Gungeria) eṉṉumiṭattiṟ kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. iṅku 424 cempu āyutaṅkaḷ kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivai ki.mu. 2000 varaiyil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉavākalām eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku 102 veḷḷit takaṭukaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷil cila vaṭṭavaṭivāyuḷḷaṉa. cila takaṭukaḷiṉmītu kompuḷḷa māṭṭut talaikaḷ varaiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. veḷḷi intiyāvil atikam kiṭaiyāmaiyāl atu piṟa nāṭukaḷiliruntu vantirukkalām. cempu intiyāviṟ kiṭaikkak kūṭiyatu. ivaiyallāmal, cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa uḷi, eṟiuḷi, vāḷ, īṭṭittalai mutaliyaṉavum kiṭaittaṉa. ivai kōṉpūr vātelkār (Fatelgarh) maṇipuri muttā eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷilum vaṭa intiyā muḻumai yilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
teṟkil vāḻnta makkaḷē vaṭakkiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉar
ārāycciyāḷar, teṉṉintiyāvil vāḻntukoṇṭirunta paḻaiya kaṟkāla makkaḷē mattiya intiyā kaṅkai yamuṉai veḷikaḷ vaṭa mēṟku intiyā mutal imayamalai varaiyil ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. ki. mu. 4,000 varaiyil intiyā mutal pāracīkam ekiptu kirēttā caipiras varaiyil orē iṉamakkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ.1
arappā, makañcotarō
arappā mokañcotarō kālam intiyāviṉ veṇkalak kāla nākarikattai veḷiyiṭukiṉṟatu. in nakaraṅkaḷiṉ nākarikam ki. mu. 3500 varaiyil eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. nākarikam vaḷarvataṟku iṉi iṭam illai eṉṉumpaṭi akkāla nākarikam uyarnilai aṭaintiruntatu. nakaraṅkaḷ naṉṟāka amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. māṭiyuṭaiya vīṭukaḷ kiṇaṟu kaḷōṭu kūṭiyaṉavākavum, nīr ōṭum kālkaḷ uṭaiyaṉavākavum vītikaḷiṉ iru maruṅkukaḷilum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. makkaḷ payircceykai vāṇikam eṉpavaikaḷāl poruḷīṭṭiṉar. vāṇikam mecapetēmiyā varaiyil naṭaipeṟṟatu. yāṉai oṭṭakam nāy āṭu māṭu mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. villu, ampukaḷ, cempiṉālum veṇkalattiṉālum ceyta kōṭarikaḷ, īṭṭikaḷ, mayir maḻikkuṅkattikaḷ, kattikaḷ mutaliyaṉa payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, yāṉaittantam, kaṇṇāṭi ōṭukaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷāl āparaṇaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. pañciṉāl āṭai neyyappaṭṭatu. maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ aḻakākavum niṟaṅ koṭuttum vaṉaiyappaṭṭaṉa. ōviya eḻuttup pōṉṟa oruvakai eḻuttu muttiraikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāykkaṭavuḷ, cūriyaṉ, pacupati, iṭapam, maram mutaliyaṉa vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.
āriyar varukai
teṟku intiyāvilē mēṟkil tāṉyūp natikkum, kiḻakkil kāspiyaṉ kaṭalukkumiṭaiyē vāḻnta veṇṇiṟamakkaḷ āriyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ mantai mēykkum vāḻkkaippaṭiyai aṭain tiruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ tam mantaikaḷuṭaṉum peṇṭu piḷḷaikaḷuṭaṉum kūṭāraṅkaḷaip peyarttukkoṇṭu iṭamiṭamākat tirivar. ivvāṟu tiriyum mārkkattil ivarkaḷil oru piriviṉar kaipar kaṇavāy vaḻiyāka intiyāvai aṭaintaṉar. itu ki.mu. 2000 varaiyil eṉṟu collappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷ intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟku mūlaikaḷil taṅkic ciṟitu ciṟitāka ēḻu natikaḷ pāykiṉṟa pañcāp tēcam muḻuvataiyum kaippaṟṟi ṉar. āriyar varuṅkāl aṅku uṉṉata nākarikattiṉarāy vāḻntukoṇ ṭirunta tamiḻ makkaḷaippaṟṟi āriyamakkaḷ taṅ kaṭavuḷarai vaḻuttip pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālaṅkaḷil cīrtiruntiya cāti yiṉarai muraṭṭuc cātiyiṉar paṭaiyeṭuttu veṉṟārkaḷ. itu kirēkkarai urōmar veṉṟatum, haṉs (Huns) eṉṉum muraṭṭuc cātiyiṉar āciyā, airōppā mutaliya nāṭukaḷaik kalaṅkac ceytatum pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷi ṉāl naṉku aṟiyalākum.
āriya makkaḷ tammai etirtta tamiḻarai acurar, taittiyar, tācar, nākar eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. vēta pāṭalkaḷāl tācukkaḷaippaṟṟi aṟiyak kiṭappaṉa varumāṟu:
tācukkaḷ nakaraṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉar. an nakaraṅkaḷil aracar vāḻn taṉar. avarkaḷiṭattil tiraṇṭa celvam iruntatu. ac celvam pacu tēr kutirai mutaliyaṉa. ivai nūṟu katavukaḷuḷḷa kōṭṭaikaḷuḷ vaittuk kākkappaṭṭaṉa. tācukkaḷ maitāṉaṅkaḷilum malaikaḷilum cottukkaḷai vaittirunta celvap perumakkaḷ. avarkaḷ poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷai aṇintārkaḷ. tācukkaḷ poṉ veḷḷi irumpuk kōṭṭaikaḷil vāḻntaṉar. intiraṉ taṉṉai vaṇaṅkum tēvōtācarkaḷiṉ poruṭṭu nūṟu kaṟkōṭṭaikaḷilirunta tācarkaḷai veṟṟikoṇṭāṉ. tācukkaḷ āriyariṉ kaṭavuḷarkaḷai ēṟṟukkoḷḷavillai. avarkaḷ mikac cīr tiruttam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. kiruṭṭiṇaṉ eṉṉum tācaṉ taṉ patiṉā yiram vīrarkaḷuṭaṉ intiraṉai etirttāṉ. tācukkaḷil pāṇiyar eṉṉum oru piriviṉar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ. kaṭalvaḻiyākavum taraivaḻiyākavum vāṇipañ ceyta tamiḻnāṭṭu vaṇikar.
ki. mu. 2000kkum ki. mu. 1400kku miṭaiyil āriya makkaḷ pañcāp muḻumaiyum kaippaṟṟiṉar. intiyāvai aṭainta āriyamakkaḷai viṭa aṅku vāḻnta tirāviṭa makkaḷ kūṭiya tokaiyiṉarāyiruntaṉar. āriya makkaḷukkum tamiḻ makkaḷukkumiṭaiyil vivākak kalappukaḷ nērntaṉa. vētapāṭalkaḷaic ceyta muṉivarkaḷ palar-vaciṭṭar, akattiyar, vicuvā mittirar mutaliyōr-tamiḻ marapiṉar eṉpatu avarkaḷ niṟampaṟṟit teriya varukiṉṟatu. varuṇaṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷē acuraṉ eṉappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.1
pi.ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār ‘mantira kālattil intiya makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai’ eṉṉum nūlil kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
paṭai eṭuttuvanta oruciṟu kūṭṭattiṉarāl periya cātimāṟṟam ēṟpaṭṭirukka iṭamillai. tirāviṭa moḻiyaip pēciya makkaḷ vēṇṭiya aḷavu cīrtiruttamuḷḷavarkaḷākavum putiyavarkaḷai viḻuṅkiviṭakkūṭiya tokaiyiṉarākavum iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ. tamatu toḻil vaḷarcci kaḷukkēṟpac ceḻippaṭaintu kiṭanta tam coṟkaḷai avarkaḷukku utaviṉārkaḷ.2
moḻiyaḷavil tamiḻarkaḷ evvaḷavukku āriyarkaḷāṉārkaḷō, avvaḷavukku āriyar koḷkaikaḷil tamiḻarkaḷāṉārkaḷ eṉac cilāṟṟar eṉṉum āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷār. intiya makkaḷiṉ araciyal vāḻkkai varalāṟukaḷ orē vakaiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
vēḷvikaḷ
paḻaiya ulakamakkaḷ ellōrum uyirppaliyiṭṭuk kaṭavuḷarai vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.3 āriyamakkaḷ alaintu tiriyum vāḻkkaippaṭiyil irun tamaiyiṉ avarkaḷ taṅ kaṭavuḷarukku nilaiyāṉa kōyilkaḷ amaittup paliyiṭṭu vaḻipaṭavillai. ceṉṟa ceṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷaik kuṟittup paliyiṭṭaṉar. ituvē maṟṟaiya makkaḷiṉ vēḷvikaḷukkum āriya makkaḷiṉ vēḷvikaḷukkum vēṟṟumai. piṟkāla makkaḷ, vēḷvikaḷ allatu yākaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭuvaṉa āriyamakkaḷukku māttiram uriyaṉa veṉṟu taṭumāṟuvārāyiṉar. āriyar periya irācciyaṅkaḷaik kōliya piṉ iv vēḷvikaḷ mikavum āṭamparamāka naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. ak kālaṅ kaḷil vēḷvikaḷ puriyum kiriyai muṟaikaḷ (pirāmaṇaṅkaḷ) varaiyap paṭṭaṉa. ikkiriyai muṟaikaḷaip payiṉṟu avaṟṟil vallunarāṉōr pirāmaṇar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. piṟkālaṅkaḷil ip peyar, kōyiṟ kurukkaḷ purōkitaraiyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl pirāmaṇar eṉpōr āriyamakkaḷ eṉṉum eṇṇam palar karuttil patintuḷḷatu. iṉṟu teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kāṇum kōyiṟ kurukkaḷum purōkitarum muṟkālattil ekiptiya pāpilōṉiya nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa kurumār pōṉṟavarkaḷē.
pirāmaṇa kālam
ki.mu. 1400kkup piṉ āriyar (kalappu āriyar) kiḻakku nōkkic ceṉṟu aṅkuḷḷa tamiḻar irācciyaṅkaḷai veṟṟi koṇṭaṉar. ak kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ yākaṅkaḷai āṭamparamākac ceyya ārampittaṉar. ataṉ poruṭṭu yākakkiriyai muṟaikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.1 kaṅkai āṟṟu veḷikaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷum āriya makkaḷum vivākak kalappāl oṟṟumai yaṭaintu valuvaṭaintaṉar. pirāmaṇaṅkaḷukkuppiṉ āraṇiyaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. āraṇiyaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa kāṭṭil vācañ ceyyumpōtu paṭittaṟkuriya pāṭalkaḷ. vēta pāṭalkaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattil kāṭṭiṟpōyt tavañceyyum vaḻakkuk kūṟappaṭavillai eṉṟum, iv vaḻakkup pūrva kuṭikaḷiṉ muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi āriya makkaḷāṟ kaik koḷḷappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar.1
pāratam
āriyar kaṅkaic camaveḷikaḷil irācciyaṅkaḷait tāpitta kālattilēyē pāratappōr nikaḻntatu. itu ki. mu. 1300 varaiyil nikaḻntateṉap perumpālum ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. pāratap pōril kalantu koṇṭavarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷē. puṟanāṉūṟṟil tarumaṉaip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pāṭal oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. viyācar, aruccuṉaṉ, turaupati, kiruṭṭiṇaṉ mutalāṉōr kariya niṟattiṉarākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. cila peṇkaḷ poṉṉiṟa muṭaiyavarkaḷākac collappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.2 veṇ ṇiṟamuṭaiyavarāka yārum kūṟappaṭavillai.3
pāratam kālattukkuk kālam eḻutic cērkkappaṭṭa pala pāṭal kaḷuṭaiyatu. itu yavaṉar pallavarkaḷaip paṟṟiyum kūṟukiṉṟatu. alak cāntariṉ paṭaiyeṭuppukkup piṉṉarē intiyar yavaṉaraippaṟṟi aṟin tārkaḷ. itil ki. pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil cērkkappaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷ uṇṭu eṉpatu varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷatu kūṟṟu.
irāmāyaṇam
āriyar kaṅkaic camaveḷikaḷai aṭaintapiṉpu nikaḻntatākak kūṟappaṭum varalāṟē irāmāyaṇam.4 itu ki. mu. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil viḷaṅkiya vāṉmīkarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. irāmāyaṇak katai1 cātakakkataikaḷil vēṟu vakaiyākak kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu. vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇattil irāmar irāmēcuvarattil iliṅkam vaittup pūcitta varalāṟu kāṇappaṭavillai. irāmar kariya niṟattiṉarākak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟār. teṉṉāṭṭu varalāṟoṉṟiṉai eṭuttuc cōṭittu vāṉmīkar irāmāyaṇamāka eḻutiṉārō eṉṟu cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. cītai yiṉ pitāvākak kūṟappaṭum caṉakaṉ ki. mu. 1000 varaiyil viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ic caṉakaṉē yākñavalkiyarukku uṇmai ñāṉattai upatēcittavaṉ. ivaṉ ak kālattil kalviyiṟ ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. in nūlil pala kālaṅkaḷiṟ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷ cērkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vāṉmīki irāmāyaṇat til irāmar viṣṇuviṉ avatāramākak koḷḷappaṭṭilar.
upaniṭata kālam
āriya makkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu kaṭavuḷukku oṉṟaik koṭuttut tamakku vēṇṭiya oṉṟaippeṟum koḷkaiyaḷavil iruntatu. avarkaḷ ulakam uyir kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum mupporuḷ uṇmaikaḷaippaṟṟi aṟiyātirun taṉar.2 maṟupiṟappaippaṟṟi avarkaḷ vētaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭavillai. iṟanta piṉ uyirkaḷ nērē kūṟṟuvaṉulakukkuc ceṉṟu aṅku neṭuka vāḻn tirukkum eṉa avarkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. āriyar intiya nāṭṭai aṭaiva taṉ muṉṉarē tamiḻ makkaḷ mupporuḷ uṇmaikaḷai naṉku ārāyntu aṟintiruntārkaḷ. av vuṇmai ñāṉaṅkaḷiṉ veḷiyīṭākiya upaniṭa taṅkaḷē tamiḻarkaḷatu ñāṉamākum. itaṉai ārāycciyāḷar ellōrum ēṟṟukkoḷḷukiṉṟaṉar.
upaniṭataṅkaḷil ciṟappittuc collappaṭum yākñavalkiyar intiyāviṉ kiḻakkuppākaṅkaḷil vāḻntavarākalām. ivar eḻutiyuḷḷa nūlkaḷ kuru pāñcālam kōcalam vitēkam eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. yākñavalkiyar vāḻnta iṭam ak kālattil kalvit tuṟaiyil uyarntu viḷaṅkiṟṟu. aṟivup poruḷait tēṭi aṅkup palar vantārkaḷ. yākñavalkiyar kalvi kaṟṟapiṉ uṇmai ñāṉattait tēṭi iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum alaintu tirintār. ivarkaḷuṭaṉkūṭat tirintavarkaḷ cuvētakētu aruṇēyaṉ cōmacusma cattiyañāṉi eṉpōr. ivarkaḷ kalviyiṟ ciṟanta caṉakaṉ eṉṉum vitēkanāṭṭu aracaṉaik kaṇṭārkaḷ. avaṉ viṉāviya kēḷvikaḷukku yākñavalkiyar ēṟṟa viṭaiyaḷittār. caṉakaṉ avarukku nūṟu pacukkaḷait tāṉam vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. caṉakaṉ, tēril ēṟic celvataṉ muṉ taṉakkum avarkaḷukkum uṇmaiñāṉa aṟivil evvaḷavu vēṟupāṭu irukkiṟateṉpatai viḷakkiṉāṉ. itu pirāmaṇar kaḷākiya avarkaḷukku avamāṉamāka viruntatu. yākñavalkiyar caṉaka ṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟu avaṉiṭam ariya ñāṉaṅkaḷaip payiṉṟār. avar kālat til avarē periya ñāṉiyāka viḷaṅkiṉār. yākñavalki ceyta1 upaniṭa tamē ñāṉa nūlkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu. yākñavalkiyar kālattil pirāmaṇar cattiriyariṭattil māṇākkarāka amarntu ñāṉam kaṟṟaṉar. caṉakaṉaip pōlavē kāci aracaṉāṉa acantakētu, yaivāli aracaṉ piravākaṉaṉ mutaliyōr viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ.2 yākñavalkiyar kālam ki. mu. 1000 varaiyil eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. ki.mu. 1000 varaiyil vintamalaikku vaṭakkē tamiḻ moḻi vēṟu moḻikaḷākat tirintu vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.3 ākavē, ak kālam tamiḻ ulakattiṉ vaṭakkellai vintiya malaiyāyiruntatu. vintiya malaikku vaṭakku āriyavartta meṉappaṭṭatu.
pēccu vēṟupaṭuvataṉ kāraṇam
vīṭṭiṟ pēcuvatu pōla nām capaikaḷiṟ pēcuvatillai. iv vakai yāṉa pēccukaḷum oru kūṭṭattiṉariṉ pēccai uṇṭākkum (māks mūlar). vēṟṟumoḻic coṟkaḷ vantu oru moḻiyuṭaṉ kalappatālum iru vēṟumoḻikkuriyavarkaḷukkiṭaiyil vivākakkalappu uṇṭākic cantati perukuvatālum moḻikaḷ māṟupaṭum.
moḻikaḷai ilakkaṇa amaippukkoṇṭu aṟiyalām. moḻikaḷ māṟiviṭiṉum ilakkaṇam ilakuvil māṟupaṭātu. ilakkaṇam āṟṟiṉ irumaruṅkumuḷḷa aṇaiyum, coṟkaḷ avaikaḷiṉiṭaiyē ōṭum nīrum pōṉṟaṉa. tamiḻiṉ vaḻimoḻikaḷ tamiḻaic cērntaṉa eṉṟu kāṭṭu vataṟku am moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷaiviṭa ilakkaṇam utavipuri kiṉṟatu. oru moḻiyait tāṅki niṟpatu ilakkaṇam.
ippoḻutu vaḻaṅkum vaṭa intiya moḻikaḷ amaippil tamiḻai ottuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu moḻiyārāycciyāḷar nuvalkiṉṟaṉar. ākavē ak kālattil vaṭamoḻi ik kālam evvāṟu vaḻakkilirukkiṉṟatō avvāṟu ilakkiya moḻiyāka vaḻakkiliruntiruttal vēṇṭum; pēccumoḻi vēṟāka iruntirukka vēṇṭumeṉa yūkikka vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu.
pirākirutam tamiḻ mayamākkappaṭṭatu
āriyar intiyāvai aṭainta kālattil avarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻi paḻaiya pāracīkam pōṉṟa āriyam. itaṟku ilakkaṇa varampu illai. im moḻiyilēyē vēta pāṭalkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
“vēta pāṭal ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattil aṅku ellōrālum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa oru vīṟuṭaiya moḻi iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. am moḻi1 pirākirutattiṉiṉṟum uccarippu muṟaiyil vēṟupaṭṭatu. vēta pāṭalkaḷil pirākiruta uccarippiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭa pala coṟkaḷ āḷappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ic coṟkaḷ maṟṟa moḻiyiṉiṉṟum kaṭaṉ vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataiviṭa vēṟu vakaiyākak kūṟamuṭiyātu”2
“mantira kālattil mūṉṟu iṉamoḻikaḷ intiyāvil vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ vētamoḻiyākiya intu cermaṉiya moḻi oṉṟu. iraṇṭāvatu, vētamoḻiyōṭu amaippil māṟupaṭṭa tirāviṭa moḻi. itu, muṉṉatiṉ amaippu, coṟkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟaip peritum vēṟupaṭuttiṟṟu. āriyamoḻi, intiyāvil vaḷarcci yaṭaintapōtu tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉ valimaiyāl pala māṟutalkaḷaṭaintu tirāviṭa muṟaikaḷait taḻuviṟṟu.”
“tirāviṭa makkaḷ poruḷkaḷukkum kalaikaḷukkum iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷ ellām tamiḻ. avai piṟa moḻiccoṟkaḷalla. ik karuttu iṉṉum naṉku ārāyappaṭavillai. ārāycciyil camakkiruta moḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum kalaiccoṟkaḷ tamiḻiṉiṉṟum iraval vāṅkap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu pulappaṭutal kūṭum. intu cermaṉiya moḻiyākiya pirā kirutam intiyāvil vaḻaṅka ārampittapōtu ataṉ a, e, o eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷ ellām a pōlāyiṉa; ataṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ciṟitu ciṟitākat taṇivupaṭṭaṉa.”1
mantira kālattil āriyar
vintiyattukkut teṟkē kuṭiyēṟavillai
mantirakāla nūlkaḷ ak kālattil viḷaṅkiya nāṟpatu kūṭṭat tiṉaraippaṟṟi māttiram kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avai vintiyamalaikkut teṟkē uḷḷavarkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟavillai. ak kālattil teṉṉintiyāvukkum vaṭa intiyāvukkum pōkkuvarattiruntamai mantiraṅkaḷil muttaip paṟṟik kūṟappaṭutalāl aṟiyalām.2
puttar kālam (ki. mu. 557-477)
upaniṭata ñāṉam vaḷarcciyuṟṟa kālattil puttar tōṉṟiṉār. ivar tirāviṭa marapiṉar.3 ivaratu ñāṉamum upaniṭata ñāṉaṅkaḷai aṭippaṭai yākak koṇṭatu. ivar vētamatattukku māṟāṉa puraṭciyaik koṇṭu vantār. ivar tam upatēcaṅkaḷai makata moḻiyiṟ1 (pāḷi) ceytār. ākavē, puttar kālattil pāḷimoḻi makkaḷiṉ pēccumoḻiyāka irun tiruttalvēṇṭum. pāḷi puttar kālattukku iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷiṉ muṉ tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum. pauttamatam kaliṅkanāṭṭil mikavum vēkamākap paraviṟṟu. ākavē, aṅkup pāḷimoḻi atikam payilap paṭṭatu. ataṉāl aṅku vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻ citaintu vaṭuku allatu teluṅku āyiṟṟu. ilaṅkaip pauttamakkaḷ kaliṅka tēcak kalaikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟiṉar. aṅku vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻum pāḷi moḻik kalappiṉāl ciṅkaḷa māka māṟiṟṟu.2 makkaḷ emmoḻiyaic camaya moḻiyākak koḷḷukiṟār kaḷō, am moḻic coṟkaḷ avarkaḷiṉ moḻiyiṟ kalantu moḻiyai vēṟupaṭutti viṭukiṉṟatu. peruntēvaṉār pāratattiṟ kāṇappaṭum maṇippiravāḷa naṭaiyum itaṟku utāraṇamākum. tamiḻmakkaḷ tamiḻ uṇarcciyiṉāl āriyattai etirttup pōrāṭivantamaiyiṉ, tamiḻ vēṟu moḻiyākac citaintu viṭāmal piḻaittatu āyiṉum, ataṉ ōraṅkaḷ kaṉṉaṭam teluṅku tuḷu malaiyāḷam eṉa māṟupaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟaiya tamiḻ moḻiyil vaṭamoḻik kaṟai atikam ēṟiyiruttalai nām aṟiyalām. puttar kālattiṟkup piṉ kaliṅka nāṭu vaṭuku nāṭāka māṟiyiruttal vēṇṭum. itu ki. mu. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil eṉa uttēcamākak kūṟalām. tolkāppiyam ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭak kellai vaṭavēṅkaṭam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu “centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum” eṉappaṭukiṉṟamaiyāṉum ap paṉṉiru nāṭukaḷum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷukkuṭpaṭa viruntamaiyāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. ākavē tolkāppiyam ki. mu. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭukkuc ciṟitu muṉpiṉ ceyyappaṭṭiruttal kūṭum. “malai tēyntu maṇṇāṅkaṭṭiyāṉatu” eṉpatu nāṭṭup paḻamoḻi. atai oppak kumari mutal imayam varaiyil parantu kiṭanta tamiḻ nāṭṭaip piṟamoḻikaḷ kavarntu koḷḷa iṉṟu tamiḻ intiyak kuṭānāṭṭiṉ ciṟiyavellaiyuḷ māttiram vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. ataṉ ōraṅkaḷaiyum ataṉ vaḻimoḻikaḷākiya teluṅku kaṉṉaṭam hinti mutaliya moḻikaḷ ciṟitu ciṟitākak kavarntu varukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu tirāviṭa moḻikaḷai vaḻaṅkum makkaḷiṉ eṇ 63,000,000 eṉak kaṇak kiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻ vaḻaṅkuvōriṉ eṇ 19,000,000.

iyal - 2
āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar nākarikam
iṉṟu intiya makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum piṟavum paḻaiya tamiḻar nākarikattai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭaṉaveṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. caṅka kālat tamiḻar nākarikam kalappillāta paḻantamiḻ nākarikamēyākum. oru moḻiyiluḷḷa paḻaiya coṟkaḷ am moḻik kuriya makkaḷiṉ paḻaiya nākarikattai naṉku viḷakkuvaṉa. arappā mokañ cotarō eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa paḻam poruḷkaḷaikkoṇṭu aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟaip paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. oru moḻiyiluḷḷa ovvoru paḻaiya collum ov voru paḻamporuḷukku nēr. pārai eṉṉum col kallāl ceyyap paṭṭa kiṇṭum karuviyaik kuṟittatu. ap peyarē iṉṟu irumpu mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa avvakaik karuviyaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. ulōkattaippaṟṟi aṟiya muṉmakkaḷ kallāṟ ceyta pāraiyaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ eṉap pārai eṉṉum col aṟivikkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu paṇṭaiyōr tāmaṟinta poruḷkaḷukkum, ceyalkaḷukkum iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ avarkaḷatu varalāṟṟiṉaik kūṟuvaṉavākiṉṟaṉa. āriyar varukaikku muṉ tamiḻar nākarikam evvāṟiruntateṉpatu im muṟaiyil aṟintu kūṟutal iyaṉṟatu.
nilappirivum makkaṭ pirivum
ivvulakam malaiyum kāṭum vayalum kaṭaṟkaraiyum pālainilamu meṉap palavakai iyaṟkaip pirivukaḷuṭaiyatu. ivvakaip pirivukaḷ tamiḻar vāḻnta 1intiya nāṭṭilum uṇṭu. ovvoru nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷum vevvēṟu vakai vāḻkkai naṭattiṉar. malai kāṭu vayal kaṭal eṉpaṉavum, avai cārnta nilaṅkaḷum kuṟiñci mullai marutam neytal eṉṉum peyarkaḷ peṟṟaṉa. maṇalveḷi pālai eṉappaṭṭatu.
an nilaṅkaḷil vāḻntōr muṟaiyē kuṟavar iṭaiyar uḻavar paratavar maṟavar eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉar. nilam paṟṟiya ip pirivaiyaṉṟi antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar aṭiyōr eṉat toḻiṟ pākupāṭu paṟṟiya piriviṉarum vāḻntārkaḷ. viṉaivalar pala toḻil purivōrā yum, aṭiyōr kālvaḻiyākat toṇṭu purivōrāyu miruntaṉar. aṭimai kaḷai viṟṟu vāṅkum vaḻakkup paṇṭai nāṭkaḷil ellā nāṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭatu. cuntaramūrtti cuvāmikaḷ varalāṟum ariccantiraṉ kataiyum itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷākum. viṉaivalar aṭiyōr eṉṉum iru piriviṉarum kīḻōreṉavum ēṉaiyōr mēlōreṉavum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar.
maṟavar
ivarkaḷ eyiṉar vēṭar puḷiñar eṉavum kūṟappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ alaintu tiriyum tiruntāta vāḻkkaiyar; maṇal veḷikaḷiṉ iṭai yiṭaiyē kiṭakkum paṟṟaikaḷil taṅki vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum, vaḻiccel vōrai vaḻipaṟittum, mullai nilattē ceṉṟu pacukkaḷaik koḷḷai yaṭittum vāḻunar. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ip pālainila makkaḷiṉ koṭiya ceyalkaḷ palavāṟu varuṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaikaḷiṟ kūṟap paṭṭavaikaḷiṉ curukkam varumāṟu: maṟavar cemmaṟikkaṭāviṉ kompu pōṉṟu muṟukkiya mīcaiyum puliyiṉ mīcai pōṉṟa tāṭiyumuṭaiyar; neruppil nīr viṭṭāṟpōṉṟa niṟattiṉar; cerupput toṭṭa kāliṉar; ippiyum cōkiyum kalantu kōttu aṇinta kaḻuttiṉar; muṟukiya caṭai yiṉar; villum ampum koṇṭa kaiyiṉar; vaḻippōkkarai etirppārt tirukkum iyalpiṉar; taṟukaṇmaiyē urukkoṇṭālaṉṉa koṭumaiyar; ivarkaḷ vaḻiccellum vaṇikar kūṭṭattavariṉ talaikaḷaik koytu avarkaḷiṉ vilaiyuyarnta paṇṭaṅkaḷaik koḷḷaiyiṭuvar; ap paṇṭaṅkaḷaic ciṟu vilaikku viṟpar; vaḻipōkkariṉ kaṭṭuc cōṟṟaip paṟittuc cīlai uṟiyiṟ pattirappaṭuttuvar; vaṇikariṭam koḷḷai koṇṭa peṇkaḷaṇiyum mēkalāparaṇaṅkaḷaiyum acōkan taḻaiyaiyum uṭuppar. kaiyiṟpaṭṭa vaḻippōkkarai āṭeṉa niṉaintu kāḷikku veṭṭip paliyiṭuvar.
arampōṉṟa kaṟkaḷuṭaiya pālai nilattē iṟantavarkaḷai iṭṭu mūṭiya kaṟkumpaṅkaḷ pala kiṭakkum. taṭpak kālattum vemmai tōṉṟum avviṭattu vaṟanta cuṉaikaḷum āṟukaḷum kūvalkaḷum kāṇappaṭum.1
maṟavariṉ kuṭil vīṭukaḷ eliyum aṇilum nuḻaiyātapaṭi īntiṉ ōlaikaḷāl vēyappaṭṭirukkum. maṟappeṇkaḷ pāraiyaikkoṇṭu eṟumpup puṟṟukaḷaik kiṇṭip peṟṟa pullariciyai muṟṟattē kiṭakkum nila uraliṟ ṟīṭṭi, kiṇaṟṟu nīrai moṇṭu pāṉaiyil ulaivārttu ākkiya cōṟṟai uppukkaṇṭattōṭu tēkkilaiyil uṇpar.
maṟavar kūṭṭamāka vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷ paṟantalai eṉappaṭum. ik kuṭiyiruppu ūkampullāl vēynta matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭirukkum. talai vāyilil ampuk kaṭṭum paṟaiyum tūṅkum; vāyiliṭattē caṅkiliyiṟ kaṭṭiya, nāy niṟkum. in nilattukkut teyvam kāḷi. koṟṟavai vintai, kāṭukiḻāḷ eṉpavaiyum kāḷiyiṉ maṟupeyarkaḷ. kāḷi eṉpatu kaṟuppi eṉṉum poruḷ tarum peyar. kāḷikku narapaliyum piṟa uyirppaliyu miṭappaṭum.1
kuṟavar
malaiyil vāḻum kuṟavar alaivatum alaiyātiruppatumākiya vāḻkkaiyiṉar. avarkaḷ villum ampum koṇṭu vēṭṭaiyāṭiṉar; vēṭṭaiyāṭik koṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ tōlai uṭuttiṉar. avar makaḷir maravuri taḻai uṭaikaḷai aṇintaṉar. ākavē, maṉṟaṟkālaṅkaḷil maṇa makaṉ maṇamakaḷukkut taḻaikaḷālum pūkkaḷālum kaṭṭiya uṭaikaḷaik koṭukkum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itu malaiyāḷattil muṇṭu koṭukkum vaḻakkamāka iṉṟum nilavukiṉṟatu; maṟṟaiya nāṭukaḷil kūṟai koṭukkum vaḻakkamāka nilaipeṟukiṉṟatu. makaḷir mūṅkilai vārntu kūṭai muṭainta ṉar. it toḻil avarkaḷiṭaiyē iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. avar kaḷiṉ kulateyvam murukaṉ. murukakkaṭavuḷ ātikālam mutal malai uccikaḷil vaittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭār. muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya āṭal vēlaṉ āṭal allatu veṟiyāṭṭu eṉappaṭum. muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya ivvāṭal murukapūcai ceyvōṉāl āṭappaṭṭatu. murukakkaṭavuḷaip pōla vēlaik kaiyil vaittiruttalāl avaṉum vēlaṉ eṉappaṭ ṭāṉ. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭai eṉṉum caṅka nūlil kuṟamātu muruka vaḻipāṭu ceyyum muṟai naṉku kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu varumāṟu:
malaiyiṭattuḷḷa akaṉṟa nakariṭattē kuṟamātu kōḻikkoṭiyai naṭṭu ataṟku neyyum veṇ ciṟu kaṭukum appuvāḷ; vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriya mantiraṅkaḷai uccarittu aḻakiya malarkaḷait tūvuvāḷ; māṟupaṭṭa iraṇṭu niṟamuṭaiya paṭṭāṭaikaḷai uḷḷoṉṟum puṟa moṉṟumāka uṭuppāḷ; civanta nūlaik kāppākak kaiyiṟ kaṭṭuvāḷ; veṇpori kaḷait tūvuvāḷ; irattattōṭu kalanta veḷḷariciyaip paliyāka iṭuvāḷ; cevvalari mālaikaḷaiyum piṟa mālaikaḷaiyum orē aḷavāka naṟukki acaiyumpaṭi tūkkuvāḷ; malaippakkattē uḷḷa ūrkaḷaip paciyum piṇiyum pakaiyum varuttātoḻika eṉa vāḻtti naṟiya tūpaṅ kāṭṭuvāḷ; malaiyiṭattu vīḻkiṉṟa aruviyōṭu vāttiyaṅkaḷ cērntu olikkac civanta malarmālaikaḷait tūvi utiramaḷainta tiṉaiyaip parappi murukakkaṭavuḷ makiḻumpaṭi vaḻipāṭu ceyvāḷ.
murukaṉāṟ kuṟainērnta peṇkaḷukku muruka pūcai ceyyum vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭi āṭṭukkuṭṭiyaip paliyiṭṭu av virattattait toṭṭu avaṉ neṟṟiyiṟ ṟaṭavuvatum vaḻakku.
kuṟiñci nilattaippaṟṟiyum an nilamakkaḷaip paṟṟiyum caṅkanūl kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa cila varumāṟu:-
1malaiyiṭattē palāvum vāḻaiyum ceḻittu vaḷarum. kuṟavariṉ vīṭukaḷ pullāl vēynta ciṟu kuṭicaikaḷ. vīṭṭiṉ muṟṟattē vēṅkai maramum palāvum niṟkum. kuṟavar ammaraṅkaḷiṉ kīḻ iruntu tēṭkaṭuppup pōṉṟa nāṭpaṭṭa kaḷḷaik kuṭittu, cantaṉakkaṭṭai viṟakāṟ cuṭṭa iṟaicciyai yuṇṭu makaḷirōṭu kuravaiyāṭuvar. malai mukaṭukaḷilē tēṉkūṭukaḷ toṅkum. kuṟavar uyarnta mūṅkil ēṇikaḷaic cārtti ēṟi at tēṉ kūṭukaḷai aḻittut tēṉ koḷvar. irākkālattē vēṭṭai yāṭac ceṉṟa kuṟavaṉ pakaṟkālattiṟ pulittōl mītu paṭuttu uṟaṅkuvāṉ. kaḷḷai uṇṭa kuṟavaṉ irākkālattē paraṇmītiruntu tiṉaippuṉattaik kāval kākkak, kuṟatti cantaṉampūciya taṉatu kūntalait tuḷarntu āṟṟik kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭuvāḷ. tiṉaippuṉattai mēyavanta yāṉai av vōcaiyaik kēṭṭu meymaṟantu tūṅkum. tiṉaippuṉattai mēyavarum paṉṟi, kuṟavaṉ paraṇmītu māṭṭiya koḷḷiyaik kaṇṭuniṟkum. kuṟac ciṟumiyar kaiyiṟ caṅkuvaḷaiyaṇintu2 mutukiṟ ṟūṅkumpaṭi kūntalaip piṉṉi viṭuvar; tiṉaimītu viḻum kiḷikaḷait taṭṭai kuḷiri mutaliya karuvikaḷait taṭṭiyum, acōkan taḷirai ōcciyum, āyō eṉac catta miṭṭum ōṭṭuvar; acōkilum tāḻaiyilum kaṭṭiya ūcalkaḷil ēṟiyāṭu var; vēṅkaip pūvaik koytu pulipuliyeṉac cattamiṭuvar. ciṟumikkut teyvattāl nērnta kuṟaiyaip pōkka aṉṉai pūkkaḷaip paliyākat tūvi vīṭṭaik kāval ceytu veṟiyāṭum poruṭṭu muruka pūcai ceyvōṉai vīṭṭukku aḻaippāḷ. palāp paḻattāṟ camaitta kaḷḷaik kuṟattiyar vārkka vārkkak kuṟavar vāṅki uṇṭu makiḻvar. yāṉaikaḷiṉ kompaip peṟutaṟkuk kuṟavar yāṉaikaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭuvar. miṉmiṉi moyttirukkum kaṟaiyāṉ puṟṟaik karaṭi irākkālattē iruntu tōṇṭum; akkāṭci kollaṉ irumpil vēlai ceyvatu pōṉṟatu. yāṉaiyai vēṭṭaiyāṭap puli patuṅkiyirukkum. piṟaipōṉṟa kom puṭaiya paṉṟi paṟaikkaṇ pōṉṟu niṟainta cuṉaiyiṭattē nīraip parukic cēmpiṉ kiḻaṅkai uṇṇum. civanta āramuṭaiya kiḷi, paṟittut tūkka māṭṭātu pōṭṭuc ceṉṟa cāmaik katiraik kōḻikaḷ niṉṟu uṇṇum. muṟṟi viḷaiyumpaṭi kaḷ mūṅkiṟ kuḻāykaḷil vaikkappaṭṭirukkum. kuṟavar kaṭappa marattiṟ koṭikaṭṭi mālai tūkkip palavakai vāttiyaṅ kaḷai olittuk kūttāṭi murukaṉai vaḻipaṭuvar.
vēḷāḷar
āṟṟōraṅkaḷil vāḻntu tāṉiyaṅkaḷai viḷaivittōr vēḷāḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ āṟṟu nīraip payaṉpaṭutti vēḷāṇmai ceytaṉar. āṟṟukkuc cēymaiyil vāḻntōr maḻai nīrai ērikaḷilum kuḷaṅkaḷilum tēkkivaittu ēṟṟam kapilai piḻā iṭā mutaliyavaikaḷāl iṟaittuc cāmai avarai tuvarai mutaliya tāṉiya vakaikaḷaiyum viḷaivittaṉar. ataṟku appāl parutti viḷaintatu. kaṟkālattilēyē intiya makkaḷ parutti āṭai uṭuttārkaḷ. ak kālattil maṟṟaiya nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ tōl kampaḷi caṇal āṭaikaḷai uṭuttiṉar; allatu āṭaiyiṉṟi yiruntaṉar. āṟṟuc camaveḷikaḷil āṭai neytaṟku vēṇṭiya kaṟkālap poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl akkāla makkaḷ āṭai neyvatil tērccipeṟṟiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. avarkaḷ marattiṉāl vīṭukaṭṭi vāḻntārkaḷ; neṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷ marattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vaṇṭiyum marattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. vaṇṭi ural ulakkai toṭṭil mutaliyaṉa ik kālattiṟ pōlavē iruntaṉa. ik kālat taccar payaṉpaṭuttum āyutaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu kallāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavai putiya kaṟkālak kuṭiyiruppukaḷiṟ kaṇṭeṭuk kappaṭṭaṉa. in nilattukkuriya kulateyvam vēntaṉ. ak kāla makkaḷ nalla aracaṉiṉ āḷukaiyil ellā naṉmaikaḷum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu nampiṉārkaḷ. ākavē avarkaḷ ellā naṟkuṇaṅkaḷum amainta aracaṉait teyvamāka vaṇaṅkiṉārkaḷ.1 piṟkālattil vēntaṉ intiraṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. itu tamiḻar āriyar kalappu nērnta piṟkāla vaḻakku. āriyar, intiraṉ tēvarkaḷukku aracaṉ eṉak karutiṉar. vēḷāṇ makkaḷ intiraṉukku viḻākkaḷ ciṟappāka eṭuttaṉar. ivvakai viḻākkaḷaip paṟṟic cilappatikāram, maṇimēkalai mutaliya nūlkaḷ aḻakuṟak kūṟukiṉ ṟaṉa. vēḷāṇ makkaḷ uḻutuṇpōr uḻuvittuṇpōr eṉa iruvakai yiṉar. ivaruḷ uḻuvittuṇpōr vēḷ eṉappaṭṭuk kuṟunila maṉṉa rākavum āṭcipurintaṉar. iv vakuppiṉar aracaṉāl kaṇikkappaṭṭa peru makkaḷāvar.
māṭu orukālattiṟ celvamāka matikkappaṭṭatu. piṉpu tāṉiyam pōṉṟa uṇavuvakaikaḷ celvamāka matikkappaṭṭaṉa. atika uṇavup poruḷkaḷai vaittiruppavaṉē celvaṉāka matikkappaṭṭāṉ. vēḷāṇ makkaḷ atika tāṉiyaṅkaḷai viḷaivittuc celvarāy vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ periya vīṭuvāyilkaḷaiyum amaittaṉar. avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ, ulōkap pāttiraṅkaḷ ērttoḻilukku vēṇṭiya kalappai nukam, koḻu mutaliyaṉa, avar makaḷir aṇiyum āparaṇavakai, uṭukkum āṭaikaḷ ivaiyum ivai pōṉṟavaikaḷum ceytutavum toḻilāḷar marutanilattē vantu taṅkiṉar. piṟa nilaṅkaḷil vāḻvōr tam nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭaikkum paṇṭaṅkaḷait tāṉiyaṅkaḷukkup paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyya vantaṉar. ivvāṟu marutanilam, makkaḷ pōkkuvarat tum āravāramumuṭaiya nakaramāka māṟiṟṟu: iṅkup poḻutu pōkkuk kuriya āṭal, pāṭal, viḻā mutaliyaṉa nikaḻntaṉa, iṉpakkalaikaḷ vaḷarcci yuṟṟaṉa. ovvoru toḻilāḷarum vāḻum vītikaḷuṭaiya nakaraṅkaḷ iṅkē tāṉ tōṉṟiṉa. tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ marutanila makkaḷiṉ uṇṭāṭṭuk kaḷiyāṭṭu nīrāṭṭu viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷaiyellām aḻakākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
paratavar
kaṭaṟkarai makkaḷ mīṉ piṭittum uppu viḷaittum vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ uppuk kaṇṭattaiyum uppaiyum tamakku vēṇṭiya nelluk kum piṟaporuḷkaḷukkum paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ marattaik kuṭaintu kuṭaivu tōṇikaḷaiyum peruṅkaṭaliṟ cellattakunta nāvāy kaḷaiyuñ ceytaṉar. muṉṉar avarkaḷ karaiyai aṭuttut tōṇikaḷiṟ ceṉṟaṉar; piṉpu karuṅkaṭal vaḻiyākat tūratēcaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟu teṉṉintiya āṭaikaḷaiyum maraṅkaḷaiyum piṟanāṭṭup poruḷkaḷukku māṟṟiṉar.1 makaḷir taḻaiyuṭai yuṭuttaṉar. tantaiyar kaṇavar koṇarnta mīṉ iṟāl eṉpavaikaḷaik kāyaviṭṭup puṉṉai niḻalkaḷiliruntu puṭkaḷ oppik kāval kāttaṉar. neytal nilamakkaḷ paṉaic ceṟivukaḷil ciṟu kuṭilkaḷamaittu vāḻntaṉar. avai pullu allatu paṉaiyōlai yāl vēyappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ pūraṇaik kālaṅkaḷil cuṟākkōṭu naṭṭup pūmālai aṇintu kūttāṭi varuṇaṉai vaḻipaṭṭaṉar; talaiyil taḻai kaḷai aṇintaṉar. varuṇaṉ tamiḻar kaṭavuḷeṉṟum ik kaṭavuḷ āriya makkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭateṉṟum muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.1
kaṭaṟkarait tuṟaimukaṅkaḷil periya paṇṭacālaikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaikaḷil arēpiyā, yavaṉa tēcam mutaliyavaikaḷiliruntu vanta paṇṭaṅkaḷ cuṅkaṅkoḷḷum poruṭṭu aṭukkik kiṭantaṉa. piṟa nāṭu kaḷukku aṉuppum poruṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷilum kaḻutaikaḷilum potimāṭu kaḷilum koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa mūṭaikaḷ aracamuttirai kuttappaṭṭup paṇṭacālaiyiṉ iṉṉōriṭattu aṭukkappaṭṭuk kiṭantaṉa. paṇṭacālai yil araca ūḻiyam puriyum kaṇakkarum cēvakarum palar kaṭamai purintaṉar. kaṭaṟkarait tuṟaimukaṅkaḷil vāṇikam vaḷarcciyaṭaintatu. aṅku vaṇikar kūṭiṉar. periya māḷikaikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. vaṇṭikaḷ celvataṟku nalla vītikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭaṉa. māḷikaikaḷ kuṉṟu nimirntatu pōṉṟa uyaramuṭaiyaṉavākavum pala cāḷaraṅkaḷōṭu kūṭiya māṭikaḷum ōṭu kaṟṟai mutaliyavaṟṟāl vēyappaṭātu mūṭu cāntiṭṭu nilāmuṟṟaṅkaḷ viṭap paṭṭaṉavāyumiruntaṉa. avai ‘vēyā māṭam’ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. cāḷaraṅkaḷ māṉiṉ kaṇpōṉṟaṉavākac ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kaṭaliṟ cellum marakkalaṅkaḷ ticaiyai aṟiyumporuṭṭu uyarnta kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkukaḷ vaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivaikaḷ kalaṅkarai viḷakkaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ik kalaṅkarai viḷakkaṅkaḷ alakcāntiriyāvil tālami cōtar1 amaitta veḷicca vīṭṭukkup paṉṉeṭuṅkālam muṟpaṭṭatu. kappalōṭṭum mālumi mīkāṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. mīkāṉ eṉpatē mīkāmaṉ eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkum. caṅka nūlkaḷil neytal nilattaip paṟṟiyum an nilamakkaḷaip paṟṟiyum kāṇappaṭuvaṉa cila varumāṟu:
kuṟac ciṟumiyar puṉṉai niḻaliliruntu karuvāṭṭaik kāval kāttup puḷḷōppuvar. umaṇar erutukaḷ pūṭṭiya vaṇṭikaḷil uppai yēṟṟic ceṉṟu viṟpar. paṉaimaraṅkaḷil irākkālattē aṉṟiṟ paṟavaikaḷ taṅkum. iravil mīṉpiṭikkum paratavariṉ timil viḷakkup pācaṟaiyil uṟaiyum aracaratu yāṉaimukattu neṟṟippaṭṭampōl viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum. nāvāykaḷiṉmītu periya koṭikaḷ acaiyum. kaḻiyiṭattē viḷainta uppai umaṇap peṇkaḷ eṭuttu nellukku uppu eṉa vilai kūṟi viṟpar. paratavar ciṟumiyar uppuk kuppaṅkaḷil ēṟi niṉṟu kaṭalil varum timilkaḷai eṇṇuvar. peṇkaḷ māntaḻai neytal ilai mutaliyavaṟṟai uṭuppar. uppu vāṇikarōṭu avarkaḷ vaḷarkkum mantiyum kūṭac cellum. parattiyar mīṉkaḷai vilaipakarntu celvar. ciṟumiyar kaṭaṟkaraiyil vaṇṭaṟ pāvai ceytum, ciṟṟil puṉaintum ciṟucōṟaṭṭum viḷaiyāṭuvar. paratavar muṟṟattē cuṟāk kiḻitta valai tūṅkum.
kōṉ
araciyal tamiḻariṭaiyē mikap paḻamaipeṟṟatu. kōṉ, ēntal, vēntaṉ, maṉṉaṉ, kuricil, iṟaivaṉ, vaḷḷal, aṇṇal, kuṟunilamaṉṉar mutaliya paḻantamiḻc coṟkaḷē itaṟkuc cāṉṟu. aracaṉ kuṭikaḷai āḷvatu āṭciyeṉappaṭṭatu. āḷ eṉpatu makkaḷ orumai. āṭci eṉpatu iṭaiyaṉ mantaikaḷaik kāppatupōla makkaḷaip pakaivariṉiṉṟum kāppatu eṉapporuḷpaṭṭatu. ēntal eṉpatu pōlavē vēntaṉ eṉpa tum. ēntu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatākalām. aracaṉukkup paḻaiya peyar kō allatu kōṉ. itaṟkup pacukkaḷai mēyppavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. itu, āṭci mutalil mullai nilattil ārampittu vaḷarcci yaṭaintateṉpatai viḷakkum. in nilaimaiyilēyē makkaḷ māṭu pacu eṉappaṭum celvattait tēṭa ārampittārkaḷ. āyar tamatu celva mākiya mantaikaḷuṭaṉ puṟṟaraikaḷiṟ ṟaṅki vāḻavēṇṭiyavarkaḷāyiṉar. maṟṟac celvaṅkaḷpōlallāmal āṭumāṭukaḷ kaṉṟukaḷai īṉṟu perukum iyalpiṉa. āyariṉ putalvar taṅkuṭumpattukkuriya mantai kaḷaip pirittukkoḷḷalām. mēyccal nilaṅkaḷaip pirittal muṭiyātu. mēyccal nilaṅkaḷ ōraḷavukkumēṟ ciṟiyavaiyākkappaṭṭāl avai āṭu māṭukaḷ mēyvataṟkup payaṉpaṭā. ākavē kuṭumpaṅkaḷ oṉṟu cērntu vāḻum muṟai uṇṭāyiṟṟu. kuṭumpattiṉ talaivaṉ atikāra muṭaiyavaṉāṉāṉ. piṉ pala kuṭumpaṅkaḷukku atikāramuṭaiya oruvaṉ talaivaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉ ciṟappākak kōṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. ivvāṟu aracaṉ uṇṭāyiṉāṉ.
aracaṉ vāḻum maṉai kōṭṭai eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉatu atikāram uyara uyarap pōrum vaḷarcciyaṭaintatu. kōṭṭaiyaic cūḻntu matil araṇ ceytatu. ākavē, kōṭṭai, araṇmaṉai eṉappaṭṭatu. matil maṇṇōṭu kēppaik kaḻi kalantu kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. itu ceṅkal matiliṉum valivuṭaiyatu. kōṭṭaiyaic cūḻntu akaḻ, akappā, allatu akaḻi iruntatu. ic coṟkaḷ akaḻ eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantaṉa. uṭu, ōṭai, kayam, parikam, parikai, pāmpuri eṉpaṉavum akaḻiṉ maṟupeyarkaḷ. pāmpiṉ uri pāmpai neruṅki mūṭiyiruppatupōl iruttaliṉ akaḻ pāmpuriyeṉap peyar peṟṟiruttal kūṭum. kōṭṭaiyiṉ vāyil koṭṭi eṉappaṭṭatu. matiliṉ piṉpuṟattē nilattiliruntu mēlē yeḻuntu mēṭaipōliruppatu kottaḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. matiliṉ mītu vīrar maṟaintiruntu ampu eytaṟku amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa maṟaiviṭam ñāyil ēvaṟai eṉappaṭṭatu. araṇmaṉaiyiṟ pala aṟaikaḷ iruntaṉa. tāṉiyakkaḷañciyam kottaṟai eṉavum, aracaṉ vīṟṟirukkum maṇṭapam koluvirukkai eṉavum, āyutaṅkaḷ vaikkum aṟai āyutak koṭṭil eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. koluvirukkai koṭi tukil tōkai pū ilaikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. aracaṉ vaṭṭitta kūrnuti muṭi yaṇintāṉ. aracarum vaḷḷalkaḷum mukattai maḻittiruntārkaḷ. aracaṉ araiyil taṅkap paṭṭikaiyum, kaiyiṟ kaṭakamum, kamakkaṭṭukkum muḻaṅkaikkumiṭaiyil cataṅkaikaḷaiyuṭaiya kaḻalum, kaḻuttil pavaḷa muttu mālaikaḷum, kālil vīracceyal poṟitta vīra taṇṭaiyum, kātiṟ kuṇṭalaṅkaḷum aṇintiruntāṉ. avaṉ koluvirukkum ācaṉam kaṭṭil eṉappaṭṭatu. aracaṉ cāyvataṟku ariyaṇaiyiruntatu. avaṉaic cūḻntu aṭiyār, uḻaiyar, pukaḻ kūṟum akavar, kaṭṭaḷaikaḷai veḷip paṭuttum vaḷḷuvar, aracaṉatu vīram koṭai ākiyavaṟṟaip pāṭum pulavar, aṇṇal, vaḷḷal mutaliya perumakkaḷ iruntaṉar. aracaṉ kuṭimakkaḷiṉ kuṟaikaḷai nēriṟ kēṭṭu muṟai ceytāṉ. avaṉ paricaṉar puṭaicūḻa vīṟṟiruntāṉ. ari, kavari, kuyavu, kūviram, tikiri, vaiyam eṉpaṉa tēriṉ peyarkaḷ. ākavē, ak kālattup palavakait tērkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉṟu terikiṟatu. tērkaḷ yāṉai allatu erutukaḷāl iḻukkappaṭṭaṉa. tēriṉ naṭuppākam taṭṭu allatu nāppaṇ eṉṟum, ataṉ taṭṭup pār eṉṟum, tēraic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa palakaikaḷ kiṭuku eṉṟum, cakkaram ār eṉṟum, tēriṉmītu ēṟutaṟku amaikkappaṭṭa paṭikaḷōṭu kūṭiya mēṭai, muṭṭi allatu pirampu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉa. tēr āṭai kaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. araca varuvāy aracaṉukkuriya nilaṅkaḷāl māttiramaṉṟu. vari, cuṅkam, tiṟai, kappam mutaliyavaikaḷālum vantaṉa.
āṭci mullai nilattiṟṟōṉṟi marutanilattil vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu. ākavē, aracaṉatu nakar perumpālum maruta nilattilēyē iruntatu.
pārppār
ellāt tēcaṅkaḷilum ālayaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷukkup palicelut tum kurumār iruntaṉar. avvakaiyiṉar tamiḻnāṭṭilum iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ pārppār eṉappaṭṭaṉar. kōyil toṭarpāṉa karumaṅkaḷaip pārttaliṉ ippeyar avarkaḷukku iṭappaṭṭirukkalām. kaṭavuḷ vaḻi pāṭṭiṉum piṟavakaiyiṉum ivarkaḷ potumakkaḷukku aṟivu koḷutti vantaṉar. im muṟaiyil ivarkaḷ makkaḷiṉ purōkitarumāṉārkaḷ. purōkitam eṉpatu pura eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatākalām. purōkitaṉākiya pārppāṉē akapporuṭṭuṟaiyil varum pārppaṉap pāṅkaṉāvaṉ. purōkitaṉ aracaṉatu uṟutic cuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ oruvaṉākavum iruntāṉ.
tolkāppiyattil, “nūlē karakam mukkōl maṇaiyē, āyuṅ kālai antaṇark kuriya,” eṉakkūṟappaṭum antaṇar, tuṟavikaḷāy iṭam viṭṭu iṭam cellum tuṟavōrāvar. iṅku nūleṉak kūṟappaṭṭatu pottakak kaṭṭai. itu:
1“pūtiyu maṇṇum pottakak kaṭṭum
māṉuri maṭiyu mantirak kalappaiyum
kāṉeṭu maṇaiyuṅ kaṭṭuṟut tiyātta
kūṟai veḷḷurik kuṇṭikaik kāviṉar” (utayaṇaṉ katai)
eṉpatāl naṉi viḷaṅkum.
kātal
muṟkālap pulavōr kātal, pōr eṉṉum iru tuṟaikaḷilum pāṭalkaḷ puṉaintaṉar. ip pāṭalkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum oḻukkaṅkaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉtoṭṭu varuvaṉa. makaḷirum maintarum oruvarai oruvar virumpik kātalittuk kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyarātalē paṇṭai nāḷ maṉṟal. piṟaraṟiyāvakai cilanāṭ karantoḻukum kātaloḻukkam kaḷa veṉavum, palaraṟiya maṇantu kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyarāy illiruntu nallaṟam naṭattum oḻukkam kaṟpeṉavumpaṭum. kūṭiya kātalar pirintaviṭattu iraṅkal, pirintavar varuvāreṉṟu avarai etirnōkki āṟṟiyiruttal, oruvarōṭu oruvar piṇaṅki ūṭutal eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷaip palavāṟu kaṟpittup paṇṭaip pulavōr pāṭal puṉaintaṉar. malaināṭṭu makaḷirum maintarum ātikālattiṟ kaḷaviṟkūṭip piṉ maṇantu koṇ ṭaṉarātaliṉ puṇartalum atu kāraṇamāyuḷḷa poruḷkaḷum kuṟiñcikku urittākkappaṭṭaṉa. mullai nilattil maṇaṅkaḷ mutiyavarkaḷāl oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. mullainilamakaḷir kālaiyil mantaikaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟa kātalar mālaiyil mīḷum varaiyum avarai etirpārttiruttaliṉ mullaikku iruttal urittākkappaṭṭatu. kaṭaṟkarai makaḷir marakkalaṅ kaḷiṟ ṟolaiviṭañ ceṉṟa kaṇavarai niṉaintu iraṅkiyiruttaliṉ, iraṅkal neytaṟku urittākkappaṭṭatu. poruḷ tēṭutal aracar ēvalai niṟaivēṟṟutal mutaliya ētukkaḷai muṉṉiṭṭuk kātalar pālainila vaḻiyē cellutaliṉ, pālai piritalukku urittākkappaṭṭatu. iv voḻukkaṅkaḷ ellām ellā nilaṅkaḷilum nikaḻattakkaṉa.
maṉṟal
ak kāla maṉṟal purōkitar iṉṟi naṭantaṉa. kaṇi eṉappaṭṭa cōtiṭar kaliyāṇattukkēṟṟa nalla nāḷai ārāyntu kūṟiṉar. caṅka nūl kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum maṉṟal muṟai varumāṟu:
urōkiṇi cantiraṉōṭu kūṭum pūraṇai nāḷil kaliyāṇa mukūrttam vaikkappaṭṭatu. vīṭṭiṉ muṟṟattē kālkaḷ naṭṭu aḻakiya pantal iṭappaṭṭatu; taraiyil āṟṟiṉ veṇmaṇal parappappaṭṭatu. aḻakiya pūmālaikaḷaiyum ilaikaḷaiyum tūkkip pantal alaṅkarikkap paṭṭatu. paruppu iṭṭu ākkiya cōṟṟukkuviyal palar kūṭiyiruntu uṇṭa piṉṉum mikuntu kiṭantatu. periya mēḷaṅkaḷum maṇavāttiyaṅkaḷum olittaṉa. aṟukaṅkiḻaṅkai nūlāṟ cuṟṟic ceyta teyva uruvam vāḻai yilaiyiṉ mītu vaittup pūvāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. viḷakkukaḷ koḷuttappaṭṭaṉa. kaliyāṇattaip pārppataṟkup pūppōṉṟu aḻakiya kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya peṇkaḷ vantu kūṭiṉārkaḷ. putiya nīrkkuṭaṅkaḷait talaiyilum, kaiyil putiya vāyakaṉṟa pālikaic caṭṭikaḷaiyumuṭaiya peṇkaḷ vantārkaḷ. appoḻutu peṇkaḷ āravārañ ceytaṉar. avarkaḷ ap pāttiraṅkaḷai oruvar kaiyiliruntu maṟṟavar kaikku māṟṟiṉar. putalvaraip peṟṟōrum tālitarittōrumākiya aḻakiya nāṉku mutupeṇkaḷ an nīrk kuṭaṅkaḷai vāṅki maṇamakaḷai muḻuk kāṭṭiṉar. maṇamakaḷiṉ kariyakūntal nīruṭaṉ vanta nellālum malarālum maṟaintatu. am mutupeṇṭir “nī koṇṭāṉukkiṉiyaḷāy avaṉaip pēṇi iṉitu vāḻuti” eṉa vāḻttiṉar. maṇamakaḷukku āparaṇaṅkaḷ niṟaiyap pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avvāparaṇap poṟaiyaip poṟukkamāṭṭā maiyāl avaḷukku viyarvai tōṉṟiṟṟu. aḻakiyapeṇkaḷ viciṟi koṇṭu vīciṉar. kuṟṟamillāta mukūrttam vantatu. tamar viraivil vantu kiḻattiyaik kiḻavaṉukku aḷittārkaḷ.
marutanilattē āṭal pāṭalkaḷiṟṟērnta kūttiyar viṟaliyar mutaliyōr vāḻntaṉar. ivarkaḷ parattaimaiyiṉar. ivarkaḷ nimittam marutanilat talaivaṉ, talaiviyarkaḷukkiṭaiyil ūṭal, pulavi mutaliya piṇakkaṅkaḷ nērvatuṇṭu. kaikkiḷai, peruntiṇai eṉṉum iru vakaik kātalkaḷum caṅka nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kaikkiḷai eṉpatu tammāṟ kātalikkappaṭṭa makaḷir tammaik kātaliyātaviṭattu maintar tam kātalai veḷippaṭuttip palakūṟutal. peruntiṇaiyāvatu āṭavaṉ kātaliyātaviṭattu avaṉaik kātalikkum orutti kātal mikuti yāl pala moḻintu karaital. tām kātalitta makaḷir tammaik kātalittu maṇakka uṭaṉpaṭātaviṭattu maintar, paṉaṅkarukkiṉāṟ ceyta maṇi kaṭṭiya kutirai mītu ēṟi āvirampū, erukkampū, elumpu mālaikaḷaic cūṭip, peṇṇiṉ vaṭivai ōlaiyil eḻutik, kaiyiṟpiṭittu (ciṟuvar iḻukka) vītiyil ūrtalum peruntiṇai eṉappaṭum. itu nallatu iṉiyatu eṉap pulavar nāṭṭiya pulaneṟi vaḻakkeṉpa.
pōr
tamiḻnāṭṭu maṉṉar piṟa aracaraik kīḻppaṭuttit tammāṇaiyai mēmpaṭuttaṟ poruṭṭuc cerukkaḷ pala malaintaṉar. ovvōr araca ṉukkum vevvēṟu koṭiyum mālaiyum iruntaṉa. pōrukkuc cellum pōtu vīrar tattam vēntarkkuriya mālaikaḷaiyaṇivar. māṟṟaracariṉ niraikaḷaik kavartalē muṟkāla aracar pōr toṭaṅkum muṟaiyākum. turiyōtaṉaṉ virāṭaṉiṉ niraiyaik kavarntatu pāratattiṟ kūṟappaṭu kiṉṟatu. māṟṟaracar niraiyiṉaik kavaraccellum vīrar tam muṭiyiṭattē veṭcip pūvaic cūṭuvar. ākavē, nirai kavartal veṭciyeṉappaṭṭatu. nirai kavartalaṉṟip pakaiyaracaṉiṉ nilattaik kavartaṟ poruṭṭup paṭai yeṭuttuc cellutal vañciyeṉṟum, kōṭṭaiyai muṟṟukaiyiṭutal uḻiñai eṉṟum, etirttup porutal tumpai eṉṟum, veṟṟipeṟutal vākai eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. avvappōtu vīrar avvap pūkkaḷaic cūṭiya kāraṇattāl av voḻukkaṅkaḷukku ip peyarkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa.
pulavōr aracaṉiṉ pukaḻaippāṭutal pāṭāṇ eṉavum, ulaka nilaiyāmaiyai uṇarum pakuti kāñci eṉavumpaṭṭaṉa. pōr ceyyum nilam, kaḷari, paṟantalai, mutunilam, kaḷam eṉappaṭṭatu. paṭaikaḷ uṇṭai, ōṭṭu, aṇi, tāṉai eṉap pala pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭu iruntaṉa. muṉṉaṇippaṭai akkam, koṭippaṭai, tār, tūci, nirai eṉṟum; piṉṉaṇippaṭai kūḻai eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉa. piṟkālaṅkaḷil tēr, yāṉai, kutirai, kālāḷeṉap paṭaikaḷ nāṉkākap pirikkappaṭṭaṉa.1 kutiraip paṭai āriyar varukaikkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyiruttal kūṭum. mokañcatarō makkaḷ kutiraiyaip paṟṟi aṟintirukkavillai.
yāṉaivīraṉ ampu tuḷaikkamuṭiyāta pulittōṟ kavacam pūṇṭu yāṉaimītu vīṟṟiruntāṉ. yāṉai karuṅkaṭaliṟ cellum nāvāypōla vum, uṭukkūṭṭattiṉ naṭuvē cellum tiṅkaḷaippōlavum ceṉṟatu. ataṉaic cūḻntu pōrmaṟavar cuṟākkūṭṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu ceṉṟaṉar. yāṉaikaḷ naṉṟākap paḻakkappaṭṭuk kantukaḷilum, taṟikaḷilum ālai yiṭattē kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷukku vellattōṭu kalanta arici ūṭṭappaṭṭatu. yāṉaikaḷum, kutiraikaḷum paḻakkappaṭumiṭam ceṇṭu veḷi eṉappaṭṭatu. kuḷaṅkaḷiṟ kuḷippāṭṭiyapiṉ avaikaḷiṉ mukam niṟappoṭikaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭapiṉ neṟṟi, ōṭai eṉṉum neṟṟip paṭṭattāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. tēril iraṇṭu allatu nāṉku kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. tērōṭṭuvōṉ valavaṉ allatu pākaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. tēril palavakai uruvaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. atu palavakai niṟampūciya taṭikaḷālum tuṇikaḷālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉmītu tukiṟkoṭikaḷ acaintaṉa.
vīrar palavakai āyutaṅkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avaṟṟuṭpala kaṟkālantoṭṭu māṟupaṭāmal vantaṉa. avai eṟuḻ, taṇṭu, vil, kokkarai, cilai, taṭi, tavar, kuṇil muṉi mutaliyaṉa. nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa āyutaṅkaḷaik kuṟikkum tamiḻppeyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.1 avai kaiviṭumpaṭai, kaiviṭāppaṭai eṉa iruvakaiyiṉa. vil nāṇil ampu vaitteyyumiṭam uṭuveṉappaṭṭatu. taṟkāppukkuriya āyutam kēṭakam; atu kiṭuku kaṭakam taṭṭu paricai palakai paṟai vaṭṭaṇai paṭṭam tōl tōṟpāram eṉap palavakaiyiṉatu. mey kākkum caṭṭai mēlakam araṇi ācu eṉavum, kaikkavacam kaippuṭai eṉavum aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.
icaik karuvikaḷ
pōrilē yāṉai kutirai kālāḷ mutaliya paṭaikaḷai ūkka mūṭṭum poruṭṭup palavakai vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkappaṭṭaṉa. iyam vāttiyam vācciyam eṉpaṉa icaikkaruvikaḷaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkum potuppeyarkaḷ. avai tōṟkaruvi tuḷaikkaruvi narampukkaruvi miṭaṟṟukkaruvi eṉa nālvakaippaṭum. kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai uḷai iḷi viḷari tāram eṉpaṉa pāṭumiṭattup piṟappikkappaṭum ēḻu tamiḻ icaikaḷākum. ūtappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ kuḻal mutaṉmaiyuṭaiyatu. paṉai āmpal koṉṟai mullai mutaliya palavakaik kuḻalkaḷum iyaṅkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ūtappaṭum periya vāttiyaṅkaḷ kākaḷam, ammiyam, ciṉṉam, kompu, kōṭu, iralai, vayir eṉpaṉa.
narampuk karuvikaḷil mutaṉmaiyuṭaiyatu yāḻ. ovvoru nilattum ovvoru vakai yāḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. nālu mutal patiṉāṉku patiṉēḻu irupattoru narampukaḷaiyuṭaiya yāḻkaḷ vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. ivai kaiviralkaḷiṉāl taṭaviyum teṟittum vācikkappaṭṭaṉa. paḻaiya yāḻkaḷ uruttira vīṇai pōṉṟa vaṭiviṉavāy mēl nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiya ‘kāp’ (Harp) eṉṉum yāḻ pōṉṟu narampu kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu karutalām. ivvakai yāḻkaḷ paḻaiya ekiptil vaḻakkiliruntaṉa. ‘kāp’ pōṉṟa yāḻkaḷ paḻaiya cumēriyarālum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
paṟaikaḷ palavakaiyiṉa.1 taṭāri, uṭukkai mutaliyaṉa pāṇar kaiyil iruntaṉa. taṭṭai, karaṭikai eṉṉum paṟaikaḷ karaṭi kattum ōcaiyaip piṟappittaṉa. pālai kuṟiñci marutam cevvaḻi eṉpaṉa ak kālattu vaḻaṅkiya paṇkaḷ. icai nuṇukkam pēricai ciṟṟicai mutaliya nūlkaḷ tamiḻicaiyilakkaṇaṅkūṟa eḻuntaṉa eṉṟu paḻaiya uraikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. an nūlkaḷ iññāṉṟu iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. aracarum perumakkaḷum vēṭṭaiyāṭumiṭattu mēṟkūṟiya paṟaikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷum makkaḷ kuṭiyiruppukaḷum
aracaṉ vāḻum kōṭṭaiyaic cuṟṟip pēṭṭaikaḷ iruntaṉa. ov voru pēṭṭaiyilum ovvoru toḻil purivōr vāḻntaṉar. ivvāṟu pala kuṭiyiruppukaḷ nakaraic cūḻntiruntaṉa. pēṭṭaikaḷaic cuṟṟi nel vayalkaḷ iruntaṉa. celvapperumakkaḷ vīṭukaḷ māṭaṅkaḷ allatu māḷikaikaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. ivai māṭikaḷuṭaiyaṉavāyc ceṅkalliṉā lum marattiṉālum kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṟappu, iṟai, tāḻvāram, muṉṟil, muṟṟam, nilāmuṟṟam, uttiram, tūlam, cuṟṟuvāri, tāḻ, tuṭai, mukaṭu, viṭaṅkam, tiṇṇai, kuṟaṭu, oṭṭuttiṇṇai mutaliyaṉa vīṭṭiṉ uṟuppu kaḷiṟ cila. vīṭṭiṉ muṉṉē tiṇṇaiyiruntatu. tiṇṇaikku muṉṉāṟ kuṟaṭiruntatu. tiṇṇaikaḷ maṇ kari cāṇi eṉpavai kalantu meḻuki aḻuttañ ceyyum karuviyāl miṉukkappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷukkuk katavuka ḷum nilaikaḷum cāḷaraṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. vīṭukaḷiṉ kaḻivunīr veḷiyē cellac cākkaṭai allatu cālakaṅkaḷiruntaṉa. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷil muk kōṇa vaṭivāṉa māṭāk kuḻikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaikaḷil maṇṇiṉāl allatu ulōkattiṉāṟ ceyta kaiviḷakkukaḷ ēṟṟi vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭṭiṟkup piṉṉāl kiṇaṟu iruntatu. kiṇaṟṟukku appāl kollaiyum, kollaiyil āṭu māṭu niṟkum koṭṭilum, appāl vayalumiruntaṉa.
ural, ulakkai, ammi, tirikai, kuḻavi, āṭṭukkal, muṟam, callaṭai, caṭṭi, pāṉai, akappai, maṉai, kaṭṭuppeṭṭi, kaṭṭil, toṭṭil, ūñcal (vīcupalakai), uṟi (cumaitūkku), kāvaṭi, cummāṭu, kaṇappu (tīccaṭṭi), paraṇ, ñelikōl (neruppuṇṭākkun taṭi), niṟaikōl, kavaṇ, pāy mutaliyaṉa vīṭṭupporuḷkaḷiṟ cila.
vaṟiyōr kuṭicaikaḷilum kuṭilkaḷilum vāḻntaṉar. cuvarkaḷ taṭikaḷai naṭṭu maṇṇai eṟintu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. avai perumpālum vaṭṭamāṉavai. kūraiyiṉ mukaṭṭil kalacam vaikkappaṭṭatu. im muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyē ālayaṅkaḷum kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. maṉṟam eṉṉum perunterukkaḷum, kōṇam eṉṉum kuṟunterukkaḷum iruntaṉa. kuṟiñcinilak kirāmam ciṟukuṭi eṉṟum, mullai nilak kirāmam pāṭi, cēri eṉṟum, maruta nilak kirāmam ūr eṉṟum, neytal nilak kirāmam pākkam eṉṟum, pālainilak kirāmam paṟantalai eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. avai kaḷil kīḻōrum mēlōrum vāḻntaṉar.
uṭai
parutti intiyāvil viḷaivatu. pañciliruntu nūlum nūli liruntu āṭaiyum neyyappaṭṭaṉa. nīḷamāka neyyappaṭṭa āṭaikaḷ tuṇṭukaḷākak kiḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē aṟuvai, tuṇṭu, tuṇi eṉpaṉa āṭaiyiṉ peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkiṉa. paṭṭu, mayir eṉpavaikaḷālum āṭai neyyappaṭṭatu. nūliḻaikaḷ ceṉṟa iṭamē teriyāta melliya āṭaikaḷ akkāla makkaḷāl aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa. palavakai āṭaikaḷum uṭaikaḷum akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉpataṟku avaikaḷaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷē pōtiya cāṉṟu.1 puṭaivaikaḷukkup palavakaic cāyaṅkaḷ tōykkappaṭṭaṉa. karampu, paṇi, paraṇam, pāḷitam eṉpaṉa paṭ ṭāṭaikaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ. kampaḷiyiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa āṭaikaḷ mayirakam, uyirakam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. āṭaikaḷ aḻakiya karaikaḷum, pū vēlaippāṭukaḷum, kaṇkavar vaṉappum uṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. āṭa varum makaḷirum kiḻittut taikkappaṭāta uṭaikaḷaiyē payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ūci, iḻai, vaṅki, caṉṉal, taiyal mutaliya coṟkaḷāl ak kālattavar taiyaṟṟoḻilai aṟiyār eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. iṟavukkai eṉpatu iraṇ ṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ tamiḻ aracarukku meykāppāḷarāka amarn tirunta kirēkka urōmaṉ aṇaṅkukaḷāl tamiḻnāṭṭil pukuttappaṭṭatu. ēvalāṭkaḷum vīrarum caṭṭai iṭṭaṉar. kuppāyam taippai meyppai eṉpaṉa caṭṭaiyiṉ peyarkaḷ. makkaḷ (talaip) pākai, kuṭai, ceruppu, mitiyaṭi mutaliyavaikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
āṭai āparaṇa alaṅkāram
