(Shin-toism) eṉṉum yappāṉiya matattil civaliṅkam mutaṉmaiyāṉatu. amerikkāviṉ pala pakutikaḷil ciṟappāka meccikkō, pēru, kaiti tīvu (Hyti)kaḷil civaliṅkam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. spāṉiyar mutal mutal amerikkāvukkuc ceṉṟapōtu ālayaṅkaḷil civaliṅkaṅkaḷ vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭataik kaṇṭārkaḷ. āppirikkāvil takōmi (Dahomi) eṉṉum iṭattil iliṅkam leṅpā (Lengba) eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.
“iliṅka vaṇakkam acīriyāviṟṟōṉṟi ayal nāṭukaḷiṟ paraviṟṟu eṉṟu cilar niṉaikkiṟārkaḷ. kirēkka nāṭṭil pakkas (Bacchus) tayōṉicas (Dionysus) vaḻipāṭukaḷ toṭarpākac civaliṅkam vaḻipaṭap paṭṭatu. kirīcil pakkas viḻāvil iliṅkam vītikaḷ vaḻiyē ulāk koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. ekiptiyariṉ ōsṟis eṉṉum teyvam pakkas eṉpataṉ iṉṉoru vēṟupāṭē. pakkas vaḻipāṭu ittāliyil mikavum parantiruntatu. pakkas kepaṉ (Hebin) allatu kampāṉiyā (Campania) eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. kepaṉ kepi (Hebe - kauri) eṉṉum tēviyuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. pakkacukku iṭapamum puliyum mikap piriyamāṉavai. avar 1pulittōlai yuṭuttuk kaiyil tiricūlattaiyum vaittiruntār. ātalāl kepaṉ civaṉ eṉṟum, kepi kauri eṉṟum kūṟalām. pakkas kaṭavuḷaip piṉtoṭarntu iṭapamum puliyum ceṉṟaṉa. avar kaiyil aruntum maṇṭaiyum iruntatu. ciṉṉa āciyāvil iliṅkakkaṭavuḷ kemōs (Chemos) molōc (Moloch) meratok (Merodock) ātōṉiyis (Adonais) capāciyas (Sabazius) pakkas allatu pākēōs (Bagaios) eṉṉum peyarkaḷuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. ves top (Westropp) eṉpavar atōṉicas eṉpatu arttanārīcuvarar eṉṟu ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. ciṉṉāṭkaḷiṉmuṉ ekiptilē ‘turkkammā’ eṉa aṭiyil eḻutiya oru turkkai uruvam oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu.”
ṟoṭēciyāvil (āppirikkā) kāṇappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷilum ciriyāviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷilum pūmālaikaḷ veṭṭappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. poṉīciya makkaḷ tayar nakarilē pakalavaṉ ālayattil marakatat tiṉālum poṉṉiṉālum ceyta iliṅkaṅkaḷai vaittu vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ. ivvakai iliṅkaṅkaḷai avarkaḷ cipiṟālṭaril nāṭṭiyiruntamaiyiṉ avviṭam iṉṟum “kekkuviciṉ tūṇkaḷ” eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷiṟ1 pēḻaikaḷ (peṭṭi) vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟu ilaṅkaiyiluḷḷa katirkāmam eṉṉum ālayattil vaḻipaṭappaṭu vatum pēḻaiyē. mēṟku āciya nāṭṭup pēḻaikaḷuḷ ciṟiya iliṅkam vaikkappaṭṭiruntatu. nilaipōla iraṇṭu kaṟkaḷai niṟutti mēl oru pāvu kallai vaittu mūṭiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṉkīḻ (Dolmen) vaittu iliṅkaṅkaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. itiliruntē pēḻaiyil iliṅkattai vaittu vaḻipaṭum muṟai ārampittatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. viviliya maṟaiyil pēḻaiyil vaikkappaṭṭa cāṭcip pattiram eṉpatu nammavariṉ “iyantirat takaṭu” eṉpatai ottatē.
iliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉ vaḷarcci
kaṭavuḷ oruvarē uḷar eṉa uṇarnta makkaḷ ammai apparkaḷiṉ aruṭkuṟikaḷē iṟaivaṉiṉ āṇ taṉmai peṇ taṉmai eṉpavai kaḷai uṇarttum eṉak koṇṭaṉar. āṇ taṉmai peṇ taṉmaikaḷai viḷakkum aṟikuṟiyāka avarkaḷ āṇkuṟi peṇkuṟi vaṭivāṉa aṭai yāḷaṅkaḷaiyum vaittu vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. piṉpu iv viraṇṭum oṉṟu cērkkappaṭṭuk kaṭavuḷ āṇ peṇ iyalpiṉar eṉṉum ilappiṉum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. iv vaṭivaṅkaḷ elōkim eṉappaṭṭaṉa. mokeñ cotarōvilum āṇ kaṭavuḷaik kuṟikka āṇkuṟiyum, peṇ kaṭavuḷaik kuṟikkap peṇ kuṟiyum vaḻaṅkiṉa.2 elōkim vaḻipāṭē intiyāvil āvuṭaiyār vaḻipāṭākum. paṇṭaiya iliṅkaṅkaḷ tūṇ vaṭiviṉa. āṇ peṇkuṟi vaṭivukaḷ airōppāvilē kiṟittava ālayaṅ kaḷil iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.3 erucalēmilē oru kālattil ov voru vītiyilum iliṅkaṅkaḷ vaittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.1 ñāyiṟṟaiyum tiṅkaḷaiyum oppat tīyum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iliṅkam tīyiṉ vaṭivākavum koṇṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.
tī vaḻipāṭu
ñāyiṟṟaiyum tiṅkaḷaiyum oppa oḷiyuṭaip poruḷākiya tīyum makkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. viviliya maṟaiyiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭil nim ṟoṭ eṉṉum aracaṉ tīvaḻipāṭṭaip palastīṉ nāṭṭil ārampittāṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaḻipāṭu iṉṉum pāracīkariṭaiyē corāsriya matak koḷkaiyāka nilavukiṉṟatu. tīyum iliṅka vaṭivil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. pīṭattōṭu kūṭiya iliṅkattukkut tīkkaṭai kōlai oppiṭṭut, tīk kaṭaikōlaiyē iliṅka vaṭivamāka amaittu makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ eṉac cila ārāycciyāḷar karutiṉar. tamiḻ makkaḷiṭaiyē muccuṭar vaḻi pāṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. tīyai enta nēramum uṇṭākki vaḻipaṭutal kūṭum. ñāyiṟṟaiyum tiṅkaḷaiyum enta nērattilum kaṇṭu vaḻipaṭutal aritu. ākavē avarkaḷ ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṉum muccuṭarkaḷiṉ vaṭivaik kuṟikkum vaṭṭam, vil allatu piṟai, mukkōṇam vaṭivāṉa kuṇṭaṅ kaḷai amaittu avaikaḷil tī vaḷarttuk kaṭavuḷai vaḻipaṭuvārāyiṉar. ituvē muttī vēṭṭal eṉṉum vaḻakku. āriya makkaḷiṉ tī vaḻipāṭu iv vuṇmaikaḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu eḻuntataṉṟu. avarkaḷ kālaiyil eḻuntu tōyntu, tīmūṭṭi erittuk kuḷir kāyntu ataṉ pakkattē niṉṟu kaṭavuḷait tutittaṉar. iv vaḻakkiliruntē avarkaḷatu akkiṉikōttiram eṉappaṭum tī vaḻipāṭu eḻuntatu. caṉakaṉ yākñaval kiyaiyum avaruṭaṉ niṉṟa pirāmaṇaraiyum nōkki nīvir akkiṉikōt tiram eppaṭic ceykiṟīrkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭapōtu avarkaḷ cariyāṉa viṭai aḷikkavillai. itaṟkuk kāraṇam āriyaratu tī vaḻipāṭum tamiḻaratu tī vaḻipāṭum vevvēṟu vakaiyiṉavātalē. ākamaṅkaḷum vētaṅkaḷum nērmāṟāṉavai eṉpatu ārāycciyāḷar muṭivu. tī vaḻipāṭu turāṉiya makkaḷukkuriyateṉṟē varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.2
“koṭinilai kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟa
vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ mutalaṉa mūṉṟum
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē” (tol. puṟa. 33)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭum vaḻipāṭukaḷ muṟaiyē ñāyiṟu tī, tiṅkaḷkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭukaḷē. paḻaṅkālap palipīṭaṅkaḷum piṟkālam iliṅkamāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa.1 tiruvaṇṇāmalaiyil iṟaivaṉ cōti vaṭivāy niṉṟāṉeṉpatu iliṅkavaḻipāṭu tī vaḻipāṭṭiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi yatu eṉṉum aitīkattaik kuṟippatu. muṟkālattu oṭuṅkiya koṭumuṭi kaḷuṭaṉ nimirntu vaḷarnta malaikaḷ iliṅkaṅkaḷāka vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa.2
vēntaṉ
makkaḷ ñāyiṟṟait taṉipperuṅ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭa kālattil aracaṉ ñāyiṟṟiṉ 3putalvaṉeṉṟum iṟantapiṉ avaṉ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷāka māṟukiṟāṉ eṉṟum makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.4 ākavē aracaṉiṉ cāmātiyiṉmītu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷiṉ aruṭkuṟiyāka iliṅkam naṭap paṭṭatu. av viṭaṅkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭukkuriya ālayaṅkaḷākavum iruntaṉa. iv vakai ālayaṅkaḷ pala ekiptil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavu ḷāka māṟiya aracarkaḷ ekiptil iṟā (iravi) eṉṉum peyar peṟṟaṉar. ṟā eṉpatiliruntu irācaṉ eṉṉum col piṟantateṉac cilar kūṟuvar.
nantivaḻipāṭu
appaṉ eṉṉum tantaik kaṭavuḷiṉ ūrtiyākiya nanti mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkuriyatāyiruntatu. palastīṉ nāṭṭil āṉkaṉṟu allatu nanti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭamai kiṟittava maṟaiyiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāvītiṉ (David) maṉaivikkē āṉkaṉṟu eṉṟu peyar.1 kitaiti nāṭṭilē eyuk (Eyuk) eṉṉumiṭattilē nantic cilai karuṅkaṟ pīṭattil vaittu makkaḷ ellōrālum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iv viṭapak kaṭavuḷaic cēvikkat taṉikkuru iruntār. ekiptiya makkaḷ ōciṟis kaṭavuḷiṉ vākaṉamākaya apis eṉṉum iṭapattai vaḻipaṭ ṭārkaḷ. ellā mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷālum iṭapam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. iṭapat tiṉmītu cattiyañ ceyvatai makkaḷ peritum añciṉārkaḷ.2
mukkaṭ ceylavar
makkaḷ, ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ tī eṉpavaikaḷukku mēlāṉa taṉip perumporuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉṟu koṇṭa kālattil avarkaḷ paḻaiya appaṉ vaṭivattukku muccuṭarkaḷaik kaṇkaḷ eṉak koṇṭaṉar. āṅkāṅku makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa pēykaḷ avariṉ parivāraṅkaḷāyiṉa. ulaka makkaḷellārālum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa pāmpu avariṉ āparaṇaṅkaḷāyiṉa; payaṅkaramāṉa tāyk kaṭavuḷ ippoḻutu cānta vaṭiviṉaḷāy avariṉ pāriyāyiṉaḷ. umai, eññāṉṟum kaṉṉi eṉappaṭṭār.3 mokañcota rōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa civaṉ tiruvuruvattukku mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.4
mātorupāti iṟaivaṉ
ammai appark kaṭavuḷar, pāti āṇ, pāti peṇ vaṭivilum vaittu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭaṉar. iv vaṭivu “artta nārīcurar” vaṭivam eṉap paṭum. arttanārīcura vaṭivamē ātōṉis eṉa mēṟkutticai nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉa muṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu. iv vaṭivam paḻaiya cumēriya makka ḷālum vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu.5 mokañcotarō makkaḷ apparai āṇ eṉṟum ammaiyai ammai eṉṟumē vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. cumēriyā nāṭṭi lum itē peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ammai appariṉ putalvar cumēriyarāl eṉlil eṉappaṭṭār. mokañcotarōvil ammai mīṉkaṇṇi eṉappaṭṭār. ippeyarē mīṉāṭci eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
pāmpu vaṇakkam
iv vulakiṉ ellāp pākaṅkaḷilum pāmpu vaṇaṅkappaṭṭatu. mocē veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyta pāmpai isravēlar vaṇaṅki vanta varalāṟu, kiṟittuva maṟaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. kiṟittuva maṟaiyiṟ kāṇappaṭum 
paṭaippu varalāṟu mutaliyaṉa cālaṭiya makkaḷiṭamiruntu kiṭaittaṉa eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cālaṭiya makkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷē eṉpatu varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷatu muṭivu. pāmpu vaṇakkam illāta nāṭu iv vulakil illai. itaṉai ārāyntu viḷakkum taṉi nūlkaḷ pala āṅkila moḻiyil eḻutap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟum cila nāṭukaḷil uyirp pāmpukaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa; cila nāṭukaḷil pāmpiṉ cilaikaḷ vaṇaṅkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
“āppirikka makkaḷiṉ mukkiya teyvam pāmpu. maḻaiyiṉmai, pañcam, piṇi mutaliya kālaṅkaḷil makkaḷ pāmpukaḷaic ciṟappāka vaḻipaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. iv vakaiyāka urōmarum orukālattiṟ ceytaṉar. oru nikirō vaṉāvatu vēṇṭumeṉṟu pāmpukku oruvakaiyāṉa tīmaiyuñ ceyyamāṭ ṭāṉ. taṟceyalāyp pāmpu oṉṟaik kāyappaṭuttukiṟavaṉ niccayamākak kollappaṭuvāṉ. orumuṟai āṅkilak kappaṟkārar cilar tāṅkaḷ taṅkiya viṭṭiṉuḷ vanta pāmpu oṉṟai aṭittukkoṉṟārkaḷ. ataṟkāka ivarkaḷ aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷāl koṭumaiyākat tākkik kollappaṭ ṭārkaḷ. pāmpukaḷ uṟaivataṟkeṉak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kuṭicaikaḷ nāṭu eṅkum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷukku mutiya peṇkaḷ tiṉamum uṇavu koṭukkiṟārkaḷ. pāmpukaḷukku aḻakiya periya kōyilkaḷum irukkiṉ ṟaṉa. ovvoru vakaip pāmpaik kavaṉippataṟkum ovvoru vēlaik kāraṉ irukkiṟāṉ.”
“niyukiṉiyil cōlaikaḷil pāmpukaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ iruk kiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷukkup paruva kālaṅkaḷukkēṟṟavāṟu paṉṟi, āṭu, kōḻi mutaliyaṉa uṇavāka aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa”1 itu pāmpu vaṇakkattiṟku ōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu.
ivvaṇakkam evvāṟu uṇṭāṉateṉṟu varalāṟṟu āciriyar kaḷāl naṉku aṟintu kūṟa muṭiyavillai. itaṉ toṭakkam maṟaivāka irukkiṉṟatu. ulakil kāṇappaṭum makkaṭ kūṭṭattiṉar tattam kulakkuṟi kaḷāka (Totem) mayil, āṭu, muyal, āmai pōṉṟa yātēṉum oṉṟaik koṇṭiruntaṉar; iṉṟum koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ik kulakkuṟikaḷ piṟpāṭu vaḻipaṭavumpaṭṭaṉa. iv vakaik kāraṇattaip pāmpu vaṇakkat tiṟkum koḷḷalāmō eṉpatu ārāyattakkatu. intiya nāṭṭil pāmpu vaḻipāṭu naṭaipeṟṟu varuvataik kūṟavēṇṭiyatillai. pāmpuc cilaikaḷ illāta ālayaṅkaḷē illai eṉak kūṟalām.1
muruka vaḻipāṭu
muruka vaḻipāṭu ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkattiṉ iṉṉoru vakai. kaṭalmīteḻum utayakāla ñāyiṟu,2 mayilmīteḻuntaruḷum murukaṉāka uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭaṭuḷḷatu. murukaṉ eṉpataṟku iḷaiyavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷ. yūtamakkaḷum pakaṟ kaṭavuḷai mutiyapakal, iḷampakal (Ba-al) eṉa iruvakaiyāka vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. mutiya pakal tāṭiyuṭaiya mutiya vaṭivuṭaṉum, iḷampakal kuḻantai vaṭivuṭaṉum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. yūta makkaḷukkuc camaya aṟivu koḷuttiyavarkaḷ tamiḻ marapaic cērntōr eṉappaṭukiṉṟamaiyiṉ avarkaḷiṭaiyē tamiḻarkaḷuṭ kāṇappaṭum pala koḷkaikaḷ nilaviṉa. iḷam kaṭavuḷ yūtarāl pōrk kaṭavuḷākavum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. iḷampakalē piṉ urōmariṉ mārs eṉṉum pōrk kaṭavuḷākavum māṟiṟṟu. murukakkaṭavuḷukkum acurar eṉappaṭṭa makka ḷukkum iṭaiyil nikaḻnta pōrkaḷ eṉpaṉa muruka vaḻipāṭṭiṉarukkum civavaḻipāṭṭiṉarukkum nikaḻnta pōrkaḷākum. acurar civavaḻipāṭṭiṉar eṉpatu muṉṉamē kūṟappaṭṭatu. yekōvā eṉpatu el kaṭavuḷaiyē kuṟikkum peyareṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. yekōvā vaḻipāṭ ṭiṉar elliṉ vēṟuvakai vaḻipāṭukaḷākiya pakal (Ba-al) moloc mutaliya vaḻipāṭukaḷai aḻikka muṉaintu niṉṟa varalāṟṟuṭaṉ itaṉai oppu nōkkuka. makkaḷiṭaiyē nikaḻnta pōrkaḷ makkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa tey vaṅkaḷmītu ēṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. vēta pāṭalkaḷ, intiraṉ tācukkaḷiṉ celvaṅkaḷaik koḷḷaikoṇṭu avarkaḷai aḻittāṉeṉṟu kūṟu kiṉṟaṉa. āriya makkaḷ tām peṟṟa veṟṟikaḷait tām vaḻipaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ mītu ēṟṟik kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. ivvāṟu kūṟutal akkāla marapupōlum.
māl vaṇakkam
māl (mā+1el) vaṇakkamum ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkattiṉ iṉṉoru vakai. tirumāliṉ kaiyil irukkum cakkaram ñāyiṟṟaiyē kuṟikkiṉṟatu. “ariyallāṟ ṟēviyillai aiyaṉaiyāṟaṉārkkē”. perumāḷ (peru māṉiṉ peṇpāḵ) eṉa varuvaṉa ārāyattakkaṉa. orupōtu kariya tāyk kaṭavuḷ māel eṉa vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatō eṉpatu cintikkattakkatu. pārata kālattukkup piṉ tirumāl vaḻipāṭu kaṇṇaṉ vaḻipāṭāka māṟiyuḷḷatu. tirumāl mūvulakai aḷantāreṉa varum purāṇakkatai ñāyiṟu kālai, naṇpakal, mālai eṉṉum mūṉṟu kālaṅkaḷil cellum celaviṉai uṇarttumeṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
civaṉ
ñāyiṟē civaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl ātiyil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa teṉpatu ulakamakkaḷiṉ paḻaiya varalāṟukaḷai nōkkumiṭattu naṉku pulaṉākum.2 “arukkaṉ pātam vaṇaṅkuvar antiyil-arukkaṉāvāṉ araṉuruvallaṉō” (tirunā-tē) eṉpatum itaṉaiyē uṇarttum. ñāyiṟu, āṇṭiṉ ovvoru mātattilum ovvoru vīṭṭil taṅkum. ovvoru vīṭṭil niṟkumpōtum atu vevvēṟu vaṭivil vaḻipaṭap paṭṭatu.3 ākavē, ovvoru tiṅkaḷum ovvoru kaṭavuḷukku urittākkappaṭṭiruntatu. iv vaḻakku mēṟku āciya ekiptiya intiya nāṭukaḷukkellām potu. epirēya makkaḷiṉ āṇṭiṉ mūṉṟāvatu mātam civaṉ eṉa aṟiyappaṭṭatu.1 āciyāvilum, pāpilōṉilum āṇ ṭiṉ mūṉṟām mātam civaṉ allatu cimāṉu eṉappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉil itē peyaruṭaiya iṭam oṉṟum iruntatu.
iḵtu aṅkē kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷil eḻutivaikkappaṭṭa uṟuti (Documents) kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.2 itaṉāl pāpilōṉ āciyā pālastīṉa nāṭukaḷil civaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ oṉṟu iruntateṉpatu teḷḷitiṟ pulappaṭum. arēpiyarum yūtarum cālatiyāvi luḷḷa ūr eṉṉum nakariṉiṉṟum ceṉṟu kāṉāṉ tēcattiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya āpirakāmiṉ (ki. mu. 2153) cantatiyiṉar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ākavē yūta makkaḷ cālatiyaraip piṉpaṟṟiyē tiṅkaḷ peyarkaḷaiyum vaḻaṅki ṉārkaḷātal vēṇṭum. cālatiyāvil ip peyar cālap paḻaiyatāy vaḻaṅkiṉamaiyiṉālēyē atu tiṅkaḷukkup peyarākkappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉ makkaḷum pālastīṉarum elcaṭai eṉa vaḻaṅkiya kaṭavuḷ, civaṉaip pōlavē malaimukaṭukaḷiliruppavarākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.3 kiṟittuva maṟaiyiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭu ki.mu. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum,
ki.mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭaiyil eḻutappaṭṭatu.4 itil, mocē eṉpavar, yekōvā aruḷiya pattuk kaṭṭaḷaikaḷaic ciṉāy malaimītu niṉṟu civaṉ mātattiṉ āṟāvatunāḷ makkaḷukku veḷiyi ṭṭār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉāl civaṉ eṉṉum peyar tiṅkaḷiṉ peyarākak ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu eṉpatu ūkittaṟiyappaṭum. kiṟittuva maṟaiyilē ciyaṉ eṉak kūṟappaṭum kaṭavuḷ civaṉē eṉpatai mēl nāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷē ārāyntu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.1 ātarlilli eṉpār kūṟuvatu piṉvarumāṟu: “civaṉ eṉṉum col valittum melittum intiyāviṉ pala pakutikaḷil uccarikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. pirāṉcu makkaḷ ‘ccivaṉ’ eṉa uccarippar. kiṟittuva maṟaiyilē (Amos. V. 26) varum ciyaṉ eṉpatu civaṉē eṉa ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷārkaḷ”. epirē yariṉ yekōvā eṉṉum col civā eṉpatu pōṉṟa hevā, cevā eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.2 ekiptilē civaṉ eṉṉum pālainilap pacuntarai oṉṟu uṇṭu. iṅkup paḻaiya amaṉ ālayam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. amaṉ ekiptiyariṉ ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ. amaṉ vaḻipāṭu paṇṭu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟatāka avarkaḷ varalāṟukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. amaṉ vaḻipāṭu ciyas (Zeus) yūpita r vaḻipāṭukaḷākak kirēkka urōmai nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ciyas yūpitar vaḻipāṭukaḷum ñāyiṟṟu vaḻipāṭukaḷē. ip pālaivaṉap pacun taraiyil, ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ, civaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭamai yiṉālēyē ataṟkuc civaṉ eṉṉum peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉa eḷitil ūkittaṟiyalām. civaṉ eṉpatē amaṉ eṉap piṟkālan tirintu vaḻaṅ kutalkūṭum. amaṉ (Amon) eṉpatē kiṟittavarkaḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṭivil kūṟum āmeṉ eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟateṉpatu varalāṟṟu āciri yarkaḷ cilaratu karuttu. mēṟku āciyāvil iṭaṅkaḷukkum mātattukkum māttiramaṉṟu; makkaḷukkum civaṉ eṉṉum peyar iṭṭu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. 3coyṟō rōyis eṉṉum cumēriya aracaṉatu maṉaiviyiṉ peyar civaṉ. ivaḷukku māttiram civaṉ eṉṉum peyar iṭappaṭṭateṉak karututal kūṭātu. ak kāla makkaḷ palarukku ip peyar iṭṭu vaḻaṅkap paṭṭateṉṟum am marapupaṟṟi ivaḷukkum ip peyar koṭukkappaṭṭa teṉṟum naṉku uyttaṟiyalākum. amerikkāvilē kolaṟaṭōp paḷḷattākkiluḷḷa pīṭapūmiyil pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa civaṉ ālayam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. kirēkkariṉ ciyas eṉṉum kaṭavuṭ peyarum civaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripē. itu, ciyas malaiyil iruppavarāyum mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷuṭaiyarāyum kūṟappaṭutalāl naṉku aṟitum. yūpitar eṉṉum peyarum cevā eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar.1 ukkirō piṉṉiya turāṉiya moḻikaḷil civaṉ civō eṉa vaḻaṅkum. ilāppiyariṉ kāttaṟ kaṭavuḷ civaṉ. turukki moḻiyil cev eṉpatu aṉpai uṇarttum. akkēṭiya moḻiyil ce karuṇaiyaiyum, cep uḷḷattaiyum uṇarttum.2 āriya makkaḷiṭaiyē vētakālattil civākkaḷ (Sivas) eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉar iruntaṉar. pāṇiṉiyiṉ ilakkaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa civapuram3, ivarkaḷiṉ nakaramākalāmeṉak karutap paṭukiṉṟatu. mecapetēmiyāvilē paḻaiya pāpilōṉ makkaḷiṉ marapiṉa rākiya ciṟukūṭṭattiṉar cilar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ kapil civā, ṟapil civā eṉṉum kaṭavuḷarai vaṇaṅkiṉar. paḻaiya ilaṅkai aracar palar civaṉ eṉṉum peyarait tamatu peyariṉ iṟutiyiṟ cērttuvaḻaṅ kiṉar. aracarallāta cilarukkuc civaṉeṉṟum peyariruntamai makāvamica meṉṉum pautta nūlālaṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. civaṉ oḷimalai eṉṉum peyar puttar kālattukku muṟpaṭṭatu. kirēttā(Crete)viluḷḷa ōr iṭattukkum civaṉ eṉṟu peyar.
maravaṇakkam
maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ kaṭavuḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷ vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭamaiyiṉ, makkaḷ maraṅkaḷilum kaṭavuḷ uṟaivatāka niṉaintu avaikaḷai vaḻipaṭalāyiṉar. iv vaṇakkamum ulakam muḻumaiyilum kāṇappaṭṭatu; iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.4 iṉṟu kiṟittavariṉ tēvālayattukkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkum ‘cēṭc’ eṉṉum peyar, karuvāli marattaikkuṟikkum ‘kērk’ eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟum piṟantateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kāṭṭi yuḷḷārkaḷ.5 maravaṇakkattil maraṅkaḷiṉ uccikkiḷaikaḷmītu āṭait tuṇṭukaḷaik koṭipōlak kaṭṭip paṟakkaviṭuvatu vaḻakku.
naṭarāca vaṇakkam
naṭarāca vaṭivil iṟaivaṉai vaḻipaṭum vaḻakku mokañcotarō kālattilēyē kāṇappaṭṭatu.1 aṅkuḷḷa muttiraikaḷil iṟaivaṉaik kuṟikkat tāṇṭavaṉ eṉṉum peyar poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu; aracaṉ oruvaṉ tāṉē oppaṟṟavaṉ eṉṉum perumitattiṉāl āṭum āṭalai iv vaṭivam kuṟikkiṉṟatu. kāliṉ kīḻ oruvaṉai mitittu niṟṟal ‘oṉṉārtteṟal’ eṉpataiyum, amartti niṟkum karam aṭaintārai ātarittu aṭaikkalam kāttalaiyum kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. tammaip paṇiyum ciṟṟaracarkaḷ muṭimītu tamatu kālaittūkki vaittu aruḷvatu ak kālap perumaṉṉar marapu. av vaḻakkē ‘tiruvaṭit tīkkai’yumām.
kōyil
maraṅkaḷiṉkīḻ irunta aruṭkuṟikaḷaic cuṟṟik kaṟkaḷai aṭukki matil allatu vēli ceyvatiliruntu kōyil ārampamāyiṟṟu. maṉita ṉiṉ vīṭu oṉṟaip pārttu amaikkappaṭṭatē kōyil.2 kōyil eṉpatu aracaṉiṉ vīṭu eṉap poruḷpaṭum. ālayam kōyil eṉappaṭṭatu pōlavē aracaṉatu illam kōyil eṉappaṭṭatu.3 mokañcotarō vil, ciṟitu aṟaikaḷum taṭitta cuvarkaḷumuṭaiyatākak kāṇappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭam ak kāla ālayam eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. ataṉ amaippaik koṇṭu atu pala māṭiyuṭaiyatāyiruntateṉak karutappaṭṭatu. māṭikaḷē iṉṟu kōpuraṅkaḷaḷavil curuṅkiviṭṭaṉa pōlum.
mēṟku ulakattilum ātiyil ālayaṅkaḷ maramum iliṅkamum kiṇaṟum palipīṭamum aḷavil iruntaṉa. palipīṭameṉpatu mēlē ciṟiya tī mūṭṭakkūṭiya oru kal. “ārampattil maramum, piṉ cōlaiyum kiṇaṟum, piṉ iliṅkamum, piṉ cetukki maṭṭañ ceyyappaṭāta kallākiya pali pīṭamum, piṉ koṭikaḷ allatu pāmpukaḷum muṟaiyē ōriṭattil kūṭi ālayaṅkaḷāyiṉa. mekkāvil uḷḷa kāpā, maramum iliṅkamum kiṇaṟumākiya ivvaḷavil uḷḷa kōyilāka iruntatu. iṅkilāntiluḷḷa kal vaṭṭaṅkaḷukku ayalil tōppukkaḷum, kiṇaṟukaḷum iruntaṉa.”1 eṉṟu parlāṅ eṉṉum āciriyar ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷār. “ātiyil, caturamāṉa allatu vaṭṭamāṉa kal vēlikaḷiṉ naṭuvē iliṅkamum ataṉ muṉṉāl palipīṭamum vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. ivvakaik kalvēli yiṉ orupuṟattē mūṉṟu kalliṉāl eṭukkappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷ (kaviḻnta ‘pa’ pōṉṟaṉa) poṉiciyāviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ik koḷkaiyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭē pāpilas (Babel) pālpek2 ālayaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. mekkāvil uḷḷa kāpā ālayam muṟkūṟiya vakai ālayat tukku utāraṇamākum” eṉa ‘cīriyāvil kaṟkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum varalāṟu’ eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu.3
teṉṉintiyāvil mikap paḻaiya ālayaṅkaḷiṉ aḻipāṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. teṉṉintiyāvil ālayaṅkaḷ marattiṉāṟ kaṭṭap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, piṉpu am marakkōyilkaḷiṉ amaippaip piṉ paṟṟik kalliṉāl ālayaṅkaḷ eṭukkappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, ataṉālēyē mikap paḻaiya ālayaṅkaḷum avaikaḷiṉ aḻipāṭukaḷum teṉṉintiyā viṟ kiṭaikkavillaiyeṉṟum paḻaiya kaṭṭiṭak kalai ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. teṉṉintiya kaṭṭiṭakkalai ārāycciyāḷaruḷ ciṟanta yōvu tepuruil (Jouveau Dubreuil) eṉpār teṉṉintiya kaṭṭiṭak kalaiyaippaṟṟik kūṟiyiruppaṉa cila piṉvaruvaṉa: “tirāviṭariṉ kaṭṭiṭak kalai 1500 āṇṭukaḷ varaiyil maṟṟaiya nāṭṭu av vakaikkalai yiṉāl pātikkappaṭātu taṉimaiyāka niṉṟatu. itu maṟṟaiya nāṭṭavariṉ kaṭṭiṭak kalaikaḷiṉiṉṟum etaiyum kaṭaṉpeṟavillai. itu paṭippaṭiyē vaḷarcciyaṭaintirukkiṉṟatu. tirāviṭa nāṭṭiṉ ovvoru kālattuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷaiyum nōkkumpōtu avaikaḷiṉ amaippu metuvāka vaḷarcciyaṭaintuḷḷateṉṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. itu maṉita nūlār kuraṅku pōṉṟa maṉitaṉuṭaiya maṇṭai mutal iṉṟaiya maṉitaṉiṉ maṇṭai varaiyil paṭippaṭiyāka vaḷarcciyaṭaintu vantuḷḷamaiyaik kāṭṭutal pōṉṟatu. māmallapurattil kāṇappaṭum tērkaḷukkum tiruppāppuli yūriṟ kāṇappaṭum tērkaḷukkum mātiriyil vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. itu koramāṅkaṉ1 maṉitaṉ maṇṭaikkum iṉṟaiya maṉitaṉ maṇṭaikku muḷḷa vēṟupāṭu pōṉṟatu.”
“kaṟcippikaḷ tam muṉṉōr tamakkuk kaṟpitta paramparai muṟaip paṭiyē kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷai amaittārkaḷ. tamiḻilē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa eḻuttuk kaḷiṉ vaṭivukaḷ kālantōṟum māṟupaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa. itu pōlavē moḻiyum, moḻi vaḻakkukaḷum, makkaḷiṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum māṟu paṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa; camayamum kiriyai muṟaikaḷum camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷum taṇivu peṟṟu vantuḷḷaṉa. mikap paḻaiya uruvac cilaikaḷukkum iṉṟaiya avvakaic cilaikaḷukkum vēṟṟumai kāṇalām. nila nūlār nilattil aṭukku aṭukkākak kāṇappaṭum pāṟaikaḷaip pārttu avaṟ ṟiṉ vayataik kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ. itupōlavē kaṭṭiṭakkalaiyil tērnta oruvaṉukkuk kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum kūṭu, allatu pōtikaiyiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ ārāyccikkup pōtumāṉavai. itu pāṟaikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum tolluyirkaḷiṉ eṉpuk kūṭukaḷ (Fossils) ap pāṟaikaḷai iṉṉa iṉṉa pāṟai eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟku nilaviyalārukkup payaṉpaṭuvatupōlākum.”
verkūcaṉ (Fergussion) eṉpār teṉṉintiya ālaya amaippukaḷ ekiptiya kōyilkaḷai ottaṉaveṉṟu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2 tēsraṉ eṉpār intiyāviṉ paḻaiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷil ekiptiya cālaṭiya poṉīciya amaippu muṟaikaḷ kalantuḷḷaṉa eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.1 malaiyāḷattil cila ālayaṅkaḷ kuṭilpōla vaṭṭavaṭivāka amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vaṭṭa vaṭiviliruntē piṉ catura vaṭivu ārampittiruttal vēṇṭum. makkaḷ ātiyil kaṭṭi vāḻnta vīṭukaḷ vaṭṭavaṭiviṉavē. karuvukku (karppak kirukam) mēl uḷḷa pākam iṉṟum vaṭṭavaṭivākavē amaikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṭi eṉpatu kuṭil vīṭukaḷukku ucciyil vaikkappaṭṭa pāṉaiyē yākum. aracaṉ illattaic cuṟṟi avaṉuṭaiya cuṟṟattiṉar cēṉāpatiyar iruppatupōlak kōyiliṉ uḷ vītiyaic cuṟṟip parivāra teyvaṅkaḷukku ālayaṅkaḷ amaikkappaṭṭaṉa.2 ātiyil maramum iliṅkamuṅ kiṇaṟumākavirunta ālayam ivvāṟu vaḷarcciyuṟṟatu.
talaviruṭcam
iliṅkattaic cuṟṟik kaṭṭiṭameḻuntatu. appoḻutu maram iṭam peṟavillai. enta marattiṉkīḻ iliṅkam ātiyil vaittu vaḻipaṭap paṭṭatō am maram av vālayattiṉ talaviruṭcam eṉappaṭṭatu. mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum ekiptilum kirēttāvilum teṉṉintiya ālayaṅ kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum koṭimaram pōṉṟa marattūṇkaḷ ālayaṅkaḷil niṟut tappaṭṭu āṇṭuviḻāk kālaṅkaḷil kiriyaikaḷuṭaṉ vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. koṭi marattiṉ nuṉiyil mūṉṟu kiḷaikaḷ kāṇappaṭutalpōla am maraṅkaḷukkum kiḷaikaḷ iruntaṉa. talaviruṭcamē, kālattil koṭimaramāka māṟiyuḷḷatō veṉpatu ārāyattakkatu.3 iliṅkam vaikkappaṭṭuḷḷa maraṅkaḷiṉ kiḷaikaḷil, āṇṭil orumuṟai ciṟappāka naṭaipeṟum poṅki maṭaiyiṭutal pōṉṟa vaḻipāṭṭai aṟivikka āṭait tuṇiyaik kaṭṭi viṭutal intiya nāṭṭilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa vaḻakku.
tirai
aracaṉ uṟaiyum uḷ maṇṭapattiṉ etirē tirai iṭappaṭṭiruk kum.1 ataṉai oppa ālayaṅkaḷilum uḷmaṇṭapattiṉ etirilum tiraic cīlai iṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ālayaṅkaḷil iṉṟum tiraic cīlaiyiṭṭirup patai nām kāṇalām. mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum ālayaṅkaḷukkut tiraiccīlaiyiṭappaṭṭiruntatu.2
aiyar
araṇmaṉaiyil aracaṉaic cēvikkum paṇiyāḷar palar irunta ṉar. avarkaḷ palataraṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntārkaḷ, avaruḷ oruvaṉ talaivaṉākaviruntāṉ. itaṉai oppavē ālayaṅkaḷilum aiyaṉaic cēvikkum aṭiyavarkaḷ palar iruntaṉar. aracaṉ tuyilviṭṭu eḻuntatu mutal avaṉ tuyilkoḷḷap pōkum varaiyil avaṉ aṭiyōr eṉṉa eṉṉa paṇikaḷaic ceykiṉṟārkaḷō av vēlaikaḷaiyellām aiyaṉatu aṭiyārum ceytaṉar.3 aiyaṉaic cēvittaliṉ ivarkaḷ aiyareṉ ṟum, kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷaip pārttaliṟ pārppār eṉṟum peyar peṟṟa ṉar. aṉṉōr paḻaiya nāḷil āṟu taraṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉar.4 kiṟittuva maṟaiyil cērmaiyā, nēcaiyā eṉa aiyāvil muṭiyum pala peyarkaḷaik kāṇalām. tamiḻmoḻiyil māttiramaṉṟu, mēṟku āciya moḻikaḷilum aiyar eṉpatu kaṭavuḷaik kuṟittatu. arāpi makkaḷ pāpilōṉ nāṭṭaic ciṉ-aiyar eṉa vaḻaṅkiṉar. ciṉ-aiyā eṉpataṟku ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ eṉṟu poruḷ. paḻaiya keltiya moḻiyilum aiyar eṉpatu kaṭavuḷaik kuṟittatu.1
aiyar eṉpatu āriyar eṉpataṉ tiripu eṉac cilar karutalāyiṉar. itu ciṟitum poruntuvataṉṟu. tolkāppiyattil “aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇameṉpa” eṉa aiyar eṉṉum peyar vantuḷḷatu. ārāycciyāḷar aiyar eṉpatu tūya tamiḻccol eṉṟē aṟutiyiṭṭuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.2 vaṭamoḻip piraḷayam teṉ tēcattē paṭai eṭuttu vanta oru kālattil, tamiḻppeyarkaḷiṉ iṭattai vaṭamoḻip peyarkaḷ eṭuttaṉa. avvāṟu vanta peyarkaḷuḷ pirāmaṇar eṉpatum oṉṟu. iṉṟum kōyiṟṟoṇṭu ceyyum kulattavar aiyar, aiyaṅkār, nampi, paṭṭar mutaliya tamiḻp peyarkaḷālēyē aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭuk kurumārai mēṟku āciya nāṭṭuk kurumārōṭu oppiṭṭup pārkkumiṭattup pala uṇmaikaḷ veḷiyākiṉṟaṉa. ekiptiya kurumāriṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷ perumpālum tamiḻnāṭṭuk kurumāriṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷai ottaṉaveṉpatu keratōtacu āciriyar kūṟiyuḷḷavaikaḷāl viḷaṅkum.3
purōkitar
aiyar, makkaḷukku camayap poruḷil vaḻikāṭṭiyāka viruntār. avar iṟantavar, iruppavarkaḷuṭaiya naṉmaiyai muṉṉiṭṭup pala kiriyaikaḷaic ceyya ārampittaṉar. makkaḷ avar puriyum kiriyaikaḷil aḷavu kaṭanta nampikkai vaittaṉar. makkaḷai ellāt tīmaikaḷiṉiṉṟum kāppavar eṉṉum poruḷil avar purōkitar eṉappaṭṭār. cōtiṭam, vāṉa ārāycci, mantira vittai mutaliyavaikaḷ ellām muṟkālattiṟ kurumārkaḷiṭattilēyē iruntaṉa. itaṉāl potu makkaḷ kurumārukkup peritum aṭaṅki naṭantārkaḷ. itu ulaka varalāṟṟil ellā nāṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭa uṇmai. purōkitaṉ aracaṉatu uṟutic cuṟṟaṅkaḷuḷ oruva ṉākavum iruntāṉ. aracar avaikaḷil aiyarukkut taṉippaṭṭa matippuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. araca capaikaḷil aiyarukkut taṉatu karuttukkaḷai eṭuttukkūṟum atikāram iruntatu.1 aiyarum purōkitaṉum āriyaṉ eṉṟu niṉaippatu tavaṟuṭaittu.
kōyiṟ kiriyaikaḷ
ōr aracaṉukku avaṉ vēlaikkāraṉ nittirai viṭṭeḻuntatu mutal nittiraikkuc cellumvaraiyil eṉṉeṉṉa vēlaikaḷaic ceytāṉō, av vakai vēlaikaḷai ellām kaṭavuḷukkuc ceyvatē ālayak kiriyaikaḷākum.2 viḻākkaḷ eṉpaṉa aracaṉ ulāvarum kālat til naṭaipeṟuvatupōla naṭattik kaṇṭu makiḻtal, viḻākkaḷaiyum nittiya kiriyaikaḷaiyum koṇṭu paṇṭai aracariṉ vāḻkkaiyai iṉitu aṟintu koḷḷutal kūṭum. avvattēca vaḻakkukaḷukkēṟpa ik kiriyaikaḷ ciṟitu vēṟupaṭum. tiruvuruvattukku naṟumaṇattailam tēyttal, tirumuḻuk kāṭṭutal, āṭai uṭuttal, naṟum pukaiyum tīpamum (ālatti) kāṭṭutal, uṇavu paṭaittal pōṉṟa kiriyaikaḷ ellā nāṭukaḷukkum potu vāṉavai. kiṟittuva ālayaṅkaḷil naṭaipeṟuvaṉa pōṉṟavē. cālamaṉ kaṭṭiya ālayattil naṭaipeṟṟa kiriyaikaḷum iv vakaiyiṉa. cīriyā, ṟoṭēciyā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅkaḷil pūmālaikaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.1 cīriyāvilē kaṭāṭ (Hadad) eṉṉum civaṉ kaṭavuḷuṭaiya iṭapattiṉ neṟṟiyil pūmālai kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.2 itaṉāl poṉīciyā cīriyā āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil vaḻipāṭṭil pūmālaikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. yūtar vaḻipāṭu paḻaiya akkēṭiya poṉīciya vaḻipāṭukaḷai ottatu. ātaliṉ ak kālattu an nāṭukaḷilum iv vakaik kiriyaikaḷē naṭaipeṟṟaṉa veṉpatu teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum. ālatti kāṭṭutal teṉṉāṭṭup paḻaiya vaḻakku. aracarukku muṉṉālum itu kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. tīpam ñāyiṟṟaik kuṟikkumeṉac cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.3
makkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu
aracaṉaik kāṇuvataṟku makkaḷ eppaṭic cellukiṟārkaḷō appaṭiyē ālayattukkum ceṉṟaṉar. perumakkaḷaik kāṇac cellu miṭattu veṟuṅkaiyōṭu celvatu tamiḻar marapaṉṟu. “pōṉavaṉ veṟuṅ kaiyōṭē pōkāvaṇṇañ ceṉṟu ñāṉa caṟkuruvai naṉṟāy vaṇaṅki niṉṟu” (kaivalyam); ākavē, avarkaḷ cellumiṭattuk kaiyuṟaiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. naṭuvirukkai eṉṉum karumimūlam aracaṉukkut tamatu ceyti kaḷai aṟivittalpōla avarkaḷ tamatu kaiyuṟaiyai naṭuvirukkaikaḷākiya kurumār (aiyar) mūlam kaṭavuḷiṭattil cērppittut tamkuṟaikaḷaiyum avar mūlam uṇarttiṉar. itaṟkuk kaimāṟākak kurumār kūliyum peṟṟaṉar. pattarkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ etirē niṉṟu avaṉ pukaḻpāṭik kumpiṭṭuk kūttumāṭiṉar.1 mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil iv vakaik kūttu iṉṟum naṭaipeṟuvatai varalāṟukaḷiṟ kāṇkiṉṟōm. cālamaṉ aracaṉiṉ tantai yākiya tāvītu (David) pēḻaikku muṉṉāl tuḷḷi āṭiṉa varalāṟu kiṟittava maṟaiyil (2. Sam VI, 14, 15) kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
tēvatācikaḷ
aracariṉ araṇmaṉaikaḷil aracaṉaip pāṭṭālum kūttālum makiḻvikkum makaḷir palar iruntaṉar. avvāṟē ālayaṅkaḷilum av vakai makaḷir palar iruntaṉar. intiya nāṭṭil māttiramaṉṟu, mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷilum urōmilum iv vakai makaḷir iruntaṉar. ivar kaḷiṉ oḻukkam iṉṟu intiya nāṭṭut tēvatācikaḷiṉ oḻukkattaip peritum ottatu.2 pālastīṉ ālayaṅkaḷilum tēvatācikaḷ iruntārkaḷ. itaṟku ātāram kiṟittuva maṟaiyiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. irācarācaṉ kaṭṭiya tañcāvūr ālayattil nāṉūṟu tēvatācikaḷ iruntārkaḷ eṉṟu oru kalveṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. makamat kacaṉi kālattil cōmanāta ālayattil 300 tēvatācikaḷ iruntaṉar.
caṅku vāttiyam
teṉṉintiya ālayaṅkaḷil caṅku vāttiyam oru ciṟappu. civaṉ, kātil aṇintiruppatu “caṅka veṇkuḻai”; tirumāl kaiyil vaittirup patu caṅku. “caṅkāpiṭēkam” eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ iṉṟum ālayaṅkaḷil māperum kiriyaikaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. intiya makkaḷ paricuttamākak koḷḷum poruḷkaḷuḷ caṅkum oṉṟu. caṅku intiyanāṭṭil māttira maṉṟu; iv vulakam muḻumaiyilum tūymaiyuṭaiyatākak karutappaṭṭu ālayaṅkaḷil vāttiyamākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭu vantatu. eliyaṭ cimit eṉṉum āciriyar, caṅkai vāttiyamākakkoḷḷum vaḻakkam teṉṉinti yāvil ārampittuk kiḻakku nōkkic cīṉā yappāṉ kīḻaittīvukaḷ pacupik kaṭaṟṟīvukaḷukku ūṭākac ceṉṟu amerikkāvaiyum aṭaintateṉak karutiṉār.1 kirēttāt tīviṉ paḻaiya makkaḷ, ālayaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷai vīti vaḻiyē aḻaittu varumpōtu caṅku vāttiyam ūtiṉārkaḷ.2 iṉṟum kiṟittuva ālayaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟil caṅku vāttiyam ūtappaṭuvatai varalāṟṟu āciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṟum cīṉāvilē pautta ālayaṅkaḷil caṅku ūtappaṭukiṉṟatu. kiṟittuvamaṟaiyiṉ putiya ēṟpāṭṭil caṅku oli, uyirtteḻuvatiṉ aṭaiyāḷamākak karutap paṭṭatu.1 mekcikkōvil paḻaiya kaiyeḻuttuc cuvaṭi oṉṟil, iraṇṭu kurumār taṅkaḷ kātukaḷait tuḷaittu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷukkup pali celuttuvatupōlavum, iraṇṭu kurumār cāmpirāṇip pukai iṭuvatu pōlavum, iṉṉum iraṇṭu kurumār caṅku ūtuvatupōlavum uḷḷa paṭam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ik kaiyeḻuttuc cuvaṭi ippoḻutu puḷō ṟaṉs (Florance) eṉṉumiṭattil uḷḷatu.2 ñāyiṟṟu vaṇakkam eṅkeṅku kāṇappaṭṭatō aṅku ellām caṅku vāttiyamum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. 
kaṭal uyirkaḷiṉ ōṭukaḷ mika muṟpaṭṭa kāla makkaḷāl puṉitamākak karutappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ kavaṭi (cōki) pōṉṟavai vaḻipaṭap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, kaṭavuḷ taṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉavātaliṉ kavaṭikaḷ makkaḷāl aṇiyappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, piṟkālaṅkaḷil avaikaḷukkuppatil avai (caṅku) koṭukkum muttu cīva oḷi aḷippaṉa eṉakkaruti aṇiyappaṭa lāyiṉa eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. muttukkaḷai aṇiyum vaḻakkiliruntē piṟkālattil cīvacattu uṭaiyatākiya uruttirākkam aṇiyum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. muttu nakai, muttup pallakku, muttu āparaṇam eṉpaṉa aracar, kaṭavuḷarkaḷukkuc ciṟantaṉavākak koḷḷappaṭalāyiṉa.
tuvarāṭai
civaṉ, tirumāl aṭiyavarkaḷ tuvarāṭai uṭukkiṉṟaṉar. tuvarāṭai uṭuttaṟkum paḻaiya varalāṟu uṇṭu. iv vaḻakkup paṇṭai makkaḷ pala riṭaiyē kāṇappaṭṭatu. caṅku vāttiyam eṅkeṅkup payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatō aṅkellām oruvakaic civappuc cāyam uṟpattiyākkap paṭṭatu.3 poṉīciya makkaḷ civappuc cāyam ūṭṭiya āṭaikaḷai ulakiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu vāṇikam purintaṉar. poṉīciyāviṟ ceyyappaṭṭa civanta āṭaikaḷ ak kālattu uyarnta vāḻkkaip poruḷ kaḷuḷ oṉṟāka viruntaṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil ataṉ vilai poṉṉukkuc camaṉākaviruntatu.1 ak kāla aracarum perumakkaḷum kurumārum iv vāṭaikaḷaiyē uṭuttaṉar. kōyilkaḷilum iv vāṭaikaḷ alaṅ kārattiṉ poruṭṭut tūkkappaṭṭaṉa. mōcē, yekōvāviṉ kūṭṭukku (vimāṉam) civappu āṭaiyaiyē payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. kurumārum anniṟa uṭaiyai aṇintaṉar. pāpilōṉiya teyvaṅkaḷukkuc civappu āṭai kaḷē uṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiya pāpilōṉiya aracarum inniṟa uṭai kaḷaiyē uṭuttiṉar. kirīcilum urōmilum aracarum perumakkaḷum kuru mārum uyarnta karumikaḷum māttiram civappu uṭai aṇintaṉar. ēṉaiya makkaḷukku iv vurimai maṟukkappaṭṭiruntatu. ik kaṭṭuppāṭṭai mīṟic civappu āṭai aṇiyum potumakkaḷ kaṭuntaṇṭaṉai aṭaintārkaḷ; cila camayaṅkaḷil kolait taṇṭaṉaiyum peṟṟaṉar. pappuṟā (PauPra) eṉṉum collē civappai uṇarttum pēppiḷ (Purple) eṉṉum collāka māṟiṟṟu. poṉīciya makkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ koṇṭuvantu viṟṟa civappu āṭai paccaivaṭam eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. paccai eṉpatu pappuṟā eṉpataṉ uccarippu vēṟupāṭu eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.
intiya nāṭṭilum civappu uṭai uyarntatākak karutappaṭṭatu. teyvaṅkaḷukkuc civanta āṭaikaḷē uṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. tirumurukāṟṟup paṭaiyil teyva makaḷir tampalappūcci pōṉṟa niṟa āṭai uṭuppavarkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paripāṭal, tirumāl civanta paṭṭāṭai uṭutti yiruppavarākak kūṟukiṉṟatu. murukak kaṭavuḷ uṭuttiyiruppatu civanta āṭai (muruku). maṟṟum kaṭavuḷarum civanta āṭaiyaiyē uṭuttiyirup patākap paṭaṅkaḷ varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. māṇikkavācakar tirupperuntuṟai yil tīkkai peṟṟapōtu aṇintiruntatu civappu āṭai (tiruvā. pu).
itaṉāl kaṭavuḷar perumakkaḷ kurumāriṉ uṭaikaḷ civappu niṟamā yiruntaṉa veṉṟum, ammarapaip piṉpaṟṟiyē kaṭavuḷ aṭiyār tuvar āṭai aṇiyum vaḻakku ēṟpaṭṭateṉṟum naṉku tuṇiyappaṭukiṉṟatu.
cuvattikam
cuvattika aṭaiyāḷam puttar kālattukkup piṉpē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa teṉap palar karutalām. ñāyiṟṟu vaḻipāṭu, caṅku vāttiyam eṉpavai kaḷaippōlavē ik kuṟiyum mikap paḻamaiyuṭaiyatu. varalāṟṟuk kālat tukku muṟpaṭṭa cuvattikam poṟitta paḻamporuḷkaḷ teṉ intiyāviṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1 ātikāla makkaḷ ñāyiṟṟaik kuṟikkap puḷḷaṭiyai (X) allatu puḷḷaṭiyaiccuṟṟi vaṭṭamiṭṭa kuṟiyai vaḻaṅ kiṉar.2 ippuḷḷaṭi kuricu (Cross) eṉa āṅkila moḻiyil vaḻaṅkum. puḷ ḷaṭiyiluḷḷa kōṭukaḷiṉ vaḷaivē cuvattikak kuṟiyākum. itaṉāl puḷḷaṭi cuvattikamāka vaḷarcciyuṟṟateṉa aṟiyalām. cuvattikam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻippeyar. āṅkilō cākcaṉiyar itaṉai ‘vavarvoṟ’ (Fowerfot) eṉṟum, pirāṉciyar kuṟōyikskiṟāmi (Croix Grammee) eṉṟum, skāntiṉēviyar tōrkkaṭavuḷiṉ cutti (Thor’s Hammer) eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiṉar. cuvattikam eṉpataṟku makiḻcci eṉṟu poruḷ. patañ caliyiṉ yōka cūttirattil yōkācaṉattil oṉṟu cuvattikam eṉappaṭu kiṉṟatu. itu intiyāvilō kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷ oṉṟilō tōṉṟi iv vulakam muṟṟilum paraviyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉa ārāycciyāḷar tuṇikiṉ ṟaṉar. māntar nūlār ulakil ellāp pākaṅkaḷiṉum iv vaṭaiyāḷam camayakkuṟiyāka vaḻaṅkiyateṉak kūṟi ulakappaṭattiṉ ellāp pākaṅkaḷi lum puḷḷiyiṭṭuk kāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.3 civaṉ eṉṉum peyar ñāyiṟ ṟukkuc cālap paḻaiya peyar eṉṟu muṉ viḷakkappaṭṭatu. cuva eṉpatē civa eṉa vantateṉa moḻiyārāycciyāḷar kūṟuvar. civaṉ campantamāṉa kuṟi eṉṉuṅ karuttuppaṟṟiyē ñāyiṟṟukkuṟikku cuvattikam eṉap peyar iṭappaṭṭatu eṉa nām naṉku uyttuṇaralām. cuvattikakkuṟi āriyariṉ intiya varukaikku muṟpaṭṭatu. 
heras pātiriyār kūṟuvatu: mokañcotarōvil vāḻnta tirāviṭamakkaḷ ciṟiya caturamāṉa nakaikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avaikaḷil cuvattikam poṟikkappaṭṭiruntatu. aṅkuk kiṭaitta paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷilum ik kuṟi kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaṭaiyāḷam tirāviṭa makkaḷuṭaiyatu. putitāka intiyāvai aṭainta āriyartām putitākak kaikkoṇṭa palavaṟṟōṭu iv vaṭaiyāḷattaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ic cuvattika aṭaiyāḷam tamiḻaruṭaṉkūṭa mēṟkē kirēttā (crete), kirīs, ēṟṟūṉā (Etruna), aipīriyā (speyiṉ) mutaliyavum piṟavumākiya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟatu. mokañcotarōvilum, ilaṅkaiyilum vaḻaṅkiyatupōla atu an nāṭukaḷilum cukam eṉṉum poruḷaik kuṟittatu. kuṭikkum kiṇṇat til iv vaṭaiyāḷam irukkumāyiṉ atu kuṭippavaṉukkuc cukam taruva tāka eṉṉum poruḷaik kuṟittatu. iv vaṭaiyāḷam eṟṟuskiya (Etrus-cian) camātit tūṇkaḷilum, kiṟittavariṉ kallaṟaikaḷilum poṟikkap paṭṭaṉa. itu, iv vaṭaiyāḷattiṉ kīḻ aṭakkañceyyappaṭṭavar maṟu ulakil cukamāka irukkaṭṭum eṉṉum poruḷaik kuṟittatu.
ākamaṅkaḷ1
ālayam amaikkum muṟaikaḷaiyum, aṅku naṭakkavēṇṭiya kiriyai vakaikaḷaiyum, makkaḷ ālayaṅkaḷil kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭu ceyya vēṇṭiya muṟaikaḷaiyum kūṟum nūlkaḷ ākamaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭum. vētaṅ kaḷum ākamaṅkaḷum nērvirōtamuṭaiyaṉa.2 vētaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum teyvaṅkaḷum ākamaṅkaḷiṟ kiḷakkappaṭum teyvaṅkaḷum vēṟu. vētaṅkaḷ varuṇāccirama tarumaṅkaḷai valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. ākamaṅkaḷ cariyai kiriyai mutaliyavaikaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ālaya vaḻipāṭu vētaṅ kaḷukku aṉṉiyamāṉatu. ākamaṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭirut taliṉ avai tamiḻaruṭaiyaṉavākā eṉac cilar karututalkūṭum. em moḻi yil ākamaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉavēṉum avaikaḷiṟ potintuḷḷa poruḷkaḷaikkoṇṭu avai evarukkuc contam eṉa aṟital eḷitu. ākamaṅkaḷ tamiḻaruṭaiyaṉavē eṉpatu varalāṟṟu nūlār naṉku ārāyntu aṟutiyiṭṭa muṭivu. upaniṭataṅkaḷ pāracīka moḻiyilum ilattīṉ moḻi yilum moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāracīkarum, ittāliyarum an nūlkaḷ taṅkaḷuṭaiyaṉaveṉṟu vātāṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. ālayam kaṭṭum ciṟpakkalai, tiruvuruvaṅkaḷaic ceyyummuṟai mutaliyaṉavum ivai pōṉṟaṉavum perumpālum avvat toḻilkaḷukkuriya kūṭṭattiṉa riṭaiyē ēṭṭileḻutappaṭātu paramparaiyākat toṭarntu vantuḷḷaṉa. iv vakai vaḻakkukaḷ ellāvaṟṟaiyum tiraṭṭip piṟkālattil aiyarmār eḻutivaittaṉar. tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ ulōkaṅkaḷil amaikkum muṟaikaḷ nīṇṭakālam eḻutappaṭātu paramparaiyāka vantateṉpataik kaṉkūli eṉpār teṉṉintiya veṇkalat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.1
ākamaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭum yōkam, ñāṉam mutaliya pakutikaḷ piṟ kālattu eḻutappaṭṭaṉavātal vēṇṭum. tamiḻil aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam eṉṉum muppālāka nūl ceytalē vaḻakku. vīṭupaṟṟi nūl ceytal vaḻak kiṉṟu.2
“annilai maruṅkiṉ aṟamutal ākiya
mummutaṟ poruṭkum uriya veṉpa” (tol. ce. 106)
iv vaḻakkuppaṟṟiyē tiruvaḷḷuvarum muppālāka nūl ceytār. āriya riṉ muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟiyē tamiḻil nūlkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cilar vātippar. itu tantaikku makaṉ muppāṭṭaṉ eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōr koḷkai. nāṟpālāka nūl ceytalē āriyar marapu.
purāṇaṅkaḷ
purāṇaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉa paḻaiya varalāṟukaḷ. purāṇaṅkaḷil paḻaiya aracar varalāṟukaḷum avarkaḷ kālaṅkaḷil nikaḻnta periya ceyalkaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aracarkaḷatu capaikaḷilē pulavarkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aracaṉatu paramparaiyiluḷḷa varalāṟukaḷai ellām tiraṭṭip perunāṭkaḷil araca capaikaḷil kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam. ivvāṟu vaṭanāṭṭu aracar capaikaḷil irunta pulavarkaḷ cūtar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. itaṉālēyē purāṇaṅkaḷ cūta muṉivarāṟ collappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu kaṉṉa paramparaiyil vanta varalāṟukaḷ piṟkālattut tiraṭṭi eḻutappaṭalāyiṉa. ivaikaḷē paḻaiya intiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷākum. maṟṟaiya nāṭukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum paḻaiya varalāṟukaḷ purāṇa varalāṟukaḷai viṭac ciṟantaṉa eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu. apastampar (ki. mu. 400), kauṭaliyar mutalāṉōr purāṇaṅkaḷaik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷamaiyiṉ ak kālattilēyē purāṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruntaṉaveṉa pārkittar (Pargiter) eṉṉum āriyar kūṟuvar.
purāṇaṅkaḷai naṉku ārāyntu, intiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟuk kataikaḷ eṉa nūl eḻutiya pārkittar eṉpār kūṟiyiruppatiṉ curukkam piṉ varumāṟu:
purāṇakkataikaḷ eṉappaṭum paḻaiya varalāṟukaḷil pirāmaṇar kai yiṭṭu avaikaḷai vēṇṭiyaḷavu perukkiyum, pirāmaṇak kataikaḷaiyum kaṭṭukkataikaḷaiyum cērttum, taṅkaḷ cāttirak koḷkaikaḷaiyum kiriyaikaḷaiyum pukuttiyum irukkiṉṟaṉar. pirāmaṇariṉ kataikaḷ pirāmaṇarukku eppaṭip pulappaṭumō avvāṟu eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ak kataikaḷil pirāmaṇariṉ karuttukkaḷum, pirāmaṇariṉ uyarvum, avar kaḷiṉ puṉitat taṉmaikaḷum, avarkaḷ maṉitarukku appāṟpaṭṭa nilai yil iruppatum, ivaipōṉṟaṉavumākiya avarkku māttiram vāyp puṭaiya koḷkaikaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ak kataikaḷ ciṟitum porutta maṟṟaṉa. pirāmaṇarkaḷiṉ piḻaiyiṉāl purāṇaṅkaḷ matippai iḻantu viṭṭaṉa.1 varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷākiya purāṇaṅkaḷ pirāmaṇariṉ kaiyīṭṭiṉāl camaya nūlkaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉa.
ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉ ciṟitāyuḷḷa irapparp paiyai (Baloon) eppaṭi ūtip perukkac ceykiṟāṉō avvāṟu ciṟiya aḷavil irunta paṇṭai makkaḷ varalāṟṟai ataṉiṉum paṉmaṭaṅku peritākap pirāmaṇar tam conta nalam karutip perukkiyuḷḷaṉar.
itikācaṅkaḷ
itikācam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ vaḻaṅkuvaṉavum purāṇaṅkaḷ pōṉṟa paḻaiya varalāṟukaḷē. ivvaralāṟukaḷilum pala kaṭṭukkataikaḷ nuḻaintuḷḷaṉa. purāṇaṅkaḷil kaiyiṭṭatupōlavē itikācaṅkaḷilum pirāmaṇar kaiyiṭṭu avaikaḷiṉ perumaiyaik kuṟaittuviṭṭaṉar. pārkittar kūṟuvatu: “pirāmaṇariṉ kataikaḷ potuvil nampattakkaṉa valla. irāmāyaṇattil pirāmaṇak koḷkaikaḷ peritum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kataiyum putumuṟaiyākavum nampakkūṭātatākavum irukkiṉṟatu.”1
mattiyatarai makkaḷ ellōrukkum ākamam oṉṟē
mēl nāṭṭu makkaṭkula nūlār mattiyataraic cātiyiṉar eṉappirik kum peruṅkūṭṭattil polinīciyar, tamiḻar, ekiptiyar, pāpilōṉiyar, kirēttā makkaḷ paḻaiya ittāliyar mutaliyōrum, iṉṉum mattiya taraikkaṭalai aṭutta nāṭukaḷil vāḻum makkaḷum aṭaṅkuvar. im makkaḷ ellōrum orukālattu oru kūṭṭamāka vāḻntu piṉpu pirintu ceṉṟōr. mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa makkaḷē orukālattil intiyāvai yaṭaintu tamiḻar eṉṉum cātiyiṉarākap perukiṉārkaḷ eṉak karutap paṭṭatu. paḻaiya makkaḷ varalāṟukaḷ ōraḷavu veḷiccam aṭaintuḷḷa ik kālattu, tamiḻ makkaḷē mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ eṉṉuṅ koḷkai valuttuḷḷatu. heras pātiriyār ārampattil muṉṉaiya koḷkaiyuṭaiyavarākavē iruntār. mokañcotarō, arappāviṟ kiṭaitta āyirattu eṇṇūṟu eḻuttuppoṟitta muttirai kaḷai vācittapiṉ avar tamiḻarkaḷē mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷiṟceṉṟu kuṭi yēṟiṉārkaḷ eṉap palavakaiyil ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.2
viviliya vētattiṉ paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭil, mattiyatarai makkaḷ ellōruṭaiya vaḻipāṭukaḷum oruvakaiyiṉa eṉpataṟkuriya ātāraṅkaḷ niṟaiyak kiṭaikkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟai īṇṭut tarukiṉṟōm. avaṟṟai iṉṟum intiyanāṭṭiṟ kāṇappaṭum vaḻakkukaḷōṭu oppu nōkki uṇmai ōrka.
“yekōvā mocēyai nōkki atiuttama cukanta tiraviyaṅ kaḷākiya cittamāṉa veḷḷaippōḷattil paricutta snāṉattiṟkuc cēkkaliṉpaṭi 5000 cēkkalum, karuvāppaṭṭaiyil atiṟ pātiyākiya 2500 cēkkalum cukanta vacampu 2500 cēkkalum, ilavaṅkappaṭṭaiyil 500 cēkka lum, cītaviruṭcatteṇṇeyil oru kiṉṉum eṭuttu, parimaḷa tailak kāraṉuṭaiya ceykaiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa parimaḷa tailattaippōla, avai kaḷiṉāṟ cutta tailattaic ceyvāyāka. atuvē apiṣēka tailamāka irukkavēṇṭum. ataṉālē capaiyiṉ āvācattukkum, cāṭcip peṭ ṭikkum, pīṭattukkum kiḷai viḷakkukkum tūpavētikaikaḷukkum, takaṉa palivētikaikkum apiṣēkam paṇṇakkaṭavāyāka. ārōṉum avaṉ puttirarum eṉakku ācāriyattoṇṭu puriyumpaṭi nī avarkaḷukku apiṣēkam paṇṇi avarkaḷaip paricuttappaṭuttuvāy. (yāt: 30. 22-33) (yāt: 25. 30) eṉ cannitiyil eppoḻutum pīṭattiṉmītu appam vaikkavēṇṭum. naivēttiyattukku oru kūṭaiyil eṇṇeyiṟ picainta puḷippillāta melliya māviṉāṟ ceyta atiracaṅkaḷaiyum eṇṇey taṭavappaṭṭa puḷippillāta aṭaikaḷaiyum koṇṭuvarak kaṭavaṉ. avaikaḷai ācāriyaṉ koṇṭuvantu yekōvāviṉ cannati yil... paliyāka iṭakkaṭavaṉ. naivēttiyamākum poruṭṭu aṭukkun tōṟum cuttakunturuvai iṭakkaṭavāy.......nittiyamāka yekōvāvukku akkiṉiyiliṭappaṭum naivēttiyaṅkaḷuḷ avai mikavum paricuttamāṉa vaikaḷākaiyāl avaikaḷ ārōṉukkum avaṉ puttirarkaḷukkum urittākum; avaikaḷaip paricutta stāṉattiṟ pucikkakkaṭavarkaḷ. (lēvi. 16 12-13.) yekōvāviṉ cannitiyilirukkum vētikaiyileriyum nerupput taṇalkaḷiṉālē takaṉatūpa kalacattai niṟaittu, tūḷākkappaṭṭa cukanta tūpa varkkattil taṉ kai niṟaiya aḷḷit tiraikkuḷḷē koṇṭu vantu cāṭcip pattirattiṉ mēlirukkum kirupācayattai mūṭattakkatāka, yekōvāviṉ cannitiyil akkiṉiyiṉmēl tūpa varkkattaip pōṭak kaṭavaṉ.
(lēvi. 24. 1-4) yekōvā mocēyai nōkki nittamum viḷak kēṟṟumpaṭikku isravēlar iṭittuppiḻinta cītaviruṭcattiṉ cuttamāṉa eṇṇeyai uṉṉiṭattil koṇṭuvarumpaṭi avarkaḷukkuk kaṭṭaḷai yiṭu. ārōṉāṉavaṉ tiraiccīlaikku veḷiyē cāyaṅkālam toṭakkam viṭiyaṟkāla pariyantam yekōvāviṉ cannitiyil nittamum viḷakku ēṟṟakkaṭavaṉ; (eṇ 8. 1-4) yekōvā mocēyai nōkki ārōṉukku nī viḷakkēṟṟumpōtu ēḻu tīpamum, kiḷaiviḷakkut taṇṭukaḷukku nērē oḷikoṭukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu col eṉṟār.
(nāḷā 15. 15-16) yekōvāviṉ vākkiṉpaṭi, mocē kaṭṭaḷai yiṭṭa pirakāram tēvaṉuṭaiya peṭṭiyait taṇṭōṭum taṅkaḷ tōḷiṉ mēl eṭuttukkoṇṭu vantārkaḷ; taṅkaḷ cakōtararait tampuruvum, kiṉṉaramum, kaittāḷaṅkaḷumākiya kītavāttiyaṅkaḷ muḻaṅka āṉantak kūttuṭaṉ uratta cattamāyp pāṭumpaṭi ēṟpaṭuttumpaṭi tāvītu kaṭṭaḷai yiṭṭāṉ (mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi ati. 2.). ūtu kompōṭum, pūrikaikaḷōṭum, kaittāḷattōṭum, tampuruvum, kiṉṉaramum muḻaṅka isravēlaraṉai varum yekōvāviṉ uṭaṉpaṭikkaip peṭṭiyaik koṇṭuvantārkaḷ.
(eṇ. 8-10) uṅkaḷ āṉanta tiṉattilum, uṟcava nāḷilum mācāram paṅkaḷilum uṅkaḷ takaṉa palikaḷaiyum stōttira palikaḷaiyum celuttumpōtu ekkāḷaṅkaḷai ūtavēṇṭum. (caṅ. 18-5) kiṉṉaratti ṉālum kiṉṉarattōṭu, kītattiṉōcaiyiṉālum yekōvāvaip pāṭuṅkaḷ. (2. cāmu 6.5) tāvītum isravēl cantatiyār camastarum yekō vāviṉ camukattil tēvatāruviṉāṟ ceyta kiṉṉaramum, tampuruvum, miru taṅkamum, vīṇaiyum, kaittāḷamumākiya nāṉāvita vāttiyaṅkaḷaiyum muḻakkiṉārkaḷ.
(lēvi. 2-3) ōyvunāḷum mācappiṟappum amāvāciyum, pūraṇai yum, uṟcava nāḷkaḷum puṇṇiya kālaṅkaḷ. antak kālaṅkaḷilē nitti yākkiṉiyilē iṟaicci mutaliyavaikaḷaiyiṭṭut takaṉapali ceytal vēṇṭum. taṅkaḷ caṅkaṟpattiṉpaṭiyē niyamitta tiṉaṅkaḷilē upavacittu viratamaṉuṭṭittal vēṇṭumeṉṟuñ collappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. (lēvi. 12. 1-5) oru stiri karuppavatiyāki āṇpiḷḷai peṟṟāl acuciyiṉ nimittam rutumatiyaippōla ēḻunāḷ acuciyāyirukkakkaṭavaḷ. piṉ avaḷ utiracuci nimittam muppattu mūṉṟunāḷ varaiyum acuciyāyirup pāḷāka. cucikkēṟṟa tiṉaṅkaḷ niṟaivēṟumaḷavum entap paricutta vastu vaiyum paricikkavum paricutta stāṉattiṟ piravēcikkavum kūṭātu. (nāḷā. 15. 19-24) rutumatiyāṉa stiri taṉ carīrattiṉ ūṟaliṉimittam ēḻu nāḷa ḷavum acuciyāyiruppāḷ. avaḷaip paricikkiṟavaṉ evaṉoruvaṉō avaṉ cāyaṅkāla pariyantam acuciyākaviruppāṉ. avaḷ acuciyāyiruk kaiyil avaḷ paṭutta entap paṭukkaiyum avaḷirunta enta ācaṉamum acuciyāyirukkum.”
1ācāriyapiṭēkam, viratam aṉuṭṭittal, maraṇa, ceṉaṉa tīṭṭuk kāttal, ālayaṅkaḷil kiriyaipurital, tīyil paliyai iṭutal, ivaiyum ivai pōṉṟaṉavum tamiḻar āriya makkaḷiṭamiruntu kaṟṟukkoṇṭaṉa valla veṉpataṟku mēṟkāṭṭiyavai ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākum. paḻaiya tamiḻnāṭṭu vaḻakkukaḷaiyē nammavar tavaṟāka āriyak koḷkai kaḷeṉak karuti varukiṉṟaṉar.
camayattiṉ akavaḷarcci
itukāṟum kūṟiyaṉa camayattiṉ puṟavaḷarcci muṟaikaḷ. camayam iṉṉoru vakaiyilum vaḷarcciyaṭaivatāyiṟṟu. ataṉai akavaḷarcci eṉak kūṟalām. makkaḷ iv vulakattaiyum, tammaiyum oruṅkē iyakkum mēlāṉa oru poruḷaiyumpaṟṟi ārāyat talaippaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ am mēlāṉa poruḷ tamatu cintaikku varātateṉṟum atuvē ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum iyakkukiṉṟateṉṟum kaṇṭārkaḷ. ākavē avarkaḷ apperum poruḷukku ‘uḷḷattaik kaṭantatu’ eṉṉum poruḷil kaṭavuḷ eṉṟum, ellāvaṟṟaiyum iyakkuvatu eṉṉum poruḷil iyavuḷ eṉṟum peyarkaḷai iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. ip peyarkaḷ. tamiḻil mikap paḻamaiyuṭaiyaṉa. mokañcotarō muttiraikaḷil kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum peyar iruttalai heras pātiriyār kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.1 mokañcota rōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa tiruvuruvam oṉṟu, kaṭavuḷ, pacupati eṉṉum karuttai viḷakkā niṟkiṉṟatu. avvuruvam yōkattilamarntirukkum tirukkōlat tuṭaṉumuḷḷatu.2 itaṉāl ak kāla makkaḷ mupporuḷ uṇmaikaḷai yum, cintaiyaiyaṭakkic cummāvirukkiṉṟa yōkamuṟaikaḷaiyum naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷeṉpaṉa teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭum. maṟṟaiya nāṭukaḷil iv vuṇmaikaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭiruntamaikkuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇappaṭa villai. tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camaya akavaḷarcci mika uyarnilai peṟṟiruntamai yiṉālēyē avarkaḷ putitāka vanta āriya makkaḷiṉ camayakkoḷkai kaḷukku uṭampaṭārāyiṉar. āriyarukkum, tācukkaḷ eṉa avarkaḷāl iḻittuk kūṟappaṭṭa tamiḻarkaḷukkumiṭaiyil nērnta pōrkaḷukku mukkiya kāraṇam, tācukkaḷ āriyar teyvaṅkaḷai vaḻipaṭa maṟuttatōṭu avarkaḷ vēḷvikaḷaiyum veṟuttamaiyēyākum. irāvaṇaṉ ātiyōr vaṭa nāṭṭu muṉivarkaḷ uñaṟṟum vēḷvikaḷukku iṭaiyūṟāy niṉṟu avai kaḷai aḻittu vanta varalāṟum irāmāyaṇattiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
āriyar, intiyāvai aṭaintapōtu ulakam, uyir, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum mupporuḷ uṇmaikaḷai aṟintirukkavillai. maṟupiṟappaip paṟṟiyē vētaṅkaḷ aṟiyā eṉṟu muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. vētamatam tattuvak koḷkai (Philosophy) illāta matam. āriyamakkaḷ intiya nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya kālattil āriyar tamiḻar eṉṉum iru kūṭṭattiṉa rum kalantu oṉṟupaṭṭaṉar. ak kālattil pirāmaṇar pirāmaṇarallātār āriyar āriyarallātārkaḷiṉ kalapput tirumaṇaṅkaḷaik kaṭṭuppaṭuttum caṭṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyirukkavillai.3 ak kālattil tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ koḷkaikaḷ palavaṟṟai āriyamakkaḷ piṉpaṟṟalāyiṉar. avaṟṟuḷ kāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu tavañceytalākiya vaḻakku oṉṟu. kāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu tavañceytalaippaṟṟi irukkuvētattiṟ kāṇappaṭavillai eṉa ār. ci. tattar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. itaṉāl iv vaḻakkut tamiḻarkaḷuṭaiyatē eṉṟu naṉku tuṇitum.
“kāmañ cāṉṟa kaṭaikkōṭ kālai
ēmañ cāṉṟa makkaḷoṭu tuvaṉṟi
aṟampuri cuṟṟamoṭu kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum
1ciṟantatu payiṟṟal iṟantataṉ payaṉē.” (tol. kaṟ-51)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiramum itaṉai valiyuṟuttum.
upaniṭataṅkaḷ
upaniṭataṅkaḷē tamiḻarkaḷiṉ paṇṭaimaṟaikaḷ eṉpatu muṉ ōriṭattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu. āriyap pirāmaṇarkaḷuṭ cilar uṇmaiñāṉaṅ kaḷai aṟiyum tākam uṭaiyavarkaḷāy iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum alaintu tirintaṉar. ivvāṟu tirinta yākñavalkiyum avaruṭaṉ ceṉṟa maṟṟaiya pirāmaṇarkaḷum vitēka nāṭṭaracaṉākiya caṉakaṉaik kaṇṭārkaḷ. yākña valki caṉakaṉiṭattil māṇākkaṉāka amarntu uṇmai ñāṉattaip payiṉṟār. itaṉpiṉ pirāmaṇar, tām muṉṉorupōtum aṟintirāta uṇmaiñāṉaṅkaḷai aracamarapiṉariṉ pātaṅkaḷiṉkīḻ māṇākkarāyiruntu payiṉṟaṉar. ataṉpiṉpē āriyaruṭaiya matam tattuvak koḷkaiyuṭaiya tāka māṟiṟṟu. viṉraṇic2 eṉṉum aṟiñar upaniṭataṅkaḷaip paṟṟi ārāyntu kūṟiyiruppatiṉ curukkam piṉvarumāṟu:
“upaniṭatañāṉam aracarkaḷiṭam māttiramaṉṟu, peṇkaḷiṭamum aiyamāṉa kulattavarkaḷiṭaiyum iruntatu. pirāmaṇaṉ māttiram āciriya ṉāka amarntu mūvarukkum vētaṅkaḷaik kaṟpikkalām eṉa vaṟpuṟutti āriyarkaḷuṭaiya nīti nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.3 āṉāl aracarum aracar kulattavarkaḷumē uyarnta ñāṉattaip peṟṟiruntaṉar eṉṟum, avarka ḷiṭamiruntē pirāmaṇar avvaṟivaip payiṉṟaṉareṉṟum upaniṭataṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. aracar avvāṟu aḷitta ñāṉam maṟupiṟapput toṭarpuṭai yatu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. iññāṉam pirāmaṇar ciṟitum aṟiyātatu. upaniṭata meṉpataṟkuk kiṭṭa iruppatu eṉṟu poruḷ. āciriyaṉ oruva ṉiṭattil cila maṟaivāṉa uṇmaikaḷaip paṭittaṟku māṇākkaṉāyirut tal eṉa itu poruḷpaṭum. avvāṟu koṭukkappaṭum ñāṉam maṟai (irakaciyam) eṉappaṭṭatu. upaniṭataṅkaḷ, ammaṟaiyait takutiyillātavar kaḷukku aḷittal kūṭāteṉa vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu, im maṟaiyait tantai taṉ mūtta putalvaṉukku allatu uṇmaiyāṉa māṇākkaṉukku aḷikkalām; iv vulakam muḻuvataiyum peṟuvatāyirun tālum maṟṟavarkaḷukku aḷittal kūṭātu. oruvaṉ āciriyaṉiṭam māṇāk kaṉāka amarntu palamuṟai varuntik kēṭṭapōtu iññāṉam veḷi yiṭappaṭṭateṉa upaniṭataṅkūṟukiṉṟatu.” upaniṭata kālattai ārāycci yāḷar palavāṟu kūṟuvar. 1ṟaṉēṭ eṉpār upaniṭata kālam ki.mu. 1200kkum ki.mu. 600kkum iṭaiyil eṉak kūṟuvar. itu kāṟum maṟaivāka maṟai eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ nilaviya tamiḻarkaḷiṉ uṇmaiñāṉaṅkaḷ av vāriya makkaḷ vāyilāka upaniṭatam eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ veḷivaralāyiṉa. ak kālattilēyē civavaḻipāṭu āriyarukkuḷ talaimai peṟṟiruntatu. āriyariṉ uruttiraṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷum civaṉum oruvarākak karutap paṭṭaṉar.2
upaniṭataṅkaḷ uyir, ulakam, iṟaivaṉ eṉṉum mupporuḷ kaḷiṉ uṇmaikaḷai ārāykiṉṟaṉa; yōkattaippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa; ōṅkārattai uccarikkumpaṭi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa; māyai uḷporuḷ eṉak koḷkiṉṟaṉa. ivaikaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum uṇmaikaḷ ellāk kālattukkum ellāmakkaḷukkum potuvāṉavai.1 upaniṭatañāṉam ñāyattukku uṭpaṭṭatu; vētaṅkaḷukku māṟāṉatu.2
upaniṭataṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭum piraṇavam tamiḻukkē uriyatu. vaṭa moḻiyil e, o eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷ illai. enta moḻiyil e, o eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavō am moḻikkē ō, ē eṉṉum neṭilkaḷum uriyaṉa. im muṟaiyaikkoṇṭu, pantākkar, pālimoḻi yil e, ō eṉṉum kuṟilkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamaiyiṉ makatanāṭṭil tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiyateṉṟum, ekara okaraṅkaḷ tamiḻukkē uriyaṉaveṉṟum kāṭṭi yuḷḷār.
cāṅkiyam
upaniṭataṅkaḷukkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyatu kapilariṉ cāṅkiya nūl. cāṅkiya matakkoḷkai ‘catkāriyavātam’ eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. itu māyai uḷ poruḷeṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu; viṉai, viṉaippayaṉkaḷai valiyuṟuttu kiṉṟatu. itu kaṭavuḷaippaṟṟi yātum pēcavillai. ākavē, itu nīrīccura cāṅkiyam eṉavumpaṭum. kapilariṉ nūṟkoḷkaikaḷukku aṭippaṭai upaniṭataṅkaḷ. cāṅkiya nūl ki. mu. 760 varaiyiṟ ceyyappaṭṭateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. kirēkka tattuvacāttiriyākiya patakōras eṉpavar intiyāvukku vantu kapilariṉ cāṅkiyanūṟ karuttukkaḷaip payiṉṟu avaṟṟaik kirīcil veḷiyiṭṭār eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu.3 purāṇaṅ kaḷilē kapilariṉ vēḷvik kutiraiyaip piṭittukkaṭṭiya kapilar oruvar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟār. avar tamiḻmarapiṉar eṉa olṭkām eṉṉum āciriyar ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷār.4 kautamaputtar kapilarmarapil vantavareṉṟum, avar tamiḻmarapiṉareṉṟum karutappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.1 cāṅkiyanūl ovvoṉ ṟaiyum niyāyavāyilāka ārāykiṉṟatu. cāṅkiyam vētamārkkattukku muṟṟum muraṇpaṭṭatu.
pauttam
kapilarukkuppiṉ tōṉṟiyatu puttamatakkoḷkai. im matakkoḷ kaikku aṭippaṭai upaniṭataṅkaḷum cāṅkiyanūlum. puttamatam māyaiyai ilporuḷeṉak kūṟum. ākavē, am matak koḷkai cūṉiyavātam eṉappaṭum. cāṅkiyam, puttam mutaliya mataṅkaḷ, upaniṭatak koḷkaikaḷ evvāṟu ciṟitu ciṟitāka māṟupaṭṭu vevvēṟu matak koḷkaikaḷāka māṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kāṇpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukaḷākum. puttamatam vēta matattukku māṟāṉatu. puttariṉñāṉam tamiḻar ñāṉattai aṭippaṭai yākak koṇṭateṉa mēlnāṭṭu āciriyar tāmum eḷitilaṟintu kūṟiyuḷ ḷār.2 ālṭaṉpēk eṉṉum āciriyar upaniṭataṅkaḷ puttarukku evvāṟu vaḻikāṭṭikaḷāyiruntaṉaveṉpatai naṉku ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.3
yōkam
kuru māṇākka muṟaiyil neṭuṅkālam vantaṉavum upaniṭataṅ kaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭaṉavumākiya yōkanūṟ poruḷkaḷaip patañcali muṉivar tiraṭṭi (ki.mu. 150) yōka cūttiraṅkaḷākac ceytār.4 yōkam eṉpatu maṉattai ōriṭattil niṟuttic ceyyappaṭum tiyāṉam. yōkanūlil maṉattai ōriṭattil niṟuttutaṟkuriya payiṟcikaḷum yōkattiṉāl aṭaiyumpēṟum naṉku viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. yōkanūlum niyāya muṟaiyāka uṇmaikaḷai ārāyntu. yōkattiṉ muṭivu, piṟappai oḻittal. kaṭavuḷai aṭaital eṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. ātaliṉ itu, ‘īcuvara cāṅkiyam’ eṉappaṭum.1 yōka nūl piraṇavattai uccarikkavēṇṭum eṉa vaṟpuṟuttukiṉṟatu. maṉam cārntataṉ taṉmaiyai aṭaikiṉṟateṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu;2 paṟṟai aṟukka vēṇṭumeṉa moḻikiṉṟatu; ap paṟṟai aṟuppataṟku īcuvaraṉaippaṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉa navilkiṉṟatu. kaṭavuḷ patti, yōkaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟantateṉa aṟaikiṉṟatu.3 akattūymai puṟattūymaikaḷaip paṟṟip pēcukiṉṟatu. nallavar iṇakkattaippaṟṟic cāṟṟukiṉṟatu; maṉam uyiriṉ akak karuvi yātalaippaṟṟip pukalkiṉṟatu. cāṅkiyanūlum yōkanūlum uyirkaḷ pala eṉak koḷkiṉṟaṉa.4 iv vakai yōkavaḻikaḷai ellām tamiḻmakkaḷ mikamika muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu mokañ cotarōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷāl naṉku teḷivuṟukiṉṟa teṉpatu muṉṉōriṭattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu.
yōkamum ālaya vaḻipāṭum
yōkap paḻakkattukku oru vaṭivai maṉattil niṟuttutalvēṇṭum. allāviṭiṉ maṉam ōr iṭattil nillātu. ākavē, kuṇaṅkuṟiyillāta kaṭavuḷai oru vaṭivam koṭuttu niṉaikka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ataṟku ālayaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tiruvuruvaṅkaḷ payaṉaḷittaṉa. camaya tīkkai, mantiraṅ kaḷai nūṟṟeṭṭu āyiratteṭṭu eṉṟu uccarittal mutaliyaṉa. avvāṟu ceyyumiṭattu maṇikaḷai eṇṇutal mūccuppayiṟci purital pōlvaṉa yōkap payiṟciyiṉ mutaṟpaṭikaḷākum. ippoḻutu, ālaya pūcai aracaṉukkuc ceyyum vaḻipāṭupōl karutappaṭātu, yōkanilaiyiṟ kai vantār oruvar iv vulaka makkaḷ ellōrum tīviṉaikaḷiṉiṉṟum nīṅki nalvaḻippaṭavēṇṭumeṉa niṉaintu kaṭavuḷai niṉaippatu eṉak karutap paṭuvatāyiṟṟu. itu parārtta pūcai (piṟarporuṭṭucceyyum pūcai) eṉappaṭalāyiṟṟu.
“vāḻka antaṇar vāṉavar āṉiṉam
vīḻka taṇpuṉal vēntaṉum ōṅkuka
āḻka tīyatellām araṉ nāmamē
cūḻka vaiyakamum tuyar tīrkavē.”
eṉṟavāṟu vēṇṭutal parārtta pūcaikku eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. yōka nilai kaivarappeṟāta pārppāṉaiyē tirumūlar “pērkoṇṭa pārppāṉ” eṉak kūṟiṉār ātalkāṇka.
yōkam kaivanta periyōr niṉaippaṉa niṉaittapaṭi ākum. eṉa makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.1 cittarkaḷ eṉappaṭṭōr yōkattiṉ makimaiyi ṉāl pala putumaikaḷaic ceytōrkaḷēyāvar. ellāc camayattavarkaḷum tattam camaya mutalvarkaḷ putumaikaḷai viḷaittārkaḷeṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iṟaivaṉiṭattē, uḷḷam paṭintu niṟkum uyirkaḷiṭattu iṟaivaṉiṉ iṟaimaik kuṇaṅkaḷ cila uṇṭāvatu iyalpu eṉac caivacittānta nūlkaḷ kūṟum. maruttuvam cōtiṭam pōṉṟa periya uṇmaikaḷ yōki kaḷāl tamatu teḷinta uḷḷakkāṭciyiṟ kaṇṭu kūṟappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu nampappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tirumantiram
tamiḻmakkaḷiṭaiyē vaḻaṅkiya uṇmai aṟivukaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṉ tiraṭṭāka veḷivantuḷḷatu tirumūlar aruḷiya tirumantiram eṉṉum nūl. in nūliṟ kāṇappaṭum akaccāṉṟukaḷāl itiṟ kūṟappaṭum poruḷkaḷ tamiḻmakkaḷiṭaiyē vaḻaṅkiyavai eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. paṟṟaṟuttal, aṉpu kaṉintu iḷakiya uḷḷattar ātal, kaṭavuḷiṭattil patti ceytal pōṉṟaṉavē muttikku vaḻi eṉa ivar kūṟuvar. ellā ñāṉaṅkaḷum pattiyil aṭakkam.
“eṉpē viṟakā iṟaicci yaṟuttiṭṭup
poṉpōṟ kaṉaliṟ poriya vaṟuppiṉum
aṉpō ṭuruki akaṅkuḻai vārkkaṉṟi
eṉpoṉ maṇiyiṉai eytavoṇ ṇātē”
“aṉpuñ civamum iraṇṭeṉpar aṟivilār
aṉpē civamāva tāru maṟikilār
aṉpē civamāva tāru maṟintapiṉ
aṉpē civamāy amarntirun tārē”
“ācai aṟumiṉkaḷ ācai aṟumiṉkaḷ
īcaṉō ṭāyiṉum ācai yaṟumiṉkaḷ
ācai paṭappaṭa āyvarun tuṉpam
ācai viṭaviṭa āṉanta māmē” (tirumantiram)
“paṟṟuka paṟṟaṟṟāṉ paṟṟiṉai yappaṟṟaip
paṟṟuka paṟṟu viṭaṟku” (kuṟaḷ. 350)
tirumantiram ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ ceyyappaṭṭateṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. cittar nūlkaḷilum tirumantirattiṟ kūṟappaṭum uṇmaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tirumuṟaikaḷ
tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ camayak karuttukkaḷ tirumuṟaikaḷilum āḻvār pāṭalkaḷilum tatumpikkiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. tōttiraṅkaḷuṭaṉ avai miṭaintu kiṭattaliṉ avai cāttiramākap payaṉpaṭā. āyiṉum cāttiraṅ kaḷukku mēṟkōḷ at tōttiraṅkaḷē.
cittānta nūlkaḷ
paṇṭutoṭṭut tamiḻmakkaḷiṭaiyē ‘maṟai’ eṉappeyar peṟṟu vanta camaya tattuvakkoḷkaikaḷ ellāvaṟṟaiyum tokuttu muṟaip paṭuttic ceyyappaṭṭaṉavē caivacittānta nūlkaḷ patiṉāṉkum.1 “ōrum vētāntameṉ ṟucciyiṟ paḻutta-cāraṅ koṇṭa caiva cittāntat tēṉamutu” (kumarakuruparar) vētāntam - upaniṭatam.
vīṭu
tamiḻmakkaḷ vīṭu eṉakkoṇṭatu meyyuṇarviṉāṟ paṟṟaṟṟut tiruvaruṭ paṇpuṟutalēyākum. “paraṉainiṉaintimmūṉṟum viṭṭatu pēriṉpavīṭu” (oḷavaiyār). tolkāppiyattilum vīṭupeṟa niṟṟal tuṟavu eṉak kūṟappaṭṭatu. tirukkuṟaḷil nīttār perumaiyum itaṉaiyē kuṟik kiṉṟatu. caivacamayattiṉ muṭivu, “avaṉaruḷālē avaṉṟāḷ vaṇaṅki” avaṉai aṭaital.
“kāṇuṅ kaṇṇukkuk kāṭṭumuḷam pōṟ
kāṇavuḷ ḷattaik kaṇṭu kāṭṭaliṉ
ayarā vaṉpiṉ araṉkaḻal celumē” (ci. pō. -10cū.)
civa vaḻipāṭṭiṉ toṉmai
civa vaḻipāṭu aṟiya muṭiyāta paḻaimaiyuṭaiyatu. cintuveḷip putaipporuḷ ārāycci nikaḻttiya cēryōṉmārcal eṉpār civa matattiṉ paḻaimai kaṟkālattukkum appāṟ celkiṉṟateṉṟum, ip poḻutu ulakiṟ kāṇappaṭum camayaṅkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum atu muṟpaṭṭa teṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.1 paṉēci eṉpār, “mokañcotarō arappā eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaitta, kaḷimaṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷum, kaṭavuḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷum iliṅkaṅkaḷum nām itukāṟum eṇṇiyatai viṭat turkkai civa vaḻipāṭukaḷ mikap paḻaimaiyuṭaiyaṉaveṉṟu aṟi vikkiṉṟaṉa” eṉak kuṟippiṭṭaṉar.2
paḻaiya camayanūlkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭamaikkuk kāraṇam
muṉṉōriṭattiṟ kūṟiyāṅkut tamiḻmakkaḷ vīṭṭu neṟikkuriya uṇmai ñāṉaṅkaḷaik kurumāṇākkar muṟaiyiṟ payiṉṟuvantaṉar. iv vuṇmaikaḷait tamiḻarvāyilāka aṟinta āriyamakkaḷ cilar avaṟṟai maṟṟaiya tam kuḻuviṉarkaḷukku muṇarttuvāṉ viḻaintu, avaikaḷai avarkaḷ paṭittu viḷaṅkakkūṭiya moḻiyil veḷiyiṭṭaṉar. vaṭakki luḷḷavarkaḷiṉ moḻiyait teṟkiluḷḷavarkaḷum aṟintiruntaṉar. teṟkiluḷḷavarkaḷ tammiṭattiluḷḷaṉavum vaṭakkiluḷḷavarkaḷ naṉku aṟiyātaṉavumākiya uṇmaip poruḷkaḷai avarkaḷ kaṇṭu viyakku māṟum nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉar. itu, ik kālam tirukkuṟaḷ, cilappatikāram, tamiḻar nākarikam pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷait, tamiḻ aṟiyāta mēlnāṭṭu aṟiñarum vaṭanāṭṭu aṟiñarum kaṇṭu viyakkumāṟu tamiḻ aṟiñar āṅkilamoḻiyil veḷiyiṭṭu varutal pōṉṟatōr vaḻakku. āṅkila moḻiyil eḻutappaṭum nūlkaḷ ellām āṅkilamakkaḷ eḻutiyaṉavākā taṉapōla vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ ellām vaṭavar eḻutiyaṉa vākā. vaṭamoḻi vaṭakkē uḷḷavarkaḷatu eṉṟu karutappaṭiṉum teṟkē uḷḷavarkaḷē ataṉaiyum naṉku kaṟṟu am moḻiyil ariya nūlkaḷ iyaṟṟi ataṉaiyum vaḷampaṭuttiṉar. pōtāyaṉar, kāttiyāyaṉar, cāṇakkiyar, irāmāṉucar, caṅkarar, nīlakaṇṭar pōṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ciṟanta nūlkaḷ ceytu am moḻiyai vaḷampaṭuttiyavarkaḷ yāru millai. kapilarum avarmarapil vanta cittārtta puttarum tamiḻarkaḷē eṉpatu muṉpu viḷakkappaṭṭatu. paḻaiya kaṭṭiṭakkalai irukkiṉṟatu; entat tēcattiṟ kāṇappaṭum paḻaiya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷaiyum nōkki, avai iṉṉa nāṭṭuk kaṭṭiṭakkalai amaippuḷḷaṉa eṉa, an nūl aṟiñar eḷitil aṟintukūṟa vallunarāvar. ataṉai oppavē emmoḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭiruppiṉum nūlkaḷil poruḷaiyum pōkkaiyumkoṇṭu avai evarukkuriyaṉa eṉa eḷitil niccayam ceyyalām. tamiḻtteyvaṅ kaḷukku aiyarmār vaṭamoḻiyaic cāṟṟiyē pūcai ceykiṉṟārkaḷ eṉiṉ, itu vaṭamoḻiyil nampikkai vaitta piṟkāla aracarkaḷiṉ āṇaiyāl uṇṭāṉa vaḻakku. “vallāṉ vakuttatē vāykkāl” araciṉar talaiyīṭṭi ṉāl periya māṟṟaṅkaḷ eḷitil uṇṭākum. itaṟkuc cāṉṟu tāḻn tōrum tāḻttappaṭṭōrum ālayaṅkaḷiṟ pukuttappaṭuvatu oṉṟu; inti kaṭṭāyak kalviyāka vantatu maṟṟoṉṟu.
camayac coṟkaḷ
camayat toṭarpākat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum, āṇavam, kaṉmam, māyai, pati, pacu, pācam pōṉṟa coṟkaḷum piṟavum vaṭamoḻic coṟka ḷeṉap perumpālum karutappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. tamiḻmakkaḷil orucārār paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷil āḷappaṭṭuḷḷa coṟkaḷai māttiram tūya taṉit tamiḻc coṟkaḷākakkoṇṭu ēṉaiyavaikaḷaip piṟamoḻic coṟka ḷeṉa otukkivarukiṉṟaṉar. mēlnāṭṭu moḻikaḷil eḻutappaṭum nūl kaḷil ovvoru kalaikkum uriya taṉiccoṟkaḷ (kalaiccoṟkaḷ) āḷap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. im muṟai ellāmoḻiyiṉarukkum potu. yāpparuṅkala viruttiyil, āṭai nūl aṇikalanūl eṉa, ettaṉaiyō tuṟaikaḷait taṉittaṉi virittukkūṟum palanūlkaḷ tamiḻil nilaviṉa eṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ovvoru tuṟaikaḷaiyum viḷakka eḻunta taṉinūl kaḷil avvattuṟaikaḷukkuriya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ (kalaiccoṟkaḷ) āḷap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal kaṟpaṉai eṉal cālātu. paṟpala kāraṇacceṟivāl paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cila nīṅkalāka ēṉaiya māṇṭoḻintaṉaveṉpa tum kaṟpaṉaiyaṉṟu. ippoḻutu paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa perumpālaṉa ilakkiyac coṟkaḷē. iv vilakkiyac coṟkaḷai māttiram paḻantamiḻc coṟkaḷ eṉakkoṇṭu avaiyallāta piṟavaṟṟaip piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷ eṉa otukkiviṭutal niyāyamaṉṟu. iṉṟum, ōṭañcey vōr, āṭaineyvōr, ciṟpavēlaipurivōr, pāṉaicaṭṭivaṉaivōr pōṉṟu ovvoru toḻilpuripavarkaḷiṭaiyē avvattoḻilkaḷuk kuriyaṉavum yām aṟiyātaṉavumākiya pala tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkutalaik kāṇalām. yōvuṭupiṟiyil (Jouveau Dubreuil) eṉṉum pirāṉciyar eḻutiya teṉṉintiyak kaṭṭiṭakkalai eṉṉum nūlil kaṭṭiṭa uṟuppukaḷai uṇarttum pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai namakkup putiyaṉavākat tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. oru kalai enta makkaḷiṭattil, ātiyiṟ ṟōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭaikiṉṟatō, am makkaḷiṉ moḻiyil ataṟkuriya kalaiccoṟkaḷ pala tōṉṟivaḻaṅkum. akkalai iṉṉoru nāṭṭukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭu atu aṅku vaḷarcciyaṭaiyuṅkāl ak kalaic coṟkaḷ ayal moḻikaḷil karuttiṟkēṟpa moḻipeyarttu allatu, am moḻikaḷukkēṟṟa uccarippu muṟaiyil tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭutal iyalpu. muṟkālattu intiyāviṉiṉṟum ekiptu, kirīcu, pāpilōṉ, pālastiṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭa kuraṅku, mayil, arici, iñci, karuvā, tantam, tippili, āṭai mutaliyavaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ an nāṭṭu moḻikaḷil tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭamaiyē1 itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭu ākum.
āriyamakkaḷ intiyāvukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vantōr. ivarkaḷatu moḻiyum paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum kirēkka, urōma, tiyutōṉiya, cilāviya makkaḷiṉ moḻikkum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷukkum iṉamuṭaiyaṉa. makkaḷ tammaic cūḻntuḷḷa piṟamakkaḷiṉ cērkkaik kēṟpap paḻakkavaḻakkam, moḻi, kuṇam ākiyavaṟṟāl māṟupaṭum iyal piṉar; “nilattiyalpāl nīrtirin taṟṟākum māntarkku-iṉattiyalpa tāku maṟivu” (kuṟaḷ. 452). intu airōppiya makkaḷukkup potuvākiya coṟkaḷum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum palavuṇṭu. putitāka vanta āriyamakkaḷ putitākak kai yāṇṭa paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum putiya coṟkaḷaiyum aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭumāṉāl intu airōppiya makkaḷukkumāttiram potuvāyuḷḷa avaikaḷai intu āriya makkaḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟiliruntu kaḻittup pārttalvēṇṭum. intu airōppiya makkaḷukkup potu allātaṉavellām intiya āriya makkaḷukku, intiya pūrvamakkaḷiṭa miruntu kiṭaittaṉavē. āriya makkaḷukkuḷḷavaṟṟai āriyarukku illātavaṟṟiṉiṉṟu pirittaṟiya vēṇṭumāyiṉ im muṟaiyaik kaiyāḷa vēṇṭumeṉa ārāycci nuṇṇuṇarvu paṭaittār nuvalkiṉṟaṉar.2
āriyar muṉ vāḻnta nāṭu mikka kuḷir uṭaiyatu. avarkaḷ putitāka vantaṭainta mattiya veppataṭpa nilaiyuṭaiya nāṭṭil tām muṉ aṟintirunta kuḷirtēcat tāvaraṅkaḷaiyum paṟavaikaḷaiyum vilaṅku kaḷaiyum piṟavaṟṟaiyum kāṇamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷ intiyanāṭṭai aṭaintapiṉ tām putitāka aṟinta poruḷkaḷukku, āti makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷaiyē iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉpatil oru putumaiyum illai. ivvāṟu kol (Kol) moḻiyiṉiṉṟum vēta moḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu ēṟiyuḷḷa pala coṟkaḷai piṟisluski eṉpār, ‘āriyarukkum tamiḻarukkum muṟpaṭṭa intiyā’ eṉṉum nūlil naṉku ārāyntu kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. koṇṭar, muṇṭar, cāntāl, eṉappaṭum makkaḷ tamiḻariṉ piriviṉarē yeṉṟum, avarkaḷ moḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ vaḻakku niṉṟupōṉa paḻantamiḻc coṟkaḷeṉṟum ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ. itaṉai muṇṭar eṉṉum talaippiṉkīḻ viḷakkutum. cattēci eṉpār irukku vētattiṟ kāṇappaṭum aṇu, araṇi, kapi, karumārā, kālam, kāṉam, nāṉā (pala) nīlam, puṭpam, pūcāṉa pala (paḻam), pīcam, mayūram, irāttiri, uruvam mutaliya pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.1 tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ ceṉṟapōtu, muttā (muttā) mīṉā (mīṉ) eṉpaṉapōlac ciṟitu vēṟupāṭukaḷai aṭaintaṉa. ivvāṟu kalanta pala coṟkaḷ mēlum mēlum tirintu urutteriyāvakai karantaṉavāyiṉum, moḻi ārāycciyil ac col mūlaṅkaḷ tamiḻē eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv vakaic coṉmūlaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟai yāḻppāṇam, vaṇa. cuvāmi ñāṉappirakācar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻ oppiyal akarātiyiṟ kāṇalākum.
airōppiya āriyar intu āriyarkaḷiṉ camayaṅkaḷ orē vakai yiṉavalla. ippōtaiya allatu vētakāla āriyamatattiliruntu intu airōppiyarukkup potuvāṉa camayak koḷkaikaḷai kaḻittup pārppiṉ eñci niṟpaṉa tamiḻarkaḷuṭaiyaṉavē. im muṟaiyaikkoṇṭu vēta kālattilēyē tamiḻar camayakkoḷkaikaḷ āriya matattait taṉ vaṇṇa mākat tirikkattalaippaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa veṉpatu teḷitalkūṭum. mokañ cotarō kālattil tamiḻarukku ālayaṅkaḷum ālayak kiriyaikaḷum iruntaṉa. ālayaṅkaṭṭum vitikaḷaiyum ālaya vaḻipāṭṭaiyum paṟṟik (kiriyaikaḷ) kūṟum nūlkaḷ ākamam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. vētaṅkaḷuḷ ākamak koḷkaikaḷ (kiriyaimuṟaikaḷ) kalantuḷḷaṉaveṉṟu ārāycciyāḷar kaṇṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ākamak koḷkaikaḷ vētaṅkaḷil mukiḻttu āraṇi yakaṅkaḷ kālattil arumpi moṭṭākip pirāmaṇa kālattil muṟukkaviḻa lāyiṉa.1 pirāmaṇak kiriyaikaḷ ākamaṅkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟiṉavaiyē. āraṇiyakaṅkaḷ kālattu ākamaṅkaḷaippaṟṟiya pukaḻ iruntatu.2 tamiḻar uḷḷattiṟṟōṉṟi malarnta ākamak koḷkaikaḷ, upaniṭata ñāṉaṅkaḷ eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu toṭarpuṭaiya tamiḻccoṟkaḷ iruntaṉaveṉpatu naṉku uyttaṟitum. ‘paṇṭai intiyāvil makkaḷ vāḻkkai’ eṉṉum nūl eḻutiya pi. ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār avarkaḷ, “tamiḻarkaḷiṉ toḻil, kalait toṭarpāka vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ ellām, tamiḻ camakkirutatti ṉiṉṟu iraval peṟṟaṉavalla iv vārāycci naṉṟāka ārāyappaṭṭu muṭivu peṟavillai; ārāycciyil itu uṇmaiyāka muṭital kūṭumāṉatu. kalait toṭarpāka vaṭamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ tirāviṭa moḻiyi ṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉpeṟṟavai eṉṟu tirumpalām”3 eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
tirumūlar aruḷiya tirumantirattil camayattoṭarpāṉa coṟkaḷ nirampak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tām kūṟum ñāṉaṅkaḷ it tamiḻ ulakil muṉṉamē uḷḷaṉa eṉpatait “tamiḻmaṇ ṭalamaintum tāviya ñāṉam” “centami ḻāti teḷintu vaḻipaṭu - nanti yitaṉai navamuraittāṉē” eṉap palaviṭaṅkaḷiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tirumūlar nūlil vētameṉa varuvatu āriyamakkaḷiṉ pāṭalkaḷeṉat teriyavillai. vētam caṉakāti muṉivarukku iṟaivaṉāṟ kallāla nīḻaliṉkīḻiruntu aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭa teṉpatu, paṇṭu toṭṭuḷḷa tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ aitīkam. iṟaivaṉ mauṉamā yiruntē vētattai aṟivuṟuttār eṉpataṉāl tamiḻar kūṟum maṟai, pāṭal kaḷum pāṭaṟ ṟokuppukaḷumaṉṟu; tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷukku mauṉatēcikar aruḷiyatupōṉṟa upatēcam. tamiḻariṉ paṇṭaic camaya ñāṉaṅkaḷaik kūṟum tirumantirattil vantuḷḷa camayaccoṟkaḷ tamiḻ eṉak koḷḷalā meṉat terikiṉṟatu. caivacittāntameṉpatu tamiḻariṉ conta uṭaimai. cittāntapporuḷkaḷai uṇartta vaḻaṅkum coṟkaḷ ārāycciyiṉṟip piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷeṉa vilakkaṟpālaṉavalla. tamiḻccoṟkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyiṟṟirintu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu, avai maṟupaṭiyum tamiḻil vēṟu uruvat tuṭaṉ pukuntiruttal kūṭum. itaṟku utāraṇam ceṉṉaiyiluḷḷa ampaṭṭaṉ pālamē. ātiyil hāmilṭaṉ pālameṉappaṭṭa ip pālam uccarippōratu cōrviṉāl ampaṭṭaṉ pālamāka nilavukiṉṟatu. āṅkila moḻiyil atu “pārpars piriṭj” eṉa vaḻaṅkum. “hāmilṭaṉ” “pārpar” (ampaṭṭaṉ) āṉatupōlap pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyiṟ pukuntu tamatu uruvattai māṟṟikkoṇṭu mīṇṭum tamiḻukku vantiruk kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu yām karutukiṉṟōm.
“āriyamakkaḷ intiyanāṭṭai aṭaivataṉmuṉ tamiḻarukku, nākarikamillai; camayamillai; kalaikaḷillai. āriyar vanta piṉputāṉ tamiḻar āriyaraip pārttu mellamella nākarikamaṭaintu miruka nilaiyi ṉiṉṟu mēlnōkki vantārkaḷ” eṉṉum pollāta koḷkai paḻaiya varalāṟṟāciriyariṭattiṟ kuṭikoṇṭiruntamaiyāl avarkaḷ tamiḻar kaḷiṭattuk kāṇappaṭum uyarvukaḷellām āriyamakkaḷiṉ cērkkai yāl nērntaṉaveṉṟu kuruṭṭuttaṉamākak kuṟippiṭṭaṉar. cēryōṉ mārcal eṉpavarē (mokañcotarō aḻipāṭṭu ārāyccikku) oru kaṇattukkumuṉ “tamiḻarkaḷ oru kālattil nākarikamaṭaintiruntārka ḷeṉṟu ivvulakam aṟiyavillai” eṉa viyappuṟṟuk kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
vaṭa intiya makkaḷ taṉi āriyarē eṉṟu karutiviṭutal kūṭātu. avarkaḷiṭaiyē neruṅkiya tirāviṭak kalappu uṇṭu. itaṉāl avar kaḷiṉ moḻiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum māṟupaṭṭaṉa.2
mokañcotarōt tamiḻariṉ camayam
heras pātiriyār mokañcotarōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa āyirat teṇṇūṟu paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷai naṉku ārāyntum, avaikaḷil eḻutap paṭṭavaṟṟai olimuṟaiyil vācittum aṟinta uṇmaikaḷ cilavaṟṟai mātaveḷiyīṭukaḷ vāyilākap pulappaṭuttiyuḷḷār. avaṟṟiṉ curukkamō virivō itukāṟum tamiḻil veḷivantila. pampāyp palkalaikkaḻaka veḷiyīṭṭil (yūlāy-1936) avar veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa kaṭṭurai oṉṟaic curukki īṇṭut tarukiṉṟōm.
oru paṭṭaiyattil “iṟuvaṉ kommiṉṉir” eṉap poṟikkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟatu. (kaṭavuḷiṉ) kompu vāttiyamūtum mīṉavar eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. iṉṉoru paṭṭaiyattil “kōyil ellāk kaṭavuḷ āṇ” eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ālayattiluḷḷa ellāk kaṭavuḷukkum talaivar eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
iṉṉoru paṭṭaiyattil nālūriyirukkum kaṭavuḷ “viṭukaṇ” eṉak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. viṭukaṇ eṉpatu tiṟanta kaṇ. kaṭavuḷ imaiyā tavar (“imaiyā nāṭṭam” uṭaiyavar) ik kālat tamiḻar aitīkattōṭu itu ottuḷḷatu. kaṭavuḷ makkaḷukku ellā utaviyum aḷippavar eṉavum ak kālattavar nampiṉar. itu, “itu utavu miṉ aṭu āṇ” eṉap poṟik kappaṭṭa paṭṭaiyattāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. (kaṭavuḷ) utavi ceykiṉṟa mīṉavariṉ kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ.
oru muttiraiyil mūṉṟu mukamum yōkattil vīṟṟirukkum kōlamumuṭaiya oru vaṭivam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaṭivaic cuṟṟip pala vilaṅkukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. itu, kaṭavuḷ pacupati eṉṉum karuttai viḷakka amaikkappaṭṭa vaṭivam eṉak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. pala kulak kuṟi kaḷai (Totem) yuṭaiya nāṭṭu makkaḷ ellārum vaḻipaṭum kaṭavuḷ eṉpatai viḷakka itu kaṟ ciṟpiyāl veṭṭappaṭṭatākalāṉ.
ōriṭattil kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarāka eṇmai eṉṉum col kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟatu. iṉṉoru muttirai kaṭavuḷ eṭṭāka irukkiṟār eṉak kūṟu kiṉṟatu. kaṭavuḷ mīṉ vaṭivil nāṭṭil pala pakutikaḷil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭār. talaiyilē cūlam uṭaiya kaṭavuḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷ muttiraikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. kaṭavuḷ mūṉṟu kaṇṇuṭaiyavareṉṟu muttiraikaḷ teḷivākak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru kaṇṇum taṉittaṉi vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. paṭṭai yaṅkaḷil mūṉṟu kaṇ eṉpatu “mūṉkaṇ” eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. mūṉṟu kaṇṇuṭaiya periya mīṉ eṉa oru paṭṭaiyattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāykkaṭavuḷ mīṉ kaṇṇi eṉappaṭukiṉṟār. mīṉ kaṇṇiyē mīṉāṭci eṉa ik kālattu maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu pōlum.
mēṟku āciyā nāṭukaḷilum kaṭavuḷariṉ talaiyil iraṇṭu kom pukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aracarum kompukaḷai aṇintaṉar. kompukaḷ mēlāṉa atikārattaik kuṟippaṉa veṉṟum avai ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir kaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷeṉṟum karutappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
“The husband God like Baal of Tarsus was identified by the Greeks with Zeus and Lucian tells us that the resemblance of his image to the images of Zeus was in all respects unmistakable. But his image unlike those of Zeus was seated upon bulls. A similar God of thunder and lightning was worshipped from early times by the Babylonians and Assyrians. His image is represented.........wearing a cap with two horns - Golden Bough.
viṭukaṇ, eṇmai, āṇ, kaṭavuḷ, pērāṇ, tāṇṭavaṉ eṉpaṉa kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarkaḷāka mokañcotarō makkaḷāl āḷappaṭṭaṉa. nālu maraṅkaḷiṉ iṭaiyiluḷḷa tāṇṭavaṉ eṉa oru paṭṭaiyaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
mokañcotarō makkaḷ iliṅkattai vaḻipaṭṭārkaḷ eṉpatu aiyamiṉṟi viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. mokañcotarō arappā eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷil iv vakaik kaṟkaḷ pala kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. mīṉavar iv vaḻipāṭṭai ārampattil etirttārkaḷeṉat terikiṉṟatu. mīṉavar piṟkālattil maccayar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. iliṅkaṅkaḷākiya kaṭavuḷarukku vīṭukaḷ mutaliya māṉiyaṅkaḷ viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
cumēriya makkaḷiṉ mummūrttikaḷ āṇ, ammā, eṉlil eṉpaṉa. tamiḻariṉ mummūrttikaḷ āṇ, ammaṉ, murukaṉ. ammaṉ mīṉkaṇṇi eṉap paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷār. pākam peṇṇāyuḷḷa vaṭivam (arttanārīcura vaṭivam) kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaṭivam cumēriyāvilu muṇṭu. kaḻaṉikaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ, iṭikkaṭavuḷ, cākkaṭavuḷ (yamaṉ), mīṉ kaṭavuḷ eṉpaṉa akkāla makkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. cūlaṅkaḷ ālayaṅ kaḷilum vīṭukaḷilum vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. oru paṭṭaiyattil pāmpu mūṉṟu kaṇṇuṭaiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. cūlattukkup patil kōṭariyum naṭṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu.
cila paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷil makkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷaiyum tiyā ṉittārkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. puṉitamuṭaiya kaṭavuḷ tiru uruvaṅ kaḷmītu taṇṇīr allatu pāl teḷikkappaṭṭatu. (av vaḻakku iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu). tuti pāṭutal, kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarai uccarittal, (cepittal) nērttikkaṭaṉ ceytalpōṉṟa vaṇakkaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa. aracaṉ uḻavaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. aracaṉē ālayaṅkaḷil aiyarākaviruntāṉ.
viruntē ak kāla viḻāvākat terikiṟatu. (poṅkal, kurupūcai, caṅkāpiṭēkam, viḻākkaḷ eṉpavaikaḷil iṉṟum makkaḷukku uṇavu aḷikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.) ovvoru kirāmattilum vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭa cūlam allatu cūlattait talaiyiluṭaiya kaṭavuḷ aṭutta kirāmattukkuk koṇṭuppōkappaṭṭatu. akkālam periya viḻākkaḷ koṇṭāṭap paṭṭaṉa. nakarattil vītikaḷ cantikkumiṭattil ālayaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. teṉṉantōppukaḷiṉ mattiyil iliṅkaṅkaḷ vaittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭukaḷilum ciṟiya kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉa.
iṟantavarkaḷaic cuṭutalum putaittalum akkāla vaḻakku. iṟantapiṉ niyāyat tīrppu naṭappatāka makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. ñāyattīrppu eṉpataṟkut “tīrppu” eṉṉum col muttiraikaḷil āḷappaṭṭiruttal nōkkattakkatu.

iyal - 8
intiyāvum mēṟku ulakamum
nōvā pēḻai kaṭṭutaṟku muṉpu ekiptiyar tamiḻ nāṭṭukku marakkalaṅkaḷiṟ payaṇañceyta varalāṟu avarkaḷiṉ ōviya eḻuttu nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. paṇṭu nāṭṭukkup payaṇañceyta kaṉṉu eṉṉum mālumi kūṟiyiruppataṉ viparam varumāṟu: “nāṉ cēpā eṉṉum tuṟaimukattai aṭaintēṉ. yāṉ koṇṭuvaravēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷ ellām kappalil ēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. yāṉ erutukaḷaiyum pacukkaḷaiyum āṭu kaḷaiyum pali koṭuttēṉ. yāṉ paṇṭu eṉṉum paricutta nāṭṭiliruntu aracaṉukku vēṇṭiya ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum koṇṭuvantēṉ.”
ki. mu. 1600il paṇṭu tēcattukkuc ceṉṟa iṉṉoru payaṇattiṉ muḻu viparamum ōviya eḻuttu nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu isravēlar ekiptil maṟiyaṟpaṭuttappaṭṭiruntārkaḷ. ataṉ viparam varu māṟu: “patiṉoru kappalkaḷil paṇṭutēcattiṉ viyappu mikka poruḷkaḷ ēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. avai vilai uyarnta maraṅkaḷ kuṅkuliyampōṉṟa maṇapporuḷkaḷ, maṇamaraṅkaḷ, amu eṉṉum nāṭṭiṟ kiṭaikkum poṉṉaḻuttiya yāṉaittantam, kaṇṇukkut tīṭṭum mai, nāyttalaik kuraṅku, vēṭṭaināy, pulittōl, an nāṭṭu makkaḷ kuḻantaikaḷ eṉpaṉa.”
ekiptiya moḻiyil kuraṅkaiyum yāṉait tantattaiyum kuṟikka kavu, ep eṉṉum coṟkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivai muṟaiyē kavi ipam eṉṉum coṟkaḷiṉ tiripu.1
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṉ toṭṭu intiyāvukkum mēṟku āciyāvukkumiṭaiyil mūṉṟu vaṇikap perumpātaikaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mika ilakuvāṉatu cintunati mukattuvārattiliruntu arāpiyak kuṭākkaṭal vaḻiyāka yūpirātas mukattuvārattai aṭaiyum kaṭaṟpātai. iṉṉoṉṟu pāracīkattiṉūṭākat taraivaḻiyē celvatu. ceṅkaṭal vaḻiyākac celvatu maṟṟoṉṟu.
cintunati mukattuvārattukkum yūpirātas paḷḷattākkukaḷukkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa vāṇikam mikattoṉmaiyuṭaiyatu. iv vāṇikattaik kuṟitta ceyti mittaṉi (Mitanni) nāṭṭaiyāṇṭa kitaiti aracaṉiṉ paṭṭayaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ip paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷ ki.mu. 14ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭavai. acurpāṇip pāliṉ (Assurbanipal) puttaka nilaiyattil (ki. mu. 668-626) kāṇappaṭṭa cintu1 eṉṉuñcol intiyap pañcu eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ic col mikap paḻamai yuṭaiyatu. ic col akkēṭiya moḻiyil tāvara āṭai eṉṉum poruḷil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. acurpāṇippāl eṉṉum aracaṉ perumpayirt toḻil purivōṉāyiruntāṉ. avaṉ intiyāviṉiṉṟum ‘kampaḷi kāykkum maraṅkaḷai’p peṟṟāṉ. mēṟku āciyāvil naṭaipeṟṟa pañcu viyāpāram mikap paḻamaiyuṭaiyatu. kirēkkar mutal mutal intiyaraip paṟṟi aṟiyavantapōtu avarkaḷ ‘marattiṟ kāykkum kampaḷiyai’ uṭuttiruntārkaḷ. irukku vētattil iravum pakalum iraṇṭu necavu ceyyum peṇkaḷāka uvamikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1 mukayarilē (Mugheir-ur of Chaldees) tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷiṉ ālayattilum nēpucaṇ nicariṉ (ki. mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) araṇmaṉaiyilum intiyat tēkkamarat tuṇṭukaḷ kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. tēkku, karuṅkāli, cantaṉam mutaliya maraṅkaḷ parkācā (Bargaza) puṟūc eṉappaṭṭa intiyāviṉ mēṟkuk karaiyiluḷḷa tuṟai mukattiliruntu taikiracukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. iv vāṇikam ki. pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil naṭaipeṟṟatu.
cālamaṉ nēcariṉ (Salamanesar III ki. mu. 860) tūṇil kuraṅku, intiya yāṉai, paktīriya (paracīka) oṭṭakam mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. pavēru cātakam (ki. mu. 500) intiya vaṇikar pavērukku (pāpilōṉ) kaṭalvaḻiyākac ceṉṟa varalāṟṟaik kūṟukiṉṟatu. aṅkup paṟavaikaḷ arumai. intiya vaṇikar mutaṉmuṟai oru kākattai aṅkuk koṇṭuceṉṟaṉar. an nāṭṭu makkaḷ ataṉ aḻakaip pārttu atikam viyantārkaḷ. aṭutta payaṇattil av vaṇikar naṉṟāka āṭum mayil oṉṟaikkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. mayiliṉ aḻakāṟ kavarappaṭṭa an nāṭṭumakkaḷ kākkaiyiṉ aḻakaik koṇṭāṭiṉārkaḷallar.
paṇṭaik kālattil intiyar aṉupavam vāynta kaṭalōṭikaḷāka viruntaṉar. paḻaiya intiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ intiyariṉ tuṇikaramāṉa kappaṟ payaṇaṅkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. piṟkālaṅkaḷil mattiya taraikaḷil ōṭiya kappalkaḷaiviṭap periya kappalkaḷai intiyar kaṭṭiṉar. ki. mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaliṅka nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ilaṅkaikkuc ceṉṟa vicaya irācaṉiṉ kappal 700 makkaḷai ēṟṟicceṉṟatu. cātakak kataikaḷ munnūṟu mutal eḻunūṟu pērai ēṟṟiccellakkūṭiya marakkalaṅkaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. kiḻakkuk karaiyiluḷḷa periya tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ campā, tāmiralipti eṉpaṉa; mēṟkiluḷḷa paṟukācā cuppirā eṉpaṉa. vinōta māṉa paṟavaikaḷ, cintukkutiraikaḷ, tantam, āṭaikaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ, poṉ, veḷḷi mutaliyaṉa it tuṟaimukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum veḷināṭu kaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. ak kāla makkaḷ kaṭalvaḻiyāka ayalnāṭu kaḷukkuc celvatu cātāraṇa nikaḻcci. keveṭṭucūtta (Keveddhusutta) eṉṉum nūlil karaiteriyāta kaṭalil karaiyaik kāṭṭum paṟavaikaḷuṭaṉ vaṇikar payaṇamceyta varalāṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. paṟavaikaḷ eppoḻu tum karaiyirukkum ticaiyai nōkkip paṟakkum iyalpuṭaiyaṉa. karaiyai aṟivataṟku nōvā paṟavaikaḷait tiṟantuviṭṭa varalāṟu kiṟittuvamaṟai yiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. piḷiṉi, kosmas eṉpōr karaiyai aṟi vataṟkuk kuruvikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttum vaḻakkam ciṅkaḷa makkaḷiṭaiyē uṇṭu eṉa eḻutiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
pāracīkak kuṭākkaṭal viyāpāram ārampattil cālaṭiyarvacam iruntatu. ivarkaḷ kaṭaṟkoḷḷaikkārarākaviruntaṉar. ki. mu. 694il ik kaṭaṟkoḷḷaikkārar ceṉṉācerip (Sennacherib) eṉṉum cālaṭiya aracaṉāl oḻikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. iv vāṇikam piṉpu poṉīciyar kaikku māṟiṟṟu. pāracīkak kuṭāviluḷḷa pakēriṉ (Bharein) tīvukaḷil ivarkaḷ vāṇikam naṭattiyataik kuṟippiṭum paḻaiya putaiporuḷkaḷ kiṭait tuḷḷaṉa.
ki. mu. 606il pāpilōṉiyar acīriyarai veṉṟaṉar. appoḻutu niṉēvā kiḻakkuttēcaṅkaḷiṉ periya cantaiyāka viḷaṅkiṟṟu. aṅku, poṉīciyar, yūta aṭimaikaḷ, ayōṉiyar, intiyar mutaliyōr tamatu viyāpārap paṇṭaṅkaḷai viṟkum poruṭṭuk kūṭiṉārkaḷ. ak kālattil pāpilōṉiyar intiyakkaraikaḷiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyiruntiruntār kaḷātal kūṭum. alekcāntar intiyāvai aṭaintapōtu pāpilōṉil naṭattappaṭuvatu pōṉṟa vivākaccantaikaḷ1 aṅku naṭattappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu srāpō kūṟiyuḷḷār.
intiyāvukkum cemattiya makkaḷukkumiṭaiyiluḷḷa vaṇikam perumpālum kaṭalvaḻiyāka naṭaipeṟṟatu.
paḻaiya āciya nākarikam intiyā, cīṉā, ilaṅkai, mattiyatarai nāṭukaḷ varaiyil parantu kāṇappaṭṭatu. iv viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaitta paḻaiyamaṇṭapaṅkaḷ, ulōkap poruḷkaḷ, necavupporuḷkaḷ eṉpaṉa orēvakaiyiṉa. kiḻakku mattiyatarai, nākarika ulakiṉ mēṟku ellaiyāka orupōtu iruntatu.
kaḻutai oṭṭakam potimāṭu eṉpavaikaḷil paṇṭaṅkaḷai ēṟṟiccellum vaṇikar kūṭṭattiṉar, mika muṟkālantoṭṭu intiyā, cīṉā, mēṟku āciyā eṉpavaikaḷait toṭuttirunta perumpātaiyāṟ pira yāṇañ ceytaṉar. okcas (Oxus) āṟṟiliruntu kaspiyaṉ kaṭalvarai yil paṇṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭumaraṅkaḷ vāyilākak koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa.
intiyat tuṟaimukaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum carakkukaḷ ēṭiṉ tuṟaimukat tukkuc ceṉṟaṉa. calamaṉ aracaṉ poṉīciya aracaṉiṉ naṭpaippeṟṟup poṉīciya mālumikaḷiṉ utaviyāl poṉ, veḷḷi, yāṉaittantam, vālillāk kuraṅku, mayil, akil, irattiṉakkaṟkaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷai intiyāviṉiṉṟum peṟṟāṉ. opir poṉṉukkup pērpōṉatu; ākavē, epirēyattil ‘opirp poṉ’ eṉṉum mutumoḻi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. opir eṉpatu intiyāviluḷḷateṉpatu uṭaṉē teḷivākiṉṟatu. yūtar nūl kaḷil intiyamoḻic coṟkaḷ pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
paṇṭaik kirēkkap pulavarākiya hōmar āppirikka etiyōp piyar, kiḻakku etiyōppiyar eṉṉum iru makkaḷaippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. avar kūṟiya kiḻakku etiyōppiyar eṉpatu tirāviṭaraik kuṟikkum. hōmar kālattil intiyāvum āppirikkāvum oṉṟāka iṇaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉaveṉṟum, intumākkaṭal, mattiyataraikkaṭal iruppatupōla muṉpu iṭaiyil iruntateṉṟum nampappaṭṭatu. epi rēyattilum camakkirutattilum kirēkkaraik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya col yavaṉa eṉpatu.
keratōtacu ki. mu. 484il piṟantār. ivar tamatu nūlil intiyaraip paṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷār. avar kūṟuvatu: “intiya makkaḷ vevvēṟu moḻi kaḷai vaḻaṅkum pala cātiyiṉarāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. teṟkēyiruppōr etiyōppiyaraip pōṉṟōr. aṅku ōr uyiraiyuṅ kollāta oru kūṭṭattiṉarum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tāṉiyaṅkaḷaiyum tāvaraṅ kaḷaiyum uṇavākak koḷvar.”
“intiyāvil oruvakai eṟumpukaḷ uṇṭu. ivai puṟṟeṭukkum pōtu veḷiyē taḷḷum maṇaloṭu poṉtūḷum veḷiyē varukiṉ ṟatu. av veṟumpukaḷ nāyiṟ ciṟiyaṉavum nariyiṟ periyaṉavumāy iruk kum. avai poṉṉaik kāval kāttukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. naṭuppakalil avai puṟṟukkuḷ iṟaṅkicceṉṟu nittirai koḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. intiyar peṇ oṭṭakaṅkaḷil ceṉṟu poṉtūḷkaḷaic cākkukaḷil kaṭṭik koṇṭu viraintu celkiṉṟaṉar. av veṟumpukaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟaip pāracīka aracaṉ piṭittu vaittirukkiṉṟāṉ.
ik katai, camakkirutattiluḷḷa pappulikkā (Paippilika) eṉṉum eṟumput taṅkam eṉṉum poruḷ tarum colliṉiṉṟum muḷaittatā yirukkalām. ak kālattil tatistāṉ (Dardistan) eṉṉumiṭattil taṅkam arikkappaṭṭatu. keratōtacu intiyap pañcu kampaḷiyilum nayamāṉatu eṉṟum, intiya makkaḷ ataṉāl āṭai neytārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟi yuḷḷār.
keratōtacuvukkuppiṉ intiyāvaippaṟṟik kūṟiyavar keṟciyas (Ktesias) eṉṉum kirēkkar (ki. mu. 416) ivar intiyāvaippaṟṟip piṟar vāyilāka aṟintavaikaḷait tiraṭṭi intikkā (Indika) eṉṉum nūl ceytuḷḷār. ivar kūṟiyiruppaṉa perumpālum kaṭṭukkataipōlvaṉa. ivar karuvāppaṭṭaiyaippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭirukkiṉṟār. ivar ataṉaik ‘kāruppu’ (Karuppu) eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. intiyāvil nañcai aṟiyum puṟāvum kāyattai āṟṟum kallum iruntaṉaveṉṟum, aṅku oṟṟaik kompu muḷaitta kaḻutaikaḷum kutiraikaḷum uṇṭu eṉṟum, ak kompukaḷiṟ ceyta cimiḻkaḷiṟ pāṉañceyyiṉ viṭattiṉvalimai talai kāṭṭa muṭiyāteṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. (kākkai nañcai aṟiyum iyalpiṉateṉṟum, kāṇṭāmirukattiṉ kompil ūṟṟi uṇṇum pāṉaṅkaḷil nañcu kalantiruppiṉum tīmai nērāteṉṟum iṉṟum intiyamakkaḷ nampi varukiṉṟaṉar.)pāṇiṉi muṉivar peṇpāl pōṉṟa yavaṉi eṉṉum collaik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. itaṟku uraiyeḻutiya kārttiyāyaṉar atu yavaṉa ilipiyaik kuṟikkumeṉa varaintuḷḷār.
alakcāntar ki. mu. 329il intiyāmītu paṭai eṭuttuvantār. ki.mu. 315il pañcāppil kirēkkar āṭci oḻintatu. ataṉmēlum kirēkka intiyar kalappiṟṟōṉṟiya makkaḷ palar pañcāppiṟ kāṇappaṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kālattil aṅkuḷḷa makkaḷōṭu kalantu maṟaintuviṭṭaṉar. cantirakupta aracaṉē kirēkkap peṇṇai maṇantiruntāṉ. vaṭa intiyā vil cakar eṉappaṭum skittiyar āṭci ki. mu. 93il toṭaṅkiyatu.
1tālami piḷāppis (ki. mu. 321) kālattil intiya vēṭṭai nāy kaḷum, intiyap pacumāṭukaḷum ekiptil kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. intiyap peṇ kaḷum aṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. intiya maṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ oṭṭakaṅkaḷi lēṟṟikkoṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa.
